Explanatory Remarks:

1. Structural elements are enclosed in <>.
2. Editorial notes or additions are enclosed in () or [].

Text:

kāvyādarśavyākhyā hṛdayaṅgamā

<1>

prathamaparicchedaḥ

praṇamya kamalākāntaṃ bhaktābhīṣṭaphalapradam| kāvyādarśasya vivṛtiṃ vitanomi satāṃ mude| | kiṃvadantīyaṃ yat mahāmahopādhyāyaḥ śrīmān daṇḍīnāma kaviḥ granthamimaṃ svayaṃ viracya kamapi rājaputram adhyāpayāmāsa iti| granthārambhe nirvighnena prāripsitaparisamāptikāmanayā kavigrranthādhiṣṭātrītayā vāgdevatāyāḥ smaraṇarūpamaṅgalamācarannāha, caturmukheti| caturmukhasya brahmaṇaḥ, mukhānyeva ambhojavanāni padmavṛndāni, teṣu haṃsabadhūḥ haṃsausvarūpā, (etena viśeṣaṇapadena caturmukhamukhavihāriṇau śrutirūpā sarasvatī yathā pariśuddhā tādṛśī sarasvatadī mama mānasagatā bhavatu, na tu nīcajamukhavasatiriti pratīyate) | ata eva viśe, ṇāntaramāha, sarvaśukleti| sarvaśuklā sarvataḥ sarvaprakāreṇa, svarūpataḥ arthataśca ityarthaḥ, śuklā doṣarahitatvena nimrmalā, kāvyāpakarṣakadoṣahīnoti yāvat, sarvāvayavena śvetavarṇāca, sarasvatī vāgadhiṣṭhātrī devī, bhagavato viṣṇormūrtiriti yāvat, (etenāsyāḥ kāvyarūpatā pratīyate, kāvyarūpāyāśca viṣṇumūrttitvaṃ viṣṇupurīṇe'bhihitaṃ yathā, "kāvyālāpāśca ye kecidgītakanyakhilāni ca| śabdamūrttidharasyaite viṣṇoraṃśā mahātmanaḥ| | " iti) , nityaṃ satataṃ, mama mānase cetasi, tadākhyasarasi ca, ramatāṃ viharatu| mānase sarasi padmavaneṣu haṃsīva yā brahmaṇo mukhakamaleṣu śrutirūpeṇa satataṃ viharati, sā sarvaśuddhā vāgdavī mama hṛdaye satataṃ vasatu iti niṣkarṣaḥ| " nityam" ityatra "dīrgham" iti pāṭhe dīrghaṃ suciramityarthaḥ| (atra mukhe padmavanatvāropaḥ sarasvatyāṃ haṃsavadhūtvārope hetuḥ, ataḥ paramparitarūpakālaṅkāraḥ) | | 1| | 

samprati "jñātārthaṃ jñātasambandhaṃ śrotuṃ śrotā pravatrtate| granthādau tena vaktavyaḥ sambandhaḥ saprayojanaḥ| | " iti śāstramanusmaran granthasyāsya abhideyaṃ nirdiśan pratijānīte, pūrvasāstrāṇīti| pūrveṣāṃ kavīnāṃ bharatādīnāṃ, śāstrāṇi kāvyagranthān, saṃhṛtya saṅkṣepeṇa tebhyaḥ sāramākṛṣya, perayogān mahākāvyādiṣu prayuktān viṣayān, upalabhya samālocya ca (etana mahākaviprayogaviruddhaṃ prācīnaśāstramapi parihṛtya prayogānusāripūrvaśāstrānusāreṇā smābhiretat kriyate iti sūcitam) | asmābhiḥ yathāsāmathryaṃ yathāśakti, yathājñānamiti yāvat, kāvyasya lakṣaṇaṃ svarūpaṃ, lakṣyate jñāyate aneneti vyutpattyā lakṣaṇam itaravyavacachedakaḥ asāmānyadharma iti yāvat, kriyate "iṣṭārthavyavacchinnā padāvalī" iti vakṣyamāṇavākyena nirṇīyate| (etena kāvyameva granthasya abhidheyaṃ darśitaṃ, prayojanañcātaḥ paraṃ vakṣyate| sambandhaśca yathāyathaṃ svadhiyānirūpaṇīyaḥ) | | 2| | 

idānīṃ granthasya prayojanakathanāya vācāṃ vyavahāropayogitvam anvayavyatirekābhyāmāha, iheti ca| iha saṃsāre, śiṣṭaiḥ dhīraiḥ maheśvarādibhiḥ, (taduktaṃ mahābhārate" na pāṇipādacapalo na netracapalo muniḥ| na ca vāgaṅgacapala iti śiṣṭasya lakṣaṇam| | " iti) , anuśiṣṭānāṃ sādhitānāṃ, prakṛtipratyayādivibhāgena siddhānāṃ pracalitānāṃ prākṛtadeśīyānāṃ, vācāmeva prasādena anukampayā, sāhāyyeneti yāvat, sarvathā sarveḥ prakāraiḥ, lokānāmuttamamadhyamādhamabhedena trividhānāṃ, yātrā vyavahāraḥ, pravatrtate peracalati, tatrottamāḥ saṃskṛtayaiva vācā vyavaharanti, madhyamāstato nikṛṣṭayā sādhubhāṣayā, adhamā nīcabhāṣayā, deśajātibhedena evameva bhāṣāvyavahāra iti phalitārthaḥ| | 3| | 

idamiti| yadi śabdaḥ āḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavayaḥ abhidhānaṃ yasya tat śabdanāmakaṃ, vāṅgamayamityarthaḥ, jyotiḥ tejaḥ, prakāśakasvarūpadharmabalena vāṅmaye tathātvāropaḥ (etena śabdasya brahmatvañca sūcitam| śabdabrahmaṇaśca sūkṣmā paśyantī madhyamā vaikharī ceti catvāro bhedāḥ, tatrāvikāradaśā sūkṣmā, sā hi sarvasya prāṇāpānāntarālavartinī vigataprādurbhāvatirobhāvā samyakprayogapariśīlanātmanā karmayogena, mananādinā jñānayogena ca samyagadhigamyate| tasya brahmaṇaḥ anādyavidyāvaśādādyaḥ pariṇāmaḥ paśyantī, sā hi varṇāvibhāgādakramaḥ svayaṃprakāśaśca| pūrvāpara svāvasthe paśyatīti paśyantītyucyate| tataḥ paramavidyopādānādantaḥ saṅkalparūpakramavān śrotragrāhyavarṇābhivyaktirahitastṛtīyaḥ pariṇāmaḥ madhyamā| dvayoḥ pariṇāmayormadhye tiṣṭhatīti madhyamā ityucyate| anantaraṃ dūraprasṛtāyāmavidyāyāṃ sthānakaraṇaprayatnavyajyamānaśrotrasaṃvādivīṇādundubhinādaparicayagadgadāvyaktākārādivarṇasamudayātmakasturīyaḥ pariṇāmaḥ vaikharī| ādyāstisnaḥ nityā atīndriyāḥ, caramā lokavyavahārarūpā iti, taduktaṃ, "sthāneṣu vivṛte vāyau kṛtavarṇaparigrahā| vaikharī vāk prayoktṛṇāṃ prāṇavṛttinibandhanā| | prāṇavṛttimatikramya madyamā vāk pravatrtate| avibhāgāttu paśyantī sarvataḥ saṃhṛtakramā| | svarūpajyotirevāntaḥ sūkṣmā sā cānapāyinī| | " iti| śrutirapyāha, "catvāri vāk parimitā padāni tāni vidubrrāhmaṇā ye manīṣiṇaḥ| guhā trīṇi nihitā neṅgayanti turīyaṃ vāco manuṣyā vadanti" iti| tataśca tat śabdabrahmarūpaṃ jyotiḥ, āsaṃsāraṃ jagat abhivyāpya, na dīpyate na virājate, tadā idaṃ kṛtsnaṃ sarvaṃ, bhuvanānāṃ svargamattyarasātalānāṃ, trayam andhatamaḥ gāḍhāndhakāravyāptamiti yāvat, ("dhvānte gāḍhe'ndhatamasam" ityamaraḥ) , jāyeta| yadvā, tamaḥ mohāndhakāramayaṃ, sat, andhaṃ netrahīnamiva jñānaśūnyaṃ bhavet| sūyryodayābhāve iva vāgabhāve jagat andhakāramayameva syāt, vāgbhireva sarveṣāṃ jñānalābhāditi bhāvaḥ| (etena asya prayojanaṃ vāgvyavahārarūpameva kathitam| abhidheyaprayojanayośca parasparaṃ jñāpyajñāpakatvādirūpaḥ sambandhaḥ yathāyathamavagantavyaḥ| eṣāñca granthādau avaśyavaktavyatoktāiti) | | 4| | 

vācāmupādeyatvaṃ veyavahāratvena prāk pradaśrya tāsāmeva guṇālaṅkārādisabhdāvena kāvyasvarūpatāmupagatānāmidānīmupādeyatvaṃ darśayati, ādirājeti| ādirājānāṃ manvikṣvākuprabhṛtīnāṃ, yaśovimbaṃ kīrttirūpaṃ prativimbaṃ katrtṛ, vāṅgamayaṃ kavigaṇanibaddhaṃ kāvyaprabandharūpam, ādarśaṃ darpaṇaṃ, prāpya teṣām ādirājānām, asannidhāne'piasattāyāmapi, na naśyati na vilīyate, iti svayaṃ paśya avalokaya iti rājaputraṃ prayuktiḥ| (darpaṇe vimbapatanaṃ sannihitānāmeva, iha tu vāṅmayarūpe darpaṇe asannihitānāmapīti upameyādhikyavarṇavyatirekālaṅkāraḥ| tataścapurāvṛttavarṇitānāṃ yaśaḥ kītrtanaṃ tathā kāvyapraṇetuḥ yaśaḥ prāptiprabhṛtīnyapi prayojanāntarāṇyavagantavyāni; taccokte prakāśakṛtā yathā, "kāvyaṃ yaśase'rthakvate vyavahāravide śivetarakṣataye| sadyaḥ paranirvṛtaye kāntāsammitatayopadeśayuje| | "iti) | | 5| | 

kāvyasyopādeyatvaṃ sāmānyenoktā tasya guṇālaṅkārādisadbhāvatve doṣābhāvatve ca yatitavyamiti kītrtayannāha, gauriti| samyak prayuktā guṇālaṅkārādimattvena doṣarāhityena ca sunibaddhā, gauḥ vāk, ("svargeṣupaśuvāgvajradiṅnetraghṛṇibhūjale| lakṣyadṛṣṭyā striyāṃpuṃsigauḥ"ityamaraḥ) , budhaiḥ vidvadbhiḥ, sahṛdayaiḥ sudhībhirityarthaḥ, kāmadughā kāmadohinī, yathā'bhilaṣitapūraṇīti yāvat, gauḥ dhenuḥ smayryate manyate| "ekaḥ śabdaḥ suprayuktaḥ samyak jñātaḥ svarge loke ca kāmadhuk bhavati"iti śruteḥ| uktañca vāmanenāpi, "kāvyaṃ sat dṛṣṭadṛṣṭārthaṃ prītikīrttihetutvāt" iti| proktasūtravṛtau ca, "kāvyaṃ sat cāru dṛṣṭaprayojanaṃ prītihetutvāt adṛṣṭaprayojanaṃ kīrtihetutvāt| atra ślokāḥ" pratiṣṭhāṃ kāvyabandhasya pratiṣṭhāṃ saraṇṃ viduḥ| akīrttivartanīṃ tvevaṃ kukavitvaviḍambanām| | kīrtiṃ svargaphalāmāhurāsaṃsāraṃ vipaścitaḥ| akīrttiñca nirālokanarakośadūtikām| | tasmāt kīrttimupādātumakīrttiñca vayapohitum| kāvyālaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍakārasāstrārthaḥ prasādyaḥ kavipuṅgavaiḥ| | "iti| aparañca, "dharmārthakāmamokṣeṣu vaicakṣaṇyaṃ kalāsu ca| karoti kīrtiṃ prītiñca sādhukāvyaniṣevaṇam"| | iti punaḥ kintu, sā eva gauḥ, duṣprayuktā sadoṣatvena guṇālaṅkārādirāhityena ca prayuktā nibaddhā satī, prayoktuḥ prayogaṃ kurvataḥ, kaveriti yāvat, gotvaṃ vṛṣatvam, ajñatvamiti bhāvaḥ, śaṃsati sūcayati (etena sadoṣasya rasavataḥ vākyasya kāvyatvahānirna, api tu ninditatvameva pratīyate iti bodhyate) | | 6| | 

taditi| tat tasmāt prayojakasya gotvakhyāpanāt, kāvye alpamapi padapadāṃśamātrasthamapi, duṣṭaṃ doṣaḥ, (bhāve ktapratyayaḥ) kathañcana kenāpi prakārema, na upekṣyaṃ na kṣantavyaṃ, sarvathā doṣaḥ kāvyasyāpakarṣaka iti parityājya ityarthaḥ| nanu guṇālaṅkārādisadbhāve sāmānyadoṣeṇakā kṣatiriti ceta tatrāha, syāditi| vapuḥ śarīraṃ, sundaramapi svabhānataḥ guṇādimattayā vā sudarśanamapi, ekena ekāṅgavarttinā kṣudreṇāpi, śvitreṇa dhavalarogeṇa, durbhagaṃ ghṛṇitaṃ syāt| (etena doṣaḥ kāvyasyāpakarṣako na tu kāvyatvavighātaka ityāyātam| uktañcānyatra yathā, "kīṭānuviddharatnādisādāraṇyena kāvyatā| duṣṭeṣvapi matā yatra rasādyanugamaḥ sphuṭaḥ| | "iti| ekena śvitrarogeṇa yathā sundaraśarīramapi ninditaṃ bhavati, tathā padāṃśādigatālpadoṣajātamapi kāvyaṃ dūṣayati iti vimbaprativimbabodhanāt atra dṛṣṭāntālaṅkāraḥ| taduktaṃ sāhityadarpaṇe, "dṛṣṭāntastu sadharmasya vastunaḥ prativimbanam" iti| etena na kevalaṃ padapadāṃśādidoṣeṇaiva kāvyasyāsādhutvaṃ, kintu prabandhuḥ pratibhādaurbalyakulavaikalyābhyāmapi bhavati| taduktaṃ kavigajāṅkuśe, "śunīdugdhamiva tyājyaṃ padya śūdrakṛtaṃ budhaiḥ| gavāmiva payo grāhyaṃ kāvyaṃ grāhyameva| taduktaṃ bhāmahena, "upaślokyaseya māhātmyādujjvalāḥ kāvyasampadaḥ"iti| bhaṭṭodbhavenāpi bhaṇitam, " guṇālaṅkāracārutvayuktamapyadhikojjvalam| kāvyamāśrayasampattyā meruṇevāmaradrumaḥ| | "iti) | | 7| | 

doṣasya kāvyāpakarṣakatvābhidhānena guṇānāmutkarṣajanakatvamarthādabhihitaṃ bhavati, utkarṣāpakarṣaparijñānañca śāstropadeśamantareṇa na sambhavatīti śāstrasyopayogitāṃ darśayati, guṇadoṣāniti| aśāstrajñaḥ avidvān janaḥ, guṇadoṣān kāvyasya upādeyatvavyañcakā dharṇā guṇāḥ tān, heyatvavyañcakā darṇā doṣāḥ tāṃśca, kathaṃ vibhajate viśeṣeṇa budhyate? naivetyarthaḥ prasādādiguṇānāṃ śrutikaṭvādidoṣāṇāñca kāvyapāṭhaśravaṇamātreṇa sāmānyataḥ parijñāne'pi śāstravijñāne viśeṣajñānāsambhavāditi bhāvaḥ| tathā hi rūpāṇāṃ saundayryādīnāṃ, bhedopalabdhiṣu, andhasya adhikāraḥ śaktiḥ, astikim? naivetyarithaḥ| śāstrapayryālocanena janā guṇadoṣavicāro samarthā bhavanti, tasmādeva mamāyamudyamaḥ iti bhāvaḥ| (atrāpi dṛṣṭāntālaṅkāraḥ) | | 8| | 

ata iti| ataḥ kāraṇāt guṇadoṣāṇāṃ vibhāgādeḥ śāstragamyatvāt hetoḥ, sūrayaḥ pūrvapaṇḍitāḥ bharatādayaḥ, prajānāṃ lokānāṃ, vyutpatiṃta vāgvaicitryajñānam, abhisandhāya abhipretya, uddiśya ityarthaḥ, vicitramārgāṇāṃ vicitrā vividā mārgāḥ panthānaḥ rītayaḥ gauḍaīprabhṛtayaḥ yāsāṃ tathābhūtānāṃ, vācāṃ kāvyaprabandhānām ityarthaḥ, kriyāvidhiṃ racanāvidhānaṃ, nibabandhuḥ cakruḥ, kāvyagranthān racayāmāsuḥ iti yāvat, (etena yathā bharatādīnāṃ lokavyutpattyarthaṃ grantharacanaṃ mamāpi tathā etadganthaviracane samudyamaḥ, na tu yaśaḥ khyāpanārthamiti sūcyate) | | 9| | 

idānīṃ prācīnasaṃvādanirdeśapuraḥsaraṃ dharmmabhūtaṃ kāvyaśarīraṃ nirūpayati, tairiti| taiḥ pūrvapaṇḍitaiḥ, kāvyānāṃ śarīrañca guṇadoṣalaṅkārāṇāmādhāratayā rasādivyaṅgyamātmanaḥ dehabhūtaḥ āśrayaśca, tathā alaṅkārāśca alaṅkriyate kāvyaśarīramutkarṣīṃkriyate ebhiḥ ityalaṅkārāḥ iti vyutpattyā śleṣādiguṇāḥ anuprāsopamādayaḥ alaṅkārāsca ityarthaḥ, "utkarṣahetavaḥ proktā gumālaṅkārarītayaḥ"ityādyukteriti bhāvaḥ; darśitāḥ lakṣaṇanidrdeśena nirūpitāḥ| atra "ca"dvayamubhayaprādhānyadyotakam| yadvā, śarīrañceti cakārastairityanena sambadhyate, taiśca ityarthaḥ, alaṅkārāśca iti cakāraḥ anuktasamuccayārthaḥ, alaṅkārāḥ guṇadoṣādayāśca ityarthaḥ| kāvyānāmiti bahutvaṃ prakārabhedavivakṣayoktamavagantavyam| (nanu kāvyaṃ tāvat kimiti preśne "kavanīyaṃ kāvyam"iti locanakāraḥ| "kauti śabdāyate vimṛśati rasabhāvān iti kaviḥ tasya kamrmakāvyam"iti bhaṭṭagopālaḥ| "lokottaravarṇanānipuṇakavikamrma kāvyam"iti kāvyaprakāśakāraḥ| "prajñā navanavonmeṣaśālinī pratibhā matā| tadanuprāṇanājjīvet varmanānipuṇaḥ kaviḥ| | tasya kamrma smṛtaṃ kāvyam"iti bhāmahaḥ| cārutāśāliśabdārthayugalaṃ kāvyamiti rūḍho'rthaḥ) | kiṃ tat śarīramityākāṅkṣāyāṃ svamatamuddhāṭayati, śarīramiti| tāvacchabdo vākyālaṅkārārthaḥ| jhruktañca vāmanena, "vākyālaṅkāraprayojanantu nānarthakam" iti| iṣṭāḥ hṛdyāḥ, rasādyanugamena manoharā ityarthaḥ, ye vācyalakṣyavyaṅgayabhedena vividhā arthāḥ, taiḥ vyāvacchinnā vibhūṣitā, padāvalī padasanaṣṭiḥ, kāvyasya śarīramiti vatrtulārthaḥ, atreṣṭatvaṃ lokottarāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍalādajanakatvam| "ripuste mṛtaḥ, putraste jātaḥ"ityādau āḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍalādasya lokottaratvābhāvāt na kāvyatvam| tādṛśārthasca doṣābhāvāt guṇālaṅkārasadbhāvācca jāyamānamanojñatvenavyaṅgyavaiśiṣṭyena ca jñeyam| (tataśca dhvaniguṇībhūtavyaṅgyacitrabhedena kāvyasya traividhyamanyairuktamatrānusatrtavyam| tathā hi, yatra vyaṅgyacamatkṛtirvācyacamatkṛterantarlīnā tatra guṇībhūtavyaṅgyam| yatra ca vyaṅgyacamatkṛterabhāvaḥ vācyacamatkṛtireva paraṃ prakarṣamupagatā tatra citramiti| atra vācyalakṣyavyaṅgyabhedenārthasya traividhyamavagantavyam| abhidhayā pratipādyāḥ arthaḥ vācyaḥ, lakṣaṇayā bodhyaḥ lakṣyaḥ, vyañcanayā gamyaḥ vyaṅgyaḥiti| atra vyaṅgyasyāpi vastvalaṅkāraratyādibhedāt trividho bhedaḥ; sa ca anyatra vistara iti noktaḥ) | padāvalīśabdo'tra vākyamātrārthakaḥ, nirākāṅkṣapadasamūhasya kāvyatvāvyavahārāt sākāṅkṣeti tasyāḥ viśeṣaṇaṃ deyam| (tataśca arthavadvākyaṃ kāvyaśarīraṃ, na tu śabdārthāvityāyātam; tena ca "kāvyaṃ paṭhyate""kāvyādartho'vagamyate" ityādivyavahāro'pi saṅgacchate| navyāstu "śabdārthau śarīraṃ, rasaścātmā, doṣāḥ kāṇatvādivat, guṇāḥ śauyryādaya iva , alaṅkārāḥ kaṭakakuṇḍalādivat"ityāhuḥ| iha rasasyāṅgitvamapi nādriyate, nīrasānāmapi guṇālaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍakārasadbhādvastvalaṅkāravyañcakatvācca vaicitryajanakatvena kāvyatayā sakalālaṅkārikairaṅgīkṛtatvāt| śarīraṃ cātra "arthaḥ sahṛdayaślāghyaḥ kāvyātmā yo vyavasthitaḥ| vācyapratīyamānākyau tasya bhedāvubhau smṛtau| | " ityuktalakṣaṇaḥ guṇālaṅkāropaskṛto vācyārthaḥ vastvalaṅkārarasādirūpovyaṅgyārthaśca ityavagantavyaḥ) | | 10| | 

idānīṃ kāvyabhedān darśayati, gadyamiti| tat kāvyaṃ gadyaṃ padāyaṃ miśrañceti tridhaiva triprakāreṇaiva, vyavasthitaṃ siddhāntitaṃ, prācīnairiti śeṣaḥ| gadyaṃḍajhrṭhagada vyaktāyāṃ vāci"iti dhātoḥ "gadamadacarayamaścānupasarge" iti karmaṇi yatpratyaye siddhaṃṭa, pādeṣu bhavaṃ padyaṃ, (bhavārthe yatpratyayasya bhasaṃjñāyāṃ padādeśa ca siddhaṃ) , padyasya kṛcchrasādhyanirvāhatayā prathamaṃ gadyanirdeśaḥ| tatra padyalakṣaṇamāha, padyamiti| caturṇāṃ padānāṃ samāhāraḥ catuṣpado catuścaṇanibaddhaṃ padyaṃ syāt, dvipādādipadyasya vedādau darśanāt lokeṣu pādacatuṣṭayātmakasyaiva prasiddheśca prāyeṇa mahākāvyādau tādṛśānāmeva niveśanācca catuṣpado ityuktam; vastutastu chandobaddhaṃ vākyameva padyam| (yadāhuḥ, "chandobaddhapadaṃ padyam, "tathā, "chandoniyamavadvākyaṃ padyamityabhidhīyate"iti ca) , tadapi vṛttaṃ jātiriti dvividham, akṣarasaṅkhyayānibaddhaṃ vṛttaṃ, mātrayā nibaddhā jātiriti| gadyamiśrayorlakṣaṇamanatiprayojanamiti na tadatra pradarśitaṃ, gadyaṃ chandorahitaṃ, miśraṃ gadyapadyobhayātmakamiti jñeyam| | 11| | 

chanda iti| chandāsi vicīyante vijñāyante aneyati tasyāṃ chandovicityāṃ piṅgalādikṛtacchandogranthe chandovicitināmakasvakṛtacchandogranthe vā, sakalaḥ nikhilaḥ, tatprapañcaḥ teṣāṃ vṛttajātiprabhṛtīnāṃ prapañcaḥ vistāraḥ, nidarśitaḥ nirūpitaḥ sā vidyā chandojñānamiteyarthaḥ, gambhīram agādaṃ, durgamamityarthaḥ, kāvyasāgaraṃ kāvyarūpamarṇavaṃ, titīrṣūṇāṃ taritumicchūnāṃ, nauḥ taraṇisvarūpā ityarthaḥ, atigambhīraṃ hi mahāsāgaraṃ titīrṣūṇāṃ yathā bahuvastuparipūrṇā naurupayogāya bhavati, tathā bahulacchandobaddhavākyātmakaṃ rasabhāvādibhiratigahanaṃ kāvyaṃ racayituṃ pariśīlayituṃ vā kṛtayatnānāṃ chandojñānamiti bhāvaḥ| | 12| | 

idānīṃ mahākāvyaṃ nirūpayiṣyan tadavāntarabhedakathanasya tadantaḥ pātitvādanupayogitvaṃ darśayati, muktakamiti| muktakaṃ śloka evaikascamatkārakṣamaḥ satām"iti) | kulakaṃ pañcaślokātmakaṃ padyam| (taduktaṃ, "dvābyāntu yugmākaṃ jñeyaṃ tribhiḥ ślokaiḥ viśeṣakam| caturbistu kalāpaṃ syāt pañcabhiḥ kulakaṃ matam| | "iti) | koṣaḥ anyonyanirapekṣaḥ padyasamūhaḥ| (uktañca, "koṣaḥ ślokasamūhastu syādanyonyānapekṣakaḥ"iti) | saṅghātaḥ pratyekaparisamāptārthakapadyaiḥ kathāsamāptiḥ| (taduktaṃ, "yatra kavirekamarthaṃ vṛttanaikena varṇayati kāvyaṃ| saṅghātaḥ sa nigadito vṛndāvanameghadūtādiḥ| | ") iti evam, atra "iti"padam evamarthakam; tādṛsaḥ tathāvidhaḥ yugmakādiśca, padyavistaraḥ padyānāṃ vistaraḥ, sargabandhasya ṇahākāvyasya, aṅgarūpatvāt avayavasvarūpatvāt, nirūpayiṣyamāṇamahākāvyaikadeśasvarūpatvādityarthaḥ, anuktaḥ na pradarśita ityarthaḥ, granthāntareṣu viśeṣeṇoktatvādiha granthavistārabhiyā iti bhāvaḥ| | 13| | 

sargabandhāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍagarūpatvānmuktakādayo noktā iti prāgabhihitatvāt tadākāṅkṣāyāṃ samprati mahākāvyaṃ nirūpayati, sargabandha ityādi| sargema avāntarārthavarṇanena upalakṣitaḥ, bandhaḥ ślokasaṅghātaḥ mahākāvyaṃ, tasya mahākāvyasya, lakṣaṇaṃ svarūpam, ucyate etadanantaraṃ nirūpyate, āśauḥ śubhāśaṃsanaṃ, namaskriyā namaskāraḥ, svāpakarṣabodhakavacanaṃ, kāyikavācikamānasikatvena namaskārasya traividhyāditi bhāvaḥ; vastunirdeśo vā'pi athavā vasati prastutavṛttānto'sminniti vastu prakṛtasya netā tatsambandhī vā kaścidaparaḥ, tasya nirdeśaḥ kathanaṃ, tasya mahākāvyasya mukham ādi, āśīḥprabhṛtīnāmanyatamaṃ prathamaṃ varṇanīyamiti niṣkarṣaḥ| tatra kīcakabadhādāvāśīḥ, raghuvaṃśādau namaskāraḥ, śiśupālavadhādhau vastunardeśa iti| | 14| | 

itihāseti| itihāsānāṃ rāmāyabhāratādīnāṃ, kathayā rāmayudhiṣṭhirādyanyatamavṛttāntena, udbhūtaṃ nibaddhaṃ, vā athavā san satyabhūtaḥ vṛttāntaḥ, āśrayo varṇanīyo yatra tat tādṛśam, itarataṃ rāmāyaṇamahābhāratādivyatiriktamapi vṛttaṃ, mahākāvye prayojyamiti śeṣaḥ, bhāratādiprasiddhaṃ lokaprasiddhaṃ vā sadāśrayaṃ caritaṃ mahākāvye varṇanīyamiti bhāvaḥ| (etena asatyavṛttaṃ mahākāvye na varṇanīyamiti pradarśitam) | caturṇāṃ darṇārthakāmamokṣāmāṃ, vargaḥ caturvargaḥ sa eva phalaṃ, tena upetaṃ yuktam; dharmādiphalacatuṣṭayānāmekatrāmbhavādapi varṇanīyāḥ sarve eva, kintu phalamanyatamamiti mantavyam| (yaduktaṃ, "catvārastatra vargāḥ syusteṣvekañca phalaṃ labhet"iti) | caturaḥ kāyryakuśalaḥ, udāttaḥ mahān, nāyakaḥ pradhānapuruṣaḥ yasmin tathābhūtam| (nāyakalakṣaṇamuktaṃ darpaṇe yathā, "tyāgī kṛtī kulīnaḥ suśrīko rūpayauvanotsāhī| dakṣo'nuraktalokastejovaidagdhaśīlavān netā| | "iti| atra caturaśabdasya dhīrarthakatvaṃ, tena ca dhīrodāttanāyaka eva mahākāvye abhipreta iti mantavyam| dhīrodāttalakṣaṇamuktaṃ yathā, "avikatthanaḥ kṣamāvān atigambhīro mahāsattvaḥ| stheyānnigūḍhamāno dhīrodāttaḥ dṛḍhavrataḥ kathitaḥ| | "iti| | 15| | 

kevalaṃ nāyakavarṇanena kavīnāṃ na caritārthatā, itihāsādereva tajjñānasya siddhatvāt, ataḥ nāyakavarṇamāśritya tadānuṣaṅgikanagarādikamapi varmanīyaṃ, tathātve kāvyasobhājananāt śrotṝṇāmunmukhīkaramaṃ syādityāśayenāha, nagaretyādi| nagaraṃ bahusamṛddhajanākīrṇaḥ pradeśaḥ, arṇavaḥ samudraḥ, śailaḥ parvataḥ ṛtuḥ vasantādiḥ, candrārkodayaḥ candrasūyryayorudayaḥ, (upalakṣaṇametat, tena astagamanamapi) , eteṣāṃ varṇanaiḥ, udyānasalilayoḥ krīḍaā madhupānaṃ ratotsavaḥ sambhogaśrṛṅgāraḥ taiḥ, vipralambhaiḥ sambhogaśrṛṅgārāt pūrvavarttibhiḥ pūrvarāgaprabhṛtibhiḥ caturvidhabhāvaiḥ, ("yatra tu ratiḥ prakṛṣṭā nābhīṣṭamupaiti vipralambho'sau"| | 16| | 

iti vipralambhalakṣaṇam| sambhogapūrvavarttitvañca "na vinā vipralambhena sambhogaḥ puṣṭimaśnute"etallakṣaṇenaiva siddhaṃ, caturvidhatvañca pūrvarāgamānapravāsakaruṇātmakatveneti mantavyam) | vivāhaiḥ pariṇayavyāpāraiḥ, kumārodayasya putrotpattervarṇanaiḥ, tathā mantraḥ ripuparājayārthaṃ guhyavādaḥ mantribhiḥ saha kriyamāmā mantramā ityarthaḥ, dūtaḥ rājñā kāyryeṣu preṣyaḥ puruṣaḥ, sa ca nisṛṣṭārthamitārtha sandeśahārakabhedāt trividhaḥ, prayāṇaṃ ripujayādyarthaṃ yuddhayātrā, ājiḥ yuddhaṃ, nāyakasya abhyudayaḥ jayalābhaḥ, taiḥ alaṅkṛtaṃ śobhitamiti vakṣyamāṇaślokoktenānvayaḥ, nagarādayo mahākāvye varmanīyā ityarthaḥ| (uktañca, "sandhyāsūyryendurajanīpradoṣadhvāntavāsarāḥ| prātarmadhyāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanamṛgayāśailatrtuvanasāgarāḥ| | sambhogavipralambhau ca munisvargapurādhvarāḥ| raṇaprayāṇopayammantraputrodayādayaḥ| varṇanīyā yathāyogaṃ sāṅgopāṅga amī iha| | "iti) | | 17| | 

alaṅkṛtamiti| asaṅkṣiptaṃ na saṅkṣepeṇa varṇanīyaṃ, savistaramityarthaḥ, tathā sati sahṛdayahṛdayaṅgamaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ; rasaiḥ śrṛṅgārarādibhiḥ, bhāvaiḥ prādhānyonābhivyaktaiḥ vyabhicāribhiḥ, udvuddhamātraiḥ sthāyibhiḥ ratyādibhiḥ, tathā devādiviṣayakaiḥ anurāgaiśca, nirantaraṃ saṅkulaṃ, pūrṇam ithi yāvat; (iha mahākāvye śrṛṅgāravīraśāntānāmanyatamo'ṅgitvena varṇanīyaḥ, anye rasā bhāvāścāṅgatvena ityanyairuktamapyaṅgīkāyryam; yaduktaṃ, "śrṛṅgāravīrasāntānāmeko'ṅgī rasa iṣyate"iti) | anativistīrṇaiḥ śāstraniyamitā, ṭādibhiḥ anyūnaiḥ, sargaiavāntaravṛttāntasamāptākhyaiḥ, upetamiti vakṣyamāmaślokoktenānvayaḥ| (sargāṇāmaṣṭānyūnatvatiṃraśadanadhikatve pramāṇamuktamīśānasaṃhitāyām, "aṣṭasargānna tu nyūnaṃ tiṃraśatsargācca nādhikam| mahākāvyaṃ prayoktavyaṃ mahāpuruṣakīrttiyuk| | "iti| etacca prāyikaṃ, vaiparītyasyāpi darśanāt| vastutastu sargasyānativistīrṇatvañca tiṃraśadanyūnadviśatyanadhikapadyatvena yojanīyaṃ, yaduktaṃ tatraiveśānasaṃhitāyāṃ, "nātyantavistaraḥ sargaÏstraśato vā na conatā| dviśatyā nādhikaṃ kāyryametat padyasya lakṣaṇam| | "iti) | śravyavṛttaiḥ doṣaparihāreṇagumālaṅkārasaṃyogena ca śrutisukhāvahapadyairityarthaḥ; (yaduktaṃ, "yasmin śruteca cittasya vairasyaṃ na ca hṛdyatā| tāni vajryāni padyāni prasiddhipracyutāni ca| | "iti) | susandhibhiḥ parasparasāpekṣairityarthaḥ, "sargānte bhāvisargasya kathāyāḥ sūcanaṃ bhavet"ityādyukteritibhāvaḥ| (yacca kaiścit "sandheyo nāṭakalakṣaṇoktā mukhapratimukagarbhavimarśanirvahaṇākhyāḥ pañca"iti vyākhyātaṃ tattu na samīcīnaṃ, sarvatra lakṣaṇāyogāt| viśvanāthastu, "sarve nāṭakasandhayaḥ"iti mahākāvyalakṣaṇe'bihitavān, tanmatānusāribhireva tathā vyākhyātamiti dik) | | 18| | 

sarvatreti| sarvatra bhinnāḥ vṛttāntā yeṣu taiḥ, sargeṣu varmanīyaviṣayāṇāṃ prabhedāditi bhāvaḥ; athavā bhinnaṃ pṛthakchandasā racitaṃ, vṛttaṃ padyam, ante yeṣāṃ taiḥ, upetaṃ yuktaṃ, pratyekasargānte prāyaśastathā darśanāt, "ekavṛttamayaiḥ padyaiḥ avasāne'nyavṛttakaiḥ"iti vacanācca| (ekavṛttamayasargasya sravatrādarśanāt asya prāyikatvamavadheyam; yathā śisupālabadhe caturthaḥ sargaḥ nānāvṛttairupanibaddhaḥ| taduktañca, "nānāvṛttamayaḥ kvāpi sargaḥ kaścana dṛśyate"iti) | sadalaṅkṛti satyaḥ vidyamānāḥ athavā śobhanāḥ, alaṅkṛtayaḥ anuprāsopamādayaḥ yasmin tādṛśaṃ, kāvyaṃ lokānāṃ rañcakaṃ sahṛdayamanoharaṃ sat, kalpāntarasthāyi cirasthāyi, cirakīrttikaramiti bhāvaḥ, jāyeta bhavet| | 19| | 

proktamahākāvyalakṣamasya keṣāñciddhamrmāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaghaṭakatve taddhamrmavati khaṇḍakāvye mahākāvye'pi mahākāvyatvaṃ na syāt, tatra siddhāntayitumāha, nyūnamiti| atra mahākāvye, ukteṣvaṅgeṣviti śeṣaḥ, yaiḥ kaiścit aṅgaiḥ nyūnaṃ hīnamapi, kāvyaṃ na duṣyati lakṣaṇahīnatayā ninditaṃ na bhavati, yadi upātteṣu varṇiteṣu viṣayeṣu, sampattiḥ racanāmādhuyryaṃ, tadvidaḥ kāvyābhijñān, ārādhayati rasādisadbhāvena prīṇayati| (taduktaṃ, "nāvarmanaṃ nāgayryāderdoṣāya viduṣāṃ manaḥ| yadi śailatrturātrādervaṇenenaiva tuṣyati| | "iti| "yadyupāttārthasampattiḥ" iti pāṭhe sa evārthaḥ| etena vaicitryaviśeṣajanakatvameva mahākāvyatvamiti phalitaṃ, vaicitryaviseṣaśca mahākāvye khaṇḍakāvyādbhinna eva| udyānādīnāṃ varmanantu saralamatīnāmanāyāsapratītyarthamiti dik) | | 20| | 

samprati kāvye nāyakapratināyakayorvarmane prakāradvayamāha gumatiti vaṃśeti ca dvābhyām| prāk prathamaṃ nāyakaṃ guṇataḥ upanyasya nāyakasya gumān varṇayitvā, tena nāyakena, vidviṣāṃ śatrūṇāṃ, pratināyakānāmityarthaḥ, nirākaramaṃ parājayaḥ, ityeṣa mārgaḥ varṇanarītiḥ, prakṛtisundaraḥ svabāvaramyaḥ, (atra nāyakasya guṇāḥ pratināyakasya ca doṣā eva varṇanīyāḥ, nāyakasya tyāgādīn sāmānyagumān pradaśrya viseṣapauruṣagumānāmuktatvāt pratināyakalakṣaṇe ca doṣāmāmabhihitatvāt| taduktaṃ yathā, "śobhā vilāso mādhuyryaṃ gāmbhīyryaṃ dhaiyryatjasī| lalitaudāyryamityaṣṭau sattvajāḥ pauruṣā guṇāḥ| | "iti, "dhīroddhataḥ pāpakārī vyasanī pratināyakaḥ"iti ca| tathā ca rāmāyaṇe piturājñayā dīrghavanavāsakleśasahanādisāduvratena rāmeṇa svabaladṛptasya sītāharamādipāpakarmaratasya rāvaṇasya badhavarṇanam) | | 21| | 

vaṃśet| ripoḥ śatrorapi, vaṃśaḥ kulaṃ, vīyryaṃ parākramaḥ, śrutaṃ śāstrajñānam, ādinā dayādākṣiṇyādi yathāyathamavagantavyam; etāni varmayitvā kītrtayitvā, tajjayāt tasya ripoḥ jayāt, nāyakasya utkarṣavarṇanaṃ naḥ asmān, dhinoti toṣayati, (etena pratināyakalakṣaṇānvitasya tasya vaṃśādikaṃ varṇayitvā tato'pi guṇādhikanāyakavarṇanaṃ nāyakasyokarṣameva janayati ityāyātam| yathā kirāte duyryodhanasya prajārañjanaṃ varmayitvā tato'pi gumavato yudhiṣṭhirāderutkarṣaprakatipādanārthaṃ tena tajjayaḥ varṇitaḥ) | | 22| | 

kāvyasya gadyapadyami8tvena traividhyāt kṛcchasādhyanirvāhatayā prathamaṃ padyalakṣaṇamabhidhāya samprati gadyakāvyaṃ nirūpayannāha, apāda iti nāyakenaiveti ca dvābhyām| apādaḥ chandobaddhaḥ ślokacaturthāṃśaḥ pādaḥ tadrahitaḥ, padasantānaḥ padasamūhaḥ gadyam| "cūrmakamalpasamāsaṃ dīrghasamāsamutkalikāprāyam| samāsarahitamāviddhaṃ vṛttagandhi ca" ityādyuktaṃ gadyabhedaṃ kathā''kyāyikayorantarbhāvāt pṛthaganabhidāya pradhānabhedadvayaṃ prācīnamatamāśrayannāha, ākhyāyiketi| tasya gadyasya, dvau prabhedau ākhyāyikā kathā ca| (taduktaṃ, "kathāyāṃ sarasaṃ vastu gadyaireva vinirmitam| kvacidatra bhavedāyryā kvacidvaktrāpavaktrake| | ādau padyairnamaskāraḥ khalādervṛttakatrtanam| | "| | 23| | 

iti; yathā kādambayryādiḥ| "ākhyāyikā kathāvat syāt kavervaṃśādikītrtanam| asyāmanyakavīnāñca vṛttaṃ gadyaṃ kvacit kvacit| | kathāṃśānāṃ vyavaccheda āśvāsa iti badhyate| āyryāvaktrāpavaktrāṇāṃ chandasā yena kenacit| | anyāpadeśenāśvāsamukhe bhāvyarthasūcanam| | "iti; yathā harṣacaritādiḥ) | proktalakṣaṇadvayaghaṭitaparasparabhedakadhamrmāṇāṃ prācīnaprabandheṣvanyathābhāvamākalayya lakṣaṇāntaramapi dūṣayitumāha, tayoriti| tayormadhyeākhyāyikā nāyakenaiva vācyā nāyaka eva asyā vaktā ityarthaḥ evakārādanyavyavacchedaḥ| (uktañca, "vṛttamākhyāyate yasyāṃ nāyakena svaceṣṭitam"iti) | anyā kathā, nāyakena taditareṇa vā vācyā, yathā kādambayryāṃ candrāpīḍasya nāyakasya mahāśvetayā saṃlāpaḥ| nanu nāyakasya vaktṛtve tasya svaguṇāviṣkaraṇaṃ doṣayeti śaṅkāṃ vārayannāha, svaguṇeti| bhūtārthaśaṃsinaḥ yathārthavādinaḥ nāyakasya, atra vaktṛtve, svaguṇāviṣkriyā nijaguṇakhyāpanaṃ, doṣaḥ na, bhavatīti śeṣaḥ, asatyakathanasyaiva doṣatvāt iti bhāvaḥ| | 24| | 

pūrvoktaṃ prācīnamataṃ dūṣayannāha, apīti| api tu kintu, tatrāpi ākhyāyikāyāmapi, anyaiḥ nāyakabhinnaiḥ udīramāt kathanāt, nāyakādanyeṣāmapi vaktṛtvāditi yāvat, aniyamaḥ nāyakamātravaktṛtvarūpasya virodhaḥ, dṛṣṭaḥ; tataścāvyāptidoṣa eva syāt, etādṛśabhedastu naiva karaṇīyaḥ ityabhiprāyaḥ| kathāyāñca anyaḥ nāyakabhinnaḥ, svayaṃ nāyako vā, vaktā iti bhedalakṣaṇam ākhyāyikāyā bhedakaraṇaṃ, vā kīdṛk? naiva saṅgacchate ityarthaḥ, ākhyāyikāyāṃ nāyako vaktā, kathāyāmapi yadi nāyako vaktā syāt tadā svarūpagatavailakṣaṇyābhāvāt bhedakaraṇamakiñcitkaramiti bhāvaḥ| | 25| | 

aparañca bhedakaraṇaṃ dūṣayati, vaktramityādi dvābhyām| vaktram aparavaktrañca chandasī, (taduktaṃ, "vaktraṃ nādyānnāsau syātāmabdherya'nuṣṭubhi khyātam"iti, "ayuji nanaralā guruḥ same tadaparavaktramidaṃ najau jarau"iti, "vaitālīyaṃ puṣpitāgrāccecchantyaparavaktrakam"iti ca) | ucchvāsaḥ kathāṃ'śavyavacchedasya saṃjñā āśvāsāparapayryāyaḥ, (taduktaṃ, "kathāṃ'śānāṃ vyavaccheda āśvāsa iti kathyate") , tena sahitatvañca bhedakaṃ, kathāyā iti śeṣaḥ| | 26| | 

etat trayam ākhyāyikāyāḥ ciḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ, cet kathāsu api prasaṅgena prastāvakrameṇa, ayryādivat vaktrāparavaktrayoḥ chandasoḥ praveśaḥ, kiṃ na? bhavet iti śeṣaḥ; api tu bhavedeva| (uktañca kathālakṣaṇe, "āyryāvaktrāpavaktrāṇāṃ chandasā yena kenacit") | tasmāduktabhedakaraṇaṃbhramavijṛmbhitameveti bhāvaḥ| vaktrāparavaktrakratabhedadoṣaṃ pradaśrya ucchavāsakṛtabhedaṃ dūṣayati, bheda iti lambhaḥ kathāparicchedasya saṃjñābhedaḥ, ādipadenaullāsādīnāṃ grahaṇaṃ "vā"śabdo'tra cārthe, lambhādiḥ ucchvāsaśca kathā''khyāyikayoḥ bhedaḥ bhinnaḥ, pṛthakpṛthaksaṃjñakaḥ paricchedaḥ iti yāvat, kutracit kathāyāṃ lambhapadena paricchedaḥ kṛtaḥ, ākhyāyikāyāmucchvāsapadena ullāsapadena vā kṛtaḥ ityarthaḥ, dṛṣṭaḥ, astu, tataḥ kim? saṃjñāmātrabhedo na kiñcitkara ityarthaḥ, svarūpavailakṣaṇyasyaiva bhedakatvena kītrtanāt, yathā ghaṭapāṭayoḥ svarūpabhedāt ghaṭaḥ paṭāditara iti bhedapratītiḥ na tathā ghaṭakalasayoḥ saṃjñāmātrabhinnayoḥ ghaṭābhedatayeti bhāvaḥ| | 27| | 

idānīṃ svamatamāviṣkaroti, taditi| tat tasmāt, kathā ākhyāyikā ca iti saṃjñādvayena nāmabhyām, aṅkitā, na tu svarūpāta iti bhāvaḥ; ekā abhinnā, jātiḥ, ubhayorapi gadyamayatvāt iti bhāvaḥ| anyairukteṣu pañcasu gadyakāvyabhedeṣu pradhānabhedayoḥ kathā''khyāyikayorabhedaṃ prāguktarītyā samathrya itareṣāmapi bhedakadharmamasaṅgataṃ vivicya tān sādhāraṇatayā antarbhāvayati, atraiveti| atraiva kathā''khyāyikayoḥ ityarthaḥ, śeṣāḥ avaśiṣṭāḥ, ākhyānajātayaḥ ākyānatvarūpadharmaviśiṣṭāḥ khaṇḍakathādayaḥ, antarbhaviṣyanti, tā hi etayoreva prakārabhedā iti bhāvaḥ| (antarbhaviṣyanti iti bhaviṣyatprayogeṇaitat sūcitaṃ, granthakṛtā gadyakāvyasya bhedā eva nāṅgīkṛtāḥ, vastutastu tanmatamidānīmapi sarvatra na khalu pramāṇatayā pracalati, pramāṇikatamairanyairapi tattadbhedānāṃ nidrdhāraṇāt iti| taduktam āgneye, "ākhyāyikā katā khamḍakathā parikathā tatā| kathāleketi manyante gadyakāvyañca pañcadhā| | "iti) | | 28| | 

kathā''khyāyikayorbhedāntaramapi dūṣayati, kanyeti| kanyāyā ajātavivahasaṃskārāyāḥ, pātrapratipādanāya kṛtasaṅkalpāyāḥ ityarthaḥ, haramaṃ yuddhādinā balāt grahamaṃ, rākṣasodvāha ityarthaḥ, (taduktaṃ "hatvā chittvā ca bhittvā ca krośantīṃ rudatīṃ gṛhāt| prasahya kanyāharaṇaṃ rākṣaso vidhirucyate| | "iti) ; saṅgrāmo yuddhaṃ, vipralambhaḥ sambhogaśrṛṅgārabhedaḥ, sa ca pūrvarāgamānapravāsakaruṇātmakaścaturvidha iti darpaṇakāraḥ| udayaḥ sūyryacandrayoḥ, abhyunnatirvā nāyakasya, ādinā udyānanagarādayo gṛhyanta; ete guṇāḥ, ākhyāyikāyā iti śeṣaḥ, ākhyāyikāyāṃkanyāharaṇādivarṇanarūpadharmā eva kathāyā bhedakā ityanyeṣāṃ matamiti niṣkarṣaḥ| (yathā ākhyāyikāmupakramya bhāmahenoktaṃ, "kanyāharaṇasaṅgāmavipralambhodayānvitā" ityādiḥ; sargabandhasamāḥ mahākāvyasāmānyā eva, kathāyāṃ kimu vaktavyamiti bhāvaḥ, ataḥ ete vaiśeṣikāḥ guṇāḥ bhedakā dharmā na, mahākāvyavyavacchedakadharmāḥ, ākhyāyikāyāmeṣāṃ niyatatvaṃ na tu kathāyāṃ, mahākāvye tvānuṣaṅgikatvamiti ca na vācyam, asādhāraṇānāmeva bhedakatvāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍagīkārāt sāmānyadharmāmāntu tathātvānaucityāt; (uktañca, "ayameva hi bhedo bhedaheturvā yadviruddhadharmādhyāsaḥ"iti) | | 29| | 

kavericchāprayujyaṃ kimapi ciḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanameva kathā''khyāyikayorbhedakamiti mataṃ nirasyati, kavīti| kavibhāvakṛtaṃ kaveḥ bhāvakatam abhiprāyaracitaṃ, ciḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ, kathāyāṃ niveśanīyam iti bhāvaḥ; (uktañca bhāmahena, "kaverabhiprāyakṛtairaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍakaneraṅkitā kathā"iti| yathā śiśupālabadhe sargaśeṣaślokeṣu "śrī"śabdaḥ, kirāte ca "lakṣmī"śabdaḥ prayuktaḥ) | anyatrāpi ākhyāyikāyāmapi prayuktaṃ tat na dūṣyaṃ bhavati, tathā hi iṣṭārthasaṃsiddhaiya abhipretārthasādhanāya, kṛtātmānāṃ kṛtayatnānāṃ sudhīyāṃ, kiṃ hi mukhaṃ prārambhaḥ, na syāt? api tu sarvameva teṣām icchayā prārambhe niveśanīyamityartaḥ, "niraṅkuśāḥ kavayaḥ"iti bhāvaḥ| "iṣṭārthasaṃsiddhau"ityatra "saṃsiddhau"iti, "mukham"ityatra "sukham"iti ca pāṭhāntaram| | 30| | 

samprati miśrakāvyamāha, mśrāṇīti| nāṭakādīni dṛśyakāvyāni, miśrāṇi gadyapadyobhayamiśritāni, ādipadena prakaraṇādīnāṃ parigrahaḥ; (uktañca, nāṭakamatha prakaramaṃ bhāṇavyāyogasamavakāraḍimāḥ| īhāmṛgāṅkavīthyaḥ prahasanamiti rūpakāṇi daśa| | "tathaā, "nāṭikā troṭakaṃ goṣṭhī saṭṭakaṃ nāṭayarāsakam| prasthānollāpyakāvyāni preṅkhaṇaṃ rāsakaṃ tathā| saṃlāpakaṃ śrīgaditaṃ śilpakañca vilāsikā| durmallikā prakaraṇo hallauśe bhāṇiketyapi| | aṣṭādaśa prāhuruparūpakāṇi manīṣiṇaḥ| | "iti) | teṣāṃ nāṭakādīnām, anyatra bharatādigranthe, vistaraḥ bāhulyena prakāśaḥ, taddhijijñāsubhistatraiva yatitavyaṃ, granthabāhulyabhiyā na mamātra prayāsa iti bhāvaḥ| na kevalaṃ dṛśyakāvyasya miśratvaṃ, śravyakāvyasyāpi kadācitmiśratvamastītyāha, gadyati| gadyapadyamayī kācit, vāṇī iti śeṣaḥ, campūriti abhidhīyate kathyate, ākhyāyikādo gadyakāvye padyānāmatyalpatayā na campūtvam, atra hi gadyapadyayorubhayorubhayorevādhikyamityanayorbhedaḥ| (kācit ityanena asya asāvatrikatvaṃ sūcitam; tena rājastutiviṣayatvonāsya virudasaṃjñā parairuktā'vagantavyāḥ; taduktaṃ, "gadyapadyamayo rājastutirvirudamucyate"iti) | | 31| | 

"gadya padyañca miśrañca"ityanena prāk kāvyasya traividhyaṃ darśayitvā punaḥ bhāṣādibhedena caturvidhatvamāha, taditi| tat tasmāt, etat vāṅmayaṃ vāgātmakaṃ kāvyaśāstraṃ saṃskṛtaṃ devabhaṣayā racitaṃ, prākṛtaṃ tadākhyabhāṣayā racitam, apabhraṃśaḥ vakṣyamāṇāpabraṃśalakṣaṇoktabhāṣāviśeṣanibaddhaḥ, tathā miśrañca nānābhāṣāmayamityarthaḥ iti evaṃrūpeṇa, āyryāḥ vidvāṃsaḥ, caturvidham āhaḥ varmayanti| (miśrañca ityanenaitat pratīyate yat bhāṣāṇāṃ nānātvāt kavīnāñca svecchāparavaśatvāt baḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavībhirapi bhāṣābhiste kāvyaṃ racayeyuriti; tataśca ekayā dvābhyāṃ tisṛbhiḥ tato'pyadhikābhirapi bhāṣābhiḥ kavayaḥ kāvyaṃ kurvīran iti| uktañca bhojena, "saṃskṛtenaiva ko'pyarthaḥ prākṛtenaiva cāparaḥ| śakyo vācayituṃ kaścit apabhraṃśena vā punaḥ| | paiśācyā śaurasenyā ca māgadhyā'nyo nibadhyate| dvitrābhiḥ ko'pi bhāṣābhiḥ sarvābhirapi kaścana"| | iti) | | 32| | 

saṃskṛtādikaṃ vivṛṇoti, saṃskṛtamiti| daivī devasambandhinī devavyavahāyryā vā, vāk vākyaṃ, maharṣibhiḥ praktanapaṇḍitaiḥ munibhiḥ, nāma prasiddhaṃ, saṃskṛtaṃ saskārarūpatayā tadākhyamityarthaḥ, anvākhyātā saṃskṛtetyākhyāyā pascādvāvahṛtetyarthaḥ| (saṃskṛtaśabdasya klīvatve'pi uddeśyādhīnatvena strītvam; uddesyasya prādhānyañca nityasthitāyāḥ saṃskṛtavācaḥ pāṇinyādibhiḥ maharṣibhiḥ śaṣyāṇāṃ pratipattyarthaṃ saṃskāropāyapradarśanādavagantavyam; anuśabdaprayogāśca tata eva yujyate sthitāyāḥ evānvākhyānasambhavāditi) | prākṛtānāṃ nīcānāmidaṃ prākṛtaṃ, tasya kramaḥ niyamaḥ, anekaḥ bahuvidhaḥ, tathā tadbhavaḥ tasmāt saṃskṛtāt bhavaḥ utpannaḥ, tatsamaḥ saṃskṛtasadṛśaḥ, tathai deśo tattadeśapracalita ityarthaḥ| tatra sākṣāt saṃskṛtodbhavā mahārāṣṭrīyabhāṣā, śaurasenyādayaḥ saṃskṛtatulyāḥ, aparāśca tattaddeśavilasitāḥ iti bodhyam| deśonāmapi saṃskṛtasādṛśyāt dvāveva bhedāviti kecit; (uktañaca, "ārṣotthamārṣatulyañcadvividhaṃ prākṛtaṃ viduḥ"iti) | | 33| | 

prākṛtalakṣaṇamabhidhāya tasya prakṛṣṭāpakṛṣṭāvāha, mahārāṣṭrāśrayāmiti| mahārāṣṭrāśrayāṃ mahārāṣṭro nāma dakṣiṇāpathavatrtī janapadaviśeṣaḥ, tadāśrayāṃ tatsambandhinīṃ, tatratyaivryavahṛtāmityarthaḥ, bhāṣāṃ prakṛṣṭam utkṛṣṭaṃ, prākṛtaṃ viduḥ jānanti, budhā iti śaṣaḥ| sūktiratnānāṃ sūktayaḥ suvacanāni eva, ratnāni sahṛdayānandajananāt ratnasvarūpāṇi, teṣāṃ sāgaraḥ mahādādhāratayā ratnākararūpaḥ, setubandhādi setubandhaḥ tadākhyāprākṛtakāvyagranthaviśeṣaḥ tadādi, setubandhaprabhṛtiprākṛtakāvyamityarthaḥ, ādipadena daśamukhabadhādīnāṃ prarigrahaḥ, yanmayaṃ yadātmakaṃ, yanmahārāṣṭrīyabhāṣayā racitam ityarthaḥ| etenānayā bhāṣayā praktanāḥ kavayaḥ bahūni kāvyāni nirmamurityāyātam| | 34| | 

"deśo" ityanena deśānāṃ bahutvāt bahavaḥ prākṛtabhedāḥ sambhavanti ityaparāṇyapi prākṛtāni darsayati, śaurasenīti| śūraseno nāma mathurāsannihitajanaparabhedaḥ, gauḍaḥ deśa viśeṣaḥ lāṭaḥ kaścit deśabhedaḥ, tattaddeśapracalitā tathā tādṛśī tatsadṛśī, anyā ca deśīyā bhāṣā prākṛtam iti, evaṃ vyavahāreṣu sannidhiṃ yāti prāpnoti, (anyā ca tādṛśotyanena deśasaṃjñayā nirūpitāḥ prāceyāvantīmāgadhadrāviḍaībāḍhiddhaṭhṭhaḍalakīprabhṛtayaḥ sarvā eva bhāṣāḥ prākṛtanāmnā kavibhirnibadhyante ityāpi sūcitam) | | 35| | 

kramaprāptamapabhraṃśaṃ nirūpayati, ābhīrādīti| kāvyeṣu, ābhiro gopajātiviśeṣaḥ, ādipadena kaivatrtacāṇḍaālādīnāṃ grahaṇaṃ, teṣāṃ giraḥ vācaḥ, apabhraṃśa iti smṛtāḥ apabhraṃśanāmnā kathitāḥ, śāstreṣu kāvyātirikteṣu vedādiṣu tu, saṃskṛtāt anyat saṃskṛtātiriktaṃ sarvaṃ, prākṛtam ullikhitamābhīrādivacanañca ityarthaḥ, apabhraṃśatayā uditaṃ kathitam| (saṃskṛtādibhedena caturvidhānāṃ bhāṣāṇāṃ tisṛṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ nirūpitaṃ, miśralakṣamaṃ yogārthabalādeva lābhāt nirarthakamiti na kṛtaṃ, tataśca nānābhāṣāmiśritatvarūpaṃ miśralakṣaṇamityavagantavyam) | | 36| | 

saṃskṛtaprākṛtāpabhraṃśān nirūpya tattallakṣyāṇi miśralakṣyañca nirūpayati, saṃskṛtamiti| sargabandaḥ pūrvamuktaḥ prabandhaviśeṣaḥ, mahākāvyamityarthaḥ, ādipadena khamḍakāvyādīnāṃ parigrahaḥ, saṃskṛtaṃ saṃskṛtabhāṣayā racitam; (atra saṃskṛtādiśabdānāṃ tattadbhāṣārthe abhidhānāt saṃskṛtamayatvārthe lakṣaṇā| tathā ca agnipurāmaṃ, "sargabandho mahākāvyamārabdhaṃ saṃskṛtena yat| tadbhavaṃ na viśettatra tatsamaṃ nāpi kiñcana| | "iti| etena mahākāvye miśrakāvyavadvaktṛviśeṣagatvaniyamābhāvāt nīcānāmapi saṃskṛtabhāṣayaiva prayoga iti sūcitam) | skandhakādikaṃ skandhakaṃ chandobhedaḥ, ādipadena galitakādīnāṃ grahaṇam| (taduktaṃ, "chandasā skandhakenaiva tathā galitakairapi"iti) , skandhakādicchandoracitaṃ kāvyaṃ prākṛtaṃ prākṛtabhāṣāmayamityarthaḥ| āsārādīni āsārādibhiḥ chandobhiḥ nibaddhaṃ kāvyamityarthaḥ, apabhraṃśaḥ| (apabhraṃśabhāṣānibaddhakāvyeṣu sargasyakaḍavakasaṃjñā parairabhihitā granthakṛtā nādṛtā; yaduktam, "apabhraṃśanibaddhe'smin sargāḥ kaḍavakābhidhāḥ| tathā'pabhraṃśayogyāni chandāṃsi vividhāni ca| | "iti) | nāṭakādi tu nāṭakam ādi yasya tat, ādinā prakaramādirūpakāṇāṃ nāṭikādyuparūpakāṇāñca parigrahaḥ, (tatra rūpakāṇi daśa, nāṭikādyuparūpakāṇyaṣṭādaśa iti jñeyaṃ, teṣāṃ lakṣamāni granthāntare draṣṭavyāni) ; miśrakaṃ nānābhāṣāmayamityarthaḥ, (nāṭakādau vividhabhāṣāprayogaśca vaktṛviśeṣaviṣaye niyamitavibhinnabhāṣābhirevāvagantavyaḥ| tathā coktaṃ nāṭakādiprastāve, "puruṣāṇamanīcānāṃ saṃskṛtaṃ syāt kṛtātmanām| śauraseni prayoktavyā tādṛśīnāñca yoṣitām| | āsāmeva tu gāthāsu mahārāṣṭrīṃ prayojayet| atroktā māgadhī bhāṣā rājāntaḥ puracāriṇām| | ceṭānāṃ rājaputrāṇāṃ śreṣṭhināñcādrdhamāgadhī| prācyā vidūṣakādīnāṃ dhūtrtānāṃ syādavantikā| | yodhanāgarikādaunāṃ dākṣiṇātyā hi dīvyatām| śakārāmāṃ śakādīnāṃ śākārīṃ samprayojayet| | "iti) | | 37| | 

kathāyāḥ miśrakāvyatva pratipādayan bhāṣāṃ nirūpayati, kathet| hi yataḥ, kathā pūrvoktaḥ kāvyabhedaḥ, sarvabhāṣābhiḥ saṃskṛtena ca badhyate viracyate, ataḥ sā miśrasaṃjñakamiti śeṣaḥ; kathā dvividhā, miśrabhāṣāmayī saṃskṛtamayī ca, tatra miśrabhāṣāmayyā miśratvaṃ kevalasaṃskṛtamayyāḥ kādāmbayryāderna tathātvam iti bhāvaḥ| vṛhatkathāṃ tadākhyakāvyantu, bhūtabhāṣā paiśācī bhāṣā, tanmayīṃ tadracitām, adbhutārthāṃ vismayarasapūrṇāṃ, prāhuḥ, vidvāṃsa iti śeṣaḥ, paiśācyā bhāṣayā nibaddhantu vṛhatkathākhyamapabhraṃśakāvyamityavadheyam| | 38| | 

samprati dṛśyaśravyatvabhedena kāvyasya dvaividhyamāha, lāsyeti| lāsyaṃ śrṛṅgārarasāviddhastrījananṛtyam; (taduktaṃ, "lāsaḥ strīpuṃsayorbhāvastadarhaṃ tatra sādhu vā| lāsya manasijollasakaraṃ mṛdvaṅgahāvavat| devyai devopadaṣṭatvāt prāyaḥ strībhiḥ prayujyate| | "iti| anyañca, "komalaṃ madhuraṃ lāsyaṃ śrṛḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍagārarasasaṃyutam| gaurītoṣakarañcāpi strīnṛtyantu taducyate| | "iti) | chalitaṃ puruṣanatrtanam; (taduktaṃ, "puṃnṛtyaṃ chalitaṃ viduḥ"iti) | śalyā kapalādeśe karavinyāsapūrvakaṃ nṛtyam; (tadāha, "bhāle hastaṃ samāveśya nṛtyaṃ śalyeti kīrttitam"iti| "śalyādi"ityatra"sāmyādi"iti kvacit pāṭhaḥ, tathātve sāmyaṃ gītavāditrādisamanvitaṃ nṛtyaṃ rāsāparayryāyamabhihitam) | ādipadeena tāmḍavādiparigrahaḥ| (tathoktaṃ, "tallāsyaṃ tāmḍavañceti chalitaṃ śalyayā saha| hallīśakañca rāsañca ṣaṭprakāraṃ vidurbudhāḥ| | "iti| tāṇḍunoditam| bharatāya tataḥ khyātaṃ loke tāmḍavasaṃjñayā| | "iti, "vīraraundrarasā''dhāramuddhataṃ śaṅkarapriyam| puruṣeṇa samārabdhaṃ nṛtyaṃ tāmḍavamucyate| | "iti ca| hallīsaṃ strīṇāṃ maṇḍalākāreṇa nṛtyam; yaduktaṃ, "mamḍalena tu yat strīṇāṃ nṛtyaṃ hallīśakaṃ viduḥ| tatra netā bhavedeko gopastrīṇāṃ yathā hariḥ| | ") tadevaṃ lāsyādisaṅghaṭitaṃ kāvyaṃ prekṣyārthāṃ prekṣyaḥ dṛśyaḥ, arthaḥ pratipādyaṃ vastu yasmin tat, naṭaiḥ rāmādyavasthānukaraṇasya darśaviṣayatvāt, tataśca darśanaviṣayatvaṃ dṛśyalakṣaṇaṃ, tañca nāṭakādikamityavadheyam| itarat anyat, dṛśyakāvyāt bhinnamuktakādi iti yāvat| śravyameva śravaṇanātraviṣayatvāt, tataśca śravaṇamātraviṣayatvaṃ śravyalakṣaṇamityāyātam| (uktañca bhojarājena, "śravyaṃ tat kāvyamāhuryannekṣyate nābhinīyate| śrotrayoreva mukhadaṃ bhavettadapi ṣaḍavidham| | "iti) | iti itthaṃ, sā prasiddhā, eṣā api uktarūpā api, na kevalaṃ prāguktā eva khāvyānāṃ bhedāḥ dṛśyaśravyatvabhedena punarapi dvividhaḥ bhedaḥ ityaperarthaḥ, dvayī dvividhā, gatiḥ kāvyabhedapravatrtakaḥ pantāḥ, udāhṛtā, prācīnairiti śeṣaḥ| | 39| | 

evaṃ kāvyabhedaṃ nirūpya tasya rītirnirūpayati, astīti| anekaḥ vividhaḥ, sa ca śāstrāntaranirddiṣṭaḥ, parasparaṃ sūkṣmabhedaḥ svalpamātrabhedaḥ, girāṃ vācāṃ, mārgaḥ racanāpaddhatiḥ, rītiḥ ityarthaḥ, sā ca sleṣādiguṇaviśiṣṭā padaracanārūpā; jhrtaduktaṃ vāmanena, "viśiṣṭā padaracanā rītiḥ" iti, rimanti gacchanti asyāṃ guṇāḥ iti vyutpatteḥ, (taduktaṃ, "vaidarbhādikṛtaḥ panthāḥ kāvye mārga iti smṛtaḥ| rīṅ gatāviti dhātoḥ sā vyutpattyā rītirucyate| | "iti) | asti śāstrantare vatrtate, jhrtatāca, "sā tridhā vaidarbhi gauḍaīyā pāñcālī ca" iti vāmanaḥ| atiriktā lāṭoti darpaṇakāraḥ| "vedarbhī cātha pāñcalī gauḍaī cāvantikā tathā| lāṭīyā māgadhī ceti ṣoḍhā rītirnigadyate| "iti bhojarājādayaḥ| tāsāṃ lakṣamāni ca tatroktāni yathā "tatrāsamāsā niḥśeṣasleṣādiguṇagumiphitā| vipañcīsvarasaubhāgyā vaidarbhīrītiriṣyate| | samastapañcaṣapadāmojaḥkāntivivarjitām| madhurā sukumārāñca pāñcalīṃ kavayo viduḥ| | samastātyudbhaṭapadāmojaḥ kāntigumānvitām| gauḍaīyet vijānanti rītiṃ rītivicakṣaṇāḥ| | antarāle tu pācalīvaidabhryoryā'vatiṣṭhate| sā'vantikā samastaiḥ syāt dvitraistricaturaiḥ padaiḥ | | samastarītivryāmiśrā lāṭīyā rītiriṣyate| pūrvarīteranirvāhāt khaṇḍarītistu māgadhī| | "iti| tāsu vaidarbhī yathā, "manīṣitāḥ santi gṛheṣu devatāstapaḥ kva vaste| kva ca tāvakaṃ vapuḥ? | padaṃ saheta bhramarasya pelavaṃ śirīṣapuṣpaṃ na punaḥ patatriṇaḥ| | "iti| sūkṣmabhedatayā ca tannirūpaṇena granthabāhulyakaramaṃ vṛtheti sphuṭabhedayorvaidarbhigauḍaīyayormataṃ prakaṭayitumāha tatra vaidarbhādipadairupacārāt vidarbhādideśasyāḥ kavayo lakṣyante, jhrvidarbhaśabdāt "śeṣa" iti aṇ pratyayaḥ| gauḍaśabdāt"vṛddhācchaḥ" iti chapratyayaḥṭa, tatasca vaidarbhaḥ vidarbhadeśīyakavipriyaḥ, gauḍaauyaḥ gauḍadeśasthakavisampataḥ tau mārgau, vaṇryate, yataḥ tau prasphuṭam antaraṃ yayoḥ tādṛśau, ekasya sukumāratvena aparasya utkaṭatvena atyantavaisādṛśyāt iti bhāvaḥ| jhratredañca pratibhāti yat, sthitāspi baḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavīṣu rītiśu prādhānyenāca vaidarbhagauḍaīyamārgayorullekhaḥ granthakṛtā kṛtaḥ, tena rītyantarāpekṣayā vaidarbharīterutkharṣaḥ sūcitaḥ taduktarṣapradarśanārthañca tadbhaidaviśiṣṭā gauḍaīyā'pi rītirdarśiteti| tathā hi rītibhedaśca guṇabhedādbhavati, vaidarbhīrītesca sarvagumopetatvenotkarṣatvaṃ, vaidarbhīrīteḥ sarvaguṇopotatvañca vāmanenāpayuktaṃ yathā, "samagraguṇopetā vaidarbhī" iti; aparaiśca"aspṛṣṭā doṣamātrābhiḥ samagraguṇaguṇamphitā| vipañcīsvarasaubhāgyā vaidarbhīrītiriṣyate| | "iti tathā tatpraśaṃsanañca dṛśyate, "sati vaktari satyarthe sati śabdānusāsane| asti tanna vinā yena parisravati vāṅmadhu| | "iti; vakṣyati ca, "iti vaidarbhabhārgasya prāṇā daśaguṇāḥ smṛtāḥ"iti| ata eva kavikulaśiromaṇiḥ kālidāso'pi tāmeva rītimavalambā prāyeṇa kāvyāni nibabandhuḥ, uktañca, "vaidarbharītisandarbhe kālidāsaḥ pragalbhate" iti| udāhṛtañca vāmanena abhijñānaśākuntalīyaṃ śakuntalāvilokanotkalikāvaśaṃvadahṛdayasya mṛgayāvihārādviriraṃsato duṣmantasyoktirūpaṃ "gāhantā mahiṣāḥ" ityādikālidāsaracitapadyam| tato yathā sphuṭabhedatvena tathā pradhānatvena ca vaidarbharītiranena darśiteti dikṭa| | 40| | 

vaidarbhīrīterātmabhūtān śleṣādidaśaguṇān nāmato nirdiśati, śleṣaḥ ityādi dvābhyām| śleṣādayaḥ daśa guṇāḥ śabdārthotkarṣavidhāyitvena guṇaśabdavācyāḥ, vaidarbhamārgasya vaidarbhīyarītyāḥ, prāṇāḥ jīvanabhūtāḥ, tān vinā tatra vidarbhajanānāṃ kāvyatvānaṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ| etena śleṣādimatpadaracanāvattvaṃ vaidarbhītvamityuktam; smṛtāḥ, paṇḍitaiḥ iti śeṣaḥ| (smṛtāḥ ityanena prācīnasampradāyaiḥ śleṣādidaśānāmeva guṇatvamaṅgīkṛtam, anyaistu trayāṇāmiti sūcitam| uktañca kāvyaprakāśe, "mādhuyryaujaḥ prasādākhyāstrayaste na punardaśa"iti| | | 41| | 

kecittu caturviśatisaṅkhayakā guṇā ityapyāhuḥ yathā, "caturviṃśatisaṅkhayātāsteṣu ye śabdasaṃśrayāḥ| te tāvadabhidhīyante nāmalakṣaṇayogataḥ| | śleṣaḥ prasādaḥ "ityupakramya "ojastathānyadaucityaṃ proyānatha suśabdatā| tadvat samādhiḥ saukṣmāyañca gāmbhīyryamatha vistaraḥ| | saṅkṣepaḥ sammitatvañca bhāvikatvaṃ gatistatā| rītiruktistathā prauḍhirathaiṣāṃ lakṣyalakṣaṇe| | "iti| tathā śabdaguṇanāṃ vāhyatvamarthaguṇānāmāntaratvaṃ ye tu doṣatatve'pi guṇatāṃ yānti teṣāṃ vaiśeṣikatvañcāhuḥ, yathā, "trividhāśca guṇāṃḥ kāvye bhavanti kavisammatāḥ| vāhyāścābhyantarāścaiva ye ca vaiśeṣikā iti| | vāhyāḥ śabdaguṇāsteṣu cāntārāstvarthasaṃśrayāḥ| vaiśeṣikāstu te nūnaṃ doṣatve'pi hi ye guṇāḥ| | "iti| darpaṇakārādayastu ātmanaḥ śauyryadaya iva ramasya kāvyātmabhūtasya dharmā mādhuyryaujaḥ prasādā eva guṇā ityāhuḥ) | gauḍavatrmani gauḍaīyarītyām, eṣāṃ śleṣādidaśaguṇānāṃ, prāyaḥ bāhulyena, vipayryayaḥ vaiparītyaṃ, dṛśyate; vipayryayaśca kvacit sarvathā, kvacit kenacit aṃśaneti bodhyam| atra prāya ityanena kutracit etayoḥ samatā'pi| jhrvaidarbhīrītistu svalpaprāṇākṣarattavāditvena gauḍaīrītistu vaidarbhīviruddhadharmavattveneti anayorbhedaḥ samūhanīyaḥ| uktañca, "asamastaikasamastā yuktā daśabhirguṇaiśca vaidarbhī| vargadvitīyabahulā svalpaprāṇākṣarā ca suvidheyai| | "iti| "bahutarasamāsayuktā sumahāprāṇākṣarā ca gauḍaīyā| rītiranuprāsamahimaparatantrā'stobhāvakyā ca| | "iti| vāmanastu samagragumayuktatve vaidarbhī ojaḥkāntigumavattve gauḍhīyā ityevaṃ bhedamāha, yathā, "ojaḥkāntimatī gauḍaīyā iti| tatra ślokaḥ samastātyutkaṭapadābhojaḥ kāntiguṇānvitām| gauḍaīyāmiti gāyanti rīti rītiṃviśāradāḥ| | "iti| | 42| | 

idānīṃ śleṣādīnāṃ lakṣaṇāni nirūpayati, śliṣṭamityādi| alpaḥ prāṇaḥ sāraḥ, uñcāraṇaprayāsaḥ ityarthaḥ, yeṣāṃ tāni akṣarāṇi uttarāṇi pradhānāni , pracurāṇi iti yāvat, yasmin tādṛśam, alpaprāmākṣarāśca hrasvasvarā vargīyā viṣamavarṇāyarasaśceti; aspṛṣṭaṃ śaithilyaṃ yena tat vinyāsavaśāt śithilatvena alakṣyamāṇaṃ vākyaṃ śliṣṭaṃ śleṣagumayuktamityarthaḥ, tathā ca alpaprāṇākṣaraghaṭitabandhasyāpyantarāntarā mahāprāṇākṣaravinyāsena bhinnnāmapi padānāmekapadatāpratibhāsahetutvena sandarbhasyāśithilatvapratibhāsanaṃ śleṣa iti phalitam| (uktañca kramadīśvareṇāpi, "alpaprāṇeṣu varṇeṣu vinyoso'pyantarāntarā| mahāprāṇasya ca śleṣo yathā'yaṃ bhramaradvaniḥ| | "iti) | sleṣalakṣaṇamabhidhāya tadviṣayryayabhūtaṃ śithilamudāharaṇena darśayan gauḍaīmatamupanyasyati, śithilamiti| śithila śleṣalakṣaṇavipayryāsarūpamatikomalam| yathā iti udāharaṇapradarśanaparaṃ, mālatīmālā lolaiḥ capalaiḥ, alibhiḥ bhramaraiḥ, kalilā vyāptā, atra dantyavarṇaprāyatvenātikomalatvānna vaidarbhaiḥ śleṣatvamaṅgīkriyate, gauḍaīstu bandhāgāḍhatayā anuprāsavattayā ca tadiṣyate iti bhāvaḥ| (bhojastu evaṃvidhamudāharaṇamarītimadākhyadoṣaduṣṭaṃ manyate, yadāha, "guṇānāṃ dṛśyate yatra śleṣādīnāṃ vipayryayaḥ| arītimaditi prāhuḥ | | " tatra"vipayryayeṇa śleṣasya sandarbhaḥ śithilo bhavet" iti lakṣaṇaṃ vidhāya śithilamudāhṛtaṃ yathā, "ālīyaṃ mālatīmālā lolālikalilā manaḥ"iti| etena yat kenacit"ata eva śithilam ityatra apikāro'dhyāhatrtavyaḥ śithilamapi anuddhatamapi, aspṛṣṭam aprakāśitaṃ, śaithilyaṃ yasya tat" ityevaṃ vyākhyāya mālatīmālā ityādikamugṛdāharamaṃ lakṣyatvena darśitaṃ tadasaṅgatamiva pratibhāti, tathātve vaidarbhagauḍayorekodāharaṇatayā "vaṇryete prasphuṭāntarau"ityasyāsaṅgatireva syāt| tathā vakṣyamāṇe'pi "anuprāsadhiyā gauḍeḥ" ityādau"iti"ityasya ityapītyartaṃ vidhāya yat "mālatīmālā" ityādāvanuprāsasadbhāve vaidarbhagauḍayoḥ samānaviṣayatvaṃ, vakṣyamāṇo"mālatīdāma"ityādau anuprāsāsadbhāve tu bhinnaviṣayatvaṃ darśitaṃ tadapi na, tādṛśamedakalpane mānābhāvāt gauravāñca| tathā hi daṇḍimatamanusāratā bhojena viśadīkṛtyoktaṃ, "śliṣṭamaspṛṣṭaśaithilyaṃ śithilaṃ tadvipayryayaḥ| gauḍaīyairiṣyate tattu bandhaprāśastyagauravāt| | yathā līlāvilolalalanālalitālakalāsakāḥ| viluptamālatīmālā jalakālanilā vavuḥ| | "iti| kiñca, "guṇaḥ suśliṣṭapadatā śleṣa ityābhidhīyate"iti śleṣalakṣaṇaṃ vidadhatā "ubhau yadi vyomni pṛthak pravāhau"ityādyudāharaṇañca darśayatā tena śrutyanuprāsavatsandarbe vaidarbhāṇāṃ śliṣṭatvaṃ pramāṇokṛtameva| goḍamate tu anuprāsamahimnā bandhasya gāḍhatā'vabhāsādatra śleṣākhyaguṇo boddhavyaḥ iti sarvamavadātam| anye'pi gauḍaīlakṣamaśleṣamudāharanti yathā, "unmajjajjalakuñjarendrarabhasāsphālānubandhoddhataḥ sarvāḥ parvatakandarodarabhuvaḥ kurvan pratidhvaninīḥ| uñcairuñcati dhvaniḥ śrutipathonmāthī yathā'yaṃ tatā prāyaḥ preṅekhadasaṅkhayaśaṅkhadhavalā veleyamudgacchati| | "iti| atrānuprāsasadbhāvāt mahāprāṇākṣaraghaṭitatvatbandhasya vikaṭatvāñca gauḍaīlakṣaṇaḥ śleṣaḥ, anuprāsamātrasya lakṣaṇaghaṭakatve taḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavaṭitavakṣyamāṇamādhuyryādau ativyāpteriti jñeyam) | | 43| | 

anuprāsadhiyate| gauḍeḥ gauḍadesanivāsibhiḥ kavibhiḥ, anuprāsadhiyā anuprāso varṇāvṛttirūpaḥ śabdālaṅkāraḥ, tasya dhiyā bodhena, tatā bandhagauravāt anuprāsasadbhāvena racanāyā gāḍhatvāt, tat mālatīmāletyādi śleṣodāharaṇam, iṣṭam abhilaṣitaṃ, te anuprāsabahulāmejaḥkāntimatīṃ racanāṃ prāyaśa icchantīti bhāvaḥ| (etena gauḍaānāṃ sumahāprāṇākṣaravarṇaghaṭitatvābhāve'pi yatrānuprāsabāhulyaṃ bandhagāḍhatvañca bhavet tadapyabhimatam) | tataśca vaidarbhīgauḍayoḥ ko bheda ityāha, vaidarbhairiti| vaidarbhaiḥ vidarbhadeśauryaḥ kavibhiḥ, "mālatīdāma laṅghitaṃ bhramaraiḥ"iti evaṃrūpam, anuprāsahīnamapi vākyāmityarthaḥ, iṣṭaṃ śliṣṭatvenāṅgīkṛtaṃ, teṣām anuprāsāsadbhāve'pi śleṣatvakītrtanāditi bhāvaḥ| jhryadyapi anuprāsavacchleṣodāharaṇe vaidarbhīgauḍayoḥ sāmyaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ, tathā'pi anuprāsahīnodāharame gauḍaīlakṣaṇāpraveśādubhayorbhinnaviṣayatvaṃ darśitamiti dik| ayañca śleṣaḥ śabdamātragumaḥ| arthaguṇastu anyairukto yatā, "teṣāṃ śleṣaḥ iti proktaḥ saṃvidhāno susūtratā"iti| vāmanenāpi yathoktaṃ, "ghaṭanā śleṣaḥ" iti; ghaṭanā ca kramakauṭilyānulvaṇatvopapattiyogaḥ| tatra idaṃ kṛtvā idaṃ katrtavyamiti kramaḥ, lokātigāminī vakratā kauṭilyam, atimātratayā pratibhāsāvo'nulvaṇatvaṃ, kathamevamarthaḥ saṅgacchate iti anupapattisamādhānaupayikaviśeṣaniveśanam upapattiḥ; tathā ca krameṇa kauṭilyena anulvaṇatayā upapattyā yojanamarthaśleṣa iti lakṣamam| ata eva darpaṇakāreṇoktaṃ yathā, "śleṣaḥ kramakauṭilyānulvaṇatvopapattiyogarūpaghaṭanātmā| tatra kramaḥ kriyāsantatiḥ, vidagdhaceṣṭitaṃ kauṭilyam, aprasiddhavarṇanāviraho'nulvaṇatvam, upapādakayuktivinyāsa upapattiḥ, eṣāṃ yogaḥ sammelanaṃ, sa eva rūpaṃ yasyāḥ ghaṭanāyāḥ tadrūpaḥ| yathā, "dṛṣṭvaikāsanasaṃsthite priyatame" ityādi; atra darśanādayaḥ kriyā, ubhayasamarthanarūpaṃ kauṭilyaṃ, lokasaṃvyavahārarūpamanulvaṇatvam, "ekāsanasaṃsthite" "paścādupetya""nayanepidhāya" "īṣadvakritakandharaḥ"iti copapādakāni, eṣāṃ yogaḥ"iti| | 44| | 

prasādaṃ nirūpayati, prasādavaditi| prasiddhaḥ arthaḥ pratipādyavasta yasya tat ubhayārthakaśabdānām aprasiddhārthe prayogo nihatārthatārūpadoṣaḥ tadrahitamityarthaḥ, pratītyām arthaparijñāne, subhagaṃ saralaṃ, jhaṭityarthabodhakamityarthaḥ, vacaḥ vacanaṃ, prasādavat prasādagumayuktaṃ, tatra udāharaṇam, indroriti| indavaraṃ nīlotpalaṃ, tadvat dyutiryasya tādṛśaṃ śyāmavarṇaṃ, lakṣma kalaṅkaḥ, indoḥ candrasya, lakṣmīṃ śriyaṃ, tanoti vistārayati| aba indvādiśabdāḥ prasiddha candrādyartheṣu prayuktā jhaṭityarthabodhakāśceti jñeyam| jhrtathā coktaṃ kaṇṭhābharaṇe, "prasiddhārthapadatvaṃ yat sa prasādo nigadyate| "yatā, "gāhantā mahiṣā nipānasalilam"ityadi, iha prasiddhārthapadopādānāt prasādkhyaśabdaguṇatvam| arthagataprasādastu tatraivoktaḥ yathā, "yattu prākaṭayamarthasya prasādaḥ so'bhidhīyate"iti, arthasya prākaṭayaṃ vaimalyam; saata eva vāmanaḥ, "arthavaimalyaṃ prasādaḥ" iti lakṣamamāha; yathā, "ayamudayati mudrābhañjanaḥ padminīnāmudayaguruvanālībālamandārapuṣpam| virahavidhurakokadvandvabandhurvibhindan kupitakapikapolakroḍatāmrastamāṃsi| | "iti; atra padminīvikāśakaraṇe kokaśokaharaṇe tamovidāraṇe ca anukto'pi sūyryalakṣaṇo'rtaḥ prakaṭamupalakṣyate iti arthaprasādaḥ, tathā ca nirdoṣatve mati parisphaṭārthavatpadatvaṃ prasāda iti bhāvaḥṭa| | 45| | 

gauḍaauyānāṃ mataṃ darśayati, vyutpannamiti| gauḍaīyaiḥ taddeśavāsibhi paṇḍitaiḥ, na atirūḍhamapi anatiprasiddhamapi, nihatārthatvadoṣākalitamapītyarthaḥ, vyutpannaṃ vyutpattiyuktaṃ yogārthaghaṭitamityathaḥ, vākyam, iṣyate bandhagāḍhatāvaśāt vaicitrādāyakatvādaṅgīkriyate, nihatārthatvādidoṣāstu śithilabandhe kāvye eva gauḍaiḥ doṣatvena manyante, gāḍhabandhe tu vaicitrayāvahatvena na te doṣā ityādriyante ityavadheyam| udāharati, yatheti| anatyarjunājanmasadṛkṣāṅkaḥ anatyarjunaṃ nātidhavalaṃ, yat ajanma utpalaṃ tasya sadṛkṣaḥ sadṛśaḥ, aṅkaḥ kalaṅkaḥ yasya tathoktaḥ, balakṣaḥ dhavalaḥ, gauḥ kiraṇo yasya saḥ candra iti| (atra arjunaśabdaṛ kātrtavīyryārjunapāṇḍavayoḥ prasiddhaḥ śvetavarṇe aprasiddha iti nihatārthatvadoṣaduṣṭaḥ, abjanmaśabdaśca utpale kenāpi na prayuktatayā aprayuktadoṣākalitaḥ, sadṛkṣaśabdasca upamāgarbhabahuvrīhiṇaivārthabodhanāt adhikaḥ prayukta iti adhikapadatādoṣayukta eva, tathā balakṣaśabdo'pi aprayuktatādoṣakalitaḥ, śrutikaṭū ca sadṛkṣabalakṣaśabdau, tathā vṛṣādau prasiddhasya gośabdasya aprasiddhe kirame prayuktatvāt tatrāpi nihatārthatādoṣaḥ, "anatyurjanābjanma"ityatra sandhau kaśṭatvañca; itthañca bahudoṣamapi kāvyaṃsānuprāsaprabandhasya gāḍhatvāt gauḍaāḥ kāvyatvenādriyante| evañca kaṣṭatvādidoṣāsadbhāvena jhaṭityarthopasthāpakatvaṃ prasāda iti vaidarbhāḥ| gauḍaīyakavibhistathāvidhaprasādo nāṅgīkriyate iti tato bhedaḥ| kiñca sarasvatīkaṇṭhābharame tu guṇavipayryayādarītimadākhyadoṣoharaṇatvena padyādrdhatayā nibaddhamidam, "aprasannaṃ bhavet vākyaṃ prasādasya vipayryayāt| yathā, anaṅgakamalaṃ cakre mayamānā marālikā| | yasyānatyarjunābjanmasadṛkṣāṅgo balakṣaguḥ| | "iti, tena ca vaidarbhāṇāmidaṃ padyaṃ doṣatayaiva udāharaṇauyamityabhiprāyaḥ| ayañca prasādākhyaguṇaḥ arthagata eva, śabdagatastu "ojomiśritaśaithilyātmā" iti darpaṇakārokto veditavyaḥ; udāhṛtañca tenaiva, yathā, "yo yaḥ śastraṃ bibhartti svabhujagurumadāt pāṇḍavīnāṃ camūnāṃ, yo yaḥ pāñcālagotre śiśuradhikavayā garbhaśayyāṃ gato vā| yo yastatkarmasākṣī carati mayi raṇe yaśca pratīpaḥ, krodhāndhastasya tasya svayamiha jagatāmantakasyāntako'ham| | "iti| nanu viruddhayoḥ ojaḥ prasādayoḥ kataṃ melanamityapi nāśaṅkanīyam, ojomiśritaśaithilyasya prasādaguṇatvena viśuddhaśaithilyasya tu doṣatvena sarvairevāṅgīkṛtatvāt| tathā hi "gāḍhabandhatvamoja" "śaithilyaṃ prasādaḥ" iti sūtrayatā vāmanena ojovipayryayarūpasya śaithilyasya guṇavipayryayadoṣatvameva syāditi śaṅkāṃ parākatrtum ojasā miśritasya śaithilyasya guṇatvamityuktaṃ, yathā, "guṇaḥ saṃpalavāt" iti "na śuddhaḥ" iti sūtreṇa punaḥ tadamiśritaśaithilyasya guṇatvaṃ nirākṛtya doṣatvameva sthāpitam| viruddhayorojaḥ prasādayoḥ kathaṃ saṃplava ityataḥ uktaṃ, "satvanubhavasiddhaḥ" iti| atra ca ślokaḥ, "karuṇaprekṣaṇīyoṣu saṃplavaḥ sukhaduḥkhayoḥ| yatā'nubhavataḥ siddhastathaivaujaḥ prasādayoḥ| | "iti) | | 46| | 

samatāṃ nirūpayati, samamiti| bandheṣu racanāsu, aviṣamam avilakṣaṇam, upakramopasaṃhārayoḥ sadṛśamityarthaḥ, samaṃ samatākhyagumayuktaṃ, tathā ca mṛdusphuṭonbhiśravarṇānāmanyatamena upakramopasaṃhārayoḥ yadavaiṣamyeṇa bhaṇanaṃ sā samatā ityarthaḥ te bandātrividhāḥ, mṛduḥ komalasvareṣu hrasvāntyāḥ vyañjaneṣu vargāntyāḥ dantyāsca mṛduvarṇā ityarthaḥ sphuṭaḥ tīvraḥ, svareṣu dīrghasvarāḥ vyañjaneṣu nakāravarjaṭavargarephaśaṣahāḥ dantyasaṃyogāsca varṇāḥ ityarthaḥ, madhyamaḥ ubhayātmaka iti vandhānāṃ trividhatayā samatāyāstraividhyamatrāvagantavyam| bandhānāṃ mṛdutvāderhetumāha, mṛdviti| mṛduvarmānāṃ hrasvasvarādīnāṃ, sphuṭānāṃ dīrghasvarādīnāṃ, tathā unmiśrāṇām ubhayātmakānāṃ varṇānāṃ, vinyāsaḥ yoniḥ kāramaṃ yeṣāṃ tādṛśāḥ| (atra mṛdvādivarṇaghaṭitatvaṃ yathā trividhabhedahetistathā asamāsadīrghasamāsamadhmasamāsā api; yaduktam, "asamāsā samāsena madhyamena ca bhūṣitamā| tathā dīrghasamāseti tridhā saṅghaṭanoditā| | "iti| ayañca gumaḥ śabdagata eva, arthaguṇastu samatā yādṛśena prakṛtipratyayogena prakāntaṃ tādṛśenaiva yatra samāpanaṃ bhavet, tatraiveti bodhyam, ata eva "prakāntaprakṛtipratyayāvipayryāsena arthasya visaṃvāditāvicchedaḥ, sa ca prakamabhaṅgarūpadoṣaviraha eva"iti darpaṇakaireṇoktam, udāhṛtañca yathā, "udti savitā tāmrastāmra evāstameti ca"ityādi| vipayryayastu yathā, "pirasīda caṇḍi tyaja manyumañcasā, janastavāyaṃ puratakṛtāñjaliḥ| kimarthamuktampitapīvarastanadvayaṃ tvayā luptavilāsamāsyate| | "iti| atra prasīda tyaja iti katrtṛvācitayā prakrāntasya āsyate ityatra tyāgānna samatā) | | 47| | 

uktāyāḥ samatāyāḥ traividhyaṃ darśayati, kokiletyādi dvābhyām| kokilānām ālāpena vācālaḥ mukharaḥ, malayānilaḥ mām eti, pīḍayituṃ sikhayituṃ vā iti śeṣaḥ| (atra mṛdunopakrāntasya tenaiva samāpanamiti mṛdubandagā samatā) | ucchalantaḥ udgacchantaḥ, śīkarāḥ jalakamā yasya tādṛśam, acchāccham atisvaccha, yat nirjharāmbhaḥ tasya kaṇaiḥ bindubhiḥ, ukṣitaḥ abhiṣiktaḥ, suśītala iti yāvat, malayānilaḥ māmeti| (atra sphuṭabandhena upakrantasya sandarbhasya tenaiva upasaṃhāra iti sphuṭabandhagatā samatā) | | 48| | 

candanet| candane pramayaḥ saṃsargaḥ candanapramayaḥ, tena udgandhiḥ udgatagandhaḥ, mandaḥ mṛdulaḥ, malayamārutaḥ malayānilaḥ| (atra sphuṭamṛdumiśravarṇaghaṭitabandhatvānmadhyamo bandha, tenaivopakramopasaṃhārāviti madhyamabandhagateyaṃ samatā| evaṃ samatāyāḥ traividhyamanyatrāpi dṛśyate yathoktaṃ, "yanmṛduprasphuṭonmiśravarṇabandhavidhiṃ prati| avaiṣamyeṇa bhaṇanaṃ samatā sā'bhidhīyate| | "iti; ayañca śabdagataḥ samatāguṇaḥ, arthagatastu "avaiṣamyaṃ kramavatāṃ samatvamiti kīrttitam" ityuktalakṣaṇaḥ| tatrodāharamaṃ yathā, "agre strīnakhapāṭalaṃ kuravakaṃ śyāmaṃ dvayorbhāgayoḥ, bālāsokamupīḍharāgasubhagaṃ bhedonmukhaṃ tiṣṭhati| īṣaddhaddharajaḥ kaṇāgrakapiśā cūte navā mañjarī, mugdhatvasya ca yauvanasya ca sakhe! madhye madhuśrīḥ sthitā| | "iti| atra madhuśriyo maugdhayatyāgau yauvanārambhakṛtānāṃ viśeṣaṇānām avaiṣamyāt samatā| | bhojastvatra ślokādrdhagataṃ samatāyāstrividhamudāharamaṃ ruciramamanyamānaḥ "bhavet sa eva viṣamaḥ samatāyā vipayryayaḥ"iti lakṣaṇamabhidhāya prathamaślokaṃ samatāvipayryayarūpaviṣamadoṣasyodāharaṇatvena lakṣayitvā ca "atra pūrvādrdhasya mṛdubandhatvāt uttarādrdhasya ca gāḍhabandhatvāt samabandheṣu viṣamamiti viṣamo nāma śabdapradhānaḥ samatāvipayryayo doṣaḥ"ityāha; tanna ruciraṃ, sarvatrodāharamadarśanānantarameva pratyudāharaṇadarsanasya kramadarśanādityavadheyam) | samatātrayamitthaṃ sādrdhaślokena darśayitvā gauḍayometadvipayryayaṃ lakṣayituṃ vaiṣamyaṃ darśayati, spadrdhate iti| tatā ruddhaṃ nirākṛtaṃ, mama dhaiyryaṃ nāśayannityarthaḥ, vararāmāṇām uttamāṅganānāṃ, mukhānilaiḥ makhaniḥmṛtasugandhimārutaiḥ, spadrdhate sādṛśyaṃ labhate iti yāvat, saugandhyasāmyāditi bhāvaḥ| (atra sphuṭabandhena upakramaḥ mṛdubandhonopasaṃhāra iti vaiṣamyāt proktalakṣaṇasamatāyā nāvasaraḥ| gauḍaīyāsitu samatāmatrāpi svīkurvanti ityabhiprāyaḥ) | | 49| | 

niruktalakṣaṇasamatāyāṃ gauḍaīyānāmanādaraṃ darśayati, itīti| iti uktaprakāraṃ, "spadrdhate"ityādiślokoktamiti yāvat, vaiṣamyaṃ samatāvipayryayajanyadoṣam, anālocya avicāyrya, arthānāṃ kāvyārthānām, alaṅkārāṇām anuprāsopamādīnāñca, ḍambarau āḍambarau, utkarṣau ityarthaḥ, apekṣamāṇā anusarantītyarthaḥ, paurastyā pūrvadeśīyā, gauḍaīyā ityarthaḥ, kāvyānāṃ paddhatiḥ vavṛdhe, te hi arthānusārema alaṅkārānusāreṇa ca kadācit bandhasya mārdavaṃ kadācit vaikaṭaṃya kutracidvā miśrībhāvamādriyante, na tu gumapakṣapātena pūrvoktarūpāṃ vaidarbhairādṛtāṃ samatām| ayaṃ bhāvaḥ, yatrārthasya mṛdutvaṃ tatra mudulo bandhaḥ, yatra tu arthasya vaikaṭayaṃ tatra vikaṭo bandhaḥ, yatra ca artasya miśrībhāvaḥ tatra madhyamo bandhaḥ, na tu yenaivārambhastenaiva samāpanamiti| (yuktañcaitat, tathā hi, "sāraḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍagāḥ kimu vaglitaiḥ kimaphalairāḍambarairjambukā, mātaṅgā mahiṣā madaṃ vrajata kiṃ śūnye'tha śūrā na ke? | kopāṭopasamudbhaṭotkaṭasaṭākoṭeribhāreḥ sanaiḥ, sindhudvānini huṅdṛte sphurati yat tadgarjitaṃ garjitama| | " ityatrottarādrdhe kupitasiṃhavarṇanarūpārthasya vaikadayāt upakrāntamṛdubandhatyāgo guma evetiḥ; ata eva patatprakarṣatādoṣasyāpi kvacidgumatvaṃ darpamakṛtā darsitaṃ, yathā, "patatparakarṣatā tathā| tatheti kvacidguṇaḥ, yatā, "cañcudbaja"ityādi| atra caturthapāde sukumārārthatayā śabdāḍambaratyāgo guṇaḥ, iti| vaidarbāstu arthavaiṣamye'pi yena bandhena upakramaḥ tenāsamāpanaṃ doṣatvenaiva manyante ityanayorbhedaḥ| evamalaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍakāre'pi gumavadalaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍakārā api rasasyītkarṣakāḥ na tu rasanirapekṣakāḥ iti vaidarbhamataṃ, gauḍaānāntu rasanirapekṣā apītyanayorbhedaḥ iti) | | 50| | 

mādhuyryaṃ nirūpayati, madhuramiti| rasavat rasāḥ śrṛṅgārādayaḥ, tadruṇayogāt bhāvatadābhāsā api rasaśabdavācyāḥ, te vidyante yatreti tat vākyaṃ madhuraṃ mādhuyryaguṇayuktam| (kecittu śrṛṅgārakaruṇānyataraprakāśānuguṇavyāpārāveśena śabdārthayoḥ cittadrutividhāyitvaṃ mādhuyryamityāhuḥ, yathā, "śrṛṅgāra eva madhuraḥ paraḥ praḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍalādano rasaḥ| tanmayaṃ kāvyamāśritya mādhuyryaṃ pratitiṣṭhati| | śrṛṅgāre vipralambhākhye karuṇa ca prakarṣavat| mādhayryamātratāṃ yāti yatastatrādhikaṃ manaḥ| | "iti) tarhi rasa eva mādhuyryamityarthata eva āyātaṃ, paraṃ guṇānāṃsākṣāt paramparayā vā rasotmakatvamityāśaṅvyāha, vācīti| vāci vākpratipādakatādṛśavarṇe, vastunyapi vākyapratipādyabhūte arthe'pi rasasthitiḥ rasānāṃ śrṛṅāgarādīnāṃ, sthitiḥ vyañcakatvasambandhenāvasthānam| (rasavyañjakavarṇāśca śrṛṅgārakaruṇaśāntarase, "mūrddhi vargāntyavarṇena yuktāṣṭaṭhaḍaḍhān vinā| raṇau laghū ca tadvayaktau varṇāḥ kāraṇātāṃ gatāḥ| | avṛttiralpavṛttiśca madhurā racanā tathā| | "ityukitalakṣaṇāḥ| vīrabībhatsaraseṣu ca, "vargasyādyatṛtīyābhyāṃ yuktau varṇau tadantimau| upayryadho dvayorvā sarephaṣṭaṭhaḍaḍhaiḥ saha| | śakāraśca ṣakāraśca tasya vyañjakatāṃ gatāḥ| tathā samāsabahulā "etallakṣaṇoktāḥ| hāsyādbhutabhayānakaraseṣu ca tattadavaśiṣṭā madhyavarṇā madhyasamāsāśca vijñeyāḥ, tathā rasavyañjakā arthāsca ratyādisthāyibhāvāḥ eva; tataśca rasavyañjakavarṇārthādimat vākyaṃ mādhuyryam iti lakṣaṇam) | rasavat ityanena prāptasya rasasya lakṣaṇamajñātvā tadvayañjakavarṇādīnāṃ parijñānasambhavāt rasalakṣaṇamāha, yeneti| yena dhīmantaḥ sāmājikāḥ, na tu kudhiya ityarthaḥ, madhuvratāḥ bhramarāḥ, madhuneva mādyanti mattā bhavanti, tsaya lokottarāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍalādarūpatvāditi bhāvaḥ, sa rasa iti śeṣaḥ; tathā ca sudhīyāṃ mādahetuḥ tādṛśakāvyārthānuśīlanajanmā bhāvaviseṣa eva rasaḥ| jhrsa ca "ratirhāsaśca śokasca krodhotsāhau bhayaṃ tathā| jugupsā vismayaścetthamaṣṭau proktāḥ śamo'pi ca| | "ityuktalakṣaṇasthāyibhāvarūpaḥ| ratyādisthāyibhāvasca lalanādibhi rālāmbana vibhāvaiḥ janatiḥ, udyānādibhiruddīpanavibhāvairuddīpitaḥ, kaṭākṣādibhiranubhāvaiḥ pratītiyogyakriyamāṇaḥ, utkaṇṭhadibhivryabhicāribhiḥ paripoṣitaḥ nāyakaniṣṭhatayā anumīyamānaḥ vāsanāsannikarṣeṇa sāmājikaiḥ sākṣāt kriyamāṇaśca rasaḥ iti saṃjñāṃ labhate| uktañca, "vyaktaḥ sa tairvibhāvādyaiḥ sthāyibhāvaḥ rasaḥ smṛtaḥ"iti, "vibhāvenānubhāvenavyaktaḥ sañcārimā tathā| rasatāmeti ratyādi sthāyibhāvaḥ sacetasām| | "iti ca| śrṛṅgārahāsyakaruṇarodravīrabhayānakabībhatsādbhutarasānāṃ vakṣyamāṇānāṃ sthāyibhāvāsca krameṇa ratihāsaśokakrodhotsāhabhayajugupsāvismayā veditavyāḥ| śāntarasasya ca śamaḥ| atra ca rasānāmaṣṭatvameva pratipādayiṣyate| tattu "aṣṭau nāṭyerasāḥ smṛtāḥ"ityukteḥ nāṭayābhiprāyeṇa, śravyakāvye sāntarasasyāpi sambhavāt; yaduktaṃ, "na tatra duḥkha na sukhaṃ na cintā na dveṣarāgau na ca kācidicchai| rasaḥ sa śāntaḥ kathito munīndraiḥ sarveṣu bhāveṣu śamapradhānaḥ| | "iti sudhībhirbhāvyam| kecittu, "bandhe pṛthakpadatvañca mādhuyryamuditaṃ budhaiḥ"ityuktalakṣamaṃ pṛthakpadatvaṃ mādhuyryaṃ śabdaguṇa ityāhuḥ, yatā, "sthitāḥ kṣaṇaṃ pakṣmasu tāḍitādhārāḥ payodharostedhanipātacūrṇitāḥ"ityādi| atra padeṣu saṃhitābhāvāt pṛthakpadatvena mādhuyryaṃ, tañca vāmanenāpyuktaṃ, "pṛthak padatvaṃ mādhuyryam" iti| arthaguṇamāduyryañca"mādhuyryamuktamācāyryaiḥ krodhādāvapyatīvratā"ityuktalakṣaṇaṃ jñeyam; yathā, "bhrūbhede sahasodgat'pi vadanaṃ nītaṃ parāṃ namratāmīṣanmāṃ prataci bhedakāri hasitaṃ noktaṃ vaco niṣṭharam| antarvāṣpajaḍaīkṛtaṃ prabhutayā cakṣurna vispharitaṃ, kopasca prakaṭīkṛto dayitayā muktasca no praśrayaḥ| | "atra vāsavadattālakṣaṇasyārthasya kope'pi yo'yaṃ vinayālambanena kopaciḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍananiḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanavaḥ tanmādhuyryam| vāmanastu, "uktivaitriyaṃ mādhuyryam" ityuktalakṣamaṃ mādhuyryam arthaguma ityāha, tadubhayamatavaiṣamyaṃ parilakṣyateṭa| | 51| śrutyanuprāsaghaṭitavarṇasaṃhateḥ rasavyañjakatāṃ darśayati, yayeti| yayā kayācit kaṇṭhatālvādirūpayā ityarthaḥ, śratyā śravamasādhanībhūtena uñcāraṇena, samānaṃ sadṛśaṃ, prāguñcaritavyañjanatulyamityarthaḥ, anubhūyate pratīyate, tadrūpā tādṛśānubhavaviṣayabhūtavyañjanavatau, padāsattiḥ padānām āsattiḥ avyavadhānena avasthitiḥ, sānuprāsā śrutyanuprāsavatī, ata eva rasam āvahatīti rasāvahā, hiravadhāraṇārthaḥ, niścitameva rasapoṣiketeyarethaḥ| jhranuprāsasya kāvyotkarṣakaratvamuktañca, "yathā jyotsnā śarañcandraṃ yathā lāvaṇyamaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍaganām| anuprāsastathā kāvyamalaṅkatrtumihakṣamaḥ| | "iti, etena tāluradādyekasthānoñcāyryatayā vyañjanavarṇānāṃ sādṛśyaṃ śrutyanuprāsaṃ ityarthāt āyātam| uktañca darpaṇakārma yathā, "uñcāyryatvāt yadekatra sthāne tāluradādike| sādṛśyaṃ vyañjanasyaiva śrutyanuprāsa ucyate| | " iti| asya ca alaṅkāraprakaraṇīyatve'pi iha gaṇaprasaṅgāt lakṣaṇamuktaṃ, vakṣyate ca, "kāścinmārgavibhāgārthamuktāḥ prāgapyalaṅkriyāḥ" iti| | 52| | 

udāharamaṃ darśayati, eṣa iti| brāhmaṇāḥ priyā yasya athavā brāhmāmānāṃ priyaḥ, eṣa rājā yadā lakṣmīṃ rājaśriyaṃ, prāptavān, tataḥ prabhṛti loke jagati, dharmasya utsavaḥ abhavat, asau dharmeṇa prajāḥ pālayāmāsa ityarthaḥ| (iha ṣakāravakārayoḥ ekasmāt mūddhrraḥ jakārayakārayostālutaḥ dakāralatakārayośca dantāduñcaritatvena sādṛśyamityataḥ śrutyanuprāsāḥ, tataśca tasya rājaviṣayakaratibhāvavyañjakatayā mādhuyryaguṇayogaḥ, padeṣu sahitābhāvāt pṛthakpadatvenāsya śabdagatatvañca iti vibhāvanīyam) | | 53| | 

atra vaiderabhīgauḍayormatabhedaṃ darśayannāha, itīti| itipadaṃ pūrvoktadyotakam; iti pūrvoktam, idaṃ padyaṃ gauḍaiḥ gauḍavāsibhiḥ kavibhiḥ, na ādataṃ mādhuyryaguṇavattyā vaicitrayātmakatvantu nasvīkṛtaṃ, vibhinnavarṇānāmekasthānoñcaritatve'pi rasānuguṇyasyānanubhavāditi bhāvaḥ| anuprāsastu vaṣcamāṇavarṇāvṛttirūpaḥ, tatpriyaḥ teṣāṃ gauḍaānāṃ, priyaḥ rasānukūlyatayā ādṛtaḥ| vaidarbhaistu anuprāsāt pūrvoktāt śrutyanuprāsādapi, prāyaḥ bahulyena, idam eṣa rājetyādi padyam, iṣyate ādriyate, (itthañca vaidarbhāṇāṃ śrutyanuprāsavarmāvṛttirūpānuprāsayorubhayoreva priyatā, gauḍaānāntu kevalaṃ varṇāvṛttirūpe iti anayormatabheda iti dik) | | 54| | 

anuprāsamāha, varṇāvṛttiriti| varmānām akṣarāmāṃ, vyañjanānām ityāśayaḥ, āvṛttiḥ punarañcāramam, āvṛttigatasādṛśyapratītirityarthaḥ, anuprāsaḥ, sādṛśyasyaiva vaicitrayajanakatvenālaṅkāratvāṅgīkārāt| (āvṛttiśabdo'tra tadgatasādṛśyārthakaḥ, ekasya varṇasya punaruñcāraṇena kathaṃ sādṛśyaṃ tayorabhinnatvādityapi na codyam, ekasyāpi uñcāramakālabhedena bhedāt tayorbibhinnatvaṃ bodhyam| etena varṇasya varmayorvarṇānāṃ vā punarañcāramena sāmyapratītiranuprāsa iti lakṣaṇamāyātam| ata evoktaṃ prakāśakārema, "varṇasāmyamanuprāsaḥ"iti| varṇānāmiti ekaśeṣadvandavaśāt varmasya varṇayorapīti bodhyame) | sa ca dvividhaḥ, pādeṣu padeṣu ca, pādāḥ padyacaturthabhāghāḥ teṣu, padeṣu tattatpādasthasuptiṅanteṣu ca, pādapadagata ityarthaḥ| jhrpādeṣu ceti bahuvacanena sarvapādeṣu anuprāsasthitirviseṣema manorameti sūcitam| tallakṣamamuktaṃ vāmanena, "pādānuprāsaḥ pādayamakavat" iti; udāharamaṃ yatā, "kavirājamavijñāya kutaḥ kāvyakriyādaraḥ| | "iti| āvṛttiśca svaravaiṣamye'pi vaicitrayamāvahati; tathā coktaṃ darpaṇakāreṇa, "anuprāsaḥ śabdasāmyaṃ vaiṣamye'pi svarasya yat"iti| āvṛttiśca avyavahitā mukhyā, vvadhāno'pi prathamoñcaritavarṇānāṃ sannikṛṣṭatvena pūrvānubhavasaṃskārasthityavadhi grāhyeti pratipādayitumāha, pūrveti| pūrvamuñcaritasya varmasya yaḥ anubhavaḥ śrāvaṇajñānaṃ, tajjnitaḥ saṃskāraḥ bhāvanāviseṣaḥ, tasya bodinī pālanī, adūratā dvitīyādivarmasya sānnidhyaṃ, yadi, vatrtate iti śeṣaḥ, tadaivānuprāsa ityanvayaḥ; ayaṃ bhāvaḥ, saṃskārasya prathamakṣame utpattiḥ, dvitīyakṣame sthitiḥ, tṛtīyakṣame nivṛttiḥ, iti niyamena avyavadhānena kiñcidavyavadhānena vā varṇoñcāraṇasya mādṛśyapratītijananāt sādṛśyasyaiva vaicitrājanakatvenālaṅkāratvāt tādṛśavarmāvṛttijanyasādṛśyamanuprāsa iti| (ayañca dvividhaḥ, ulvamaḥ anulvaṇasca, ulvaṇa utkaṭapadavinyāsātmakaḥ, anulvaṇaḥ komalapadātmakaḥ; tatrolvamo yathā, "vallībaddhodrdhajūṭodbhaṭamaṭati raṭatkodaṇḍadaṇḍaḥ"iti| navyāstu asya pañcavidhatvamāhuḥ, chekānuprāsaḥ vṛttyānuprāsaḥ śrutyanuprāsaḥ antyānuprāsaḥ lāṭānuprāsaśceti, taduktaṃ darpaṇakāreṇa, "cheko vyañjanasaṅghasya sakṛt sāmyamanekadhā| anekasyaikadhā sāmyamasakṛddā'pyanekadhā| ekasya sakṛdapyeṣa vṛttyanuprāsa ucyate| | uñcāyryatvāt yadekatra sthāne tāluradādike| sādṛśyaṃvyañjanasyaiva śrutyanuprāsa ucyate| | vyañjanañcet yathāvasthaṃ sahādyena svarema tu| āvattryate'ntyayojyatvādantyānugrāsa eva tat| | śabdārthayoḥ paunaruktyaṃ bhede tātpayryamātrataḥ| lāṭānuprāsa ityukto'nuprāsapañcadhā tataḥ| | "iti| tatra lāṭānuprāsodāharaṇaṃ yathā tatraiva, "smerarājīvanayane| nayane kiṃ nimīlite? | pasya nirjitakandarpaṃ kandarpavaśagaṃ priyam| | "iti) | | 55| | 

udāharati, candre iti| śarannisāyāḥ śāradīyarajanyāḥ, uttaṃse śirobhūṣamarūpe, kandastavakasannibhe māghyakusumagucchabhadṛśe, candre, indranīlanibhaṃ śyāmalam ityarthaḥ, lakṣma kalaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍakaḥ, alinabhramasrya, śriyaṃ śobhāṃ, sandadhāti dhārayati | (atra prathamadvitiyatṛtīyeṣu pādeṣu śakārakakāranakāra lakārāṇāṃ varṇanām anatidūravarttinī punarāvṛttiriti sāmyapratīteḥ vṛttyanuprāsaḥ, caturthe ca dakāradhakāratakāranakārāṇāṃ dantarūpaikasthānoñcāyryatvāt vaidarbhapriyaḥ śrutyanuprāsa iti dvividhonānuprāsalaṅkāreṇa vyañjitaṃ mādhuyryaṃ śrṛḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍagārarasaṃ padyaniṣṭhaṃ parapuṣṇāti| atra rūpakopamābhyāmanuprāṇitā nidarśanā nāma alakṛtiḥ, tataśca śabdārthobhayaniṣṭhaṃ mādhuyryamiti sudhībirvibhāvanīyam) | | 56| | 

svaravaiṣamyeṇa anuprāso darśitaḥ, idānīṃ tatsādṛśyena darśayati, cārviti| he bhīru! bhayaśīle! manmathākrāntaṃ kāmātrtaṃ, manmanaḥ mama mānasaṃ, nardayaṃ yathā tathā hantumudyamaṃ cāru manojñam, etat paridṛśyamānamityarthaḥ, cāndramasaṃ vimbaṃ candramaṇḍalam ambare ākāśe, paśyetyanvayaḥ| (māninīṃ kāminīṃ prati kasyacit nāyakasya uktiriyam| iha prathame pāde cākārarukārayoḥ svarasahitayoḥ sādṛśyāt vṛttyanuprāsaḥ; dvitīyatṛtīyayośca yathākramaṃ mbakārayormanmamanmayośca sāmyat chekānupirāso'pi; tathā caturthe dakārayostakārayośca svaravaiṣamyeṇa sādṛśyāt anyavidho vṛttyanuprāsasca| mādhuyryaguṇo'pyatra śabdārthobhayaniṣṭhatvena camatkārajananāt vipralambhākhyaṃ śrṛṅgāraṃ paripuṣṇāti) | | 57| | 

adūrataivānuprāsasya prayojakamikatyāha, itīti| atidūraṃ samadhikam, antaraṃ vyavadhānaṃ syāḥ sā atidūrāntarā, na atidūrāntarā nātidūrāntarā, tādṛśī śrutiḥ śravaṇaṃ yasya tādṛśam, iti prāk pradarśitodāharaṇadvayarūpam, anuprāsam icchanti, gauḍaā iti śeṣaḥ rāmāmukhambhojasadṛśacandramā iti evaṃvidhaṃ, tṛtīyacaturthayoḥ pādayoḥ mākārayoḥ sāmye'pi atidūrāntaraśrutim ityarthaḥ, anuprāsamiti śeṣaḥ, na tu, icchantīti ca śeṣaḥ, "rāmā""candramā" iti padasthayormākārayoratidūrasthatayā pūrvavarṇasaṃskārodbodhābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ| | 58| | 

anuprāsasya rasāvahatvakītrtanāt yatra vākyasya sadoṣatvena rasasya aparipuṣṭatā tatra na mādhuyryasadbhāva ityāha, smara iti| smaraḥ kāmaḥ, kharaḥ atitīkṣṇaḥ, kāntaḥ priyaḥ, khalaḥ niṣṭhuraḥ, naḥ asmākaṃ, kāyaḥ śarīraṃ, kopaśca, kāntaṃ pratīti śeṣaḥ, kṛśaḥ kṣīṇaḥ naṣṭasca, mānaḥ gauravaṃ, cyutaḥ vinaṣṭaḥ, rāgaḥ kāntaprāptyabhilāṣaḥ, adhikaḥ, mohaḥ mūcrchā, jātaḥ, kṣaṇe kṣaṇe iti śeṣaḥ, asavaḥ prāṇāḥ, gatāḥ, prayeṇeti śeṣaḥ| (idaṃ nāyakaṃ nirākṛtavatyāḥ galitamānāyāḥ kasyāścit kāminyāḥ paścāttāpa vacanam| atra prathame pāde rephayoḥ khakārayośca dvitīye kakārāmāṃ sāmyāt vṛttyanuprāsasya tathā uttarādrdhe takārādidantyavarṇāmāṃ sāmyāt śrutyanuprāsasya doṣākulatayā tādṛśavipralambhaśrṛṅgārasya na atiparipāṣakatā iti na mādhuyryaṃ, pratyata ojovipayryayākyadoṣatvenedamudāhṛtaṃ kaṇṭhābharaṇe, yaduktaṃ padyamidamudāhṛtya, "atra satyasamastapadābhidhāne satyapi ca arthasya saukumāyrye śleṣādigumasāmagrābhāvānna vaidarbhīrītiḥ, nāpi yathoktalakṣaṇābhāvāt gauḍaīyādayaḥ iti khamḍitarītitvādayam ojovipayryayasya lakṣaṇamuktaṃ yathā, "vākye yaḥ khaṇḍayan rītiṃ bhavatyojovipayryayaḥ| asamastamiti prāhurdoṣaṃ tamiha tadvidaḥ| | "iti) | | 59| | 

pūrvokte padye, ityādīti| ityādi evamādikaṃ sānuprāsamapi padyaṃ, bandhapāruṣya bandhe racanāyāṃ, pāruṣyaṃ duḥśravatvaṃ, śaithilyaṃ patatprakarṣatādikañca, niyacchati jñāpayati| (atrapūrvādrdhe bahuvisargasattayā pāruṣyam; taduktam, "anusvāravisargau tu pāruṣyāya nirantarau"iti| pūrvādrdhe yādṛśī tāvraracanā uttarādrdhe tathā neti patatprakarṣatā) | ataḥ kāramāt, evaṃ sadoṣamityarthaḥ, anuprāsaṃ dākṣiṇātyāḥ vidarbhadesīyāḥ paṇḍitāḥ, na prayujyate na vyavaharanti, mādhuyryasya asadbhāvāditi bhāvaḥ| (dākṣiṇātyā ityupalakṣaṇaṃ, tena gauḍaīyā api doṣabahulaṃ sānuprāsamityādikamudāharaṇaṃ necchanti| yatra tu pāruṣyādike rasānukūlatayā mādhuyryamasti, tatra pāruṣyādikaṃ na doṣaḥ, pratyuta guṇa eva; tathā coktaṃ, "vaktari krodhasaṃyukte tathā vācye samuddhate| raudrādau tu rase'tyantaṃ duḥśravatvaṃ guṇo bhavet| | "iti) | | 60| | 

nanu rasavato mādhuyryagumatvāt varṇāvṛtteśca rasāvahatvāt prasaṅgato yathā anuprāsalakṣaṇaṃ nirūpitaṃ tathai varṇāvṛttirūpaṃ yamakamapi kathamatra na nirūpyate ityāha, āvṛttimiti| varṇasaṅghātaḥ svaravyañjanātmakavarṇasamūha eva, gocaraḥ viṣayo yasyāḥ tathoktām āvṛtiṃta pūrvakrameṇa punaruñcāraṇaṃ yamakaṃ viduḥ jānanti, kavaya iti śeṣaḥ| (atra varṇasaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍaghātapadasya svaravyañjanasamudāyaparatvaṃ jñeyam, anyathā anuprāsalakṣaṇe sahaikyam āpatitaṃ syāt; tathā bhinnārthaketi tadviśeṣamaṃ deyam, anyathā "yāmi yāmi"ityādau ekārthasyāpi yamakatvaṃ prasajyeta, nirarthakayorapiyamakatvāṅgīkārāt, arthasambhave sati bhinnarthatvaṃ bodhyam| āvṛttiścātra pūrvakramānusāriṇī, tena ca "damo, moda" ityāderbhinnaviṣayatvaṃ sūcitam; etena arthasambhave sati bhinnarthakasvaravyañjanātmakavarmasaṅghātasya pūrvakramavatyāvṛttiryamakamiti lakṣaṇam| taduktaṃ darpaṇakāreṇa, yathā "satyarthe pṛthagarthāyāḥ svaravyañjanasaṃhateḥ| kramema tenaivāvṛttiryamakaṃ vinigadyate| | "iti| atra dvayorapi padayoḥ kvacit sārthakatvaṃ kvacit nirarthakatvaṃ kvacidekasya sārthakatvamaparasya nirarthakatvam, ata uktaṃ, "satyarthe"iti| udāhṛtañca tatraiva, "navapalāśapalāśavanaṃpuraḥ sphuṭaparāgaparāgatapaṅkajam| mṛdulatāntalatāntamalokayat sa surabhiṃ surabhiṃ sumanobharaiḥ| | "iti| anyairapyuktaṃ yatā, "tulyaśrutīnāṃ bhinnānāmabhidheyaiḥ parasparam| varṇānāṃ yaḥ punarvādo yamakaṃ tannirūpyate| | "iti) | tattu sayamakaṃ padyaṃ, na ekāntamadhuraṃ na atiśayena mādhuyryavat, tathā hi tatāvidhe vākye prathamam arthānusandhānavyagratāyā abhāvāt rasapratīteḥ avyavahitatvamiti sānuprāse vākye mādhuyryamastīti ca bhāvaḥ| (ekānteti padena yamake'pi rasāvahaṃ mādhuyryamastyeva, param anuprāsavat samyak na iti vaktavyam, anyathā asyaanalaṅkāratvāpattirādyeta, śabdārthaśobhājananena kāvyātmabhūtasopa kāritvasya eva alaṅkāratvāt iti dhyeyam) | ataḥ paścāt alaṅkāraprastāve ityarthaḥ, vidhāsyate vakṣyate, yamakamiti śeṣaḥ| | 61| | 

mādhuyryasya śabdagatatvaṃ lakṣayitvā tasya arthagatatvaṃ darśayannāha, kāmamiti| sarvo'pi śabdagataḥ arthagata ubhayagataśca, alaṅkāraḥ anuprāsasabdaśleṣapunaruktavadābhāsopamādiḥ, arthe vācyalakṣyavyaṅgayarūpe pratipādye, kāmaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ yathā tathā, rasaṃ niṣiñcati puṣṇāti, udbodhayatītyarthaḥ, jhr"vastunyapi rasasthitiḥ" ityanenaiva sūcitamarthagatamādhuyryamiti lakṣaṇamāyātamṭa| grāmyatādoṣasya viśeṣeṇārthagatamādhuyryapratibandhakatāṃ darśayitumāha, tathā'pīti| thā'pi agrāmyatā grāmyaḥ itarajanavyavahṛtaḥ śabdo'rthasca sa na bhavatīti agrāmyaḥ tasya bhāvaḥ, bhūyasā bāhulyena, enaṃ bhāraṃ rasaniṣekarūpaṃ, vahati| (tathā ca sālaṅkārasya rasavyañjakasya eva arthasya mādhuyryamiti pratipāditam| atra agrāmyatāśabdaḥ anaślīlādīnāsupalakṣakaḥ, tatasca anyadoṣāṇāmapi mādhuyryasya pratibandhakatve'pi grāmyadoṣasya viśeṣapratibandakatvasūcanārthaṃ grāmyapadopādānam| evañcānyairuktasya mādhuyryavipayryayasyāpi doṣasyatra grahaṇam; tañcoktaṃ yathā, "mādhuyryavyatyayo yastu jāyate rītikhamḍanāt| tadanivryūḍhamityuktaṃ kāvyasarvasvavedibhiḥ| | "yathā, "nakināñcanadaunāñca śrṛṅgiṇāṃ śastrapāṇinām| viśvāso naivakatrtavyaḥ strīṣu rājakuleṣu ca| | " atra nakhināṃ nadīnāñceti ṣaṣṭāntāñcakāreṇa rītirupakrame śrṛḍhiddhaṭhṭhaḍagaṇāṃ śastrapāṇināmiti cakārānirvāhāt strīṣu rājakuleṣu ceti ṣaṣṭhaparityāgāt amadhurārthatvāñca mādhuyryavipayryayanāmāyaṃ śabdārthapradhāno gumavipayryayo doṣaḥ iti, tataścātrāpi na mādhuyryaguṇasadbhāvaḥ| bhūyasā iti grahaṇāt itarajanakathaneṣu grāmyatā na doṣaḥ, pratiyuta guṇa eva, taduktaṃ, "grāmyatvamadhamoktiṣu"guṇa iti śeṣaḥ iti) | | 62| | 

arthagatāyāḥ grāmyatayā udāharaṇaṃ darśayati, kanye iti| (vidagdhajanoktiriyam| "virahe te viṣīdantaṃ niṣīdantaṃ tavāntike"| ityasya pūrvādrdham) , he kanye! tva t tava, virahe voṣaudantaṃ tavāntike niṣīdantaṃ kāmayamānaṃ māṃ kathaṃ na kāmayase? ityatra kanye! iti sambodhanaṃ duhitṛsithānīyānāmeva sambhavati, nāyikāsambādhane tu "priye""sundari"ityādi prayoga eva sādhu | ( anye tu ukteḥ grāmyatayā kāntihīnamityarthapradhāno'yaṃ kāntivipayryayākyaḥ doṣa ityāhuḥ ; taduktaṃ, "kāntorvipayryayādvākyaṃ grāmyamityapadisyate"iti| tasmādatra arthasya grāmyatā na tu kanyāśabdasya, śabdasya grāmyatve hi vidagdhajanā na tadvāvaharanti, kanyāśabdastu teṣāmapi prayoge dṛśyate, yathā, "kanyeyaṃ kaladhautakomalaruciḥ kīrttistu nātaḥ parā"ityādi| prācīnaistu grāmyajanamātravyavahṛtānāṃ kaṭyādiśabdānāmeva grāmyatvamuktam| kiñca, kāmayamānamityādau ca kāmināṃ surateccharūpaḥ kāmaḥ śabdenāpratipādyamānaḥ kathañcit sūcyamānaḥ eva śobhāṃ janayati, agūḍhastu śabdena spaṣṭatayoktaḥ sahṛdayānāṃ lajjājanakatvena vairasyamāvahatīti spaṣṭaśabdārthābhidhīyamānakāmatvāt"kāmayamānam" itiyatrāpi arthagataḥ grāmyatādoṣaḥ| atra ca nāyakakāmarūpakārame satyapi nāyikākāmarūpasya kāyryasyābhāvāt viśeṣoktiralaṅkāraḥ; taduktaṃ darpaṇakāreṇa, "sati hetauphalābāve viśeṣoktistathā dvidhā" iti| evamalaṅkārayuktaḥ anuprāsavānapi śabdārthaḥ grāmyatvena vairasyameva pratipādayati iti na mādhuyryasadbhāvaḥ) | tañca vivṛṇoti, itīti| iti uktarūpaḥ, ayaṃ grāmyaḥ tadākhyadoṣayuktaḥ, arthātmā pratipādyasvarūpaṃ, vairasyāya rasodbodhavyāghātāya, prakalpate prabhavati| | 63| | 

agrāmyatāṃ darśayati, kāmamitiṣa he vāmākṣi! vāme sundare kuṭile vā, akṣiṇī yasyāḥ tatsambuddhau, kandarpacāṇḍaālaḥ mayi kāmaṃ nardayaḥ, diṣṭyā bhāgyena, tvayi nirmatsaraḥ sadaya ityarthaḥ, iti pūrvaślokārthaḥ eva gūḍhatayā uktarūpa ityarthaḥ, arthaḥ ahaṃ tvāṃ kāmaye tvantu māṃ na kāmayase ityevaṃrūpaḥ, agrāmyaḥ grāmyadoṣarahitaḥ, bhaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍagayā sūcyamānasya vaicitrājanakatvena rasodbodhakatvāditi bhāvaḥ ; ata eva rasam āvahati puṣṇātīti rasāvahaḥ rasopakārakatvena māduyryagumayukta ityarthaḥ| ayaṃ bhāvaḥ, vāmākṣīti sambodhanapadaṃ nāyikāyāḥ saundayryaṃ nāyakasya ca tāṃ prati cāṭūkiṃta vyañjayati, kandarpo mayi nirdayaḥ tvayi tu nirmatsara ityanena kāmayamānaṃ māṃ tvantu na kāmayase iti pūrvoktapadārtha eva bhaṅgayā sūcita iti agrāmyatā sahṛdayahṛdayaṅgamatayā rasaṃ vyjayantī mādhuyryaguṇam āvahati iti| | 64| | 

grāmyatāyāḥ śabdagatatvamapi darśayati, śabde'pīti| śabde'pi grāmyatā asti eva, sā śābdagrāmyatā, sabhyaṃ sādhu, taditarat asabhyaṃ, grāmīṇahālikādiprayojya vacanamiti yāvat, tasya kītrtanāt, vidagdhajanaiḥ iti bhāvaḥ, bhavatīti śeṣaḥ| yatā ratyutsavasya nirūpame yakārādipadaṃ yakāra ādiryasya tat padaṃ, "yabha maithune"ityasmāt niṣpannaṃ padaṃ yanbhanādikamityarthaḥ, tādṛśapadaprayoktuḥ grāmyatayā upahasanīyatvaṃ śrotuśca lajjitatvameva jāyate, sutarāṃ rasāsvādasya tirohitatvāt na atra mādhuyryagumaprasara iti bhāvaḥ| | 65| | 

aparaiḥ doṣatvena uktāyā aślīlatāyāḥ svamate grāmyatāyāmantarbhāvayannāha, padeti| padānāṃ sandhānaṃ sampraveśo yasmin tat padasandhānaṃ vākyamityarthaḥ, ("vākyasāṅghāta"iti pāṭhe, sa evārthaḥ, ) taddṛttyā vā tadgatatvena vā, punaḥ athavā, vākyārthatvena vā tadarthagatatvena vā ityarthaḥ, durduṣṭā, pratītiḥ duṣpratītiḥ tatkaraṃ lajjājugupsā'sā'maṅgalādivyeñjakaṃ, vākyasya vākyārthasya vā lajjādijanakatvarūpam aślīlatvamityarthaḥ, grāmyaṃ grāmyatāyāmantarbhūtaṃ, tadapi mādhuyryavyāghātakamiti bhāvaḥ| tatra vākyāgatamudāharati, yatheti| bhavataḥtava, yā priyā praṇayinī, iti prakṛtārthaḥ, tatasca yā bhavata ityaṃśe yābhaḥ maitunaṃ, ("yabha maithune"ityasmāt ghañi niṣpannaḥ) sa vidyate asyeti yābhavān tasyeti pratītau vīḍaāvyajjakatvāt vākyamidamaślīlatvadoṣadūṣitam, ato'tra na mādhuyryasaṅgatiriti bhāvaḥ| | 66| |
