vākyāgatāmaslīlatāmuktā tadarthagatāṃ nirdiśati, kharamiti| kaścit vīyryavān parākramaśālī, puruṣaḥ, kharam atitīkṣṇaṃ yatā tathā, prahṛtya, śatrūniti śeṣaḥ, viśrānti iti prakaraṇasaṅgato'rthaḥ, paraṃ vīyryavān śukralaḥ, kaścit puruṣaḥ, kharaṃ gāḍhaṃ yatā tathā, prahṛtya maithunaṃ kṛtvā, viśrāntaḥ, (ityapi asabhyārtho vakrādivailakṣaṇyena pratīyate, tena ca vrīḍaāvyañjakatayā arthagatāślīlatvarūpagrāmyatvam; na ca atra vākyāgatamaślīlatvamiti mantavyaṃ, yatra tu śābdāyā vyañjanayā dvitīyārthasya bodhastatra eva vākyāgatatvaṃ, yatra tu śābdayā vyañjanayā dvitīyārthasya bodhastatra eva vākyagatatvaṃ, yatrādhryā vyañjanayā vakrādivaiśiṣṭyādaparo'rthaḥ pratīyat; tathā coktaṃ darpaṇakāreṇa yatā, "anekārthasya śabdasya saṃyogādyairniyantritai| ekātrārthe'nyadhīhetuvryañjanā sā'bhidhāśrayā| | "iti| atra tu "kharam"ityādivākyasthapadānām ekārtamātrapratipādakānāṃ prakaramādiniyamanāsambhavāt vaktādivaiśiṣṭeyanāthryā vyañjanayaivāsabyarūpadvitīyārthasya pratītirityarthagatatvameva; kiñca doṣagumālaṅkārādīnāṃ śabdārthagatatvavyavasthāyāṃ śabdaparivṛttisahatvāsahatvābhyāṃ vyavasthiteśca| atra tu balaśukrobhayārthavācakasyāpi balasya śukrajatvenaikatvasvīkārādekārthasya vīyryaśabdasya parivṛttāvapi asabhyarūpārthapratyayanādaślīlatvākhamḍanamityarthagatatvameva| yatra tu śabdaparivatrtane doṣasyāpi khaṇḍanaṃ tatraiva śabdagatatvamiti dhyeyam) | evamādi proktagrāmyatādidoṣākalitaṃ, kāvyamiti śeṣaḥ, (ādipadena doṣāntaravatāṃ grahaṇam, ) uṅayorapi gauḍavaidarbhayoḥ, mārgayoṛ na śaṃsanti na ādriyante, vidvāṃsa iti śeṣaḥ| (nanu gauḍavaidarbhayoḥ vaiparītyamena dṛśyate, tatkathamatra sāmyamityapi na vācyam" eṣāṃ vipayryayaḥ prāyo dṛśyate gauḍavatrmani"ityatra prāya ityanena kutracit sāmyamiti svayamevokatatvāt) | | 67| | 

grāmyatāyāḥ pratiprasavamāha , bhaginīti| bhaginībhagavatī ādiryasya tat padam, ādipadagrāhyaṃ yoniliṅgādikañca, sarvatra kāvyeṣu vyavahāreṣu ca, anumanyate aduṣṭatayā lokaiḥ aṅgīkriyate, tattacchabdaprayoge doṣanusandānaviraheṇa sādhāraṇānāṃ vairasyānudayāt iti bhāvaḥ| (uktañca, "grāmyaṃ ghṛṇāvadaślīlāmaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍagalārthaṃ yadauritam| tat saṃvīteṣu gupteṣu lakṣiteṣu na duṣyati| | " iti| tatra saṃvītaṃ yathā, "tasmai himādreḥ prayatāṃ tanūjāṃ yatātmane yojayituṃ yatasva| yoṣitsu tadviyryaniṣekabhūmiḥ saiva kṣametyātmabhuvopadiṣṭam| | " atra tadvīyryaniṣekabhūmiḥ saiva kṣametyātmabhuvopadiṣṭam| | "atra tadviyryaniṣekabhūmiḥ iti aślīlasyāpi saṃvītatvāt guṇatatvam iti| saṃvītatvaṃ hi nādiprayogatvena sarvajanavyavahṛtatvam; tañcoktaṃ yathā, "saṃvītasya hi loke'sminna doṣānveṣaṇaṃ kṣamam| śiliṅgasya saṃsthāne kasyāsabhyatvabhāvanam| | "iti| gupteṣu asabhyārthe aprasiddheṣuḥ; yathā, "sudustyājā yadyapi janmabhūmirgajairasambādhamayāmbabhūve| sa te'nimeyaḥ subhage'bhimānī bhaginyayaṃ na prathamābisandhi| | " atra janemabhūmisambādhabhaginīśabdānām aślīlārthāntaratve'pi prathamārthaprabhāvabhāvanāguptatvāt guṇatvam| tañcoktaṃ, "vastu mahātmāguptasya padārthaprabhāvabhāvanāguptatvāt| bhaginībhagavatyādi nāsabhyatvena bhāvyate| | "iti| lakṣiteṣu lakṣaṇayā asabhyārthapratipādakeṣu; yathā, "brahmāmḍakāraṇaṃ yo'psu nidadhe vījamātmanaḥ| upasthānaṃ karomyeṣa tasmai śeṣāhisāyine| | "atra brahmāṇḍaopasthānaśabdayoḥ asabyārthasmṛtihetutve'pi anyatra lakṣitatvāt guṇatvāt| tathā surataprasaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍagasabhāyāṃ śāntabībhatsarasādau vyaṅgaye ca aślīlatvasya gumatvamanyairaṅgakṛtam) | iti uktaprakāreṇa, mādhiyryaṃ vibhaktaṃ vibhajya darśitam| adhunā sukumāratā kramaprāptastadākhyagumaḥ, ucyate| | 68| | 

anuṣṭhareti| aniṣṭharāṇi aparuṣāṇi, akṣarāṇi prāyo bāhulyena yatra tādṛśaṃ, padyamiti śeṣaḥ, sukumāraṃ saukumāyryaguṇayuktamityarthaḥ, "prāyaḥ" padena antarāntarā paruṣākṣarāṇyapi niveśanīyānīti sūcitam| (ata evoktañca, "bandhasyājaraṭhatvañca saukumāyryamudāhṛtam"iti) | tuśabdo'tra hetuvācakaḥ; yataḥ sarvakomale kevalasukumārākṣaraprayogo, bandhasya racanāyāḥ, śaithilyadoṣaḥ ślathatvarūpadoṣaḥ agāḍhatā iti yāvat, darśitaḥ "mālatīmālālolālikalilā yathā" ityatrābhihitaḥ, mayaiva iti śeṣaḥ| jhrtathā ca komalākṣarāṇāṃ bahūnāṃ madhye paruṣākṣaravinyāsena bandhasya gāḍhatayā yat sahṛdayahṛdyatvaṃ tadeva saukumāyryapadavācyam; alpaprāṇamahāprāṇākṣaraghaṭitabandhāśaithilye tu śleṣa ityanayorbhejaḥ| idañca saukumāyryaṃ sabdaguṇaḥ, arthaguṇastu arthasyāślolatvābhāvarūpatvenāpāruṣyam; taduktaṃ vāmanena, "apāruṣyaṃ saukumāyryam" iti| sāmagrosambhave'pi cittadrutimanāmādayan nīrasaḥ pīruṣyamabhidhīyate, atathābhūtastu jhaṭiti tanmayībhavanayogyāntaḥkaraṇaḥ sukumāraḥ, tadidamapāruṣyamiti tadarthaḥ| yathā, "sadyaḥ purīparisare'pi śirīṣamṛdvau sītā javātricaturāṇi padāni gatvā| gantavyamasti kiyadityamakṛdbavāṇā rāmāśruṇaḥ kṛtavatī prathamāvatāraṇ| | "iti, atra sītāyāḥ purīparisare'pi kiyadasti gantavyamiti vacanaṃ śrṛṇvato rāmasya śirīṣamṛdutadaṅgāvalokanena aśruṇaḥ pātāt tadaśrunimittabhūtā cittadrutireva saukumāyryamiti sudhībhirvibhāvyam| | 69| | 

asya udāharamamāha, maṇḍalīti| kalāpinaḥ mayūrāḥ, jīmūtamālini kāle varṣāsu, barhāṇi pucchāni, maṇḍalīkṛtya maṇḍalākāreṇa vistāyrya, madhuragītibhiḥ madhuragītisadṛśasvanaiḥ ityarthaḥ kaṇṭhaiḥ upalakṣitāḥ santaḥ, (viśeṣaṇe tṛtīyā) pranṛtyanti| (atra makārakakāradīnāṃ komalākṣarāṇāṃ madhye prathamapāde "maṇḍa"iti "kṛtya"iti "barha"ithi saṃyogatrayāmāṃ, dvitīye "ṇṭhe"ithi "rma"iti saṃyogadvayasya, tṛtīya pāde ca "naḥ"iti "pranṛtyanti"iti ca visargasaṃyuktākṣarayoḥ, caturthe dīrghasvarāṇāmantarāntarā sanniveśāt paruṣatvam; tataḥ aniṣṭhuraprāyā racaneyaṃ saukumāyryamāśrayati| atra ca kalāpiṣu natrtakavyavahārasamāropāt samāsoktiralaṅkāraḥ, taduktaṃdarpakārame, "samāsoktiḥ samairyatra kāyryaliṅgaviśeṣamaiḥ| vyavahārasamāropaḥ prastute'nyasya vastunaḥ| | "iti) | | 70| | 

uktarūpasaukumāyryasya guṇatvamanaṅgīkurvato navyān pratyāha, itīti| ithi uktavidhaḥ, arthaḥ maṇḍalīkṛtyetipadyapratipādyaḥ kalāpinṛtyarūpaḥ ityarthaḥ, na ūrjitaḥ na tejasvala eva, na atimanorama ityarthaḥ, alaṅkāraśca samāsoktayanuprāṇitaḥ svabhāvoktirūpaḥ, tādṛsaḥ vaicitrājanakaḥ, na, tathā'pi etat padyaṃ, sukumāratayā tādṛśasaukumāyryagumayuktākṣaravinyāsena, satāṃ sāmājikānāṃ, na tu sādhāraṇānāmiti bhāvaḥ, manaḥ ārohati āśrayati, mohayati iti yāvat| ayaṃ bhāvaḥ, arthālaṅkārau yathā payryāṃlocanāya pratīyete gumastu na tathā, yataḥ arthasya alaṅekārasya ca bodhanāt pūrvameva gumakṛtavaiśiṣṭyaṃ pratīyate, gumasya vinyāsaviseṣarūpatvāt| (ata evoktaṃ, "tayā kavitayā kiṃ vā tayā vanitayā tathā| padavinyāsamātreṇa yayā na hriyate manaḥ| | "iti| etena asati gumavaicitro arthālaṅkāravaicitrāṃ sadapi kāvyaṃ na tathā śobhayati| ata evoktaṃ bhojarājena, "yadi bhavati vacaścuyataṃ guṇebhyo vapuriva yauvanabandhyamaṅganāyāḥ| api janadayitāni durbhagatvaṃ niyatamalaṅkāraṇāni saṃśrayante| | "etena gumānāmātmaniṣṭhatvam alaṅkārāṇāṃśabdārthaniṣṭhatvamuktaṃ bhavati; uktañca, "prāmāḥ daśa guṇāḥ"iti| vāmanādibhiḥ rīterāmatvaṃ gumānām ātmabhūtarītiniṣṭhatvam alaṅkārāṇāṃtaccharīrabhūtaśabdārthaniṣṭhatvañcoktam| dhvanikāreṇāpi dhvanerātmatvaṃ guṇānāñca tanniṣṭhatvaṃ pratipādyaśabdārthayugalasya śarīratvamalaṅkārāṇāñca tanniṣṭhatvamevāṅgīkṛtam| evañca guṇatvāt śleṣādīnāmātmani samavāyasambandhena sthitiḥ; alaṅkāratvāt yamakopamādīnāṃ śarīre saṃyogavṛttyā4 sthitiriti granthakārasyābhimatamiti manyāmahe| na hyavipaścidapi kaścidabhivadedabhijānīyādvā guṇānāmātmani rītāviva alaṅkārāṇāṃ śarīrabhūte śabdārthayugale samavāyavattyā sthitiriti dhyeyam) | | 71| | 

gauḍavaidarbhayoḥ saukumāyrye matabhedaṃ darśayitumāha, dīptamiti| aparaiḥ gauḍaīyakavibhiḥ, dīptiraujjvalyam, utsāhodbodhena cittavisphāratvamityarthaḥ, tadyuktaṃ dīptaṃ vīraraudrabībhatsarasātmakatayā samuttejanamityarthaḥ, iti asmāt kāramāt, ojasvitvādityartaḥ, bhūmnā bāhulyena, kṛcchrodyamapi ojovyañjakatayā paruṣavarmātmakatvāt kaṣṭenoñcāyryamapi, saukumāyryarahitamapi iti bhāvaḥ, badhyate grathyate, viracyate iti yāvataṃ; gauḍaīyā hi vīrarasādirūpatejasvivyaṅgaye tadvyañjakaparuṣākṣaravinyāsasyaivaucityāt tatra saukumāyryaṃ na aṅgīkurvate iti bhāvaḥ| yathā nyakṣeṇa netrahīnena, andhenetyarthaḥ, dhṛtarāṣṭreṇeti śeṣaḥ, kṣaṇāt alpenaiva kālena, kṣatriyāṇāṃ pakṣaḥ samūhaḥ, samagrakṣatriyakulamityarthaḥ, kṣayitaḥ nāśitaḥ, kurukṣetrayuddhe iti śeṣaḥ| kecittu nyañcanti nīcakamrma kurvanti iti nyañcaḥ kārttavīyryaḥ, tān syati vināśayati iti nyakṣaḥ paraśurāmaḥ iti nyakṣapadasya kṛcchrārthaṃ suṣṭhu manyante dhṛtarāṣṭrasya kṣatriyakṣaye vīrarasasyāsaṅgateḥ| (atra vīrarasavarṇanāyāṃ tādṛśaśrutikaṭuparuṣavarṇaprāyoge eva camatkāramātanotīti saukumāyryatyāgo guṇa eveti manyante gauḍaīyāḥ| vaidarbhāstu īdṛśe'pi vīrabībhatsaraudrarasādimatprabandhe saukumāyryamādriyante ityayorbhedo'tra mantavyaḥ ; yathā, "kṛtamanumataṃ dṛṣṭaṃ vā yairidaṃ gurupātakaṃ manujapaśubhiḥ nirmayryādairbhavadbhirudāyudhaiḥ| narakaripuṇā sādrdhaṃ teṣāṃ sabhīmakirīṭināmayamahamasṛṅmāṃmaiḥ karomi diśāṃ balim| | " iti| atra raudre'pi rase na atiparuṣākṣarāṇi vinyastāni, ato vaidarbhāḥ saukumāyryatyāgāt doṣa eva; yaduktaṃ bhojarājena doṣaprasaṅge, "saukumāyryavipayryāsāt kaṭhora upajāyate"iti) | | 72| | 

arthavyakiṃta nāma gumaṃ sodāharaṇamāha, arthavyaktiriti| arthasya aneyatvam adyāhārādikaṣṭakalpanāmantareṇa prayuktapadebhya evopasthitirityarthaḥ, arthavyaktiḥ| jhruktañca kramadīśvareṇa, "yāvādvācyābhidhānaṃ yattadarthavyaktilakṣaṇam"iti| tathā bhojenāpyuktaṃ, "yatra sampūrmavākyatvamarthanyakiṃta vadanti tām"iti| itthañca yatra yādṛśo yāvāṃśca viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyaprakāro'bhimataḥ sa sarva eva upāttaśabdasamudāyamātreṇa niṣpatyūhamavagamyate tatrārthavyaktiriti niṣkarṣaḥ ; yathā, "vāgarthāviva sampṛkta, "atra "vande"ityuttamapuruṣaikavacanenaivāhamiti prāptam, "asamadyuttamaḥ" ityatra"sthānini" ityabhidhānāt; kim? iti kamrmāpekṣāyāṃ "pārvatīparameśvarau" iti, tau ca kīdṛśī? "jagataḥ pitarau" iti, kimarthaṃ namaskurute? "vāgarthapratipattaye"iti vākyasya sampūrṇatvenārthavyaktiriti| iyañca śabdamātragā, arthagāyāstu svabhāvoktalaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍakāreṇa parigṛhītatvāt na pṛthaguktiriti navyāḥ| prāñcastu ārthīmapyarthavyaktimaṅgīkurvanti; taduktaṃ darpamakāreṇa, "arthavyaktiḥ svabhāvovasphuṭīkaraṇamārthīmarthavyaktimāha, yatā, "vastusvabhāvasphuṭatvamarthavyaktiḥ" iti| tathā bhojanāpi uktam, "arthavyaktiḥ svarūpasya sākṣāt kathanamucyate"iti; svarūpasya kavipratibhaikagocaracamatkārirūpasya, yat sākṣāt kathanaṃ kaviśaktivaśāt sākṣātkārasodarapratītijanakapadavattvamarthavyaktiriti tadarthaḥ| tatrodāharaṇaṃ yathā, "pṛṣṭheṣu śaṅkhaśakalacchaviṣu cchadānāṃrekābhiraḍhiddhaṭhṭhaḍakatamalaktakalohitābhiḥ| gorocanāharitababhruvahiḥpalāśamamodate kumudamanbhasi palvalasya| | "iti, atra kumudasvarūpasya sākṣādiva pratīyamānatvena yat spaṣṭarūpābhidhānam asau artagatā arthavyaktiriti| apare tu, padānāṃjhaṭityarthabodhakatvaṃ śābdīmarthavyaktimāhuḥ ; yaduktam, artavyaktiḥ padānāṃ hi jhaṭityarthasamarpamam" iti| anyañca, "paścādiva gatirvācaḥ purastādiva vastunaḥ| yatrārthavyaktihetutvāt so'rthavyaktiḥ smṛto guṇaḥ| | "iti| yatā hariṇā varāharūpimā viṣmunā, khuraiḥ svīyaiḥ iti bāvaḥ, kṣuṇānāṃ nāgānāṃ pannagānāṃ, rasātalavāsinām, asṛgbhi śoṇitaiḥ, lohitāt raktāt, udadheḥ samudrāt, bhūḥ pṛthvī, uddhṛtā ūdrdha nītā| atra yāvanta eva śabdāstāvatāmeva arthāḥ vinā kaṣṭakalpanāṃ pratīyante iti| | 73| | 

aneyatvamarthavyaktirityabhidhānena neyatvajñānasyāpekṣaṇāt tat kīdṛgityapekṣāyāṃ neyatvaṃ darśayati, mahīti| mahāvarāheṇa lohitāt udadheḥ mahī uddhṛtā, itīyati evaṃprakāre, nirdiṣṭe prayikte sati, uragāsṛjaḥ etatpadasya, neyatvam adhyāhāreṇa bodhyatvaṃ, bhavatīti śeṣaḥ ; tathā hi lohitādityukte kathamityapekṣāyāṃ khurakṣuṇanāgāsṛjā ityaṃśo vaktavyaḥ, tañcādhyāhāramantareṇa na sambhavati iti netyatvam, anyathā arthasya sphuṭatā na syāt iti bhāvaḥ| (śaktāyā lakṣaṇayā ca yatra anvayabodhāntaramanupapattirarthasya bhavet tatrādhyāhārādinā tadupapattiḥ neyatvaṃ, tathā rūḍhiprayojanābhāve kṛtāyā lakṣaṇāyā aṅgīkāreṇārthabodhe'pi arthasya neyatvaṃ, na tatrārthavyaktiriti) | | 74| | 

atra ubhayorapi mārgayoḥ sāmyaṃ darśayati, neti| ubhayorapi vaidarbhīgauḍayoḥ, mārgayoḥ rītiviśeṣayoḥ, kavaya iti śeṣaḥ, īdṛśaṃ pūrvoktarūpaṃ neyatvayuktaṃ vākyaṃ, na bahu manyante na ādriyante, kiñcātra anyairuktaḥ arthavyaktivipayryayarūpo guṇagatadoṣa eva bhavati; (taduktaṃ, "vākyaṃ bhavati neyārtha marthavyaktervipayryayāt"iti) | hi yataḥ śabdanyāyaḥ śābdabodhaniyamaḥ, śabdopāttānāṃ vṛttyapasthāpyānāmevārthānāmanvayabodha ityevaṃsvarūpaḥ ityarthaḥ, tasya vilaṅghinī vyatikramasādhinī, pratītiḥ adhyāhārādikaṣṭakalpanena anvayabodhaḥ ityarthaḥ, na subhagā na sādhvītyarthaḥ| (adhyāhṛtopasthāpitārtapratīteḥ tātpayryārthālocanenārthāntarapratīśca śābdabodhottarajāyamānatvāt na śābdabodhatvaṃ, viśiṣṭasaktivādamatamavalambā uktamidamityavadheyam) | | 75| | 

audāyryaṃ guṇaṃ nirūpayati, utkarṣeti| yasmin vākye, ukte kathite sati, kaścit utkarṣavān varṇanīyasya viṣayasya utkarṣapratipādako lokottaracamatkārakaḥ, guṇaḥ dhrmaviśeṣaḥ, pratīyate budhyate, tat udārāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavayam audāyryaguṇayuktam, evañca utkarṣajanakadharmapratītimadvarṇanam udāratvamiti lakṣaṇam| jhrutkarṣaśca kvacit komalatvena, kvacit tyāgaśauyryādinā, kvacit grāmyatvena, kvacidagrātvena ca sambhavatiḥ ata evoktaṃ vāmanena grāmyatvaprasaṅge agrāmyatvamudāratā iti lakṣaṇañca, "agrāmyatvamudāratā" iti; udāhṛtañca tenaiva tatra, "tvamevaṃ saundayryā sa ca rucitāyāṃ paricitaḥ, kalānāṃ sīmānaṃ paramiha yuvāmeva bhajathaḥ| ayi! dvandvaṃ diṣṭyā taditi subhage! saṃvadati vā mataḥ śeṣañcet syājjitamiha tadānīṃ guṇitāyā"| | iti| tataśca sphuṭabhedayorapi gauḍavairbhayorīdṛśodāratve sāmyameva darśitamityāha, teneti| tena udāratvaguṇena, kāvyapaddhatiḥ gauḍavaidarbhīyā kāvyarīti, sanāthā pūrṇā, utkarṣavatīti bhāvaḥ| jhr"eṣāṃ vipayryayaḥ prāyo dṛśyate gauḍavatrmani" ityatra prāyaḥpadopādānāt kutracit gauḍavaidarbhayoḥ sāmyamapītyabhiprāyaḥ| idañca arthagatam; śabdagatantu vikaṭatatvalakṣaṇaṃ, vikaṭatvañca padānāṃ nṛtyatprāyasvaṃ yasmin sati padāni nṛtyantīva iti janasya varṇabhāvanā bhavati tat vikaṭatvamikatyarthaḥ, uktañca vāmanena, "vikaṭatvamudāratā" iti; udāhṛtañca tenaiva yathā, "svacaraṇaviniviṣṭairnūpurairnatrtakīnāṃ jhaṇiti raṇitamāsīt tatra citraṃ kalañca" iti| pratyudāharaṇaṃ yathā, "caraṇakamalalagnairnupurairnatrtakīnāṃ jhaṭiti raṇitamāsīt mañju citrañca tatra"iti| etenaitadapi sūcyate, vikaṭatvaṃ hi vakratvaṃ, na hi śabdārthayovekratāmantareṇa kāvyapadavīprāptiḥ sambhavati; tañcoktaṃ , "yattu vakraṃ vacaḥ śāstre loke ca vaca eva tat| vakraṃ yadanurāgodau tatra kāvyamiti śrutiḥ| | "iti; etaduvaiparītye tu alaṅkārahīnam audāyryavipayryayadoṣamaṅgīkurvantyanye; yadāhuḥ, "yastu rīteranirvāhādodāyryasya vipayryayaḥ| vākya tadanalaṅkāramalaṅkāravido viduḥ| | "yathā, "dīrghapucchaścatuṣpādaḥ kakujhān lambakambalaḥ| gorapatyaṃ balīvadrdastraṇamatti mukhena saḥ| | "tadidamapuṣṭārthatvānutkṛṣṭaviśeṣaṇamanudāraṃ nilaṅkāramācakṣate| so'yamaudāyryavipayryayo nāma śabdārthapradhāno guṇavipayryayadoṣaḥ iti| | 76| | 

udāratvasya udāharamaṃ darśayati, arthināmiti| he deva! arthināṃ kṛpaṇā dīnā, dṛṣṭiḥ tvanmukhe asakṛt vāraṃvāraṃ, patitā punaḥ kintu, tadavasthā dīnetyarthaḥ, anyasya mukhaṃ na īkṣate| (etena rājñaḥ etādṛśī prabhūtadānaśaktiryat dātrantaropasarpaṇamarthināṃ nāstīti sūcitaṃ sahṛdayānām atiśayena camatkārīti hṛdayamiti) | | 77| | 

itīti| iti uktarūpe, asmin vākye, tyāgasya dānasya, utkarṣaḥ vaicitrāviśeṣādhāyakatvaṃ, sādhu samyak lakṣyate pratīyate, sāmājikairanubhūyate ityarthaḥ| " sādhu"ityatra "khalu"iti pāṭhe khalu niścitamityarthaḥ| anyatrāpi padye, anena eva prākpradarśitenaiva, pathā rītyā, samānaḥ sadṛśaḥ, nyāyaḥ niyamaḥ yatra tat yathā tathā, ūhyatāṃ vibhāvyatām, etām eva rītimavalambā padyaṃ viracyatāmityarthaḥ| | 78| | 

matabhedaṃ darśayati, ślāghyairiti| ślāghyaiḥ viśeṣasya utkarṣādhāyakatvena sāmājikanoramaiḥ, , viśeṣaṇaiḥ (bahuvacanāt ekasya dvayoḥ vā viśeṣaṇayoḥ sadbhāve na ayaṃ guṇa iti sūcitam) yuktaṃ samanvitaṃ, vākyaṃm udāram audāyryaguṇāyuktaṃ, kaiścit kavibhiḥ, iṣyate| (abhiprāyānurūpaviseṣaṇavadvākyasyāpuṣṭārthatvadoṣarāhityāt parikarālaṅkāratvāñca guṇatvakathanamayuktamityasmin pakṣe svasyānabhimatamiti sūcanārtha kaiścit ityuktirityavadheyam) | uktamarthamudāharati, yatheti| yathā līlāmbujakrīḍaāmaro hemāṅgadādayaḥ "līlāmbuja" ityatra līleti viśeṣaṇena tadupayogino varṇākārasaurabhyātiśayasya, "krīḍaāsaraḥ" iti kathite tatra krīḍaārthakakamalakairavādijanitaśobhādikasya, tathā "hemāṅgada" ityukte aṅgadasya hemanirmitatvena sudṛśyatvādikasya pratītaḥ sahṛdayamohāriṇīti vibhāvanīyā| | 79| | 

ojoguṇaṃ nirūpayati, oja iti| samāsaḥ dvayorbahūnāṃ vā padānām ekīkaraṇaṃ, tasya bhūyastvaṃ bāhulyaṃ, dīrghatā ityartaḥ, bahubhiḥ padaiḥ samāsa iti yāvat, ojaḥ, etat gadyasya pūrvoktasya utkalikādirūpasya prabandhaviśeṣasya, jīvitaṃ prāṇabhūtam; idañca ubhayorapi mārgayoḥ sāmānyam| vaiṣamyaṃ darśayitumāha, padye'pīti| adākṣiṇatyānāṃ dākṣiṇātyavyatiriktānāṃ gauḍaānām ityarthaḥ, padye'pi apisabdo'tra gadyasaṅgahārtaṃ prayuktaḥ; idam ekam advitīyaṃ, parāyaṇaṃ paramā gatiḥ teṣāṃ bandhagāḍhatāpriyatvāditi bhāvaḥ| jhridañca śabdagataṃ, samāsasya śabdamātragatatvāt| vāmanastu bandhasya yat gāḍhatvaṃ tat śabdagatamojaḥ ityāha, yathā, "gāḍhabandhatvamojaḥ" iti abhidheyasya prauḍhatvamarthagatamoja iti ca, yathā, "arthasya prauḍhirojaḥ" iti tallakṣaṇamabhidhāya "padārthe vākvacanaṃ vākyārthe ca padābhidhā| proḍhavryāsasamāsau ca sābhiprāyatvamasya ca| | "iti tasyāḥ pañcavidhatvaṃ vyavasthāpitavān| tatra padārthe vākyāvacanaṃ yathā, "atha nayanasamutthaṃ jyotiratreriva dyauḥ| "atra "candra"ityekasmin padārthe vaktavye "atrernayanasamutthaṃ jyotiḥ" iti vākyavacanam| vākyārte padābhidhā yathā, "divyeyaṃ na bhavati, kintarhi mānuṣau"iti vaktavye "nimiṣati"ityāha iti yathā vā kvacit, "nidāghaśītalahimakāloṣṇaśarīrā sukumārī varayoṣit"iti vākyārthe vaktavye "varavariminau" iti padābhidhānam| kvacit ekasya vākyārthasya kiñcidviśeṣaniveśādanekaiḥ vākyairabhidhānaṃ vyāsaḥ, kvacit bahuvākyaprati pādyasya ekavākyena abidhānamityeṃvaṃrūpaḥ samāsaśca iti| tatra vyāso yāthā, "ayaṃ nānākāro bhavati sukhaduṛkhavyatikaraḥ sukaṃ vā duḥkhaṃ vā na bhavati bhavatyeva ca tataḥ| punastasmādūṅghe bhavati sukhaduḥkhaṃ kimapi tat punastasmādūrḍaṃgha bhavati na ca duḥkha na ca sukham| | "iti| samāso yathā, "te himālayamāmantrā punaḥ prekṣya ca śūlinam| siddhañcāsmai nivedyārthaṃ tadvisṛṣṭāḥ svamudyayuḥ| | "iti| padāntaraprayogamantarema tadarthapratyāyayanaprāgalbhaṃ sābhiprāyatvaṃ, tadyathā, "so'yaṃ samprati candraguptatanayaścandraprakāśo yuvā| jāto bhūpatirāśrayaḥ kṛtidhiyāṃ diṣṭayā kṛrtāśramaḥ| | " iti, āśrayaḥ kṛtadhiyāmityasya ca subandhusācivyopakṣepaparatavāt sābhiprāyatvam iti| kecittu proktacaturvidhaṃ prauḍhirūpabhojaḥ śabdaguṇaḥ, sābhiprāyatvantu arthaguṇa ityāhuḥṭa| | 80| | 

taditi| tat samāsabhūyastvarūpam ojaḥ, gurūṇāṃ samāsaghaṭakapadāvayavadīrghaprayatenoñcāyryamāṇavarṇānāṃ mahāprāmākṣarāṇāmiti yāvat, laghūnām alpaprayatnoñcāyryamāṇakomalavarmānām alpaprāṇakṣarāṇāñca, bāhulyaṃ prayogādhivākyam, alpatvam ūnaprayogaḥ, miśraṇaṃ sāmyaṃ, taḥ karaṇabhūtaiḥ, uñcāvacaprakāraṃ bahuvidhaṃ, tañca kvacit gurūṇāṃ bāhulyena laghūnām alpatvena, kvacit laghūnāṃ bāhulyena gurūṇāmalpatvena, kvaciñca teṣāmubhayavidhānāṃ miśraṇena ca bhavet; tat ākhyāyikādiṣu gadyagrantheṣu dṛśyaṃ lakṣṇīyam| ( ādinā campūvirudādīnāṃ grahaṇam; kiñca ākhyāyiketyupādānāt kakhāyāṃ raudrādirase'pi dīrghasamāso na vidheyaḥ ityāyātam; gauḍaīyānāntu padyagrante'pi sūyryaśatakādau samāsabāhulyaṃ pūrvamuktamavadheyam| tṛtīyapāde "tat"ityatra "sat"iti"prakāram"ityatra "prakāreṇa" "dṛśyam"ityatra "dṛṣṭam" iti ca pustakāntare pāṭhaḥ iti) | | 81| | 

gauḍaābhimataṃ padye'pi samāsabhūyastvuprakāraṃ darśayati, asteti| astamastake astācalaśrṛṅge, payryastāḥ patitāḥ, samastā ye arkāṃśavaḥ sūyryakiraṇāḥ, taiḥ saṃstaraḥ ācchādanaṃ yasyāḥ tādṛśī, vāruṇī varaṇādhiṣṭhatā, digiti śeṣaḥ, pīnastane sthitam ātāmram īṣallohitaṃ, sāyaṅkālīnasaurakiraṇasya tathātvāt iti bhāvaḥ, kamraṃ kamanīyaṃ, vastraṃ yasyāḥ tathābhūtī kāminīva, rājate iti śeṣaḥ| (atra dvayorapyadrdhayoḥ samāsabāhulyam| bhojarājenāpi, "ojaḥ samāsabhūyastvam"iti lakṣaṇamabhidhāya samāsabahulaṃ padyameva udāhṛtaṃ, yathā, "jayati bhujarajjugranthiniṣpīḍitenduḥ sravadamṛtanivṛttapretabhāvaiḥ kapālaiḥ| viracitanutibandho mūrddhi sadyaḥ purāreḥ pariṇatabahukalpabrahmaṇāṃ brahmaghoṣaḥ| | " atra bhujagarajjugranthi ityādinā samāsabāhulyādojonāma śabdaguṇa iti) | | 82| | 

gauḍaābhimataṃ samāsabhūyastvaṃ darśayitvā matāntaramavatārayati, itīti dvābhyām| paurastyāḥ gauḍavāsinaḥ, iti pūrvoktaprakāre, padye'pi ojasvinauḥ samāsabahulāḥ, giraḥ bandhanti, anye tu paṇḍitāḥ, girāṃ vācām, anākulam(atra bhāvanirdeśaḥ) anākulatvamityarthaḥ, dīrghasamāsādīnāṃ jhaṭiti pratīterantarāyatvāt tadrāhityarūpamiti yāvat, hṛdyaṃ sahṛdayamanohāri ca, ojaḥ padārthaḥ itu niṣkarṣaḥ| | 83| | 

atra udāharati, yatheti payodharo megha eva, payodharaḥ stanaḥ, sa eva taṭaḥ pradeśaḥ, tasya utsaṅge lagnaṃ sandhyātapaḥ sāyaṅkālīnasaurakiraṇābhāsa eva, aṃśukaṃ yasyāḥ tādṛśī, vāruṇī pratīcī dik ityarthaḥ, kasya cetaḥ kāmāturaṃ na kariṣyati? ityanvayaḥ| (atra pūrvādrdhe samāsabāhulyamapipūrvavat na śrotṛbuddhimākalayati paraṃ śrotrasukhameva teṣāṃ samutpādayati, parādrdhe"kāmāturama" ityatra samāsasyālpatvam, anyatra ca samāso nāsti, tathā'pi bandhaśaithilyābhāvāt sāmājikān sukhayati; evañca dīptarasānupraviṣṭārthapratipādakasandarbhe'pi samāsamantareṇa yatra dīptārthatā na pratihanyate tatra samāsābhāve'pi guṇatvamevāṅgīkṛtaṃ, na tu guṇavipayryayāt doṣatvam; uktañca, "ojaḥ samāsabhūyastvaṃ taddīptārtheṣu badhyate| vipayryayo| syāsamastaṃ taddīptañcenna doṣabhāk| | " iti; tatrodāhṛtaṃ yathā, "yo yaḥ śastraṃ bibhartti svabhujagurumadaḥ pāṇḍavīnāṃ camūnām" ityādi, "atrāsamāstatve'pi prauḍhabandhatvāt guṇatvam" iti samāveśca darśitaḥ| ata evātra ojaḥ śaithilyātmakaśabdaguṇaḥ prasādaḥ iti prāk pratacipāditaḥ iti vibhāvanīyam) | | 84| | 

kāntiguṇaṃ nirūpayati, kāntimiti| laukikasya lokasiddhasya, arthasya anatikramāt atyāgāt, tanmātrasyaiva varṇanāt ityarthaḥ, sarvajagatāṃ sādhāraṇānāṃ, kāntaṃ manoharamam, adhyāhārādidoṣābhāvādanvayabodhasaukayryaṇārthasugamatvāt sahṛdayānāṃ hṛdyamityarthaḥ, padyamiti śeṣaḥ, kāntaṃ kāntiguṇaviśiṣṭam; tathā ca sahṛdayamanoharañjakalosaddhivastuvarṇanameva kāntiguṇa iti| (uktañca kramadīśvareṇa yathā, " varṇanā'tyuktiśūnyā yā sā kāntarabhidhīyate" iti| iyaṃ kāntirarthagā, arthānusandhānamantareṇaisyāḥ pratītivirahāt) | tañca kāntimat vākyaṃ, vātrtā priyālāpaḥ, ("anāmaye priyālāpe vātrtaṃ vātrtā ca kathyate" iti vacanāt) tasyā abhidhāneṣu kathaneṣu, tathā varṇanāsu vasturūpamātranirūpaṇeṣu, dṛśyate, anāmayapraśneṣu priyālāpeṣu varṇanāsu ca yathāvat satyanirūpaṇasyaiva aucityāt ayathāvarṇane laukikavyahāravisaṃvādaḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ| (kecittu vātrtābhidhāneṣu ityasya itihāsavarṇaneṣu ityarthaṃ kalpayanti, itihāsānāṃ yathāvadvarṇanasya eva nyāyyatvāt, asatyavarṇane sāmājikānāṃ tatra mithyājñānena pravṛrttireva na syāt, tataśca prabandhasya rāmādivat pravarttitavyaṃ na rāvaṇādivat iti kṛtyākṛtyapravṛttinivṛttyapadeśaśca na sambhavatītyāhuḥ| etena vātrtāvarṇanābhyāmanyatra ayathāvadvarṇane'pi na doṣaḥ, ata eva navīvaiḥ vyañjakārthasya svataḥ sambhavitvena prauḍhoktisiddhatvena ca kāvyasya dvaividhyamuktam) | | 85| | 

vārtābhidhāne kāÏnta darśayati, gṛhaṇīti| tāni eva gṛhāṇi gṛhapadavācyāni, praśaṃsanīyāni gṛhāṇi ityarthaḥ, gṛhapadasya lakṣaṇayā praśastagṛhaparatvāditi bhāvaḥ; taporāśiḥ adhikatapāḥ, bhavāddaśaḥ mahātmajanaḥ, pāvanaiḥ pavitraiḥ pādapāṃśubhiḥ caraṇarajobhiḥ, yāni gṛhāṇi, sambhāvayati saṃśodhayati| (iyaṃ hi priyālāparūpavātrtā, tataśca mahātmajanapadasparśena sthānasya prāśastyarūpavarṇanīyārthasya lokasiddhatvādiyaṃ vātrtābhidhānarūpā kāntiriti avadheyam) | | 86| | 

varṇanāyāṃ kāntimudāharati anayoriti| he anavadyaṅgi śobhanagātre| tava bāhulatāntare bhujadvayasya madhye vakṣasi| jṛmbhamāṇayoḥ vardhamānayoḥ| stanayoḥ kucakalaśayoḥ| avakāśaḥ avasthitaye sthānam| na paryāptaḥ nālam| karikumbhasadṛśayoḥ payodharayostadapekṣayā hrasvasvarūpe vakṣasi parasparavimardena kathamapi sthitirastīti bhāvaḥ| atra varṇanāyāṃ pīnastanayorvakṣasi saṃsabādhāvasthānaṃ lokaprasiddhameva| ato laukikārthasyānatikrameṇa varṇanādatra kāntiguṇaḥ| | 87| | 

uktayoḥ padyayoḥ kāÏnta saṅghaṭayati, itīti| iti pūrvoktaślokadvayapratipādyaṃ vasta sambhāvyameva bhavitumarhati eva, na tu asambavavadapi prauḍhokttayā kalpanīyam iti bhāva; etañca viśeṣākhyānena viśeṣaḥ ākhyāyate aneneti viśeṣākhyānaṃ vaicitrāpratipādakaviśeṣaṇaṃ, tena saṃsakṛtaṃśobhitaṃ sat, lokayātrānuvarttinaḥ laukikācāraparāyaṇasya, sarvasya janasya, kāntaṃ manoramaṃ, bhavati| jhretena autkarṣapratipādakaviśeṣaṇavato lokapirasiddhavastuno varṇanenaiva kānteḥ sambhavaḥ, na tu lokasiddhavastumātravarṇaneneti; tathātve" sūyryo'staṃ yāti""gauḥ śete" ityāderapi lokasambhāvyatvena kānatimattvāt kāvyatvāpattiḥ, tādṛśaviśeṇavadvarṇanasya vaicitrājanakatvāt vaicitrāsyaiva giṇādirūpatvāt, guṇālaṅkārādimattvasyaiva kāvyatvamiti sudhībhirvibhāvyam| anye tu yatra śrṛṅgārādayo rasā dīptatayā pratibhānti, tatraivārthagā kāntirityāhuḥ, ; tathā hyuktaṃ vāmanena, "dīptarasatvaṃ kāntiḥ" iti; udāharaṇañca tatreva yathā, ṭhapreyān sāyamapākṛtaḥ saśapathaṃ pādānataḥ kāntayā dvitrāṇye padāni vāsabhavanādayāvanna yātyunmanāḥ| tāvat pracyutapāṇisampuṭalasannīvīnitambaṃ dhṛto dhāvitvaiva kṛtapraṇāmakamaho| premaṇo vicitrā gatiḥ| | "iti| aujjvalyarūpā tu śabdaguṇaḥ ; yathoktaṃ vāmanena, " aujjvalyaṃ kāntiḥ" iti| aujjvalyañca hālikādivyavahṛtapadaparityāgenālaukikaśobhāśālitvaṃ, tañca "patram"iti vaktavye "kisalayam" ityādi, "jaladhau" itivaktavye "rājani" iti, "kamalamiva" iti vaktavye "kamalāyate" ityādi| anyañca, "yadujjvalatvaṃ bandhasya kāvye sā kāntiriṣyate" iti, yathā "kuraṅgīnetrālīstavakitavanālīparisaraḥ" iti| | 88| | 

niruktakāntau gauḍaānāṃ vaimatyamāha, loketi| ivaśabdo'tra apyarthakaḥ| atyartham atisayena, lokātītaḥ alaukikaḥ api, yo'rthaḥ adhyāropya kalpayitvā, vivakṣitaḥ vakitumicchayā prayukta ityarthaḥ, tena vudagdhāḥ sahṛdayābhimāninaḥ, (iti solluṇṭhanāktiḥ) gauḍaīyā ityarthaḥ, atituṣyanti, teṣāṃ prauḍhoktipriyatvāt iti bhāvaḥ| (adhyāropādinā vivakṣitārthasya yogyatābhāvādanvayobodhābhāva ityapi na vācyaṃ, "bahulatā" ityādau rūpakālaṅkāre " satyapi bādhe pramāturitacchāprayujyatvamāhāyryam" ityuktāhāyryajñānenābhedānvayaḥ sarvairapi ālaṅkārikairaṅgīkṛtatvāt| ata evoktme, "atyantāmatyapi hyārthe jñānaṃ śabdaḥ karoti hi iti ) | itare janāḥ vaidarbhīyāḥ kavayaḥ, na, teṣāṃ yathāvadvarṇanapriyatvāditi bhāvaḥ ; sutarāmuktalakṣaṇā kāntirgauḍaiḥ na aṅgīkriyate iti phalitārthaḥ| | 89| | 

vātrtāyāṃ niruktakāntervaiparītyaṃ varṇayati, deveti| adya prabhṛti no'smākaṃ, gṛhaṃ yuṣmākaṃ pādarajaḥ pātena caraṇareṇupātena, dhautaṃ niḥśeṣaṃ, samastaṃ kilviṣaṃ pātakaṃ yasya tathābhūtaṃ sat, devidhiṣṇāṃ devatāsthānamiva, ārādhyaṃ sevanīyaṃ, bhaviṣyati iti śeṣaḥ| ( atra mahātmanāṃ caraṇarajaḥ sparśe gṛhasya pāvanatvaṃ lokasiddhamapi devāvāsasyeva ārādhyatvakītrtanamāropitamiti kāntivipayryayo'vagantavyaḥ) | | 90| | 

varṇanāyāṃ niruktakāntervaiparītyaṃ varṇayati, alpamiti| bhavatyāḥ stanayorjumbhaṇaṃ vṛddhiḥ, evaṃvidhaṃ vyāpakamityarthaḥ, bhāvi bhaviṣyati, idam anālocyaiva avicāryaiva, vedhasā vidhātrā, ākāśam alpaṃ nirmitam| (atra sarvavimṛṣyakāriṇo'pi vidhāturanālocanādeva mahato'pyākāśasyālpatvanimrmāṇam ityevamatyuktimūlakavarṇanena vilvadāḍimbaphala sadṛsayorapi stanayorasādhāraṇapīnottuṅgatvamasambhāvyamānamapi pratipāditaṃ sarvametadvaṇryamānaṃ lokāprasiddhameva, tataśca prāguktalakṣaṇā kāntirnātra sambhavatīti vibhāvyam| kecittu evaṃvidhakāntivipayryaye vaidarbhīrītimanusṛtya kāntivipayryayākhyaṃ guṇagatadoṣamāhuḥ; yathā, " kāntorvipayryayādvākyaṃ grāmyamityabhidhīyate| " yathā, virahe te viṣīdantaṃ niṣīdantaṃ tavāntike| kanye| kāmayamānaṃ māṃ tvaṃ na kāmayase katham? | | " idamuktogrrāmyatayā kāntihīnamityarthapradhāno'yaṃ kāntivipayryayo doṣaḥ) | | 91| | 

idamiti| idam īdṛśaṃ kāvyam, atyuktuḥ iti uktam atyuktisaṃjñayā kathitaṃ, kavibhiḥ iti śeṣaḥ| ( uktañca bhojarājena yathā, "lokikārthamatikramya prasthānaṃ yatra vaṇryate| tadutyuktitariti proktaṃ gauḍaānāṃ manaso mude| | "iti) | etat padyaṃ, gauḍeḥ kavibhiḥ, upalālitaṃ hṛdyatayā gṛhītam; prāk pūrvaṃ, praṇītaṃ kathitaṃ, prasthānaṃ lokasiddhavastaduvarṇanarūpam, anyasya vatrmanaḥ vaidarbhīyasya mārgasya, sāraṃ samatkurṣarūpaṃ, manoharamiti yāvat| ayameva kāntuguṇe prasiddhāprasiddhavastuvarṇanarūpaḥ gauḍavaidarbhayorbheda iti bhāvaḥ| | 92| | 

samādhiṃ sodāharaṇaṃ nirūpayati, anyadharma ityādi| lokasīmānurodhinā lokasthitimanuvatrtamānena, lokaprasidadhamanusarateti yāvat, vaktrā iti śeṣaḥ ; (etena svecchayā lokaviruddhavisadṛsadharmārope nāyaṃ samādhiriti sūcitam| yathā, "vṛhanti maśakā yatra tatra nidrā sudurlabhā" ityādau karidharmasya vṛṃhaṇasya maśakarut adhyāropaḥ lolaviruddhaḥ iti) | anyasya aparasya aprakṛtasya, yo dharmaḥ guṇakriyādiḥ, tataḥ tasmāt aprakṛtāt, anyatra tadbhinne prakṛte ityarthaḥ, yatra vākyārthe, samyak ādhīyate sādhyavasāmagauṇyā lakṣaṇayāāropyate, sa samādhitadākhyaguṇayuktaḥ, tathā ca prakṛte lastuni taddharmanigaraṇena sadṛsasyāprakṛtadharmasyāhāyryeṇa tādātmāropaḥ lokaprasiddhaścet samādhiriti phalitārthaḥ| (anyairapyuktaṃ, "samādhiḥ so'nyadharmāṇāṃ yadanyatrādhiropaṇam" iti| dharma ityanena dharmiṇaḥ samārope na ayaṃ guṇastatrātisayoktiralaṅkāra eveti sūcyate; yathā, "latāmūle līno hariṇaparihīno himakaraḥ, sphurattārākārā galati jaladhārā kuvalayāt| dhunīte bandhūkaṃ tilakusumajanmā'pi pavanaḥ, vahidrdāre puṇyaṃ pariṇamati kasyāpi kṛtinaḥ| | " ityādau dharmiṇi mukādau dharmiṇaścandrāderadhyāropaḥ) | | 93| | 

kumudānīti| yathā, kumudāni utpalāni, nimīlanti saṅkocatāṃ prāpnuvanti, kamalāni unmiṣanti vikāśante ca; (prabhātavarṇanamidaṃ, divā kumudanilanonmeṣau aprakṛtasya netrasya dharmau kumudakamalayoḥ dalasaṅkocavikāsatādātmane ārapitau, dharmadharmiṇoranayoḥ sādṛśyātiśayamahimnā lokasiddhau ca) | etadeva svayaṃ nirdiśati, itīti| iti uktabhyāṃ, netrakriyābhyāṃ nimīlanonmeṣābhyāṃ saha, adhyāsāt kumudakamaladalasaṅkocavikāśayostādātmayāropāt, tadavācinī tayoḥ nimīlanonmeṣayoḥ, vācinī pritipādikā, śrutiḥ nimīlantītyādi tattatkriyāvācakaḥ śabda ityartaḥ, labdhā saṅkocavikāśapratipādakatayā prāptā; anayoḥ sādṛśyātiśayavattvenābhinnaprāyatvādekaśabdapratipādyatvañca dṛśyate, yatā kumudaṃ nimīlati cakṣurnimīlati kamalamunmiṣati cakṣurunmiṣati ityādiṣa| jhratra hi prastutadharmaṃ saṅkocavikāśarūpaṃ nigīrṇasvarūpaṃ tañca sadṛśatayā nimīlanonmeṣatādātmona pratīyate iti samādhiguṇaḥ| ayañca arthagata eva prakṛte'rthe aprakṛtasya arthasyaiva āropāditi| anye tu arthadṛṣṭirūpasyārthaguṇasya samādheḥ ayoniranyacchāyāyoniśca iti dvaividhyamāhuḥ; tatrāha vāmanaḥ, " arthadṛṣṭiḥ samādhiḥ" iti, "artho dvividhaḥ ayoniranyacchāyāyoniśca" iti, tatra ayonirakāraṇaḥ, avadhānamātrakāraṇa ityarthaḥ ; tadayathā, " āśvapehi mama sīdhubhājanāt yāvadagradaśanairna dasyase "ityādi| anyacchāyāyoniḥ anyasya kāvyasya chāyā tadayoniśca; yathā, "mā bhaiḥ śaśāṅka mama sīdhuni nāsti rāhuḥ" ityādi| viśvanāthastvatraivaṃ manyate, ayonirarthaḥ kaviprasiddhimantareṇāpi svakapolakalpitaḥ upamānopameyabhāvādisvarūpaḥ, yathā, " sadyomuṇḍitamattahūṇacivukaprasparddhināraṅgakam" iti, atra hūṇacivukena saha nāṅgakasya sādṛśyaṃ prācīnakavibhiḥ kairapi na pratipāditaṃ kevalaṃ svabuddhayā udbhavitamṣa| anyacchāyāyoniḥ prācīnakavibhiḥ samyak vyavahṛtaḥ, kavisamayaprasiddho'pi kiñcit vaicitrābhāvena racitaḥ ; yatā, " nijanayanaprativimbairambuni bahuśaḥ pratāritā kā'pi| nīlotpale'pi vimṛśati karamarpayituṃ kusumalāvi| | "atra nayanananīlotpalayoḥ prasiddhamapi sādṛśyaṃ kiñcidvicchittiviśeṣeṇa nibaddham| śabdaguṇaḥ samādhistu gāḍhabandhamadhye śithilabandhoḥ pādāne śithilabandamadhye ca gāḍhabandopādāne vairasyābhāvaḥ ; yadāha vāmanaḥ, "ārohāvarohakramaḥ samādhi" iti| "ārohāvarohayoḥ kramaḥ ārohāvarohakramaḥ samādhiḥ parihāraḥ| "ārohasyāvarohe sati parihāraḥ, avarohasya vā ārohe sati iti; tatra ārohapūrvakaḥ avaroho yathā, "nirānandaḥ kaunde madhuni paribhuktojjhitarase" iti| avarohapūrvakastu āroho yathā, "narāḥ śīlabhraṣṭā vyasana iva majjanti taravaḥ" iti| pūrvatra"nirānandaḥ kaunde" ityatra gurvakṣarabāhulyādārohaḥ, "madhūni"ityatra lavakṣaraprācuyryāt avarohaḥ, avarohe pravatrtamāne sati ārohasya parityāgaḥ| uttaratra "narāḥ" ityatra śaithilyādavarohaḥ, "śīlabhraṣṭāḥ" ityatraḥ gurvakṣarapracuratvādārohaḥ, ārohe pravatrtamāno ca avarohasya parityāgaḥ iti samādherhaividhyam ityabhiprāyaḥ| kecittu krameṇa ārohaṇaṃ krameṇa avarohaṇam ityarthaṃ kalpayantiṭa| | 94| | 

samādhiṃ gauṇalakṣaṇakrāntaṃ darśayati, niṣṭhūrateti| niṣṭhūratam udgūrṇaṃ vāntam ityādipadaṃ, gauṇī yā vṛrttirlakṣaṇā, guṇayogāt iti bhāvaḥ, saiva vyāpāśrayaḥ viśiṣṭaḥ āśrayo yasya tat, mukhyārthasadṛśe'rthe bhāvaḥ ; anyatra tadabhāve, gauṇavṛttyāśrayābhāve mukhyārthasadṛśe'rthe prayuktamityarthaḥ, atisundaram atimanoramaṃ, tadeva samādhisthānamiti bhāvaḥ ; anyatra tadabhāve gauṇavṛttyāśrayābhāve mukhyārthe eva prayoge iteyarthaḥ, grāmyakakṣāṃ grāmyatādoṣakāluṣyaṃ, vigāhate labhate, tathā ca mukhyārthe prayuktaṃ niṣṭhūtādipadaṃ grāmyameva lākṣaṇike'rthe tu tādātmayāropāt guṇavaditi phalitārthaḥ ; (tathā coktaṃ, " grāmyaṃ ghṛṇāvadaślīlāmaṅgalārthaṃ yadīritam| tat saṃvīteṣu gupteṣu lakṣiteṣu na duṣyati| | "iti) | | 95| | 

uktamarthamudāharati, padmānīti| padāmāni arkāṃśuniṣṭhūtāḥ sūyryamayūkhairnikṣiptāḥ, pāvakavipruṣaḥ agnisphuliṅgān, pītvā udgīrṇā aruṇā reṇavaḥ parāgāḥ yaiḥ tādṛśaiḥ, mukhaiḥ (viśeṣaṇe tṛtīyā) bhūyaḥ punaḥ punaḥ, vamantīva| (sāyaṅkālīnapavanāsaṅgena skhalatparāgāṇāṃ padmānāṃ varṇanamidam| atra niṣṭhūyatodgīrṇaśabdau daihikakaphādinikṣepe śaktau api sāmānyanikṣeparūpe'rthe lākṣaṇikau, lakṣaṇā cātra kaphanikṣapo vaḍhiddhaṭhṭhaḍanakaṇānikṣape iva iti sādṛśyapratītyā gauṇī; yaduktaṃ viśvanāthena, " sādṛśyetarasambandhāḥ śuddhāstāḥ sakalā api| sādṛśyāttu matā gauṇyaḥ| | " iti; tena ca sādṛśyāpratītyā tadarthasya sahṛdayahṛdyatayā samādhiravyāhataḥ| "vamanti iva" ityatra tu padmakatrtukavamanasyānvayabodhābhāvāt sādṛśyāsambhavāñca lakṣaṇā avaṣyamaṅgīkāyryā iti cenna, ivaśabdasya sambhāvanrthatvāt sambhāvanāyāmapi bādhodayasyākiñcitkaratvena āhāyryavadanvayabodhāpratibandhakatvāt anvayabodho nirbādha eva; pratyuta sambhāvanāyāḥ lakṣaṇājanyatādrupyapratiteravi vaicitrājanakatvamityavadheyam| etena prāgabhihitagauṇavṛttipadaṃ sambhāvanāyā upalakṣaṇamavagantavyam) | | 96| | 

itīti| iti pūrvoktaṃ niṣṭhūtādipadaṃ, hṛdyaṃ gauṇalakṣaṇayogena samādhimattvāditi bhāvaḥ| badhūḥ niṣṭhovati kaphādikaṃ tyajati, iti vākyantu, ahṛdyaṃ grāmyatādoṣakaluṣitatayā sahṛdayānām apriyam| (abhidhayā vṛttyā mukhyārthe prayuktaṃ niṣṭhīvati iti padaṃ jugupsāṃ prakaṭayati, tatrāpi badhūpadaṃ grāmyatāmatiśayaṃ vadrdhayati badhvāḥ kaphavamanasyātyantajugupsākaratvāt) | prāguktasya anyadharmasyānyatrāropaḥ samādhirityuktalakṣaṇasya cārutvātiśayapradarśanārthaṃ bahūnāṃ dharmāṇāmāropamāha, yugapaditi| tatra naikadharmāṇāṃ bahūnām anyadharmāṇāṃ, yugapat samakālameva, na tu kālabhedena anyatra aprastutādanyatra prastute ityarthaḥ, adhyāsaḥ taddhamrmanigaraṇena sadṛśatayā tādātmayāropaḥ, smṛtaḥ cārutvātiśayajanakatvādutkṛṣṭasamādhitvena paṇḍitaiḥ svīkṛtaḥ; tathā ca ekasya anyadharmasya anyasmina, tathā bahūnāmapi anyadharmāṇāmantra samāropaḥ samādhiriti phalitārthaḥ| | 97| | 

uktamarthamudāharati, gurviti| guravaḥ jalapūrṇatayā mahatyaḥ, anyatra, bhrūṇasambandhena sthūlodara ityarthaḥ ; yadvā, guroḥ mahataḥ, prabhūkṛtasyetyarthaḥ, anyatra, paripuṣṭasyetyarthaḥ, (garbhaviśeṣaṇam) tathā garbhasya antarvarttijalapūrasya, anyatra, kukṣisthajīvasya, bhareṇa klāntāḥ mantharāḥ, stanantyaḥ garjantyaḥ, anyatra, klāntijanitakunthanādikaṃ kurvatyaḥ, imāḥ meghapaṅktayaḥ, acalasya parvatasya, adhityakā ūṅdyabhūmiḥ, ("upatyakā'drerāsannā bhūmirūḍrchamadhityakā" ityamaraḥ) tasyā utsaṅgaṃ madhyabhāgam, anayatra, sakhyāḥ kroḍaṃ, samadhiśerate| (atra meghapaṅktidharmeṣu taddharmanigaraṇena sādṛśyāt garbhiṇīdharmāṇāṃ bahūnāṃ yugapadadhyāsāt vaicitrātiśayaḥ sahṛdayahṛdayahārīti bhāvyam, alaṅkāraśca samāsoktiriti) | | 98| | 

uktaśloke garbhiṇīdharmaṃ darśayati, utsaṅgeti| atra ukte śloke, sakhyā utsaṅgaśayanaṃ stananaṃ gauravaṃ klama iti uktarūpāḥ, ime bahavaḥ, garbhiṇīdharmāḥ darśitāḥ, āropitatveneti śeṣaḥ| ( yadi ca "stanitaṃ garjitaṃ meghanirghoṣe" ityādyamaroktyā stananasabdena meghagarjitameva budhyate tathā'pi śabdamātrārthakastanadhātuniṣpannatvena "stanantya" iti padasya garbhiṇyāstādṛse'pi śabde prayogo yujyate| anyañca, "mañjīrādiṣu raṇitaprāyaṃ pakṣiṣu ca kūjitaprabhṛti stanitamaṇitādi surate meghādīṣu garjitapramukham"iti vacanāntareṇa stanitaśabdasya suratavācakatvamapi siddhaṃ, tasmāt stanadhāto, kevalaṃ meghagarjitamevārtho na, api tu sāmānyaśabdamātramapi| nanu śabdamātrārthakatāyāṃ siddhāyāmapi kathaṃ stanantya iti padena garbhiṇyā kunthanādisabdaviśeṣasya upapattiriti cenna, utsaṅgaśayanādīnām asādāraṇagarbhiṇīdharmāṇām antaḥpatāt vaicitrādhāyakatvāñca stananasyāpi garbhiṇīdharmarūpatvasya yuktatvāt vaicitrāsyaiva kāvyatvāt, avaśyameva tadapekṣaṇaṃ nyāyyam iti vivecanīyam) | | 99| | 

samādhiguṇam upasaṃharati, taditi| tat tasmāt, uktaprakāreṇa atīvavaicitrāvahatvāt ityarthaḥ, samādhirnāma yo guṇaḥ etat kāvyasya sarvaṃ svaṃ dhanaṃ, sārabhūtamityarthaḥ, samagraḥ sakalaḥ, kavisārthaḥ api kavisampradāyo'pi, gauḍaīyaḥ vaidarbhīyo vā ityarthaḥ, taṃ tathābhūtam, enaṃ samādhim, anugacchati etadanusāreṇa kāvye pravatrtaṃte ityarthaḥ| (yadi ca eteṣāṃ guṇānāṃ daśānāṃ madhye kecit tattaddoṣābhāvarūpatayā keciñca alaṅkṛtirūpatayā navyaiḥ anādṛtāḥ kevalaṃ traya eva guṇā darsitāḥ yathā, "mādhuyryaujaḥ prasādākhyāstrayaste na punardaśa" iti, tathā'pi prācāṃmatamanusṛtya granthakṛtā guṇatvena daśa eva nirūpitāḥ ityavadheyam) | | 100| | 

itthaṃ gauḍavaidarbhayorbhedaṃ varṇayitvā tannirūpaṇam upasaṃharati, itīti| iti uktaprakāreṇa, tayoḥ gauḍavaidarbhayoḥ, svarūpanirūpaṇāt lakṣaṇakītrtanāt, mārgadvayam ullikhitarītidvayaṃ, bhinnaṃ parasparavilakṣaṇam; tadbhedāḥ tābhyāṃ mārgābhyāṃ, bhedaḥ bhinnā ityarthaḥ, lāṭapāñcālādayaḥ iti yāvat "kvadibhihito bhāvo dravyavat prakāśate" iti nyāyāt| yadvā, tadbedāḥ tasya kāvyamārgasya, bhedāḥ gauḍavaidarbhābhyāmanye prākāraviśeṣāḥ, pratikavi tattaddeśīyakaviṣu, sthitāḥ, tu kintu, te vaktuṃ na śaktayante, ativistārāditi bhāvaḥ| (teṣāṃ lakṣaṇāni anyaiḥ uktāni yathā, "tatrāsamāsā niḥśeṣaśleṣādiguṇagumphitā| vipañcīsvarasaubhāgyā vaidarbhīrītiriṣyate| | samastapañcaṣapadāmojaḥ kāntivivarjitām| madhurāṃ sukumārāñca pāñcāloṃ kavayo viduḥ| | samastātyudbaṭapadāmojaḥ kāntiguṇānvitām| gauḍaīyeti vijānanti rītiṃ rītivicakṣaṇāḥ| | antarālo tu pāñcālīvaidabhryoryā'vatiṣṭhate| sā'vantikā samastaiḥ syāt dvitraistricaturaiḥ padaiḥ| | samastarītivyryāmiśrā lāṭīyā rītiriṣyate| pūrvarīteranirvāhe khaṇḍarītistu māgadhī| | "iti| tāsu pāñcālī yathā, "gātrābhighātadalitāṅgadajarjarāṇāṃ gaṇaḍastalīlulitakuṇaḍalatāḍitānām| kṣobhasphuṭanmukuṭakoṭivighaṭṭitānāṃ nādo'bhavajjhaṇajhaṇāmukharo maṇīnām| | " atraujaḥ kāntivirahitā mādhuyryasaukumāyryavatī ṣaḍadhike pade samāsarahitā ca pāñcālīrītiḥ| tathā āvantikā yathā, "etāni niḥsahatanorasamañjasāni śūnyaṃ manaḥ piśunayanti padāni tasyāḥ| ete ca vatrmataravaḥ prathayanti tāpamāliṅgitojjhitapariglapitaiḥ prabālaiḥ| | "seyaṃ samastadvitricatuṣpadā vaidarbhīpāñcālyorantārālavarttinī āvantikānāma rītiḥ| tathā lāṭīyā yathā, "ayamudayati mudrābhañjanaḥ padminīnāmudayā girivanālībālamandārapuṣpam| virahavidhurakokadvandvabandhurvibhindan kupitakapikapolakroḍatāmrastamāṃsi| | " seyaṃ samastarītimiśrā lāṭīyā nāma rītiḥ| tatra māgadhī yathā, "karikavalanaśiṣṭeḥ śākasākhāgrapatraiḥ aruṇasaraṇayo'mī , rvato bhīṣayante| calitaśavarasenādattagośrṛṅgacaṇḍadhvanicakitavarāhavyākulā vindhyapādāḥ| | " seyamārabdharīteranirvāhāt khaṇḍarītirmāgadhī) | athavā tadbhedāḥ tayoḥ gauḍavaidarbhayoḥ bhedāḥ antargataviśeṣā ityarthaḥ| | 101| | 

uktamarthaṃ dṛṣṭāntena samarthayati, ikṣiviti| ikṣukṣīraguḍaādaunām, (ādinā madhuramātrāṇāṃ parigrāhaḥ) mādhuyryasya antaraṃ bhedaḥ, mahat sphuṭatayā pratītiyogyaṃ, tathā'pi sarasvatyā'pi vāgdevyā'pi, kimu anyairiti bhāvaḥ; tat antaram, ākhyātuṃ kathayituṃ, na śakyate; evamikṣuprabhṛtināmiva kāvyamārgāṇāṃ madhurimṇaḥ ānantyāt tadṅedākyānamasambhavamiti sphuṭatayā pratīyamānabhedaṃ bhedadvayameva viśiṣya nirūpitamiti tātpayryārtaḥ| | 102| | 

itthaṃ kāvyasvarūpaṃ nirūpya tatkāraṇamāha, naisargikīti| naisargikī svabhāvasidvā, pratibhā sphurantī matiḥ, bahu nānāvidha, vyākaraṇakoṣakalācchandogajāśvādilakṣaṇātmakamityarthaḥ, nirmalaṃ samyak pariśīlanena sarvatā visuddhaṃ, śrutaṃ śāsjñānaṃ lokācārādijñānañca, tathā amandaḥ pragāḍhaḥ, abhiyogaḥ abhiniveśaḥ, kāvyābhijñāt śikṣaṇenābhyāsa ityarthaḥ, etat trayam asyāḥ kāvyasampadaḥ kāvyarūpāyāḥ sampatteḥ, kāramaṃ hetuḥ| jhr kāraṇamiti ekavacananirdeśena samastasyaiva kāraṇatā, na tu pratyekasya iti sūcitam| tathā hi kāvyanirmitau yatnavyutpattihīnānāṃ bālakādīnāṃ satyāmapi pratibhāyāṃ kāvyotpatterabhāvadarśanāt, tadanantare ca kāle sañjātavyutpattīnāmabhyāsavatāṃ teṣāmevā kāvyadarsanāt tathā yatnavyutpattiyuktānāñca keṣāñcit pratibhāmantarema tadabhāvadarśanāt, tatā kṛtayatnānāṃ darsanāt, akṛtayatnānāñcādarsanāt samastasyaiva kāraṇatvaṃ yuktamiti nirṇayaḥ| uktañca prakāśakāraṇena, " śaktirnipuṇatā lokaśāstrakāvyādyavekṣaṇāt| kāvyajña śikṣayā'bhayāsa iti hetustadudbhave| | " vyākhyātañca tatraiva, "iti trayaḥ samuditā na tu vyastāḥ, tasya kāvyasya udbhave nirmāṇe samullāse ca, hetuḥ, na tu hetavaḥ" iti| kecittu pratibā eva kāvyakāraṇaṃ, vyutpattistu tasya cārutve hetuḥ, abhyāso vṛddhihetusca, pratibā ca kvacit vā devānugrahāt, bhāvikavitvānāṃ bālānāṃ satyamapi pratibhāyāṃ kāvyānudayāt kālasya tatsahakāritvamaṅgīkāyryamityāhuḥ| uktañca, "kavitvaṃ jāyate śaktervadrdhate'bhyāsayogataḥ| tasya cārutvaniṣpattau vyutpattistu garīyasau| | " etānyeva vāmanena kāvyāṅgatayā darśitāni; tatra hi prathamaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamātmakasya lokasya vṛttajñānamantareṇa kiṃ vaṇryate ityapekṣāyāṃ lokavṛttaṃ, tataśca saṃskṛtāḥ śabdāḥ, tadanu tadarthāḥ, anantaramitivṛttavaicitrāhetuḥ śrṛṅgārāṅgaṃ kalākauśalaṃ, tato rasopayogi kāmavyavahāraḥ, tataścārthānarthavivekaheturdaṇḍanītiḥ, pascāt lakṣyajñatvādayaḥ ityādi krameṇopadesārthaṃ, "loko vidyā prakīrṇañca kāvyāṅgāni" ityetat sūtraṃ kṛtaṃ, tataśca tasyaiva parisphuṭārtaṃ, " lokavṛttaṃ lokaḥ" "śabdasmṛtyabhidhānakoṣacchandovicitikalākāmaśāstradaṇḍanītipūrvā vidyā" "lakṣyajñatvamabhiyogaḥ vṛddhasovā'vekṣaṇaṃ pratibhānamavadhānañca prakīrṇam" "tatra kāvyaparicayo layajñatvam" kāvyabandhodyamo'bhiyogaḥ " "kāvyopadeśaguruśuśrūṣaṇaṃ vṛddhasevā" "padādhānoddaraṇamavekṣaṇam" "kavitvavījaṃ pratibhānam" "cittaikāgrāmavadhānam" ityetaiḥ sūtraiḥ kāvyālaṅkārasūtrakṛtā yat sūtritaṃ tena pratibhāditrayasya kāvyānāṃ nirmāṇe samullāse vā hetutvamapi darśitam, etairvinā kāvyaśarīraṃ na sambavatīti kāvyāṅgatvameteṣāṃ sidhyati tadidaṃ sarvamanavadyamṭa| | 103| | 

uktakāraṇānāṃ sarveṣāmabhāve'pi yatnavatāṃ śāstrānuśīlanaparāṇāṃ kathañcit phalasiddhirityāśayenāha, neti| yadyapi pūrvavāsanā prāktanasaṃskāraḥ, svabhāvikī śaktirityartaḥ, tathā adbhutam alaukikaṃ, guṇānubandhi vaicitrāvahaṃ, kāvyanirmāṇenotkar, janakamityartaḥ, pratibhānaṃ sucikkaṇāṃ buddhiḥ, na vidyate, tathā'pi śrutena śāstrānuśīlanena, kāvyajñopadeśena vā, yatnena abiniveśena ca, upāsitī sevitā, vāk dhruvaṃ niścitaṃ, kamapi anugrahaṃ karoti eva avaśyameva kāvyanirmāṇe kiñcit sāmathryamātanoti ityarthaḥ| ( samyak sāmathrye tu uktatritayameva heturiti na parasparaṃ virodha iti) | | 104| | 

upasaṃharati, taditi| tat tasmāt, astatandrai) , astā tandrā yeṣāṃ taiḥ ālasyarahitaiḥ ityarthaḥ, kīrtiṃta kavitvajanitayaśaḥ, īpsubhiḥ, janaiḥ iti śeṣaḥ, śramāt pariśramamavalambya, sarasvatī khalu niścayena, aniśam upāsyā sevyā, avaśyameva śāstraṃ pariśīlitavyamiti bhāvaḥ| kathamityāha, kṛśa iti| kavitve kāvyakaraṇasāmathrye, kṛśe alpe'pi, kṛtaśramāḥ kṛtaśāstrapariśīlanaprayāsā janāḥ, vidagdhagoṣṭhīṣu kavisamājeṣu, vihatrtum īśate prabhavanti, alpasyāpi asya jñāne karaṇe ca aihikāmutrikaphalasiddhirbhavati, "ekaḥ śabdaḥ suprayuktaḥ samyak jñātaḥ svarge loke ca kāmadhugmavati" iti śruteḥ| | 105| | 


<2>
<3>
tṛtīyaparicchedaḥ

dvitīyaparicchede "kāvyaśobhākarān dharmān alaṅkārān pracakṣate" ityanena śabdārthālaṅkārasāmānyalakṣaṇamabhidhāya śabdasya prathamabuddhiviṣayatvena prathamaṃ lakṣayitumucitānapi śabdālaṅkārān ativaicitryādhāyakatvābhāvāt anirūpya vaicitryātiśayacādhāyakāḥ arthālaṅkārā eva darśitāḥ| samprati prathamaparicchede prāptāvasaratayā anuprāsabhinnān tadviśeṣān nirūpayiṣyan doṣalakṣaṇañcābhidhāyasyan uktalakṣaṇamapi yamakanūdya tadbhedamanuvadati , avyapeteti| avyapetaḥ avyavahitaḥ, vyapetaḥ vyavahitaśca, ātmā svarūpaṃ yasyāstathābhūtā, varṇasaṃhateḥ svaravyañjanasṅghātasya, vyāvṛttiḥ viśeṣeṇa punarāvṛttiḥ yamakam| (asya viśeśalakṣaṇaṃ pūrvamevokitamiti nātroktam| yamakaghaṭakapadānāmavyavahitavyavahitvena bhedapradarśanāya uktamavyapetetyādi, pūrvoccāritavarṇasaṅghasya kvacidavyadhānna kvacit vyavadhānena ca punāravṛttiriti prathamaṃ bhedadvayam abhihitam| uktañca sarasvatīkaṇṭhābharaṇe, "vibhinnārthaikarūpāyā yā''vṛttirvarṇasaṃhateḥ| avyapetavyapetātmā yamakaṃ tannigadyate| | " iti) | yamakasya tasya sthīnanirūpaṇārthamāha, tacceti| tacca pādānām ekadvitricaturātmakānāṃ ślokacaraṇānām, ādiśca madhyaśca antasca gocaraḥ viṣayaḥ yasya tat| jhrtataśca prathamapādasyādibhāgagataṃ madhyabhāgagatam antabhāgagatañceti vibhāgaprakāraḥ, evaṃ dvitīyādipadānāmapi ādigataṃ madhyagatam antagatañca| tataśca pādānāṃ khaṇḍatrayagatatvameva yamakasyeti sthānaniyamastu nāśaṅkyaḥ, madhyapadena sambhavatkhaṇḍamātrasya prāpteḥ caturādikhaṇḍeṣvapi asya niyamaḥ jñātavyaḥ, yaduktaṃ prakāśakāreṇa, "trikhaṇḍe tiṃraśataṃ catuḥkhaṇḍe catvāriṃśat" iti| etadupalakṣaṇaṃ, tena pādagatamapi bodhyam uktañca vāmanena, "pādaḥ pādasyaikasyānekasya cādimadhyāntabhāgāḥ sthānāni" iti| atrāpi pāda ityupalakṣaṇaṃ, tena pādaḥ pādakhaṇḍaāḥ padyādrdhaṃ samastapadyañca yamakasya sthānānīti bodhyam| kaṇṭhābharaṇe'pi, "tadavyapetayamakaṃ vyapetayamakantathā| sthānāsthānavibhāgābhyāṃ pādabhedācca bhidyate| tatra pādādimadhyāntāḥ sthānaṃ teṣūpakalpāte| | "iti| | 1| | 

sthānabhedena yamakabhedamabhidhāya pādagataṃ bhedaṃ darsayan krameṇa tattadbhedān nirdiśati, eketyādi| ekadvitricatuṣpāda yamakānām avimiśrāṇāmiti bhāvaḥ, vikalpanāḥ " pādānāmādimadhyāntagocaram" ityanena anantarameva sāmānyata uktāḥ prabhedāḥ, jñātavyā iti śeṣaḥ, padacatuṣṭayātmakasya padyasya pratipādam ādimadhyāntagatatvena tridhā binnasya tasya punaḥ dvādaśavidhatvaṃ jñātavyam ityarthaḥ| (te ca amiśrayameka eva veditavyāḥ| tathā hi, amiśritaṃ prathamapādādigataṃ dvitīyapādādigataṃ tṛtīyapādādigataṃ caturthapādādigatañceti caturvidhamādibāgayamakam, evaṃ prathamādipādamadhyāntabhāgagatayamakamapi, tataśca militvā dvādaśavidhatvamavimiśritayamakasya iti saṅkṣepaḥ| ayañca bhedaḥ avyapetavyapetasādhāraṇaḥ| kecittu ekadvītyādi ślokādrdhamevaṃ vyākhyānayanti, tadyathā, "padyasya catuṣpādātmakatvāt pratikhaṇḍaṃ catvāraścatvāro bhedā jñātavyā ittharthaḥ, yathā avimiśritamādibhāgayamakaṃ prathamapāda eva dvitīyapāda eva tṛtīyapāda eva caturthapāda eveti caturvidhaṃ, tathā madhyādibhāgayamakamapi pratyekaṃ caturvidhamiti"iti) | abhiśrabhedaṃ darśayitvā miśrayamakabhedaṃ darśayati, ādīti| sambhedaḥ sajātīyayamakānāmanyonyasammiśraṇaṃ yoniryeṣāṃ tādṛśaḥ vimiśrā ityarthaḥ, teṣāṃ yamakānāṃ, bhedāḥ ādimadhyāntamadhyāntamadhyādyādyantasarvataḥ sthitatvena atyantabahavaḥ ativistarāḥ, jñeyā iti śeṣaḥ| (padacchedastu ādimadhyānta madhyānta madhyādya ādyanta sarvataḥ ityavaṃrūpeṇet| sarvatra madhyapadasya dvitīyatṛtīyapādabodhakaḥ, śeṣamādipādānāṃ caturthapādasya pūvasthitatvenāditvāt; tataśca ādimadhyāntecyatra madhyapadasya dvitīyatṛtīyapādadvayātmakatvāt ādimadhyagatatvena dvau ādyantagatatvenaikaḥ iti militvā trayo bhedāḥ jñeyāḥ ; te ca trayaḥ prathamadvitīya (ādimadhya) pādagatatvena, prathamatṛtīya (ādimadhya) pādagatatvena, prathamacaturtha(adyānta) pādagatatvena ceti boddhavyam| madhyāntetyatrāpi madhyapadasya dvitīyatṛtīyapādadvayabodhaṃkatvātbhedatrayameva bodhyam; tadyatā, dvitīyatatīyapādayoḥ (madhyamadhyayoḥ) ekaḥ, dvitīyacaturthapādayoḥ tṛtīyacaturthapādayośca madhyāntatvena dvau iti militvā trayaḥ prākāraḥ| madhyādyetyatra dvitīyatṛtīyapādayormadhyatvāt prathamadvitīyatatīyapādagatatvenaika eva bhedaḥ| atra ādyaśca madhyau ceti vigrahe dharmārthādipāṭhāt ādyaśabdasya paranipātaḥ| ādyantetyatra ādipadasya prathamadvitīyatṛtīyapādaparatvāt traya eva bhedāḥ sambhavanti, yathā, prathamadvitīyacaturthapādagataḥ prathamatṛtīyacaturthapādagataḥ, dvitīyatṛtīyacaturthapādagataśceti tat prakārāḥ| sarvata ityatra ca prathamadvitīyatṛtīyacaturthapādagatatvenaika eva bhedaḥ| evaṃ militvā vyāmiśrayamakasya ekādaśabhedāḥ boddhavyāḥ, anye ca asthānasthūlasūkṣmādiyamakabhedāḥ bhojādau draṣṭavyāḥ| sarvata iti saptamībahuvacanāntapadaṃ, sarveṣu pādeṣu ityarthaḥ) | te ca sukarāḥ susādhyāḥ subodhāśca, duṣkarāścaiva duḥsādhyāḥ durbodhāścaiva, kavīnāṃ boddhṛṇāñceti śeṣaḥ, ataḥ atra kecana daśryante udāhriyante ityarthaḥ, na tu sarve iti bhāvaḥ| ("te'tra" ityatra " tatra" iti bhojoktapāṭhaḥ) | | 2| | 

3| | 

tatra prathamapādasthamavyavahitamamiśram ādibhāgayamataṃ nirdiśati, māneneti| he sakhi! priye jane praṇayini, anena īdṛśena, atiprabalatvādasmākamapi pīḍaājanakeneti bhāvaḥ, mānena īṣryākopena saha, ("mānaḥ kopaḥ sa tu dvedhā praṇayeṣryāsamudbhavaḥ" iti lakṣaṇāt) praṇayaḥ paricayaḥ, taveti śeṣaḥ, mā bhūt, priyajanaṃ prati etādṛsaṃ mānaṃ mā kuru ityarthaḥ, khaṇḍitā, "pāśrvameti priyo yasyā anyasambhogaciḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanataḥ| sā khaṇḍiteti kathitā dhīrairīṣryākaṣāyitā| | " ityuktalakṣaṇā, api tvamiti śeṣaḥ, kaṇṭhamāśliṣya āliṅgya, taṃ priyameva, satrapaṃ salajjaṃ, kuru, apakāriṇi praṇayapradarśanameva tasya lajjākaratvāt guru śāsanamiti bhāvaḥ| ( atimānīnīṃ khaṇḍitāṃ prati tatsakhyā uktiriyam atra "mānena mānena" iti avyavahitamādidagatamādibhāgayamakaṃ, tacca pādāntarāsaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍakīrṇatvādamiśram| yatra tu pādāntaramatatvaṃ tatra miśratvamanyatra śuddhatvamiti bhedopi vyapetāvyapetavat savayamūhanīyaḥ iti) | | 4| | 

dvitīyapādagataṃ tadeva nirdiśati, meghanādenetiṣamadanaḥ kāmaḥ ratyā svapatnyā anurāgeṇa ca , saha haṃsānāṃ madanodinā garvamapanayatā, meghanādena meghagarjitena, varṣāsu haṃsānāṃ nirmadatvaprasiddheriti bhāvaḥ, nunnaḥ apanītaḥ, māno yasmāt tathoktaṃ, strīṇāṃ manaḥ cittaṃ, vigāhate āloḍayati vyakulayatītyarthaḥ, meghagarjanasya uddīpakatvāt varṣāsu pramadānāṃ manaḥ sānurāgaṃ sakāmañca bhavatīti bhāvaḥ| (atra"madano madano" iti dvitīyapādagatamavimiśramavyavahitamādibhāgayamakam) | | 5| | 

tṛtīyapādagataṃ tadevodāharati, rājanvatya iti| caturambhodhirasanāyāḥ catvāraḥ ambhodhayaḥ sāgarā eva, rasanāḥ kāñcīdāmān, kaṭībhūṣaṇaviśeṣā ityarthaḥ, yasyāḥ tasyāḥ, catuḥsāgaraparicchinnāyā iti yāvat, uvryāḥ pṛthivyāḥ, karagrahe ṣaṣṭhāṃśarūparājagrāhyabhāgagrahaṇe pāṇigrahe ca, caturam nipuṇam, āsamudrakaragrāhiṇamityarthaḥ, bhavantaṃ satpatiṃ surakṣakaṃ subhatrtārañca, prāpya prajāḥ rājanvatyaḥ sunṛpatiyuktāḥ, ("surājñi deśe rājanvān syāttato'nyatra rājavān" ityamaraḥ) jātāḥ| (atra" caturaṃ caturam" iti tṛtīyapādagatamavyavahitamādibhāgayamakaṃ śiddham) | | 6| | 

caturthapādagataṃ tadeva nirddiśati, apaṇyamiti| tava kaiścit ahitaiḥ śatrubhiḥ, palāyitvā jīvadbhiriti bhāvaḥ, padātirathanāgāśvarahitaiḥ caturaṅgabalāvihīnaiḥ sadbhirityarthaḥ, ("hastyaśvarathapādātaṃ senāṅgaṃ syāccatuṣṭayam" ityamaraḥ) araṇyaṃ, palāyitvā jīvitāṃ lokālaye avasthānāsambhavāditi bhāvaḥ, tathā keścicca ahitaiḥ, sammukhasaṅgrāmeṇa mṛtairiti bhāvaḥ, (atrāpi padātyādirahitairiti yojanīyam) divaukasāṃ sadma suralokaḥ, ākrāntam āśritamityarthaḥ, yuddhe maraṇasya svargaprāpakatvāditi bhāvaḥ, ("hatī vā prāpsāsi svargaṃ jitvā vā bhokṣyase mahīm" iti gītāyāṃ bhagavadukteḥ| atra "rahitairahitai" iti caturthapādagatamavyavahitamamiśramādibhāgayamakama) | | 7| | 

atha sambedayonīnavyapetayamakabhedān vivarītamādimadhyāntetyādinoktaṃ prathamadvitīyaprathamatṛtīyaprathamacaturthapādagatabhedatrayeṣu ādau prathamadvitīya(ādimadhya) pādagatamādimāgamayamakamudāharati, madhuramiti| he ambhojavadane! padmamukhi! madhuḥ vasantaḥ, te tava, netrayoḥ bhramarabhrāntyā bhramapāvimau iti bhrameṇa, mukhāmbojopari netrayorvatrtamānatvāt ambhojo bhramarayugalāvasthānasya sambhavācca bhramo yujyate iti bhāvaḥ, madhuraṃ vibhramaṃ śobhāṃ, viḍambayati, viśeṣeṇa prerayati, samarpayatītyarthaḥ, ("ḍanva preraṇe"ityasya caurādikasya rīpam) nu itaci vitarke, kim? vada kathaya; netrayoste īdṛśo vibhramaḥ bhramarabhrāntimatā vasantenaiva kiṃ samutpāditaḥ? iti bhāvaḥ| (vasantodaye mamutpanne ambhoje bhramaravilāsasambhavāt nu iti vitarkabodhakaṃ padamatra yujyata eva| kecitti ambhojavadane iti saptamyantamuktavā tatra vatrtamānayornetrayoriti yojayanti| "nu" ityatra "na" iti pāṭhe na biḍambayati kim? api tu viḍambayatyevtyarthaḥ| atra "madhuraṃ madhuram" iti "vadane vadane" iti ca yamakaghaṭakapadānāṃ prathamadvitīya (ādimadhya) pādayorādibhāgasthitatayā ādibhāgalakṣaṇaṃ, pādadvayamiśraṇācca miśrasaṃjñakaṃ, tathā padānāmavyavadhānātayā avyapetaṃ tataścedaṃ prathamadvitīya (ādimadhya) pādagatamiśrāvyapetādibhāgayamakam) | | 8| | 

tadeva prathamatṛtīya(ādimadhya) pādagatamudāharati, vāraṇa iti| he smara! yataḥ yasmāt, tava raṇoddāmo yuddhadurmadaḥ, vāraṇaḥ hastī vā, dudrdharaḥ dudrdharṣaḥ, hayaḥ aśvo vā, na, astīti śeṣaḥ, tat tasmāt yuddhopakaraṇābhāvādapītyarthaḥ, naḥ asmān, virahiṇaḥ ityartaḥ, antaṃ nāśaṃ nayataḥ prāpayataḥ, tava vikramaḥ aho āścayryabhūta ityarthaḥ| (atra "vāramo vāraṇo" "nayato nayato" iti yamakaghaṭakapadānāṃ prathamatṛtīya (ādimadhya) pādayorādigatatvāt prathamtṛtīya (ādimadhya) pādagataṃ miśramavyavahitamādibhāgayamakamiti bodhyam) | | 9| | 

tadeva prathamacaturtha (ādyanta) pādagatamudāharati, rajitairiti| ājiḥ yuddhaṃ, tatra taikṣṇetayā, yuddhadurmadatvena ityarthaḥ, rājitaiḥ śobhitaiḥ, tvādṛśaiḥ bhavādṛśaiḥ, nṛpaiḥ vasudhā pṛthivī, tatratyo jana iti bhāvaḥ, jīyate jayena ādau vaśīkriyate, yuddhavyāpāreṇa pauḍayate iti bhāvaḥ, punaḥ jayānantaramityarthaḥ, vasudhārayā vasakūnāṃ dhanānāṃ, dhārayā dhārāvarṣaṇena, aviratadānenetyarthaḥ, tṛÏpta santoṣaṃ, nīyate ca| (atra "rājitai rājitai"" vasudhā vasudhā" iti ca yamakaghaṭakapadānāṃ prathamacaturtha (ādyanta) pādayorādigatatvāt pādadvayamiśraṇāt avyavadhānaniśācca prathamacaturtha (ādyanta) pādagataṃ miśramavyahitamādibhāgayamakamiti saṃjñā jñātavyā| madhuramityādinā krameṇa ādimadhyantetyanena nirūpitāstrayo bhedā udāhṛtāḥ) | | 10| | 

itthamādimadhyāntabhedaṃ darśayati madhyāntetyanena pratipāditaṃ madhyamadhyaṃ madhyāntaṃ madhyāntañceti tasya prakāratrayaṃ katrameṇa darśayan prathamaṃ dvitīyatṛtīya (madhyamadhya) pādagatamavyapetādibhāgayamakamudāharati, karotīti| sahakārasya āmrasya, kalikā mukulaṃ, tathāmanmanaḥ, "surate karṇamūle tu nijadeśīyabhāṣayā| dampatyoḥ kathanaṃ yattu manmanaṃ taṃ parcakṣate| | " ityuktalakṣaṇaḥ karṇamūle rahasyālāpaḥ, iveti adhyāhāyryam; eṣa mattānāṃ vasantāgamanena unmadānāṃ, kokilānāṃ nisvano'pi, manmanaḥ mama mānasam, utkalikottaram utkaṇṭhākulamityarthaḥ, karoti, āmrakalikāyāḥ kokilālāpasya ca atīvoddīpakatvāditi bhāvaḥ| (virahiṇa uktiriyam| atra "kaliko kaliko""manmano manmano"iti yamakaghaṭakapadānāṃ dvitīyatṛtīyapādādigatatvena dvitīyatṛtīyapādayośca madhyatvenābhidhānāt dvitīyatṛtīya (madhyamadhya) pādagatamavyapetādibhāgayamakaṃ pādadvayamiśraṇācca miśramityavadheyam| kiñca "kalikotkalikā" itiyatra takārasya madhyapātitve'pi svaśūnyatvāt ślokīyākṣarasaṅkhyā'pūrakatayā vyavadhānamadhye agaṇanāt yamakasya na avyapetatvahāni) , sasvarasyaiva vyañjanasya vyavadhāyakatvābhyupagamāt iti bodhyam) | | 11| | 

tadeva dvitīyacaturtha(madhyānta) pādagatamudāharati, kathamiti| iha asmin pradeśe, tava upalambhaḥ prāptiḥ, tasya āśā pratyāśā, tasyāḥ vihatau vināśeṃ, aṅganāśinī śarīranikṛntanī, tādṛśī anubhūyamānā, avasthā smaradaśāviśeṣaḥ, aṅganām abalāṃ, māmiti śeṣaḥ, āroḍhum ākramituṃ, kathaṃ na alaṃ na samarthā? api tu samarthā eva, tvadvirahe ahaṃ mṛtaprāyā jātā'smīti tātpayryārthaḥ| (atra "vihatā vihatā""maṅganāmaṅganā" iti yamakaghaṭakapadānāṃ dvitīya caturthapādādigatatvena dvitīyapādasya madhyatvāccaturthapādasya antatvācca dvitīyacaturtha (madhyānta) pādagataṃ miśramavyavahitamādibhāgayamakam| kecadimaṃ yukpādayamakamāhuḥ; tallakṣaṇaṃ yathā, "dvitīye ca caturthe ca yamakaṃ yatra dṛśyate| yukpādayamakaṃ tattu vijñeyaṃ kavipuṅgavaiḥ| | " iti| udāharaṇāntaraṃ yathā bhaṭṭikāvye, "raṇapaṇḍito'gryavibudhāripure kalahaṃ sa rāmamahitaḥ kṛtavān| jvaladagni rāvaṇagṛhañca balāt kalahaṃsarāmamahitaḥ kṛtavān| | "iti) | | 12| | 

tadeva tṛtīyacaturtha (madhyānta) pādagatamudāharati, nigṛhyeti| nalineṣu padmeśu, unamukhāḥ patanābhimukhāḥ, ālinaḥ bhramarāḥ bālapallavaiḥ navapallavaiḥ, śobhate iti tathoktena, taruṇā yūnaḥ, netre nigṛhya balena gṛhitvā, karṣanti, nijadarśanotsukanayanān kurvantītyartha| (atra "taruṇā taruṇā" "nalino nalino"iti padānāṃ tṛtīyacaturthapādādigatatayā tṛtīyapādasya madhyatvena caturthapādasya antatvena ca tathā pādadvayamiśraṇājilakṣaṇatayā ca tṛtīcaturtha (madhyānta) pādagatamavyavahitamiśrādibhāgayamakam| tataśca madhyāntatvena nirūpitaṃ prakāratrayamevodāhṛtaṃ bhavati iti dik) | | 13| | 

madhyādyetyanena, pratipāditaṃ prathamādvitīyatṛtīyapādagatamekaprakāramāha, viśadeti| viśantaḥ praveśaṃ kurvantaḥ, āmattāḥ unmadāḥ, sārasāḥ jalapakṣiviśeṣa yasmin tādṛse, sārase jale sarovarajale, viśadā śubhravarṇā, iyaṃ haṃsī rājahaṃsītyarthaḥ, kurutena kutsitaraveṇa, ravasya kutsitatvamuddipakatvena virahiṇāmasahyatvāt iti bhāvaḥ, māṃ virahiṇamiti yāvat, antakasya yamasya, āmiṣaṃ bhogyavastu, ("āmiṣaṃ puṃnapuṃsakam bhogyavastuni sambhoge'pyutkoce palale'pi ca" iti medinī) | kurute māṃ hantuṃ yatate ityarthaḥ| (atra viśadā viśadā" "sārase sārase" "kurute kurute" iti padānāṃ prathamādvitīyatṛtīyapādāgatatvena dvitīyatṛtīyapādayormadhyatvena ca prathamadvitīyatṛtīya (madhyādya) pādagataṃ miśramavyavahitamādibhagāyamakam; taccaikavidhameveti bodhyam) | | 14| | 

ādyanteyatanena nirūpitaṃ bhedatrayaṃ krameṇa darśayan prathamaṃ prathamadvitīyaṭacaturtha (ādyanta) pādagatamudāharaṇena sphuṭayati, viṣamamiti| apoḍhamalayā parityaktamālinyayā, himātyayāditi bhāvaḥ, indukalayā candrakhaṇḍena saha, malayānilaḥ madanandanaḥ māṃ virahiṇaṃ na nandayatīti tathoktaḥ madaprītijananaḥ san ityarthaḥ, viṣamam asahyaṃ, viṣaṃ viṣarūpaṃ, madanaṃ kāmam, anveti anigacchati, uddīpayatītyarthaḥ, indukalāmalayānilayoḥ kāmoddīpakatvema virahipīḍakatvāditi bhāvaḥ| (atra "viṣama viṣama" "madana madana" "malayā malayā" iti padānāṃ prathamadvitīyacaturthapādadigatatayā prathamadvitīyayorādyatvena catirthasya pādasyāntatvena ca prathamadvitīyacaturtha (ādyanta) pādagataṃ miśramavyavahitamādiṅāgayamakam| "viṣamaṃ viṣamanveti" ityatrayāmyamānapadayorekasya sānusvāratvena asantoṣaścet "viṣamaṃ viṣamaṃ kṣṇauti" iti prathamapādaḥ paṭhanīyaḥ| dvitīyaviṣamamityasya vaṣeṇa mā upamā yasya iti vyutpattyā viṣasadṛśamityarthaḥ| kṣṇauti tīkṣṇaṃ karoti" iti kecit paṭhanti) | | 15| | 

prathamatṛtīyacaturtha (ādyantaḥ pādagataṃ tadeva udāharati, māninauti| he anaṅga! mā māṃ, te tava, niṣaṅgatvaṃ tūṇatvam, anavarataśarasampātena tadādhāratvamiti bhāvaḥ, ninīṣuḥ prāpayitumiccha, tathā hāriṇī hārālaṅkārabhūṣitā, ata eva hāriṇau manohāriṇī, māninī mānavatī iyaṃ nārī, tanutāṃ kṣīṇatāṃ, yataḥ gacchataḥ, prāpnuvataḥ ityarthaḥ, etatkṛtapratyākhyānajanitadacintayā iti bhāvaḥ, me mama, śarma sukhaṃ, tanutāṃ vidadhātu ityarthaḥ, yatheyaṃ māninī mānaṃ vihāya māmāśrayati tathā anugrahaḥ kāyrya iti bhāvaḥ| (atra "māninī māninī" "hāriṇī hāriṇī" "tanutāṃ tanutāṃ" iti padānāṃ prathamatṛtīyacaturthapādādigatatayā tṛtīyapādasyāpi āditvema caturthapādasyāntatvena ca prathamatṛtīyacaturthapādādigatatayā tṛtīyapādasyāpi āditvena caturthapādasyāntatvena ca prathamātṛtīyacaturtha (ādyanta) pādagataṃ miśramavyavahitamādibhāgayamakam) | | 16| | 

dvitīyatṛtīyacaturtha (ādyanta) pādagataṃ tadevodāharati, jayateti| he matpriye! mamapreyasi! asmān jayatā vaśīkurvatā, tava mukhena kaṃ jalam, alaṃkurvat śobhayat, tathā alimaddali alimanti bhramarayuktāni, dalāni patrāṇi asya santīti tathābhūtaṃ, kamalaṃ padmam, akathaṃ kathārahitaṃ, nirvivādaṃ yathā tathā ityarthaḥ, yadvā, akathamiti kamalaviśeṣaṇatvena yojanīyaṃ, kathārahitamityarthaḥ, kathanākṣamatvādacetanamiti bhāvaḥ, kathaṃ kiṃ, na jitam? api tu jitamevetyartaḥ, asmātaṃ cetanānāmapi jayinā te mukhena acetanāni tādṛśāni kamalāni jitanīti kā katheti bhāvaḥ| (atra "nakathaṃ nakathaṃ" "kamalaṃkamalaṃ" "dalimat dalimat" iti yamakaghaṭakapadānāṃ dvitīyatṛtīyacaturtha (ādyanta) pādādigatatvāt dvitīyatṛtīyapādayorāditvenābhidhānācca dvitīyatṛtīyacaturtha (ādyanta) pādagataṃ miśramavyavahitamādibhāgayamakam| evañca ādyantetyanenābhihitadāstrayaḥ bhedāḥ udāharaṇena darśitāḥ) | | 17| | 

tadeva sarvataḥ ityanena pratipāditaṃ catuṣpādagatamudāharati, ramaṇīti| pāṭalāvat tadākhyatarukusumavat, pāṭalaṃ śvetaraktam, aṃśukaṃ vasanaṃ yasyāḥ tādṛśī, tathā saurabhāspadaṃ saurabhasya saugandhyasya, āspadaṃ sthānaṃ śobhanagandhi ityartha, padminījātīyeti bhāvaḥ, ramaṇī kācitaṃ kāntā, aruṇībhūtā āraktībhūtā, saurī sūyryasambandhinī, bhā dīptiryasyāḥ tathābhūtā, vāruṇīva sureva, me mama ramaṇīyā ratipriyā, bhavatviti śeṣaḥ| (atra "ramaṇī ramaṇī" "pāṭalā pāṭalā" " vāruṇī vāruṇī"" saurabhā saurabhā " iti yamyamānapadānāṃ sarvapādagatatvāt prathamādvitīyatṛtīyacaturtha (sarva) pādagataṃ miṃśramavyavahitamāditamādibhāgayamakam| itthamādimadhyāntetyādinā nirūpatasyāvyapetādibhāgayamakasyaikādasaprakārāḥ udāharaṇai rdarśitāḥ) | | 18| | 

itīti| iti uktarūpeṇa, avyapetam avyavahitaṃ, pādādigataṃ yamakaṃ vikalpitaṃ saprabhedam udāhṛtam| tasya pūrvoktasya vyapetasya vyavahitasyaca yamakasya kecana vikalpāḥ bhedāḥ kramaprāptāḥ vaṇryante| | 19| | 

avyapetapādadibhāgayamakaṃ darśayitvā vyapetādibhāgayamakasya ekasmin pāde asambhavatvāt ādau prathamadvitīyapādagataṃ tat udāharati, madhuriti| madhurvasantaḥ, madhureṇa manohareṇa, sugandinā sahakārodramenaiva āmramukulodayenaiva udratāmramukulenavetyarthaḥ, eṇadṛśaṃ hariṇākṣīṇā, mānam īṣryākopaṃ praṇayakopaṃ vā, śabdaśeṣaṃ māneti śabdamātrāvaśiṣṭaṃ, kariṣyati, na tu arthato rakṣiṣyatīti bhāvaḥ| (atra "madhureṇa madhureṇa" iti prathamadvitīyapādādigatayamakaghaṭakapadayoḥ "dṛśāṃ mānam" iti padadvayena vyavahitatvāt prathamādvitīyapādagataṃ vyavahitaṃ miśramādibhāgayamakam) | | 20| | 

tadeva prathamatṛtīyapādagatamudāharati, kara iti| rāmāṇāṃ ramaṇīnām, atitāmraḥ atiraktaḥ, karaḥ pāṇiḥ, tantrotāḍanavibhramaṃ vīṇāvādanarūpaṃ savilāsaṃ kamrma, tathā kānte praṇayini, sāparādhe iti bhāvaḥ, seṣryam īṣryākopasahitaṃ yathā tathā, śravaṇotpalatāḍanañca karmotpalābhyāṃ prahāramapi, karoti| (atra "karoti karoti" iti prathamatṛtīyapādādigatatvāt vyavahitamiti vyapetayamakalakṣaṇasamanvayaḥ, tataśca pūrvavat prathamatṛtīyapādagataṃ vyavahitaṃ miśram ādibhāgayamakamiti jñeyam) | | 21| | 

tadeva prathamacaturthapādagatamudāharati, sakalāpeti| vātaiḥ narttitā cālitā ityarthaḥ, sakalā meghālī ghanaśreṇī, apaḥ jalāni, vimuñcati varṣati, anu paścāt, anantarameva ityarthaḥ, sakalāpollasanayā kalāpasya picchasya, ullasanena vistārīkaraṇena saha vatrtamānayā, kalāpinyā mayūyryā, nṛtyate| (varṣāvarṇanamidam| atra "sakalāpo sakalāpo" iti yamakapadayoḥ prathamacaturthapādādigatatvena vyavahitatayā prathamacaturthapādagataṃ miśraṃ vyavahitamādibhāgayamakam) | | 22| | 

tadeva dvitīyatṛtīyapādagatamudāharati, svayamiti| he kāmini| svayameva kāntānunayaṃ vinaiva, galan apagacchan, mānarūpaḥ, kaliḥ kalahaḥ yasmāt tādṛsaṃ, te tava, manaḥ iha varṣāsu ityarthaḥ, nīpasya kadambasya, kalikāṃ korakaṃ, dṛṣṭvā kāṃ daśām avasthāṃ, na spṛśet? api tu sarvāmeva smaradaśāṃ gamiṣyatītyarthaḥ, varṣāsu atikomalatvāt dhairyyavirahitaṃ te manaḥ virahe kathaṃ sāntvayiṣyasīti bhāvaḥ| (atra "kalikāmi kalikāmi" iti yamakapadayoḥ dvitīyatṛtīyapādādigatatvena vyavadhānāt dvitīyatṛtīyapādagataṃ miśraṃ vyavahitam ādibhāgayamakamiti bodhyam) | | 23| | 

tadeva dvitīyacaturthapādagatamudāharati, ārihyeti| ākrīḍe udyāne, yaḥ śailaḥ krīḍaāparvataḥ tasya, imāṃ candrakāntamaṇiviśeṣanirmitāṃ sthalīṃ sthānam, āruhya eṣa śikhābalaḥ mayūraḥ, calan itastato gacchan, ("lasat" iti pāṭhāntaram) cāruḥ manojñaḥ, yaḥ candrakaḥ mecakaḥ, tena antaḥ ramaṇīyaḥ, manoharaḥ san ityarthaḥ, yadvā, cārucandrakasya antaḥ prāntadeśo yasya tādṛsaḥ san, nṛtyati| (atraha "candrakānta candrakānta" iti yamakapadayoḥ dvitīyacaturthapādādigatatvena vyavadhānamiti dvitīyacaturthapādagataṃ miśraṃ vyavahitam ādibhāgayamakamaṃ jñeyam) | | 24| | 

tṛtīyacaturthapādagataṃ tadidaharati, uddhṛtyeti| he rājan! yā asau urvī pṛthivī, varāheṇa śūkaramūrttinā bhagavatā viṣṇunā, uddhṛtā ekārṇavādutthāpitā satī, varāheḥ śreṣṭhamāgasya vāsukeḥ, upari sthitā, purā iti śeṣaḥ, adya samprati, te tava, bhujena sā urvī rājakāt rājasamūhāt, uddhṛtya dhriyate| (atra "varāhe varāhe" iti yamakapadayoḥ tṛtīyacaturthapādādigatatvena vyavadhānatayā tṛtīyacaturithapādagataṃ miśraṃ vyavahitamādibhāgayamakam) | | 25| | 

prathamadvitīyatṛtīyapādagataṃ tadevodāharati, kareṇeti| he rājan! raṇeṣu dviṣatāṃ śatruṇām, antakareṇa vināśakena, te tava, kareṇa hastena, hatāḥ tāḍitāḥ, ata eva kṣaradraktāḥ raktasraviṇaḥ, kareṇavaḥ gajendrāḥ, ("kareṇurubhyāṃ strī nebhe" ityamaraḥ) sandhyāghanā iva sāyantanameghā iva, bhānti śobhante| (atra "kareṇa kareṇa kareṇa" iti yamakapadānāṃ prathamadvitīyatṛtīyapādādigatatvena vyapetatayā prathamadvitīyatṛtīyapādagataṃ miśraṃ vyavahitamādibhāgayamakam) | | 26| | 

tadeva prathamatṛtīyacaturthapādagatamudāharati, parāgeti| he rājan! vātaiḥ dhvastā nikṣiptā, utpāṭitā ityarthaḥ, agasya parvatasya, tarurājīva vṛkṣaśreṇīva, bhaṭaiḥ sainikaiḥ, tava iti śeṣaḥ, parā śatrupakṣīyā, camūḥ senā, dhvastā nāśitā, (akamrmakasyāpi dhvaṃsadhātorantarbhataṇyarthatvāt sakamrmakatvamatra vivakṣaṇīyam) ataśca parāgaiḥ rajobhi) , dhvaṃsāvaśiṣṭānāṃ palāyamaparāṇā śatrusainikānāṃ padotthāpitareṇubhirityarthaḥ, tataṃ vyaptam, ambaramākāśaṃ, kvāpi kutrāpi deśāntare, parāgatamiva palāyitamiva, dhūlibhirambaratalasyādṛśyatvāditi bhāvaḥ| (atra "parāgata parāgata parāgata" iti yamakaradānāṃ prathamatṛtīyacaturthapādādigatatvena vyavahitatayā prathamatṛtīyacaturthapādagataṃ miśraṃ vyavahitamādibhāgayamakam) | | 27| | 

tadeva dvitīyatṛtīyacaturthapādagatamudāharati, pātviti| navaghanadyutiḥ navīnameghaśyāmaḥ, dānavakuladhvaṃsī daityavaṃśaghātī, sadānaḥ samadaḥ, yaḥ varadantī śreṣṭhahastī, kuvalayāpīḍaākyaḥ iti bhāvaḥ, taṃ hatavān itaci tathoktaḥ sadānavaradantihā, saḥ prasiddhaḥ, bhagavān viṣṇuḥ vaḥ yuṣmān, sadā pātu| (atra"sadānava sadānava sadānava" iti yamakapadānāṃ dvitīyatṛtīyacaturthapādādigatatvena vyapetatayā dvitīyatṛtīyacaturthapādāgataṃ miśraṃ vyavahitamādibhāgayamakam) | | 28| | 

tadeva sarvapādagatamudāharati, kamitiṣa he priye! te tava, kaṃ śiraḥ, ("kaṃ vāriśrasoḥ" iti śāśvataḥ) aleḥ bhramarasya, samāḥ sadṛśāḥ, niviḍakṛṣṇā iti bhāvaḥ, keśā yatra tādṛsaṃ, tathā mukaṃ kamaleṣryākaraṃ kamalasya dveṣakaraṃ, padmavijayītyarthaḥ, atastvaṃ kamaleva la7mīriva, kaṃ janam, unmadiṣṇuṣu unmatteṣu madhye, alekhyam agaṇyaṃ, karoṣi? api tu sarvāneva unmādayasīti bhāvaḥ| ( atra "kamale" iti yamakaghaṭakapadasya sarvapādādisthatvāt vyavahitatvācca sarvapādagataṃ miśraṃ vyavahitamādibhāgayamakam) | | 29| | 

evamavyapetavyapetayorbhedamudāhṛtya samprati vyapetasyava sajātīyavijātīyatvena prakārabhedaṃ darśayituṃ prathamaṃ prathamadvitīyayorekavidhaṃ tṛtīyacaturthayoścānyavidhaṃ tadidāharati, mudāti| udārāḥ utkṛṣṭāḥ, maṇayaḥ ratnāni, bhūṣaṇam; udārāṇi bahumūlyāni, maṇibhūṣaṇāni ratnābharaṇāni iti vā, yāsāṃ tāḥ, madena madyapānajena saubhāgyādijanyavikāraviśeṣeṇa vā, bhramantyaḥ ghūrṇantyaḥ, dṛśo nayanāni yāsāṃ tathoktaḥ, tathā adabhraṃ viśālaṃ , jaghanaṃ yāsāṃ tathābhūtāḥ, pramadāḥ iti śeṣaḥ, ("jaghanāḥ" ityatra "manmathā" "manasaḥ" iti vā pāṭhakalpānā saṅgatā, prathamadvitīyapādayoḥ caturakṣaraghaṭitayamakavattayā atrāpi tadaucityāt) mudā harṣeṇa, ramaṇaṃ priyam, anvītam anugatam, adhīnamītyarthaḥ, ramaṇaṃ mudā anvītaṃ harṣayuktamiti vā, katrtuṃ kṣamāḥ śaktāḥ| (atra prathamadvitīyapādādau "mudārama mudārama" iti tṛtīyacaturthapādādau ca "madabhra madabhra" iti yamakaghaṭakapadānāṃ vijātīyatvena vyavahitatvena ca nirdeśāt prathamadvitīyayostṛtīyacaturthayośca vijātīyaṃ miśraṃ vyavahitamādibhāgayamakam) | | 30| | 

prāgaktaprakārameva prathamatṛtīyayorekavidhaṃ dvitīyacaturthayoścānyavidhamudāharati, uditairiti| he dūti! anyapuṣṭānāṃ kīkilānām, uditaiḥ utthitaiḥ, ārutaiḥ ākūjitaiḥ, samantāt udrajhaṅkārairityarthaḥ, tathā te tava, uditaiḥ kathitairapi, priyākleśasūcakairvacanairapītyarthaḥ, tathā dakṣiṇaiḥ mārutaiḥ malayānilairapi, me mama, manaḥ hataṃ vyathitamityarthaḥ| (atra prathamatṛtīyapādādau "uditai uditai" iti dvitīyacaturthapādādau ca "mārutai mārutai" iti yamyamānapadānāṃ vijātīyatvāt pādādibāgasthātvāt vyavahitatvāñca prathamatṛtīyayordvitīyacaturthayośca vijātīyaṃ miśraṃvyavahitamādibhāgayamakam) | | 31| | 

prāguktameva prathamacaturthapādayorekavidhaṃ dvitīyatṛtīyayoścānyavidhamudāharati, sureti| tanu kṣīṇaṃ, madhyaṃ kaṭideśaḥ yāsāṃ tāḥ, kṣaradbhiḥ sāttvikabhāvodayena galadbhiḥ, svedaiḥ gharmodavindubhiḥ, surājitāḥ suśobhitāḥ, mukhendavaḥ mukhacandrā yāsāṃ tādṛśyaḥ, striyaḥ kāminyaḥ, surayā madyapānena, jitāparābhūtāḥ, apagatā iti yāvat, hrorlajjā yāsāṃ tathābhūtāḥ satyaḥ, yūnāṃ taruṇānāṃ, tanuṃ śarīram, adhyāsatace adhitiṣṭhati| (atra prathamacaturthapādādau "surājita surājita" iti dvitīyatṛtīyapādādau ca "tanumadhyā tanumadhyā" iti yamakaghaṭakakapādānāṃ vijātīyatvāt vyavahitatvācca prathamacaturthayordvitīyatṛtīyayośca vijātīyaṃ miśraṃ vyavahitamādibhāgayamakam) | | 32| | 

prāk avyapetaṃ vyapetañca yamakaṃ pṛthak udāhṛtya samprati tayorekatramiśraṇāt aparān bhedān darśayannāha, itīti| iti uktaprakāreṇama, eṣaḥ vyapetānāṃ vyavahitānāṃ, yamakānāṃ prabhedo| ñapi (apikāreṇa prāk avyapetayamakaprabhedaḥ darśita iti samuccīyate) darśitaḥ udāhṛtaḥ; avyapetaḥ avyavahitaḥ vyapetaḥ vyavahitaśca, ātmā svarūpaṃ yasya tādṛsaḥ, ubhayamiśra ityarthaḥ, vikalpaḥ prabhedaḥ api, asti| tat yatheti udāharaṇārtham| | 33| | 

tatra prathamadvitīyapāde ekavidaṃ tṛtīyacaturthapāde cānyavidhamavyapetavyapetātmakamādibhāgayamakamudāharati, seti| sā ālī mama sakhī, sālambā ālambena samyak lambamānatvena saha vatrtamānāḥ, lambamānā ityarthaḥ, yāḥ kalikāḥ korakāḥ, tāḥ salati calati, āndolate ityarthaḥ, yasya tādṛśaṃ, yadvā, tābhiḥ salati śobhate iti tathoktaṃ, sālaṃ vṛkṣamātraṃ sarjavṛkṣaṃ vā, ("sālaḥ pādapamātre'pi prākāre sarjapādape" iti medīnī) vīkṣituṃ draṣṭuṃ, na alaṃ na śaktā, kalikāyāḥ uddīpakatvāditi bhāvaḥ, tathā ālīnavakulān ālīnāḥ samyaktayā āśritā ityarthaḥ, vakulāḥ vakulavṛkṣā yaistathāvidhān vakulasaktān ityarthaḥ, (ālīyante atra iti adhikareṇa ktapratyayaḥ) alīn bhramarān, apīti śeṣaḥ, na, vīkṣitum alamiti pūrvato yojanīyam; tathā alīkaṃ mithyavākyaṃ, vidyate yāsāṃ tādṛśīḥ alīkinīrapi mithyāvādinīrapi, priyaste āgamiṣyatyacireṇetyādinā mithyāvacanena sāntvayantīrapi itibāvaḥ, sakhīriti śeṣaḥ, na, vīkṣitum alam iti atrāpi pūraṇīyam| (videśasya nāyakaṃ prati nāyikāpreritāyā dūtyā uktiriyam| atra prathamadvitīyapādādau "sālaṃ sālaṃ sālaṃ sālaṃ" tṛtīyaṭacaturthapādādau ca "nālī nālī nālī nālī" iti yamyamānapadānām avyavadhānatvenāvyapetatvaṃ, tathā madhye "vakalikā" "navakulā" ityakṣarāṇāṃ vyavadhānatyena vyapetatvacceti ubhayātmakaṃ, pādānāmādigatatvāccādibhāgasaṃkhyakaṃ, pūrvādrdhāpekṣayā parādrdhasya vibhidyatayā ca vijātīyatvamiti prathamadvitīyapādagataṃ tṛtīyacaturthapādagataśca ubhayātmakaṃ vijātīyamiśrādibhāgayamakamiti bodhyam) | | 34| | 

tadeva prathamacaturthapādayorekavidhaṃ dvitīyatṛtīyayoścānyavidhamudāharati, kālamiti| kā, virahiṇī ramaṇīti śeṣaḥ, anālakṣya meghācchannatvādadṛśyāḥ, tārā mahatvaḥ, samujjvalāityarthaḥ, tārakā na7trāpi yatra tathoktaṃ, tathā tāratayā atyuñcatvena, aramyāṇi śrotravirasāni, rasitānimeghagarjitāni yasmin tādṛśaṃ, tathā kālāḥ kṛṣṇavarṇāḥ, mahantaḥ pragāḍhāḥ, ghanā meghā yasmin tathābhūtaṃ, kālaṃ virahiṇāṃ prāṇanāśakatvāt yamasvarūpaṃ, kālaṃ varṣākālam ikṣituṃ draṣṭum, alaṃ samarthā? na kā'pītyarthaḥ| (atra prathamacaturthapādādau "kālaṃ kālaṃ kālaṃ kālam"iti dvitīyatṛtīyapādādau ca "tāratāra tāratāra" iti yamyamānapadānāṃ pūrvavadavyavahitavyavahitatvena ubhayātmakaṃ vijātīyaṃ miśramādibhāgayamakam| "kālaṃ kālamānālakṣya"ityatra "kālaṃ kālaṃkṛtaṃ ghanaiḥ" iti pāṭhaṃ kecit samyaṅyanyante, kālaṃ kālam iti prathamapāde dvayorapi sānusvāratvena caturthapāde'pi tadaucityāt| ghanaiḥ kālaṃkṛtaṃ kutsitālaṅkāravantaṃ kṛtamiti tatrārthaḥ) | | 35| | 

tadeva pādacatuṣyagatamudāharati, yāmeti| yāmatrayasya praharatritayasya, adhīnaḥ āyattaḥ, āyāmo daidhryaṃ yasyāḥ tādṛśyā sudīrghapraharatrayavyāpinyā, ("triyāmāṃ rājanīṃ prāhustyaktavādyantacatuṣṭayam" iti vacanāt niśāyāḥ triyāmātmakatvaṃ bodhyam) niśā rajanyā hetunā, maraṇaṃ yāma mṛtyuṃ prāpnuma, vayamiti śeṣaḥ, (prarthanāyāṃ loṭ) niśāyāḥ sātiśayavirahoddīpakatvāditi bhāvaḥ| nanu kimityevamamaṅgalamāśāsyate ityāha, yāmiti| dhiyā manasā, yāṃ kāntām, ayāma agacchāma, yasyāścintayā kālamayāpayāma ityarthaḥ, yāṃ jīvitasavrvasvābhimānamocaramakumrma ityartho vā, sā āyāmayā atidīrghatayā, kalpakoṭiśatavat dīrghatarayā anayā rajanyā ityartaḥ, asvattaryā asūnāṃ prāṇānām, arttiḥ pīḍaā tayā dvārabhūtayā, mathitaiva prāyeṇa vināśitaiva, ūviścatīti śeṣaḥ ; yayā niśayā madviyogena sukumāratarāyāḥ kāntāyāḥ prāṇā avaśyameva yāsyanti, ato mama maraṇameva śreya iti bhāvaḥ| yadvā, maraṇaṃ yāma iti (sambāvanāyāṃ loṭ) vayaṃ mariṣyāma iti sambālayāma ityarthaḥ ; astu asmākaṃ mṛtyiḥ, na tatra kimapi duḥkhaṃ, kintu dhiyā yām ayāma (yādhātorlaṅi uttamapuruṣavabahuvacanasya rūpamidam) asvattryāyā asvartiṃ madviraheṇa prāṇavyathām, āyāti prāpnotiti tādṛśī, (āṅpūrvakāt yādhātoḥ kvipi rūpam) sā mayaiva mathitā nāsitā, videśāgatasya mamaiva praṇayino virahe dīnā sā mriyate ityeva me mahadduḥkhamitibhāvaḥ| (nisāyāṃ pradīpoktiriyam| atra "yāma yāma" iti yamakapadasya pādacatuṣṭayasyādau sthitatvena avyavahitavyavahitatvena ca sarvapādagataṃ miśramubhayātmakamādibhāgayamakam) | | 36| | 

ādibhāgayamakasya bhedānabhidhāya tat upasaṃharati, itīti| iti uktarūpasya, pādādisthayamakānāṃ vikalpasya bhedasya, īdṛśī evamprakārā, gatiḥ niyamaḥ ; itarāṇi pādamadhyāntabhāgagatāni avyapetavyapetatanmiśritāni ca, yamakāni api, evameva prāguktaprākāreṇaiva, ekadvitryādirūpeṇa tathā ādimadhyāntetyādirūpeṇaiva ca ityarthaḥ vikalpyāni bhedyāni| | 37| | 

nikhilayamakabhedā mayaiva granthakṛtā kathaṃ na darśitāstatrāha, neti| prapañcabhayāt vistārabhayāt, kātsrneyana sākalyena, bhedāḥ ākhyātuṃ na īhitāḥ na ceṣṭitāḥ, ye tu bhedāḥ, duṣkaratvena abhimatāḥ svīkṛtāḥ, anyeṣāmitiśeṣaḥ, atra te kecana, vaṇryante udāhriyante, kecana eva bhedā mayā darśayiṣyante, na tu kvatsnāḥ, apare tu prāgaktādibāgayamakarītyā svayameva veditavyā iti bhāvaḥ| | 38| | 

tatra sarvapādagatamavyapetātmakaṃ madhyayamakamudāharati, sthirāyate iti| sthirā cirasthāyinī, āyatiruttarakālo yasya tatsambuddau, ("uttaraḥ kāla āyatiḥ" ityamaraḥ) he sthirāyate! akṣavyasukhabhājana! , bhavān yatendriyaḥ jutendriyaḥ, ataḥ yateḥ saṃyamāt, samādherityartha, na hīyate cyuto na bhavati, indriyasaṃyamābhāve hi samādheḥ sthiratvaṃ na sambhavatīti bhāvaḥ, (yaduktaṃ, "balavānindriyāgrāmo vidvāṃsamapi karṣati" iti) te tava, amāyatā'pi amāyitvamapi, māyārāhityamapītyarthaḥ, iyate etatparimāṇāya, paricchettumaśakyāya ityarthaḥ, tathā kṣayaṃ nāśam, ayate agacchate, akṣayāya ityartaḥ, sukhāya parāndāya, abhūt| (atra sarveṣu pādeṣu "yate yate" iti padaṃ pādamadhye āvṛttaṃ, tacca ekasmin pāde punarāvatrtane avyapetaṃ pādāntarāpekṣayā ca vyapetamiti avyapetavyapetami8sarvapādagatamadhyabhāgayamakam) | | 39| | 

pratipādaṃ kevalavyapetamadhyamadhyayamakamudāharati, sabhāsviti| he rājan! mahīsurāṇāṃ bhūdevānāṃ, brāhmaṇānāmityarthaḥ, asurāhataiḥ surāpānena ahataiḥ anāśitaiḥ, madirāpānaparāṅamukhatayā pavitrairiti yāvat, vasunā tejasā bhavahattadhanavalābhena vā, rājitaiḥ śobhitaiḥ praphullairvā, mukaiḥ sabhāsu stutāḥ kīrttitāḥ, bhāsurāḥ samujjvalāḥ, tathā rāgātmasu anurāgaparāsu, prajāsu janeṣu, rāśitāṃ bahulībhāvaṃ, gatāḥ, prabhūtasamādaraṃ prāptā ityarthaḥ, te tava, guṇāsurān devān, na yānti na prāpnuvanti, iti na, api tu yāntyevetyarthaḥ, surā eva etādṛgguṇavantaḥsantīti bhāvaḥ| (atra "surā" iti padaṃ pratipādameva vyavahitatayā pādamadhyasthitatvācca miśraṃ vyapetañca madhyamadhyabāgayamakam) | | 40| | 

pratipādaṃ vyapetamadhyamadhyayamakamudāhṛtya samprati sarvapādagataṃ tādṛśamevāntayamakamudāharati, taveti| he asaccarita| dhūtrta! , he pramatta! anavahita! , iha asmin samaye, sampratītyarthaḥ yā tava priyā praṇayāni, janasamāje adhunā yā tvayā premāspadatvena kīttryamānā ityarthaḥ, tayā, eveti śeṣaḥ, ratotsavena yaḥ ānandaviśeṣaḥ, tena mattayā āsaktayā satyā ityarthaḥ, aṃśumat samujjvalaṃ, vibhūṣaṇam ābharaṇaṃ, dhāyryaṃ, tathā ca vibhūṣaṇadhāraṇasya ratotsavānandavidhāyakatāprayojanaṃ, na tu tvayi anyāsakte mama sakhyāḥ tat sambhavati, ataḥ kimiti tayā tat dhāyryam? tavādhunikapriyayaiva tat dhatrtuṃ yujyate iti bhāvaḥ| nanu śobhārthamapi sakhyā te vibhūṣaṇaṃ dhriyatāṃ tatra āha, prayojanamitiṣa hi tathā hi, kāntimattayā svābhāvikasaundayryavattayā hetunā, matsakhyā iti śeṣaḥ, prayojanam āvaśyakaṃ, vibhūṣaṇadhāraṇasyeti śeṣaḥ, nāsti na vidyate evetyarthaḥ, naisargikī vilakṣaṇā kāntirevāsyāḥ śobhāṃ janayati, kiṃ punaralaṅkārāntaradhāraṇeneti bhāvaḥ| (anyāsaktaṃ śaṭhanāyakamalaṅkāradhāraṇe kathamasyā virāga iti pṛcchantaṃ prati sakhyā uktiriyam| "asaccarita pramatta" ityatra " sañcaritāpramatta" iti kvacit pāṭhaḥ| atra "mattayā" iti padasya pratipādāntagatatvāt vyavahitātvācca sarvapādagataṃ miśraṃ vyapetamantabhāgayamakam) | | 41| | 

pādacatuṣṭayagatamavyapetavyapetamantabhāgayamakamudāharati, bhavādṛsā iti| he nātha! prabho! , bhavādṛśāḥ tvādṛśā janāḥ, nateḥ natabhāvasya, rasam āsvādaṃ, na jānate na vidanti, yataḥ sannataṃ samyak natibhāvaśca, inatā prabhutā ca sannatenate natiḥ prabhutā cetyarthaḥ, viruddhe viparīte, khalu niścaye, naikādhāre vatrtate iti bhāvaḥ, ye janāḥ dīnāḥ daridrāḥ, te eva kevalaṃ, tena prasiddhena, dainyarasena dainyarūpeṇa viṣeṇa hetunā, (raso gandharase jale| śrṛṅgāradau viṣe vīyrye tiktādau dravarānayoḥ| dehadhātuprabhede ca pāradāsvādayoḥ pumān| | " iti medinī) natena śirasā te tava, (taveti karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī) alam atyarthaṃ, caranti atiśayena tvāṃ sevante ityarthaḥ| (atra sarveṣu pādeṣu antagataṃ "nate nate" iti padaṃ punarāvṛttaṃ, tacca na pratipādaṃ vyavahitaṃ kintu pādāntare vyavahitamiti miśramubhayātmakam antabāgayamakam) | | 42| | 

pratipādagatavyapetamadhyāntayamakamudāharati, līleti| sā nāyikā, śucinā nirmalena, līlāsmicena savilāseṣaddhasitena, mṛdunā komalena, uditena vacanena, laghunā iṣṭena prārthitenetyartaḥ, laghunā alpena iti vā, asampūrṇenetyarthaḥ, ("traṣviṣṭe'lpe laghuḥ" ityamaraḥ) vyalokitena apāṅgadarśaṃnenetyarthaḥ, guruṇā nitambādiṅaramanthareṇa, gatena gamanena, vyājṛmbhitena viśeṣato jṛmbhayā, (jṛmbhāyāśca anurāgavyañjakatvamuktaṃ yathā, "jṛmbhate sphoṭayatyaṅgaṃ bālamāsilaṣya cumbati " iti) tathā darśitena jaghanena ca kenacit vyājena jaghānapradarśanenetyarthaḥ, hanti vyathayati, māmiti śeṣaḥ, tena ca hetunā, mama jīvitena jīvitena, galitaṃ gataṃ vinaṣṭaprāyamivetyarthaḥ| (bhāve ktapratyayaḥ| atra "tena" iti padaṃ pratipādaṃ madhyeṣu anteṣu ca punaḥ punarāvṛttaṃ, tacca vyavahitamiti miśraṃ vyavahitaṃ madhyāntayamakam) | | 43| | 

pratipādagatamavyapetavyapetamadhyāntayamakamudāharati, śrīmāniti| (atraivaṃ padavibhāgaḥ, śrīmān, amān, amaravatrmasamānamānam, ātmānam, ānatajagatprathamānamānam, bhūmānam, ānamata, yaḥ, sthitimān, amānanāmānam, ānamatam, apratimānamānam iti) he bhaktaḥ! amaravatrma ākāśaṃ, tasya samānaṃ mānaṃ parimāṇaṃ yasya tathoktam ākāśavat sarvavyāpinamityarthaḥ, ānateṣu praḍhiddhaṭhṭhaḍavabhūteṣu, upāsanārthaṃ namrībhūteṣu vā, jagastu prathamānaḥ vistāraṃ gataḥ, mānaḥ sammānaḥ, pūjanamityarthaḥ, yasya tādṛsaṃ, sarvajagatpūjyamānamityarthaḥ, bhūmānaṃ mahāntam, amānam aparimitam, asaṅkhyamityarthaḥ, nāma yasya tathābhūtaṃ, tathā ā īṣat, ananti prāṇanti, śvasantītyarthaḥ, ye te ānāḥ prāṇāyāmaparāyaṇā yogina ityarthaḥ, taiḥ mataṃ pūjitaṃ yogena jñātamityartho vā, ānamataṃ yogipūjitaṃ yogigamyaṃ vā ityarthaḥ, tathā apratimānaḥ asadṛśaḥ, māno yasya tādṛśaṃ sarvābhimatamitrthaḥ, yadvā, apratimānam anupamaṃ, mānaṃ jñānaṃ yasya taṃ kevalaṃ manaḥ saṃyamena jñāyamānamityarthaḥ, evambhūtam ātmānaṃ paramātmānaṃ brahma ityarthaḥ, ānamata namastukuta, yūyamiti śeṣaḥ| nanvevambhūtaseya cārvākādibhiranaṅgīkṛtasyātmano'ṅgīkāre kiṃ pramāmityata āha, yaitaci| yaḥ ātmā, śrīmān svayaṃ prākāśamānaḥ, tatā amān aparimitaḥ, vibhūtvāt kvacidapi na parimātrīci bhāvaḥ, tathā sthitimān saccidānandarūpatayā satatasthāyī, jagatamutpattisthitilayakāritvena nitya ityarthaḥ| (atra sarveṣāmeva pādānāṃ madhyeṣvanteṣu ca "mānamāna" iti padasya avyavadhānena punaḥ punarāvatrtanāt avyapetaṃ, tathā "maravatrmasa" ityādivarṇānāṃ vyavadhānatayāvyapetañca madhyāntayamakam| ekavarṇāt paraṃ māna ityasyāvṛtteḥ kathaṃ madhyayamakatvamityapi na śaṅkanīyam, ādyāntavarṇayorantarālasyaiva madhyatvenābhidhānāt) | | 44| |
