pratipādagataṃ vyapetādimadhyamakamudāharati, sārayantamiti| urusārāṇi svarṇabhūṣaṇāni dharatīti tathoktā, ("urusāraṃ suvarṇaṃ syāt" iti vyāṅiḥ) tathā anukṛtaḥ sārasaḥ pakṣiviśeṣaḥ, sārasakūjitamiti bhāvaḥ, yayā sā, sārasakūjitasaṃvādinītyarthaḥ, jhr"jātikālasukhādibhyaḥ parā niṣṭhā vācyā" iti niṣṭhāntasya paranipātaḥṭa sārasā saśabdā, kāñcī mekhala yasyāsthābhṛtā, sā ramaṇī, sārayantam āśliṣyantaṃ ("sṛāstaraṇe" ityasya caurādikasya rūpam) sārayantam ātmānamabhisārayantimiti vā, sārabhūtaṃ jagatsu utkarṣatavena gaṇitaṃ, taṃ jīvitasarvasvatāmāpannaṃ patimityarthaḥ, urasā vakṣaḥ sthalena, ramayantī prīṇayantī, pratyāliṅganena sukhayantī satīti yāvat, rasāyanaṃ jarāvyādhiharaṃ bheṣajamapi, jarāvyādhyākrāntupratirodhakatvena amṛtamapīti bhāvaḥ, asāraṃ tuccham, avaiti jānāti, priyāliṅganasukhādamṛtamapi tucchamavagacchatītyarthaḥ| (atra pratyekapādeṣu ādimadhyayoḥ "sārasāra" iti yamakaghaṭakībhūtapadasya vyavadhānena punaḥ punarāvṛtteḥ miśraṃ vyavahitamādimadhyayamakam) | | 45| | 

prathamatṛtīyapādayoravyapetavyapetamādyantayamakaṃ dvitīyacaturthayostādṛsamādimadhyayamakañca ekatra darśayati, nayetiṣa (atra padavicchedaprakāra evaṃ draṣṭavyaḥ, nayānayālocanayā, anayā, anayān; ayānayān ayān, tān, janaya, anayāśritān iti) he anayāyate! na ayate gacchatīti anayā apāyarahitā, āyatiḥ uttarakālaḥ yasya tatsambodhanam; he askhalitaśāsana prabho! anayā evaṃvidhayā, paridṛsyamānayetyartaḥ, anayān nayanahitān, ata eva ayasya śubhāvahavidheḥ, ("ayaḥ śubhāvahovidhiḥ" ityamaraḥ) ānaye ānayena, anuṣṭhāne ityarthaḥ, andhān ātmahitamapaśyata ityarthaḥ, janān iti śeṣaḥ, vinaya vinītān kuru, yathā te nītimārgānusāriṇaḥ bhaveyuḥ tathā vidhehi ityarthaḥ, kiñca jinayānaṃ jainamārgaṃ, yāntīti tathoktān bauddhamatāvalambinaḥ, ata eva ayānam amārgaṃ, viruddhapanthānamityarthaḥ, yāntīti tādṛśān apathagāminaḥ, ata eva anayāśritān durnayānusārimaḥ, svasyāśubhamavalambamānānityarthaḥ, yān janān, tvaṃ na ayāsauḥ na gatavānasi, yaiḥ saṅgaṃ tvaṃ na kṛtavānasi ityarakthaḥ, tān bauddhamatāvalambino janān, aṃ viṣṇuṃ yāntīti tathābhūtān vaiṣṇavadharmāvalambinaḥ, janaya kuru| (atra prathamādvitīyapadayoḥ "nayānayā" ityasya ādāvante cāvyavadhānena punarāvarttanāt tṛtīyacaturthapādayoścādau madhye cāvyavadhānenāvṛtteḥ krameṇa avyapetam ādyantayamakam ādimadhyayamakañca ekatraiva darśitamiti dik) | | 46| | 

vyapetaṃ sarvapādagatamādyantayamakamudāharati, raveṇeti| bhaumaḥ bhūmisutaḥ narakāsuraḥ iti yāvat, dhvajavartinaḥ śrīkṛṣṇa rathadhvajasthitasya, vīrasya vīyryaśālinaḥ, veḥ pakṣiṇaḥ, garuḍasya ityarthaḥ, raveṇa nādena, atulam anupamam, astrāṇāṃ gauravaṃ gurutvaṃ bahutvaṃ vā yasmina tādṛśe, bhairave bhayaṅkāre, saṃyati saṅgrāme, aveji udvejitaḥ trāsita ityarthaḥ, tathā hi, raveḥ sūyryasyeva, ugrasya tejorāśeḥ ityarthaḥ, asya hareḥ śrīkṛṣṇasya siṃhasya ca, puraḥ agre, ripuṃ śatruṃ, narakakamiti yāvat, avermeṣasya, tulyaṃ sadṛśam, aveta jānīta, yūyamiti śeṣaḥ| (śrīkṛṣṇasya narakāsureṇa yuddhavarṇanamidam| atra caturṇāmeva pādānāmādāvante ca"rave" iti padasya vyavadhānatayā punaḥpunarāvatrtanāt miśraṃ vyavahitamādyantayamakam) | | 47| | 

avyapetavyapetaṃ catuṣpādagatamādyantayamakamudāharati, mayeti| (atrāyaṃ padavibhāgaḥ, mayāmayālambyakalāmayāmayām; ayām, amayā; amaya, āmaya, amūṃ, karuṇāmaya, āmayā itai) he amaya! minoti kṣipati, tyajati dūramiti yāvat, iti mayaḥ sa na bhavatītyamayaḥ sarvāsvavasthāsu māmanuvatrtamānaḥ, tatsambodhanaṃ, ("ḍu miñ kṣepaṇe" ityasya katrtari ḍa pratyayaḥ) yadvā, he amaya! nāsti māyā yasya tatsambuddhau, he akapaṭa! ityarthaḥ, (ākāralopo mahākaviprayogādaviruddhaḥ) ata eva he karuṇāmaya! dayāmaya! sakhe! ti śeṣaḥ, (etena sambodhanapadadvayena svaprārthanāyām avaśyamevānukūlyaṃ kariṣyatīti sūcutam) āmaṃ rogaṃ, kāmapīḍaāmiti yāvat, yāti prāpnotīti āmayāḥ tena āmayā kāmatrtena ityarthaḥ, jhr"āto dhātoḥ" itiākāralopaḥṭa mayā saha, mayaḥ apacayaḥ, kalākṣayā iti yāvat, ("maya gatau" ityasya rūpam) amayaḥ upacayaḥ kalāvṛddhiśīlatvātta iti bhāvaḥ, ālambya āśrayaṇīyaḥ, yaḥ kalāmayaḥ kalātmakaścandraḥ, tasmāt āmayaḥ rogaḥ kāmapīḍaā ityarthaḥ, yasyāḥ tādṛśīṃ, candrasya kāmoddīpakatvāditi bhāvaḥ, amaṃ ramaṇīyam, yataḥ aham ayātavyaḥ aprāptavyaḥ, virāmaḥ avasānaṃ yeṣāṃ tādṛśāḥ, yāmāḥ praharā yasyāstādṛśyā dīrghayāmayā ityarthaḥ, ata eva nāsti mā parimāṇaṃ yasyāstathābhūtayā amayā sudīrghayā ityarthaḥ ata eva amayā nāsti mā śobhā yasyāḥ tathoktayā atikutsitayā ityarthaḥ, virahaṇāmiti bhāvaḥ, niśayā rajanyā hetunā, mayaḥ gamanaṃ, prāptirityarthaḥ, ("sarve gatyarthāḥ prakāptyarthāḥ"iti niyamāt) tadvaiparītyam amayaḥ tena amayena aprāptyā, tasyā iti bhāvaḥ, artiṃta pīḍaāṃ, tadaprāptyā sātiśayāṃ kāmapīḍaāmitii yāvat, ayāṃ prāpnavam| (" yā gatau" ityasya rūpam| kasyacit virahiṇaḥ sakhāyaṃ pratyuktiriyam| ("atra pratyekapādānāmādāvante ca "mayā mayā" iti padasya punaḥ punaḥ sānnidhyaprayuktatayā avyavahitaṃ tathā "lambyakalā" ityādivarṇāmāṃ madhyāsthatayā vyavahitañca miśramādyantayamakam) | | 48| | 

vyapetādimadhyāntayamakamudāharati, matāmitiṣa (padavibhāgaścaiṣaḥ, matāṃ, dhunānā, āramatām, akāmatām; atāpalabdhā, agrimatānulomatā ; matau, ayatī, uttamatāvilomatacām; atāmyataḥ, te, samatā, na, vāmatā iti) he sadho! iti sambodhanapadam atra adhyāhatrtavyam; atāmyataḥ kadā'pi akhidyataḥ, sadā prasannamanasa ityarthaḥ, te tava, matau citte, āramatām ātmanyeva ramamāṇānāṃ, viṣayavyāvṛttānāṃ yogināmityarthaḥ, matāṃ sammatāṃ, yogibhirādṛtāmityarthaḥ, akāmatāṃ niḥspṛhatām, apīti śeṣaḥ, dhunānā kampayantī, utkarṣavattayā tāmapi tiraskurvatītyarthaḥ, tathā atāpena akleśena, labdhā prāptā, svābhāvirakītyarthaḥ, tathā agrimatāyāḥ śreṣṭhatāyāḥ, anulomatā ānukūlyaṃ yayā tādṛśī śreṣṭhatvasampādinītyarthaḥ, tathā uttamatāyā guṇavattāyāḥ, vilomatāṃ pratīkūlatām, ayatī agacchantī guṇavattvasamānādhikaraṇā iti bhāvaḥ, samatā apakṣapātitvaṃ, sarvatra masadarśitvamityarthaḥ, vidyate iti śeṣaḥ, vāmatā pratikūlatā tu, na, vidyate iti śeṣaḥ| (kasyacit sadhorguṇakītrtanamidam| atra pratipādānāmādau madhye ante ca "matā" iti padasya punaḥ punavryavahitatvenāvṛtteḥ vyavahitamādimadhyāntayamakam) | | 49| | 

samprati avyapetavyapetātmakamādimadhyāntayamakamudāharati, kāleti| (padacchedastvayam; prathamapāde ekaṃ padam; kālakālaghanakālakālapanakāla, kāla; kālakālasitakālakā, lalanikā, alakālakālakā, alagatu, kālakāla, kālikāla, kāla iti) he kālakālagalakālakālamukhakālakāla! kālasya saṃhārakasya, śivasya ityarthaḥ, ("kalanāt sarvabhūtānāṃ sa kālaḥ parikīrttitaḥ" iti vacanāt) yaḥ kālagalaḥ nīlavarṇaḥ kaṇṭhaḥ, tathā kālaḥ yamaḥ, tathā kālamukhaḥ vānaraviśeṣaḥ te (eteṣāṃ dvandvaḥ) kālakālagalakālakāmukhāḥ, teṣāṃ kālakaṃ kṛṣṇatvam, ālāti ādatte iti tatsambuddhau, he śivakaṇṭhādivat kṛṣṇavarṇa! , tathā he kālakālaghanakālakālakālapanakāla! kaṃ jalam, ālāti gṛḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍaṇāti, bibhatrtītyarthaḥ, iti kālaḥ sajalaḥ, ata eva kālaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇaḥ, yo ghanaḥ meghaḥ, tasya kāle samaye, varṣāsvityarthaḥ, kāyantaci śabdāyante iti kālakālaghanakālakāḥ mayūrā ityarthaḥ, ("kai śabde"ityasya kvipi rūpam) teṣām ālapanāni mukhāni, kālakālaghanakālakālapanāni, tadvat kalate śabdayate iti tatsambuddhau, he mayūra iva ṣaḍjasvareṇa ālapanaśīla! ityarthaḥ, ("kālakālapanakālakālaghanakāla! "iti pāṭhe tu kāle varṣāsu, kāyantīti kālakāḥ mayarāḥ, teṣām ālapanaṃ ninādaṃ, kālayati prerayati, prakaṭayatītyarthaḥ tādṛśaḥ, tathā kālaḥ pūrvoktavyutpattyā jalapūrṇa ityarthaḥ, yo ghanaḥ meghaḥ, tadvat kāla! śyāmala! sajalajaladaśyāma ityarthaḥ) punaśca he kālakāla! kālasya yamasya, kālaḥ , saṃhārakaḥ tatsambuddhau, yamabhayanivatrtaka! ityarthaḥ yadvā, kālena samayātkena ātmanā, kālayati prerayati, bhūtānīti śeṣaḥ, yastatsambuddhau, ("kālasvarūpaṃma rūpantat viṣṇormaitreya! vatrtate"iti smṛteḥ viṣṇoḥ kālasvarūpatvamavagantavyam) kiñca, he kalikālakāla! kalikālasya, kaliyugasya, kālaḥ mṛtyusvarūpaḥ, kalkirūpeṇa daṇḍayitā ityarthaḥ, tatsambuddhau, he kāla! he kṛṣṇa! kālakālasitakālakā kālakena kṛṣṇavarṇatayā, ālasitaṃ śobhitaṃ, kaṃ śiraḥ yaiḥ, tādṛśāḥ alakāḥ cūrṇakuntalāḥ yasyāstādṛśī, ("kaṃ jalam, ālātīti kālaṃ saraḥ, tatraka kāyantīti kālakāḥ haṃsāḥ, tāṣāmiva ālasitaṃ līlāgamanaṃ yayoḥ tādṛśau kālakau pādau yasyāḥ sā iti kecit vyākhyāyante) alakālakālakā alakān cūrṇakuntalān, alate muktādibhirbhūṣayatīti tādṛśī alakālī, ("alaṅ bhūṣāyām"ityasya aṇi rūpam) tathā kalameva kālaṃ madhuramityarthaḥ, tataśca alakālī cāsau kālakā ceti (karmadhārayaḥ) , lalanikā anukampanīyā lalanā, rādhā ityarthaḥ, ālagatu tvayā sammilitā bhavatu iti nirgalito'rthaḥ| (śrīkṛṣṇaṃ prati śrīrādhāyāḥ sakhyā uktiriyam| atra sarveṣāmeva pādānāmādimadhyānteṣu "kālakāla" iti padamavyavahitatayā punaḥ punarāvṛttamiti avyavahitaṃ tathā "gala mukha" ityādīnāṃ vyavahitatayā vyavahitañca ādimadhyāntayamakam) | | 50| | 

samprati sandaṣṭayamakasthānaṃ nirdiśati, sandaṣṭeti| dvayoḥ pādayoḥ ūṅrghādhobhāvena sthitayoḥ sannihitayoḥ caraṇayorityathaḥ, antādī prathamapādasya antaḥ dvitīyapādasya ādiḥ, tathā dvitīyapādasyāntaḥ tṛtīyapādasyādiḥ, evaṃ tṛtīyapādasya antaḥ caturthapādasyādiśca, etau pādāṃśauityarthaḥ, sandaṣṭayamakasya sandaṃśākṛtiyamakasya, sthānaṃ viṣayaḥ, sandaśaḥ yathā ubhayabhāgamākramya vatrtate tathā idamapi pādayorantadibhāgamākramya vatrtate iti niṣkṛṣṭārthaḥ; etat mandaṣṭayamakam, ukteṣu yamakeṣusa antatargatamapi "raveṇa bhaumaḥ" ityādau patitamapītyarthaḥ, atra svātantryeṇaca pṛthagbhāvena, kīttryate vaṇryate, viśeṣāditi bhāvaḥ| (etat yamakamanyairuktatvādatrollikhyate, svamate tu prāguktabhedāntargatamevaitaditi bodhyam| kecididaṃ kāñcīyamakemityāhuḥ ; yathā, ekasyānte'parasyādau sadṛśaṃ dṛśyate yadi| tat kāñcīyamakaṃ nāma vyāhṛtaṃ munipuṅgavaiḥ| | "iti| udāharaṇāntaraṃ bhaṭṭikāvye yathā, "piśitāśināmanudiśaṃ sphuṭatāṃ sphuṭatāṃ jagāma pariviḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍalatā| ḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavalatā janena bahudhā caritaṃ caritaṃ mahattvarahitaṃ mahatā| | "iti) | | 51| | 

tatra sandaṣṭayamakamudāharati, upoḍheti| sā abalā madena yauvanajanitavikāreṇa, madyapānajena vā, upoḍhaḥ udriktaḥ, rāgaḥ anurāgo yasyāstādṛśī, mayyanurāgiṇī api ityarthaḥ, ata eva yojitaḥ abhiniveśithaḥ, ātmā manaḥ yasyāṃ tathābhūtām, anaṅgatāpitām anaṅgaṃ kāmaṃ, svakāyryakaraṇākṣamamiti bhāvaḥ, tāpayati pīḍayatīti tādṛśī anaṅgatāpinau tasyā bhāvaḥ tāṃ, gatā'pi madanirāgiṇi matkāmā ca satyapītyarthaḥ, madenasā mamāparādhena hetunā, manyurasena krodhāvegena, yojitā saṃsṛṣṭā, krodhaparavaśā satītyarthaḥ, mama iyate etatparimāṇāya, tāpāya pīḍaāyai, na āsa na jagāma, iti na, api tu jagāma evetyarthaḥ, sā mama atīva santāpakāraṇatāṃ prāpei bhāvaḥ| ("asa gatyādānadīptiṣu" ityasya rūpam| sakhāyaṃ pratikasyacit māninyā pratyākhyātasya uktiriyam| atera prathamapādānte tṛtīyapādādau ca "madena sā madenasā" tathā dvitīyapādānte tṛtīyapādādau ca "na yojitā na yojitā" tathā tṛtīyapādānte caturthapādādau ca "ṅgatāpitā ṅgatāpitā" ityeteṣāṃ sandesākāreṇāvasthānāt saṃndaṣṭayamakam) | | 52| | 

itthaṃ pādabhāgayamakāni darśayitvā samprati samastapādagatayamakāni darśayan ādau samudrayamakamāha, adrdhābhyāsa iti| adrdhasya pādadvayasya, ( adrdhaśabdo'tra na kevalaṃ ślokīyapūrvādrdhaparādrdhaparaḥ, kintu yatkiñcitpādadvayaparaḥ iti bodhyam) abhyāsaḥ punarāvṛttiḥ, samudraḥtadākhyaḥ, syāt; ( samudrāśabdena sampuṭaka ucyate, sa yathā bhāgadvayātmakaḥ tathā ayamapi pādadvayatmaka iti tathā vyapadeśaḥ ; taduktaṃ rudrabhaṭṭena, "adrdhaṃ punarāvṛttaṃ janayati yamakaṃ samudrakaṃ nāma" iti) asya samudrasya, trayo bhedāḥ matāḥ khyātāḥ| (tathā ca, prathamatṛtīyau dvitīyacaturthau ca pādau tulyau ityekaḥ, prathamadvitīyau tṛtīyacaturtho ca tathā iti dvitīyaḥ, tathā prathamacaturthau dvitīyatṛtīyau ca iti tṛtīyaḥ) | | samastapādagatayamakeṣu pādābhyāsayamakamāha, pādābhyāsa iti| pādābhyāsaḥ ekaikacaraṇasya punarāvṛttiḥ api, anekātmā anekavidhaḥ, pādasya ekadvitribhyāsatvena ekādaśavidha ityarthaḥ, (tathā ca prathamo dvitīye tṛtīye caturthe ca iti trayaḥ ; dvitīyaḥ tṛtīye caturthe ceti dvau; tṛtīyaścaturthe ityekaḥ ; punaśca prathamaḥ dvitīye tṛtīye ca, dvitīye caturthe ca, tathā tṛtīye caturthe ca, dvitīyastṛtīye caturthe ca iti catvāraḥ ; tathā parthamo dvitīye tṛtīye caturthe ca ityekaḥ, iti militvā ekādaśavidhāḥ) saḥ adrdhābhyāsaḥ pādābhyāsaśca, nidarśanaiḥ vakṣyamāṇaiḥ udāharaṇaiḥ, vyajyate vyaktīkriyate| | 53| | 

tatra samudrasya bhedatrayaṃ darśayiṣyan prathamaṃ pīrvapādādrdhasyottarapādādrdhena samatāhetukaṃ prathamatṛtīyayoḥ dvitīyacaturthayośca pādayoḥ tulyatālakṣaṇaṃ yamakaṃ darśayati, neti| paramāyataḥ atidīrghaḥ, māno yasyāḥ tādṛśyā'pi, astheyaḥ na atisthiraṃ, satvaṃ svabhāvaḥ vyavasāyo vā yasyāḥ tathābhūtayā, tvayā bhavatyā, sa nā puruṣaḥ, priyatama ityarthaḥ, na vajryaḥ na pratyākhyeyaḥ; paraṃ kintu, āyatamānayā yatnavatyā satyā, āstheyaḥ ādatrtavyaḥ, tathā āvajryaḥ sapremavyavahāreṇa vaśībhūtaḥ kāyryaśca, tava svabhāvo vyavasāyo vā na sthirataraḥ, ataḥ priyaparityāge paścāttāpa eva phalaṃ, tasmāt yathā'yaṃ svavaśe tiṣṭhet tathā yatnaḥ kriyatāmiti bhāvaḥ| ( māninīṃ prati tatsakhya uktiriyam| atra prathamatṛtīyau dvitīyacaturthau ca tulyāviti samudrabhedaḥ| udāharaṇāntaraṃ yathā bhaṭṭikāvye, "samiddhaśaraṇā dīptā dehe laṅkā mateśvarā| samiddhaśaraṇādīptā dehe'laṅkāmateśvarā| | " iti) | | 54| | 

prathamadvitīyapādayostṛtīyacaturthayośca pādayoḥ samatālakṣaṇaṃ samudrasya bhedāntaramudāharaṇena darśayati, narā iti| mānanayā sammānena, sametya, sthitena iti adhyāhāyryaṃ, mānavatetyarthaḥ, bhavatā ayanena gamanena, śaṅgrāmayātrayetyarthaḥ, jitāḥ parābhūtāḥ, narāḥ ripava ityarthaḥ, mānanayayoḥ sammānanītyoḥ, āsaṃ kṣepam, abhāvamityarthaḥ, etya prāpya, na rājitāḥ na śobhitāḥ, bhavatsakāśāt palāyya kathañcita rakṣitajīvanānāṃ śatrūṇāṃ sammānanītiśūnyatayā atīva durdaśā jātetyarthaḥ| palāyamānānāmeṣā gatiruktā, ye tu sāhasikā na tathā, teṣāṃ gatimāha, vināśitā iti| vaibhavaṃ vibhatvaṃ, tāyati vistārayati iti tathoktena, (" tāyṛña pālanavistārayoḥ" ityasmāt nandyāditvāt lyupratyayaḥ) bhavatā vināśitā vai nihatāstu, ripavaḥ iti śeṣaḥ, vinā pakṣiṇā, gṛdhreṇa ityarthaḥ, aśitāḥ bhakṣitāḥ| (rājñaḥ stuturiyam| atra parthamadvitīyau tathā tṛtīyacaturtho ca tulyau iti samudrabhedaḥ) | | 55| | 

prathamacaturthapādāyordvitīyatṛtauyapādayośca tulyatālakṣaṇaṃ samudrabhedāntaramāha, kalāpināmitiṣalāpena kekādhvaninā, ūḍhaḥ prāptaḥ, ghanakālaḥ samprāpta iti prakaṭita iti bhāvaḥ, ghanāgamaḥ varṣākālaḥ yaiḥ tādṛśānāṃ, kalāpināṃ mayūrāṇāṃ, vṛndāni samūhāḥ, cārutayā śobhayā, varṣākālajanitayā iti bhāvaḥ, upayānti saṅgacchante, śobhāṃ prāpnuvantītyarthaḥ ; tathā vṛndanilena vāraṣikasaṅghātavāyunā, apoḍhaḥ nirastaḥ, ghanaḥ nṛtyaviśeṣaḥ, ("ghanaṃ syāt kāsyātālādivādyamadhyamanṛtyayoḥ"iti medīni) tadviśiṣṭaḥ āgamaḥ āgamanaṃ, savilāsāgamanamityarthaḥ, yeṣāṃ tādṛśānāṃ, parityaktasavilāsagatikānām ityarthaḥ, ke jale, lapanti nandantīti tathoktānāṃ, kalam avyaktamadhuradhvanim, āpnuvanti iti tathoktānāṃ vā kalāpināṃ haṃsānāṃ ca ārutayaḥ madhurasvarāḥ apayanti apagacchanti, pratiruddhā bhavantītyarthaḥ, varṣāsu haṃsānāṃ madarāhityāditi bhāvaḥ| (varṣāvarṇanamidam| atra prathamacaturthau tathā dvitīyatṛtīyau tulyau iti samudrabhedaḥ) | | 56| | 

samudrayamakabhedamabhidhāya samprati anekavidhaṃ pādābhyāsayamakamudāhariṣyan, prathamaṃ prathamadvitīyapādagataṃ tadudāharati, neti| mandayā mūḍhayā, tatkālocitapratipattividhurayā ityarthaḥ, avanarjite atyakte māne prāṇayajanye īṣryījanye vā kope, sātmayā sayatnayā, yatnavatyā ityarthaḥ, yatnena mānaṃ rakṣantyā iti yāvat, ("ātmā puṃsi svabhāve ca prayatnamanasorapi" iti medinī) yadvā, āvarjitaḥ anukūlaṃ matvā svīkṛta ityarthaḥ, mānaḥ īṣryāroṣalakṣaṇo yayā sā, tathā sātmā ātmavatī tayā, (anayoḥ karmadhārayaḥ) tathā dayayā varjitau priyasya malinamukhaṃ dṛṣṭvāṣapi kṛpāvirahitau, mānasam ātmā svabhāvaśca tau yasyāḥ tathābhūtayā, mayā naman kṛtāparādhatayā pādayoḥ patan, apīti śeṣaḥ, jīviteśvaraḥ prāṇanāthaḥ, urasi vakṣasi, priyasyeti bhāvaḥ, upāstīrṇam arpitaṃ, payodharadvayaṃ stanayuglaṃ yasmin tadyathā tathā, na samāliṅgyata nāśliṣyata| ("sātmayā" ityatra "sārthayā"iti pāṭhe ekatraaparityaktamānasamūhayā, anyatradayāvarjitamānasaprayojanayetyarthaḥ| nāyakaṃ nirākṛtya galitamānāyā māninyā anutāpoktariyam| atra prathamadvitīyau pādāvabhyastau iti pādābhyāsayamakam) | | 57| | 

tadeva prathamatṛtīyapādagatamudāharati, sabheti| he rājan! mṛṇālanimrmalaiḥ mṛṇālavat pariśuddhaiḥ, tava guṇaiḥ śauyryādibhiḥ, abalā balāsurarahitā, indreṇa nihatatvāt balāsurotpātavihīnā ityartaḥ, tathā vibhūṣitā devabhogyadravyajātaiḥ viśeṣeṇa sajjitā, yadvā, vibhoḥ prabhoḥ, brahmaṇaḥ viṣṇoḥ śaṅkarasya indrasya vā ityarthaḥ, uṣitaṃ vasatiḥ yasyāṃ tādṛśī, surāṇāṃ devānāṃ, sabhā sudhamrmākhyā4, ārohi ārūḍhā, balāsuravināśādadhunā devasabhāsthā nirbhayāḥ santaḥ tava guṇān gāyantītyarthaḥ, sa tvaṃ, vibhūṣitāḥ viśeṣeṇa alaṅkṛtāḥ, abalāḥ sundayryaḥ, vihārayan ramayan san, bhāsurāṇāṃ dīpyamānānāṃ, samṛddhānām ityarthaḥ, purāṃ nagarāṇāṃ, sampadaḥ aiśvayryāṇi, nirviśa upabhuṅkṣva| (rajānaṃ prati vaitālikasyoktiriyam| atra prathamatṛtīyau abhyastāviti pādābhyāsayamakam) | | 58| | 

tadeva prathamacaturthāpādagatamudāharati, kalamiti| kalaṃ madhuram, uktaṃ vacanaṃ, vilāsinīnāmiti adhyāhāyryam; tathā tanu kṣīṇaṃ, maddhyaṃ kaṭideśaṃ, namayatīti tathoktā pṛthutaretyartaḥ ; yadvā tanormaddhyāsya nāmikā, nijabhareṇa iti bhāvaḥ, ("ajādihalanto'dīrghāt" iti kvacit pākṣiko madhyaśabdasya dhakāradvirbhāvaḥ) stanadvayī ca, tvaṭṭate tvāṃ vinā, kaṃ janaṃ, na hanti? na pīḍayati? api tu tvāṃ parityajya sarvameva ityarthaḥ, ataḥ kāraṇāt, bhavanmukhe bhavadādiviṣaye, gaṇane saṅkhyāne, bhavāṭṭaśāṃ jitendriyāṇāṃ gaṇanāyāmityarthaḥ, hi niścitam, anāmikā aṅguṣṭhataścaturthī aṅguliḥ, kalaṅkamuktaṃ nirdoṣaṃ, tanumat śarīri, bhūtam aparaṃ prāṇinaṃ, na yāti na gacchati, tvāṃ vinā anyaṃ na gaṇayati ityarthaḥ, jagatyasmin ke ke jitendriyāḥ santīti gaṇanaprastāve kaniṣṭhāṅigulyā prathamaṃ tvāṃ gaṇayitvā anāmikayā anyaṃ kamapi gaṇayituṃna śankoti bhavādṛśajitendriyāntarābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ| (kamapi mahāntaṃ pratyuktiriyam| atra prathamacaturthau pādāvabhyastāviti pādābhyāsayamakam| atredamavadheyaṃ, caturthapāde "tanumaddhyānāmikā"iti padasya tanumat hi anāmikā evaṃ viśleṣaṃ kṛtvā vyākhyāne tanumaddhyanāmiketi dhakārasya dakārayuktatvaṃ bhavati, tataśca prathamamadhyaśabdasyāpi dvirbhāvaḥ vyākhyātṛbhiraṅgīkṛtaḥ, parantu madhyaśabdasya dhakārasya dvirbhāvaḥ aprasiddhaḥ, ataḥ vayamatredaṃ manyāmahe yat, caturthapāde kalaṅkamuktaṃ tanumadhi śarīramadhikṛtya, śarīradhāri ityarthaḥ, bhūtaṃ na yāti evaṃ vyākhyīne prathamamadhyapadasya dhakārasya dvirbhāvarūpakaṣṭakalpanāṃ vinā'pi vyākhyānasaṅgatiriti sudhībhirvibhāvyam) | | 59| | 

dvitīyatṛtīyapādagataṃ pādābhyāsamakamudāharatisa yaśa iti| he ajopam! ajarājasadṛśa! nārāyaṇasam! iti vā, harasama! iti vā, ("ajā viṣṇuharacchāgāḥ"ityamaraḥ) daṃśitāḥ sannāhavantaḥ, śitāni tīkṣṇāni, āyudhāni astrāṇi yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ, tathā tarasvinaḥ mahāvegāḥ, apratihatavegā ityarthaḥ, te tava, sainikāḥ yoddhṛpuruṣāḥ, yudhā saṅgrāmeṇa, dikṣu yaśaśca tathā rajaśca pāṃśuñca, vitanvate vistārayanti; tathā dviṣāṃ śatrūṇāṃ, kulañca samūhañca, vituna deharahitam, atejaḥ tejohīnaṃ, tathā apamadaṃ nirahaṅkārañca, kurvanti vidadhati| (vijigīṣustutiriyam| atra dvitīyatṛtīyavabhyastāviti pādābhyāsayamakam) | | 60| | 

tadeva dvitīyacaturthapādagatamudāharati, bibhatrtāti| he rājan! bhujaṅgamo vāsutiḥ, te tava, bhujena amā saha, tvadbhujavalasāhāyyenetyarthaḥ, bhūmervalayaṃ bhūmaṇḍalaṃ, bibhartati dhatte| etāvatā garvo na vidheya ityāha, smarata iti| smarataḥ pūrvavṛttaṃ vijānata ityarthaḥ, mat mattaḥ, matsakāśādityartha, añcitaṃ pūjitaṃ, sarvajanasammatam ityarthaḥ, ekam uktaṃ vacanaṃ, śrṛṇu ākarṇaya; kintadityāha, svaṃ svakīyaṃ, bhujaṃ bhūdharaṃ bhūdhāraṇakṣamam, avetya jñātvā, rataḥ prītaḥ san, citaṃ pravṛddham, atyantamityarthaḥ, madaṃ garvaṃ, māsma gamaḥ na gaccha, garvaṃ mā kuru ityarthaḥ| (rājānaṃ pratyepadeśagarbhastutiriyam| atra dvitīyacaturthāvabhyastāviti pādābhyāsayamakam) | | 61| | 

tadeva tṛtīyacaturthapādagatamudāharati, smarānala iti| he tāmarasekṣaṇe! padmalocane! , ("atha tāmarasaṃ padme tāmrakāñcanayorapi"iti medinī) raktotpalanayane! iti vā, mānajanitakopeneti bhāvaḥ, ("raktotpalaṃ tāmarasam" iti koṣaḥ) he arase! arasike! , mānena vivarddhitaḥ viśeṣaṇa vṛdiṃdha gataḥ, te tava, yaḥ smarānalaḥ kāmāgniḥ, sa tataḥ vistāraṃ gataḥ san, kṣaṇena utsavena, samaṃ saha, samantataḥ sarvatobhāvenetyartaḥ, tāṃ pūrvānubhūtāṃ, nuvṛtiṃta sukhaṃ, kiṃ na apākaroti? na nirasyati? api tu rinasyatyevetyarthaḥ, nitarāṃ kāmapīḍitā'si ata idānīmeva mānaṃ muñca vilambenālamiti bhāvaḥ| (māninīṃ prati tatsakhyuktiriyam| atra tṛtīyacaturthāvabhyastāviti pādābhyāsayamakam) | | 62| | 

pādatrayābhyāsaṃ kramaśo darśayitumādau prathamadvitīyatṛtīyapādagataṃ darśayati, prabhāvata ityādi| he prabhāvataḥ prabhāvāt, svatejasā itayarthaḥ, prabhāvataḥ prabhā dīptiḥ tatsampannasya, vāsavasya indravasya indrasyāpi, nāmana! nāmayati nataṃ karitīti tatsambuddhau, yajñabhaṅgapārijātaharaṇādinā indrasyāpi garvaharaṇāditi bhāvaḥ, nāmeti prasiddhau, he anāmaeॅ nāsti āmo rogo yasya yasmādvā tatsambuddhau, tvayi viṣṭapasya bhuvanasya, prabhau adhipe sati durvṛttadamanādinā jagat pālayati satītyarthaḥ, ato'smāt karmaṇaḥ, kaṃsāsurādinidhanādityarthaḥ, navāsavasya navasya āsavasya surāyāḥ, savasya vā yajñasya ca, vicchittirvicchedaḥ, nāsīt na abhavat, bhogināṃ surāpānotsavaḥ dharmiṣṭhānāṃ yajñādikañca satataṃ pravavṛte ityarthaḥ| (śrīkṛṣṇastutiriyam| atra parthamadvitīyatṛtīyapādānāmabhyāsātpādatrayābhyāsayamakam) | | 63| | 

tadeva parthamadvitīyacaturthapādagatamudāharati, paramiti| (atra padacchedaprakāro yathā, paraṃ, parāyāḥ, balavāḥ, raṇānām; paramparāyāḥ balavāraṇānām; paraṃ, parāya! abalavāraṇānām iti) he parāya! paraḥ utkṛṣṭaḥ, ayaḥ śubhāvahavidhiryasya tatsambuddhau, he paramakalyāṇinnityarthaḥ, he balavā! balena śaktyā, vārayati nivārayati, śatrūniti śeṣaḥ, tatsambuddhau, (vārayateḥ kvipi rūpam) tvaṃ balameṣāmastīti balāḥ balavantaḥ, (arśaāditvādac pratyayaḥ) vāraṇāṃ hastinaḥ yeṣu tathoktānāṃ, tathā abalān durbalān, vārayanti vimukhīkurvantīti abalavāraṇāḥ teṣāṃ, raṇānāṃ yuddhānāṃ, paramparāyāḥ samūhasya, sthalīḥ raṇabhūmīrityrthaḥ, dhūlīrvidhāya reṇumayīḥ kṛtvā, prabhūtarathāśvasainyānāṃ pādatāḍaneneti bhāvaḥ, vyoma ākāśaṃ, rundhan ācchādayan san, dhūlībhiriti bhāvaḥ, paraṃ śreṣṭhaṃ, balinamiti yāvat, paraṃ śatruṃ, parāyāḥ gatavān, jitavān asītyarthaḥ, (parāpūrvakāt yādhātoḥ laḍaḥ madhyamapuruṣaikavacanam| rājñaḥ stuturiyam| atra prathamadvitīyacaturthāḥ pādā abhyastā iti pādatrayābhyāsayamakam) | | 64| | 

dvitīyatṛtīyacaturthapādagataṃ pādatrayābhyāsamudāharati, nti| he alajja! nistrapa! asamāhitānām anavahitacittānām, anyāsaktattvāt asayatātmanām ityarthaḥ, tathā asamā asadṛśāḥ, atibahulā ityarthaḥ, ahitāḥ śatravaḥ yeṣām, adhikaśatrūṇāmityarthaḥ, bhavadvidhānāṃ tvatsadṛśānāṃ dhūtrtānāṃ, mithyābhavat asatyambhavat, vidhānaṃ kāyryaṃ yasyāstāṃ kāyryeṣu apariṇatāmityarthaḥ, tā asamaḥ viṣamaḥ, atidāruṇa ityarāthaḥ, yaḥ ahiḥ sarpaḥ, tasyeeva tāno vistālasararaḥ, vakragatiriti yāvat, yasyāḥ tādṛśīm, ativakradāruṇāmityarthaḥ, punaśca bhave utpattau, śravaṇamātra evetyarthaḥ, dvidhā dvividhaḥ, anaḥ prāṇaḥ, artharūpaḥ iti yāvat, yasyāḥ tathābhūtām, arthānāmeva vācāṃ prāṇatvatvāt iti bhāvaḥ, yadvā, bhave dve dviprakāre, dhāne dhāraṇe, arthagrahaṇe ityarthaḥ, yasyā tāṃ, śravaṇasamakālameva dvyartabodhikāmityārthaḥ, vācaṃ na śradadhe na viśvasimi| ( śaṭhanāyakaṃ prati nāyikāyā uktiriyam| atra dvitīyatṛtīyacaturthapādānāmabhayāsāt pādatrayābhyāsayamakam) | | 65| | 

pādatrayāgataṃ dvarabhyāsatudāhṛtya idānīṃ pādacatuṣṭayagataṃtrirabhyāsamudāharati, sanniti| h san! sādho! , he āhitomānamarājasena! na namantīti anamāḥ brāhmaṇāḥ, (pacāditvādac) anamānāṃ rājā anamarājaścandraḥ, (candrasya dvirājāparapayryāyatvāt) umā pārvatī ca, anamarājaśca tau umānamarājau, āhitau svāṅge dhṛtau, umānamarājau pārvatīdvijarājau yena saḥ āhitomānamarājaḥ śivaḥ, tena senaḥ inaḥ prabhuḥ, upāsya iti yāvat, ("inaḥ sūyrye prabhau"ityamaraḥ) tena saha vatrtamāna) saśvaraḥ ityarthaḥ, tatsambuddhau, he śivabhakta! ityarthaḥ, tathā he amānama! amānā aparimāṇā, mā lakṣmīḥ, sampadityarthaḥ, yasya tatsambuddhau, he prabhutasampattiman! , punaśca he rājasena rajoguṇajakrodhādivikāreṇa, mānama! mā namyate na vaśīkriyate iti tatsambodhanaṃ, he krodhalobhādyamāyattīkṛta! ityarthaḥ, kiñca he amānamarājasena! mānaḥ sammānaḥ, mā lakṣmīḥ te māname, na vidyate māname yasyāḥ sā amānamā sammānasampadvihīnā, rājasenā vipakṣarājacamūryasya tatsambuddhau, sannahitaḥ kṛtayuddhaudyogaḥ san, na rājase na śobhase, tvamiti śeṣaḥ, yatastvaṃ sannāhitaḥ sannā avasādaṃ gatāḥ, ahitāḥ śatravo yasya tathoktaḥ, tathā sannā satpuruṣaḥ, sādhuḥ ityarthaḥ tataśca hitaḥ sarveṣāṃ hite rata ityarthaḥ, atastava yuddhayātrā na yujyate iti bhāvaḥ| ( śaivaṃ rāḍajānaṃ prati yuddhanivartakavacanam| atra sarva eva pādā abhyāstāḥ) | | 66| | 

samprati pādābhyāsamupasaṃhṛtya ślokābhyāsaṃ nirdiśati, sakvaditi| evam uktaprakāreṇa, pādasya sakvat ekavāraṃ, dviḥ vāradvayaṃ, triśca vāratrayañca, yaḥabhyāsaḥ punarāvṛttiḥ, saḥ pradarśitaḥ udāhṛtaḥ| (tatra sakṛdabhyāsaḥ pādadvayagataḥ, dvirabhyāsaḥ pādatrayagataḥ, trirabhyāsaḥpādacatuṣcayagata itaci boddhavyam) | yuktaḥ saṅgataḥ, artho'bhidheyaḥ yasya taṃ saṅgatārtham, ekavākyatāpannamityarthaḥ, ślokadvayaṃ samānānupūrvapadarṇaghaṭitaṃ padyadvitayaṃ, ślokābhyasaḥ ślokabhyāsasaṃjñakaḥ, smṛtaḥ kathitaḥ| yatheti vakṣyamāṇodāharaṇārtham| | 67| | 

tameva ślokābhyāsamudāharati, vināyakenetiṣa he rājan! vināyakena vinetrā, durjānānāṃ śāsitrā ityarthaḥ, tathā vṛttau vatrtulau upacitau pīnau ca, bāhū bhujau yasya tathoktena, mahābhujenetyarthaḥ, svamitrāṇāṃ su śobhanānām, amitrāṇāṃ śatruṇāṃ, śauyryādisampannānāmapi rupūṇāmityarthaḥ, uddhāriṇā vināśakena, tathā atulāśritā tulāṃ toulanām, āśrayatīti tulaśrita, sa na bhavatīti atulāśrit tena, kenāpi sādṛsyaṃ nabhajatā ityarthaḥ, nirupameṇa iti yāvat, (śrayateḥ kvip) bhavatā hetunā, iyaṃ pṛtvī vasudhā, abhītā durjanebhyo bhayarahitā jātā ityarthaḥ| | 68| | 

vināyakeneti| kiñca abhītā yuddhāya bhavadabhimukhamāgacchatā, (abhipūrvādiṇdhātīḥ kvipi tṛtīyaikavacanam) ariṇā śatruṇā, taveti śeṣaḥ, vināyakena nāyakarahitena, tathā vṛttau jātau, upacitau citāmupagatau, śmāśānasthacitāmāruḍhau ityartaḥ, jhr upagatau citāmiti vigraheṇa "atyādayaoḥ krāntādyarthe dvitīyayā" iti tatpuruṣaḥṭa bāhū yasya tathoktena, tathā svamitrāṇi nijabandhūn, svāni dhanāni, mitrāṇi bandhūśca iti vā, ujjahāti parityajatīti svamitroddhāḥ tādṛśena, parityaktasvajanena parityaktadhanabāndhavenetyarthaḥ vā, jhrsvamitraśabdāt utpūrvakasya jahāteḥ kvipi tṛtīyaikavacanam "āto dhātoḥ" iti dhātorākāralopaḥṭa bhavatā pūrvoktaprakāreṇa satā, pṛthvī gurvī, yamatulā yamasya antakasya, tulā parimāṇasantraṃ, vicārasthānam iti bhāvaḥ, ( mānayantre nyūnādhikatvaniścayavat kṛtāntavicāralaye'pi puṇyapāpascayakaraṇāt tasya tulāsantratulyamavagantavyam) āśritā prāptā, yamasadanaṃ gatam ityarthaḥ| (rājñaḥ stutiviṣayakaṃ ślokādvayamiti| samānavarṇādighaṭitaślokadvayasya yuktārthatvāt ślokābhyāso'yam| kecittu ślokadvayāvṛttirūpaṃ ślokābhyāsayamakaṃ mahāyamakamāhuḥ ; udāhṛtañca yathā, "abhiyātā varaṃ tuṅgaṃ bhūbhṛtaṃ ruciraṃ puraḥ| karkaśaṃ prathitaṃ dhāma sasattvaṃ puṣkarekṣaṇam| | " punarapi " abhiyā'tāvaram" ityādipaṭhanarūpaṃ ślokadvayam) | | 69| | 

pādacatuṣṭayagatasya trirabhyāsarūpayamakasya praticaraṇamavāntarābhyāse vaicitryāviśeṣasambhavāt mahāyamakasaṃjñāmāha, ekakaroti| ekākārāḥ abhinnāḥ, catvāraḥ pādā yatra tādṛśaṃ, tat yamakaṃ, mahāyaṃkāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavayaṃ mahāyamakasaṃjñaṃ, jñeyamiti śeṣaḥ ; tatrāpi ekākāracatuṣpādarūpamahāyamakasya pādamadhye'pi, abhyāsaḥ punarāvṛttiḥ, pādakhaṇḍasyeti bhāvaḥ, dṛśyate, ataḥ sā parā śreṣṭhā, yamakakriyā yamanaprakāraḥ, yamakavatpadyānuṣṭhānamityarthaḥ, pādacatu, ṭayābhyāse yatra pādakhaṇḍasyāpi abhyāsaḥ tat mahāyamakamiti niṣkarṣaḥ| (mahāyamkatvañca īdṛśayamanavidheḥ vaicitryādhāyakatayā utkṛṣṭatvāt bodhyam| nanu pādacataṣṭayābhyāsayamate yathā catuṣpādānāṃ tulyāvṛttistathaivātrāpi, kiṃ tato mahāyamake vaicitryam ityato bhedapradarśanāya pādakhaṇḍaābhyāsavaicitryameva mahāyamakalakṣaṇamityuktaṃ, tataśca catuṣpādānāmubhayatraiva ekākāratve'pi pratipādakhaṇḍhasya punarāvṛttipurasaraṃ catuṣpādānāmaikarūpyantu mahāyamakatvam iti niṣkarṣaḥ| uktañca kaṇṭhābharaṇe, "ekākāracatuṣpādaṃ mahāyamakamucyate| ślokābhyāsaśca tatrādyaṃ punarabhyāsamarhati| | " iti| kecittu catuṣpādānāmekarūpātmakamidaṃ mahāyamakaṃ sarvapādayamakamāhuḥ ; yathā, "babhau marutvān vikṛtaḥ samudro babhau marutvān vikṛtaḥ samudraḥ| babhau marutvān vikṛtaḥ samudro babhau marutvān vikṛtaḥ samudrāḥ" iti) | | 70| | 

udāharati, samāneti| (atrāyaṃ padacchedaḥ, samānayāsa, mā, anayā; samānaya, asamānayā; samānayā, samānayā; samāna, yā, samānayā iti) he samānayāsa| samānaḥ ekarūpaḥ, yāsa yatnaḥ yasyatatsambuddhau, sarvatra tulyaprayatna ityarthaḥ, tathā samāna! samadarśin! sarvatra ātmavaddarśana ityarthaḥ, yadvā, samaḥ abhinnaḥ, ānaḥ prāṇaḥ yasya saḥ, abhinnaddhadaya! sakhe! iti bhāvaḥ, yā nāyikā, samānayā mā lakṣmīḥ, śobhā ityarthaḥ, nayaḥ nītiḥ, vidyā ityarthaḥ, yadvā, samānayāsamānayā ityekaṃ padaṃ, yāsaḥ yatnaḥ, mānaḥ īṣryaroṣaḥ, tau samānau yasyāstayā ityarthaḥ, anayā nāyikayā, mā māṃ, samānaya saṅgamaya, asyāḥ saṅgamasya atīvādaraṇīyatvāt atīvapīḍaādāyakametanmānaṃ śīghraṃ sāntvayan anayā māṃ saṃyojaya iti bhāvaḥ| ( manmathonmāthāviṣṭasya kasyacit vayasyaṃ prati prārthaneyam| atra pādacatuṣṭayābhyāse pādakhaṇḍasyāpi abhyāsāt mahāyamakam) | | 71| | 

"atyantabahavasteṣām" ityādinā pratipāditeṣu bahuṣu bhedeṣu pūrvaṃ sajātīyasammiśraṇajanitaprabhedāḥ darśitāḥ, idānīṃ vijātīyamiśraṇodāharaṇaṃ darśayati, dhareti| dharādharākāradharāḥ dharāyāḥ pṛthivyāḥ, dharaḥ dhāraṇakatrtā, nāgarājaḥ ityarthaḥ, tasya ākāraṃ sādṛśyaṃ dharantīti tādṛśāḥdhārādhārākāradharāḥ atidīrghā ityarthaḥ, ahīnavikramāḥ ahīnaḥ analpaḥ vikramaḥ vīyryaṃ yeṣāṃ te athavā, ahīnasya ahīnāṃ prabhoḥ, nāgādhipasyānantasyetyarthaḥ, vikrama iva vikramo yeṣāṃ tādṛśāḥ, tathā sahasā balāt, hatārayaḥ hatāḥ nāśitāḥ, arayaḥ śatravo yaiḥ tathoktāḥ, tathā rayoddhurāḥ rayeṇa vegena, uddhurāḥ utkaṭāḥ, ativegavanta ityarthaḥ, tathā mānadhurāvalambinaḥ mānasya dhurāṃ bhāram, avalambante iti tathābhūtāḥ, sammānarakṣakā ityarthaḥ, dharābhujāṃ rājñāṃ, bhujāḥ bāhavaḥ, kramāt śatrorākramaṇāt, śaktita iti vā, svasāmathryādityarthaḥ, ("kramaścānukrameśaktau kalpe cākramaṇe'pi ca "iti medinī) mahīṃ pṛthvīṃ, pātuṃ rakṣituṃ, pālayituṃ vā, sahante kṣamante| ( atra prathame pāde avyavahitamādiyamakaṃ, pādānāñca sandhiṣu avyavahitamantādiyamakaṃ sandaśayamakañca, dvitīyapāde punaḥ varṇadvayavyavahitaṃ "mahī mahī" iti vyapetaṃ madhyayamakaṃ, tṛtīye ca pāde ekavarṇavyavahitaṃ "saha saha" iti , caturthe ca varṇadvayavyavahitaṃ "dhurā dhurā" iti madhyamakam| itthaṃ bahūnāṃ vijātīyānāṃ sammiśraṇamatra ityavadheyam) | | 72| | 

ittham anulomayakabhedānuktavā idānīṃ pratilomabhedān daupauha, āvṛttiriti| prātilomyena vaiparityena, pādādrdhaślokagocarā pādādrdhaślokāgocarā viṣayā yasyāḥ sā pādagocarā, ślokādrdhagocarā, ślokagocarā ca evaṃ trividhā ityarthaḥ, āvṛtti abhyāsaḥ, pratilomatvāt vaiparītyenāvṛttatvāt, pratilomaṃ pratilomākhyaṃ, yamakam iti smṛtam| (tathā ca yatra pūrvapādasya pratilomāvṛttyā uttarapādaḥ bhavati, uttarapādasyāpi vilomenāvṛttyā pūrvapādaḥ sambhavati, tat pādagocaraṃ pratilomayamakam; tathā adrdhagocaraṃ pratilomavṛttyā ślokapīvrvārddhaṃ cet bhavati tadā adrdhagocaraṃ pratilomayamakam ; tathā ekasya ślokasya pratilomāvṛttyā ślokāntaraṃ ślokāntarasyāpi tathāvāvṛttyā pūrvaśloko bhavati cet tadā ślokagocaraṃ pratilomayamakamiti niṣkarṣaḥ| tasya coktarītyā traividhyam) | | 73| | 

pratilomayamakasya traividhyaṃ darśayan prathamaṃ pādagocaramudāharati, yeti| he matāśa! matā jñātā, āśā anyasaṅgaviṣayiṇīṃ tṛṣṇā yasya tatsambuddhau, yā kṛśatā kṣīṇatā, kṛtāyāsā kṛtaḥ janitaḥ, āyāsaḥ kleśaḥ, mameti bhāvaḥ, yayā tathoktā, mamātyarthaṃ kleśadāyinītyarthaḥ sā yā āyātā prāptā, tava duśceṣṭitena pūvrvameva mayā mahatī kṛśatā adhigato, idānīmapi saiva bhaviṣyati, kā'dhikā? ataḥ yathāruci kriyatāmiti bhāvaḥ, he ramaṇa! santoṣajanaka! he stuteta! stutaṃ stavam, iti prāpta, stavārha! ityartaḥ athavā, stutaṃ praśastam, itaṃ gatiḥ, ācaraṇamiti yāvat, yasya saḥ tatsambiddhau, yadvā, stutāt itaḥ cyutaḥ astavārhaḥ, nunditācaraṇāt apraśaṃsanīya ityarthaḥ, tatsambuddhau, tathā he akaraṇāmara! akaraṇe akāyryānuṣṭhāne, amaraḥ devasadṛśa ityartaḥ, devavat akāyryamananuṣṭhanniti, devavat akāyryānuṣṭhānanirata iti vā bhāvaḥ, tatsambuddhau, (devānāmakārayyakaraṇamahalyājāratvādikamiti bodhyam| sarvametat sambodhanapadaṃ solluṇṭhanaparamavagantavyaṃ, prakṛtānupayogitvāt) te tava, ārakatā ṛcchatīti ārakaḥ, ("ṛṇgatau" ityasmāt ṇakapratyayaḥ) tasya bhāvaḥ ārakatā gantṛtvaṃ, yathecchagāmitā ityarthaḥ, astu bhavatu, tvaṃ yāṃ kāmayase tāmeva vraja nātra tvayā sthātavyamiti bhāvaḥ| (māninyā nāyakaṃ prati sakopoktiriyam| atra prathamapādasya pratilomāvṛttyā dvitīyapādastathā dvitīyayasya pratilomāvṛttyā prathamapādaḥ, evaṃ tṛtīyacaturthayorapi, tenātra pādaviṣayaṃ pratilomayamakam) | | 74| | 

ślokādrdhaviṣayamudāharati, nādina iti| nādinaḥ nādarūpaṃ brahma asyāstīti tathoktasya, nādabrahmānudhyānaratasya ityarthaḥ, me mama, madanādhī madanaśca ādhiśca tau kāmaḥ kāmajanitā mānasī vyathā cetyartaḥ, tathā svā nijā, kācana kā'pi, kāmitā viṣayābhilāṣaśca, na, nādabrahmānubhavatome kāmaḥ tajjanitamānasī vyathā svārthaṃ viṣayānurāgaścaite na vidyante ityarthaḥ, tathā damaḥ vāhyondriyasaṃyamaḥ, taṃ nudatadi nirasyatīti tathoktena indriyasaṃyamadhvaṃsakāriṇā, kāmena madanena hetunā, svādhīnā svaḥ ātmā, adhīnaḥ āyattī yasyāstādṛśī ātmavyākulāriṇītyarthaḥ, tāmikā ca glānirapītyarthaḥ, (tāmyati anayeti tamadhātorbhāve ṇakāpratyayaḥ strītvañca; ) na nāsti, mameti śeṣaḥ| (nādotpattiśca prāṇāyāmābhyāsajā, tathā coktaṃ sureśvarācāyryeṇa yathā, "sarecapurairanilasya kumbhaiḥ sarvāsu nāḍaīṣu viśodhitāsu| anāhatādamburuhādudeti svātmāvagamyaḥ svayameva nādaḥ| | " iti| kasyacit yogino vacanamidam| atra ślokapūrvādrdhasya pratilomāvṛttyā ślokādrdhāntaraṃ tathā ślokottarādrdhasya pratilomāvṛttyā ca pūrvādrdhaṃ niṣpannamiti pratilomayamakaṃ ślokādrdhaviṣayam) | | 75| | 

ślokagocaraṃ pratilomaṃ darśayati, yānamiti| he sakhe! iti adhyāhāyryaṃ, yānaṃ vāhanam, aśvādyanyatamamityarthaḥ, ānaya, tvamiti śeṣaḥ| kathamityāha, yāmiti| udāharatādhīnām udāharāṇāṃ mahatāṃ, dhanināmityarthaḥ, śatam adhīnaṃ vaśībhūtaṃ yasyāstādṛśīm, udāraśatasya adhīnāmiti vā, yāṃveśyāmityarthaḥ āyāṃ gatavānasmi, aham iti śeṣaḥ, mārāvikaśā māraḥ kāma eva, avirmeṣaḥ, tasya kaśā tāḍanīsa kāmināṃ kāmārttihantrityarthaḥ, tathā ūnaḥ hīnaḥ, dhanābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ, anaḥ prāṇāḥ yeṣāṃ te ūnānāḥ dhanahīnatayā mṛtaprāyā ityarthaḥ, ūnāḥ hīnāḥ, dhanavihīnāḥ santo'pītyarthaḥ, ananti jīvantīti tādṛśā iti vā, te ca te janāśceti ūnānājanāḥ, tān asyati nirasyatīti ūnānajanāsanā nirdhanān vahiṣkṛrvatītyartaḥ, sā veśyā, āyam āgamanam, anādi uktā, ( anādi iti kathānārthasya naddhātoḥ karmaṇi luṅi rūpaṃ, dvikarmatvāt sā iti gauṇakarmaṇaḥ uktatvañca) mayeti śeṣaḥ, adya tava sannidhāvāgamiṣyāmīti abhihitā ityarthaḥ| | 76| | 

sā'pi mayyanurāgiṇītyāha, setu| amuyā upasthitayā ityartaḥ, śaradā śaratkālena, uddīpanyā iti bhāvaḥsa adhītā ādhiṃ manaḥ pīḍaāṃ, madviraheṇeti bhāvaḥ, itā prāptā, ata eva dināmayamāyāmā, dine divase, āmayalasya rogasya, māyāṃ chalam, amati gacchati, prakāśayatītyarthaḥ, yā sā tādṛśī, divase sakhīnāṃ samakṣaṃ rogacchalena virahaduḥkhaṃ gopayantītyarthaḥ, kiñca nāsanājananā nāsti āsanāyā upaveśanasya, sthiratāyā ityarthaḥ, jananaṃ kriyā yasyāḥ tathoktā, astharatvāt virahaduḥkhena naikatra upaviśantītyarthaḥ, yātīti tathāktā, madīyagamanavatrma nirīkṣamāṇā ityarthaḥ, sā veśyā, śokavirāmā śokasya virahaduḥkhasya, virāmaḥ avasānaṃ yasyāḥ tādṛśīḥ, na, bhavatīti śeṣaḥ, yatataṃ virahaduḥkhamanubhavatītyarthaḥ| (anurāgiṇyāṃ veśyāyāmāsaktasya kasyacit sakhāyaṃ prati uktiriyam| atra prathamaślokasya pratilomenāvṛttyā ślokāntarapūraṇāt ślokāntarasyāpi tathaivāvṛttyā prathamaślokapūraṇācca ślokagocaraṃ pratilomayamakam) | | 77| | 

atha cutrālaṅkārān kāṃśicat nirūpayiṣyan prathamaṃ gomūtrikāṃ nirdiśati, varṇānāmiti| ( atredamavadheyaṃ citrālaṅkārabhedeṣu vaktavyeṣu prathamameva kiṃ nāma tallakṣaṇamityavaśyaṃ vaktavyamapi prasiddhatayā granthakṛtā noktam, asmābhistu śiṣyāṇāṃ śikṣāsaukayryārthaṃ kiñciducyate citramālekhyamiti, tadiva jīvitasthānīyadhvanirahitaṃ citramiti kāśmīrakāḥ| tadayaktuṃ, dhvaneḥ prādhānyānaṅgīkārāt pratīyamānabhāvasya kvicidapi asambhavācca tadā ākṛtiviśeṣayuktaṃ citramityeva bhavatu, tadapi na, avyāpakatvāt; tasmāt varṇādiniyamena prāvṛttamāścayryakāritayā citramiti citraśabdo'trāscayryārthakaḥ iti samādhānam| tacca ṣaḍvidhamiti kecit āhuḥ ; tathā hi varṇacitraṃ, sthānacitraṃ, svaracitram, ākāracitraṃ, gaticitraṃ, bandhacitrañceti; yaduktaṃ, "varṇasthānasvarākāragatibandhān pratīha yaḥ| niyamastadvudhaiḥ ṣoḍhā citramityabhidhīyate| | " iti| tatra gaticitraṃ tāvat ādāvevānena darśayitvā varṇasvarasthānacitrāṇi pradarśitāni| ākārabandhau tu naiva darśitau| tatra ākāraḥ padmādyākṛtimudraṇaṃ, bandhaḥ viviḍitaprabhṛtiḥ ; etayośca parasparabhedaḥ durādhigamaḥ ubhayoreva ākṛtigatatvāditi) | adrdhayoḥ ślokasya yathākramaṃ pīrvodrdhottarādrdhayoḥ, ūṅrghādhaḥkrameṇa paṅktidvayena likhitayoriti bhāvaḥ, varṇānām akṣarāṇām, ekāntaram ekākṣaravyavahitaṃ, yat ekarūpatvam abhinnākāratvam, ekaikākṣaravyavadhānena yat ekavidhavarṇavinyasanamityarthaḥ, tadvidaḥ citrālaṅkārajñāḥ, duṣkaraṃ sahasā katrtumaśakyaṃ, tattu tadeva, ("tat" ityatra "tam" iti pāṭhe tam alaṅkāramityarthaḥ) gomūtriketi calato gormūtradhārākāratvena ślokabandhasya ghaṭitatvāt gomūtrikāsaṃjñaṃ, prāhuḥ| (taduktaṃ yathā kaṇṭhābharaṇe, "gatiruccāvacā yatra mārge mūtrasya goriva| gomūtriketi tat prāhurduṣkaraṃ citravedinaḥ| | " iti| gomūtrikā ca pradhānataḥ trividhā, pādagomūtrikā, ślokādrdhagomūtrikā, ślokagomūtrikā ca, anye'pi avāntarabhedā asyā viparītādayaḥ santi| idantu, lakṣaṇam uttarādrdhavarṇānāmekāntaritatvena tādṛśapūrvādrdhavarṇaiḥ aikarūpyāt adrdhagomūtrikāyāṃ iti veditavyam| pādagomūtrikā punaḥ yatra prathamadvitayapādayoḥ tādṛśākṣarāṇāmaikarūpyaṃ tṛtīyacaturthayorapi anyavidhamaikarūpyaṃ tatraivāvagantavyā; udāharaṇaṃ yathā, "kāṅkṣan pulomatanayāstanatāḍitāni vakṣaḥ sthalocchitarayāñcanapīḍitāni| pāyādapāyabhayato namuciprāhārī māyāmapāsya bhavato'mbumucāṃ prāsarī| | " bandhaprākāro yathā pādagomūtrikābandhaḥ| kāṃ napu ma na sta tā tā kṣa lo ta yā na ḍi ni va stha chi ra ñca po tā pā da ya ya na ci hā yā pā bha to mu pra rī mā ma sya va 'mbu cāṃ sā ślokagomūtrikāyāṃ punaḥ ślokadvayam ūṅrghādhaḥkrameṇa sthāpanīyaṃ, tataḥ prathamaślokasya prathamapādīyaikāntaritavarṇānāṃ dvitīyaślokasya prathamapādīyaikāntaritavarṇaiḥ, tathā dvitīyādipādānāmapi ekāntaritavarṇānāṃ dvitīyādipādīyaikāntaritavarṇaiḥ ekarīpyamavagantavyam) | yathetiudāharaṇārtham| | 78| | 

ślokādrdhagomūtrikāmudāharati, madana iti| ayaṃ madanaḥ kāmaḥ madirākṣīṇāṃ mattakhañjananayananayanānām, apāṅgameva astraṃ ysaya tathābhūtaḥ san, yadi jayet māṃ praharet ityarthaḥ, tat tadā, madenaḥ mama pāpaṃ, kṣīṇaṃ, syāditi śeṣaḥ, tadā ca anaṅgāya madanāya, añjaliṃ dade puṣpāñjali dadāmi ityarthaḥ, aham iti śeṣaḥ, yadi ahaṃ kāminībhiḥ sakaṭākṣamīkṣye tadā kṛtārtho bhavāmi iti bhāvaḥ| | (atra pūrvādrdhottarādrdhayorviṣamavarṇāṇi ekarūpāṇi, samavarṇāni bhinnarūpāṇi ityekāntaratvenaikarūpyāt gomūtrikābandhalakṣaṇasaṅgatiḥ| viṣamavarṇaikarūpye bandhalikhitaślokottarādrdhasya prathamākṣaramārabhya pūrvādrdhasya dvitīyākṣaraṃ paṭhanīyaṃ, punarapi parādrdhatṛtīyākṣaraṃ paṭhitvā pūrvādrdhacaturthākṣaramityevaṃtakrameṇa paṭhane pūrvādrdhaṃ niṣpannaṃ syāt, tataḥpūrvādrdhaprathamākṣaramārabhya uttarādrdha paṭhanīyam| samavarṇānāmaikarūpyasthaletu pṛrvādrdhaprathamākṣaramārabhya uttarādrdhaṃ niṣpannaṃ syāt, tataḥ pūrvādrdhaprathamākṣaramārabhyaiva uttarādrdhaṃ paṭhanīyam| samavarṇānāmaikarūpyasthale tu pūrvādrdhaprathamākṣaramārabhyaiva pūrvādrdham uttrādrdhaprathamākṣaramārabhya ca uttarādrdhaṃ paṭhanīyaṃ| viṣamavarṇānāṃ pūrvoktasamavarṇavat paṭhanaṃ, tena ca sampūrṇaśloko niṣpadyate iti adrdhagomūtriki| bandhalikhamaparakāro yathā, etadviparītāni api sambhavanti, diṅyātramevātra darśitamiti| adrdhagomūtrikā bandhaḥ| dama rā ṇā pā strī ye yama ma no di kṣī ma ṅā ja da deya tat ṇa na yāñ liṃ de ākāracitrabandhacitrayostu bahavo bhedāḥ ; te tu khaṅgamurajapadmamuṣalaśaktihalaprabhṛtayaḥ anyairuktā jñeyāḥ| tatra khaṅgabandho yathā, "mārāriśakrarāmebhamukhairāsāraraṃhasā| sārārabdhastavā nityaṃ tadārttiharaṇakṣamā| | " "mātā natānāṃ saṃghaṭṭaḥ śriyāṃ bādhitasambhramā| mānyā'tha sīmā rāmāṇāṃ śaṃ me diśyīdumā'drijā| | " anena ślokadvayena khaṅgabandhaḥ prastīyryate| tatra muṣṭerupayryadhaḥ śākhādvayam, upari śākhācatuṣpathe prāthamika "mā" kāraṃ vinyasya tiyryagadaḥ krameṇa "rāri" ityādi "ha"kārāntāścaturdaśa varṇāne nyasedityekā dhārā| tataḥ khaṅgaśikhāyāṃ śliṣṭaṃ "sā" iti nyaset, tatastiyryagaṅrghakrameṇa "rārabdha" ityādivarṇacaturdaśakaṃ vilikhya aparāṃ dhārāṃ kuyryāt, "mā"kāraḥ "mā"kāraṃ praviṣṭaḥ; evamagre'pi tatraiva praviṣṭaṃ kalpayitvā dakṣiṇaśākhāyāṃ "tā" ityādivarṇasaptakaṃ vinyasya vāmaśākhāyāṃ tiyryagvilomena "śri" kārādivarṇasaptakaṃ likhet; "mā" kāracatuṣpathe prāthamika "mā"kāraṃ praviṣṭaḥ, tatastameva "mā" kāramārabhya adho'dhaḥ "nyāthasī" iti trayo varṇā muṣṭau lekhyāḥ| tato'dhaścatuṣpathamadhye tadantima"mā" kāraṃ vinyasya taddakṣiṇaśākhāyāṃ "rāmāṇāṃ śaṃ" iti caturo varṇān vinyaset, tato vāmalākhāyāṃ vilomena "me diśyādu" iti caturo varṇān likhet| "mā" kāraḥ adhaścatuṣpathasthe "mā" kāraṃ praviṣṭaḥ| tato muṣṭeradhaḥ tsaruke "drijā" iti varṇadvayaṃ lekhyamiti khaṅganiṣpattiḥ| murajabandho yathā, "sā senā gamanārambhe rasenāsīdanāratā| tāranādajanā mattadhīranāgamanāmayā| | " atra paṅkticatuṣṭayena likhitasya pādacatuṣṭayasyādhaḥkrameṇa yathākramaṃ prathamadvitīyatṛtīyacaturthānūṅrghakramenaṇa pañcamaṣaṣṭhasaptamāṣaṭamān varṇān gṛhutvā prathamaḥ pādaḥ| dvitīyāt prathamaṃ prathamāt dvitīyatṛtīyau dvitīyāt caturthapañcamī prathamāt ṣaṣṭhasaptamau dvitīyadaṣṭamaṃ gṛhitvā dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ| tṛtīyāt prathamaṃ tuyryāt dvitīyatṛtīyo tṛtīyāt tuyryapañcamau tuyryāt ṣaṣṭhasaptamau tṛtīyādaṣṭamaṃ gṛhitvā tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ| pādacatuṣṭayoṅrghakrameṇa prathamadvitīyatṛtīyacaturthānadhaḥkrameṇa pañcamaṣaṣṭhasaptamāṣṭamān gṛhitvā caturthāpādaḥ pāṭhyaḥ, evaṃ murajasiddhiḥ| tatra murajabandhaprakāro yathā, tathā padmabandho yathā, "bhāsate pratibhāsāra! rasābhātā hatāvibhā| bhāvitātmā śabhā vāde devābhā vata te sabhā| | " atrāṣṭadikṣu vitatāṣṭadalaṃ padmaṃ lekhyaṃ, tatra karṇikāyāmādyaḥ "mā" kāraḥ lekhyaḥ, tato dale nirgamapraveśarītyā dvau dvau varṇau lekhyau, kintu digdaleṣu nirgamaḥ praveśaśca, tatra ca prācyadale ādau nigamaḥ ślokāntapraveśca; dakṣiṇottaradalayoḥ nirgatyaiva praveśaḥ, paścimadale tu praviśyaiva nirgamaḥ| āgneyavāyavyadalayoḥ praveśa eva, naiṛteśānayostu nirgama eva iti aṣṭadalapadmabandhaḥ| tathā coktaṃ, "karṇikāyāṃ nyasedekaṃ dve dve dikṣuvidikṣu ca| praveśanirgamau dikṣu kuyyādaṣṭalāmbuje| | " iti| evamanye'pi muṣalādayo veditavyā iti dik) | | 79| | 

tatra padmabanadhanadhakāro yathā, atha adrdhabhramaṃ sarvakobhadrañca nirūpayati, prāhuriti| yadi ślokādrdhabhramaṇaṃ ślokasya ślokīyapādacatuṣṭasyetyarthaḥ, adrdhena adrdhamārgeṇa, anulomavilomātmakayoruccāraṇamārgayormadhye kevalamanulomamārgeṇeti, bhāvaḥ, bhramaṇaṃ bhramaṇena pādopasthitiriti yāvat, tadā adrdhabhramaṃ nāma citraṃ prāhuḥ| yadi sarvataḥ anulomapratilomābhyāṃ mārgābhyām ityarthaḥ, bhramaṇaṃ, tat tadā, sarvatobhadraṃ nāma citram, iṣṭam| ( tathā hi, dvividhamidam aṣṭākṣaravṛttighaṭitameva prāyaśo dṛśyate, tatra prathamaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭikoṣṭhāni aṣṭapaṅktidhaṭitāni lekhyāni, tataḥ ādyapaṅkticatuṣṭaye krameṇa pādacatuṣṭayavarṇā niveśanīyāḥ, tataśca nimnapaṅkticatuṣṭaye caturthatṛtīyadvitīyaprathamapādā vaiparītyena lekhyāḥ ; tatra adrdhabhrame adhaḥ paṅkticatuoṣṭaye parāvṛttyā sarvatobhadre tu parāvṛttyā samāvṛttyā ca caturthādipādalekhanama iti bhedaḥ| āvṛttikramastu adrdhabhrame catuṣpādātmikāyām ūṅrghapaṅktau vāmāt dakṣiṇataḥ, tathā pādacatuṣṭayātmikāyām adhaḥpaṅktau dakṣiṇāt vāmataḥ, evaṃ vāmasyoṅrghakoṣṭhādadhaḥkrameṇa dakṣiṇasya dhaḥ koṣṭhāt ūṅrghakrameṇa ca anulomāvṛttyā prathamadipādopasthitiḥ| sarvatobhadre tu dakṣiṇāt vāmataḥ vāmāddakṣiṇataśca ūṅrghadadhaḥkrameṇa adhasta ūṅrghakrameṇa ca anulomapratilomābhyāṃ sarvata āvṛttyā pādopasthitiriti bodhyam| evaṃ paṭhitibhaṅgiviśeṣātmakasya gaticitrasyaiva bhedasvarūpau adrdhabhramasarvatacobhadrau granthakṛtā darśitau, etasyaiva gaticitrasyānye'pi bhedā gatapratyāgatādayaḥ granthāntarādavagantavyāḥ| tatra diṅamātraṃ yathā, " vāraṇāgagabhīrā sā sārābhīgagaṇāravā| kāritāribadhā senā nāsedhā varitārikā| | " iti| atra prathamatṛtīyaṭapādayorānulomyena varṇāvalīṃ gṛhitvā tatpratilomarūpau dvitīyacaturthapādau gṛhyate, ata paṭhiterrṇādvarṇāntarasañcārāt gatapratyāgatākhyāṃ gaticitram| tathā ślokāntaraṃ yathā, " vāhanā'jani mānāse sārājāvanamā tataḥ| mattasāragarājebhe bhārīhāvajjnadhvani| | atra prātilomyena paṭhanādaparaḥ śloko bhavati, yathā, " nidhvanajjavahārībhā bheje rāgāsāttamaḥ| tatamānavajārāsā senā mānijanāhavā| | "ityādi) | | 80| | 

tatrādrdhamamudāharati, manobhaveti| he manobhava! kāma! , he nata! kāmijana namaskṛta! , tava anīkaṃ sainyarūpā, māninī iyaṃ mānavatī, udayāya vṛddhaye, vijayāyetyarthaḥ, taveti śeṣaḥ, na iti na, api tu udayāyayaiva| nanu vijayināṃ sainyam aparādhidaṇḍakaṃ, tvantu nāparādhī, tat tava atra kimityāha, bhayāditi| vayam enomayāḥ pāpinaḥ aparādhina ityartaḥ, mā vā na vā, kintu bhayāt ameyāmāḥ ameyaḥ aparimeyaḥ, āmaḥ rogaḥ yeṣāṃ tathoktāḥ, yadi ca vayamanaparādhinaḥ tathā'pi bhṛśaṃ bhayātrtā jātāḥ smetyarthaḥ| (asyodāharaṇāntaraṃ yathā, "sasatvaratide nityaṃ sadarāmarṣanāśini| tvarādhikakasaṃnāde ramakatvamakarṣati| | " iti) | | 81| | 

sarvatobhadramudāharati, seti| (atrāyaṃ padavibhāgaḥ, sā, amāyāmāmāyā, māsā; mārānāyāyānārāmā; yānāvārārāvā, anāyā; māyā, rāmā, mārāyasa amā iti) amāyāmāmāyā amāyasya akapaṭasya, amasya aparimitasya, āmasya rogasya, kāmapīḍaāyā ityarthaḥ, āḥ āgamanaṃ yayā tathoktā, tathā mārānāyāyānārānā māraḥ kāma eva, ānāyaḥ jālaṃ, bandhanahetutvāt iti bhāvaḥ, tasya āyānena āgamanena, ārāmaḥ prītiḥ yasyāḥ tādṛśī, satatakāmavyāpāravatī ityarthaḥ, kiñca yānāvārārāvā yānaṃ videśagamanam, āvārayati niṣedhayatīti yānāvāraḥ videśagamanapratiṣedhakaḥ, ārāvaḥ, vacanaṃ yasyāḥ tathābhūtā, madhurabhāṣaṇena mama videśagamanaṃ vāritavatītyarthaḥ, kiñca anāyā nāstī nāyaḥ nayaḥ, nītirityarthaḥ, ("nayo nāye" ityamaraḥ) yasyāḥ tādṛśī, mama pravāsākaraṇe kāyryahāniḥ bhavenna veti vivekarahitā ityarthaḥ, tathā māyā māṃ lakṣmīṃ yātīti tathoktā, atisundarītyarthaḥ, sā smṛtyupasthitā ityarthaḥ, rāmā ramaṇī, matpreyasīti śeṣaḥ, māsā candreṇa, ("chāyāmṛgadharo rājā māḥ" iti candrapayryāye trikāṇḍaśeṣaḥ) amā saha, ("amā saha samīpe ca" ityamaraḥ) mārāya mama vināśāya, tasyāḥ smaraṇaṃ candrodayaśca samprati māṃ bhṛśaṃ vyākulayatītyarthaḥ| (kāntāmanusmarato virahiṇo vilāravacanamidam| udāharaṇāntaraṃ yathā, " de vā kā ni ni kā vā de vā hi kā sva sva kā hi vā| kā kā re bha bha re kā kā ni sva bha vya vya bha sva ni| | " iti| atra paṅkticatuṣṭayena likhanam, anulomavilomābhyāmupayryadhaḥkrameṇa adhaḥ uparikrameṇa ca pāṭhaḥ| kiñca pādādrdhamātraṃ likhitvā pratilomena paṭhane'pi sampūrṇa eva pādo niṣpadyate iti sarvata āvṛtyā ślokaniṣpatterayaṃ sarvatobhadrabandha ityavagantavyam) | | 82| | 

prācīnaiścitrālaṅkāratvenaṅgīkṛtasya svarādiniyamasya bahubhedasyāpi duṣkaraṃ bhedacatuṣṭayaṃ pratyekaśo darśayannāha, ya iti| svarasthānavarṇānāṃ svarā akārādayaḥ, sthānāni kaṇṭhādīni, kaṇṭhyādivarṇā ityārthaḥ, tathā varṇāḥ kaṇṭhyatālavyādyaviśeṣeṇa vyañjanamātrāṇītyarthaḥ, teṣāṃ yo'sau niyamaḥ caturbhireva svaraiḥ tribhireva dvābhyāmeva ekenaiva tathā tādṛśaiḥ sthānaiḥ varṇaiḥ vā kāvyaṃ racayitavyamityādyavadhāraṇamityarthaḥ, prācīnairalaṅkāratayā ukta iti śeṣaḥ, duṣkareṣu āyāsakaraṇīyeṣa, eṣu madhye, iṣṭaḥ duṣṭakaratvena abhimataḥ, caturādisvarasthānavarṇaniyama eva āyāsakaratayā darśayitumucita ityarthaḥ, catuḥprabhṛti catvāraḥ catuścatuḥsaṅkhyākāḥ svarasthānavarṇāḥ, prābhṛtayaḥ ādibhūtā yatra tadyathā tathā, ( kriyāviśeṣaṇamidaṃ, caturaditvañca ekādyapekṣayā iti bodhyam) tataśca catuḥsvarādyārabhya ekasvarādipayryantam ityarthaḥ, niyamaḥ iti śeṣaḥ, daśryate, catustridvyekarūpatvāt catvāro bhedā daśryante ityarthaḥ, paraḥ anyaḥ pañcādiḥ, sukaraḥ anāyāsaniṣpādyaḥ, atastadudāharaṇaṃ yathāyathaṃ mṛgyaṃ, duṣkarasādhanārthameva prāyāsaḥ, granthabāhulyabhiyā sukarāṇāṃ pradarsanaṃ prātyākhyātamiti bhāvaḥ| ( atredamavadheyaṃ yat, ṣaḍbhedeṣu citrālaṅkāreṣu madhye gaticitraṃ kiyat darśayitvā svaracitraṃ sthānacitraṃ varṇacitramiti trividhacitraṃ tathā teṣāñca prātyekaśaścaturbhedatayā dvādaśavidhatvañca ślokenānena sūcitamiti) | | 83| | 

tatra prathamaṃ dīrghacatuḥsvaramudāharati, āmnāyānāmiti| āmnāyānāṃ śrutinām, antyā caramāsa, vāk upaniṣaditi yāvat, gītiḥ gānāni, ītīḥ ativṛṣṭyādyupaplavarūpāḥ, cittakṣobhajanakatvāditi bhāvaḥ, tathā prītiḥ putradārādiṣu prāṇayān, bhītiḥ bhayaṅkarīrityartaḥ, putrādīnāṃ viyogādinā duḥkhajananāditi bhāvaḥ, āha bravīti, ataḥ kāraṇāt bhogaḥ saṅgītādiviṣayopabhogaḥ, rogaḥ vyādhisvarūpaḥ, tathā modaḥ vaiṣayikānandaḥ, mohaḥ ajñānameva ityapyāha iti śeṣaḥ, gītiḥ ītīḥ, prītiḥ bhītīḥ kevalametadeva na āha, paraṃ bhogaḥ rogaḥ modaḥ mohaḥ ityapyāha tasmāt etat sarvaṃ heyamiti bhāvaḥ ; tarhi kiṃ katrtavyamityāha, kṣeme deśe puṇyabhūmau, dhyeye paramātmani, dhecche dhānamiti dhāḥ dhāraṇā, manaḥsamādhānam ityarthaḥ, icchā tatpraptyabhilāṣaśca te, vidheye ita śeṣaḥ| ( atra "dhecche" ityādau "cecchet kṣeme deśe" iti pāṭhāntare dhyeye dhyātavya, kṣeme kleśarahite, deśe viṣaye, mokṣalakṣaṇe ityarthaḥ, icchecca icchāṃ kiyryāt iti| | " dhecche" ityatra " dhyecche" iti pāṭhe dhyā dhyānamityarthaḥ| atra ā ī o e iti caturbhireva dīrghasvaraiḥ padyabandhaḥ| vidyunmālā vṛttamidam ) | | 84| | 

hrasvatrisvaramudāharati, kṣitīti| kṣiteḥ pṛthivyāḥ, vijitiḥ vijayaḥ, sthitermayryādāyāḥ, varṇāśramalakṣaṇāyā iti bhāvaḥ, vihitiḥ vidhānaṃ, te eova vrate niyamau, kṣitivijitisthitivihitivrate, tayoḥ ratiḥ anurāgaḥ yeṣāṃ tathoktāḥ, tathā parā uttamā, gatijrñānaṃ, ("gatiḥ strī mārgadayoḥ jñāne yātrā'bhyupāyayoḥ" iti medinī) yeṣāṃ tādṛśāḥ, kuravaḥ kuruvaṃśajā nṛpāḥ, yudhi yuddhe, svaṃ nijam, arikulaṃ śatrusamūham, uru atyarthaṃ yathā tathā, rurudhuḥ pratiruddhavantaḥ, tathā guru atiśayaṃ yathā tathā, dudhuvuḥ kampitavantaḥ| (atra i a u iti tribhireva hrasvasvaraiḥ padyabandhaḥ| tvaritagatirvṛttamidam) | | 85| | 

dīrghadvisvaramudāharati, śrīdīptī iti| śrīḥ lakṣmīḥ, dīptīḥ kāntiśca te, hrīḥ lajjā, kārttiḥ yaśaśca te, dhīrbuddhiḥ, nītiḥ nayaśca te, goḥ vāk, vāgamitā iti madhuravāk iti vā bhāvaḥ, prītiḥ santoṣaśca te, dve dve etat dvayaṃ dvayaṃ, te eva, edhete vadrdhete, ye ime dve dve, deveśe devānāmūśvare, indre'pi ityartaḥ, na, vidyate iti śeṣaḥ| (atra ī e iti dvābhyāmeva dīrghasvarābhyāṃ padābandhaḥ| vāṇīvṛttamidam| hrasvadvisvarodāharaṇaṃ yathā, " kṣitisthitimitikṣiptividhivinnidhisiddhiliṭ! | mama tryakṣa! namaddakṣa! hara! smarahara! smara| | " iti) | | 86| | 

dīrghaikasvaramudāharati, seti| padyamidaṃ sarvatodrodāharaṇatvena pūrvaṃ likhitaṃ vyākhyātañca| (atra ā ityanenaikasvareṇa padyabandhaḥ| udāharaṇāntaraṃ yathā, "vaidhairainairaiśairaindraiḥraijairailairjainaiḥ saiddhaiḥ| maitrairnaikairdhaiyryairvairaidaiḥ svaiḥ svairairdaivaistaistaiḥ| | " iti| ḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍalasvaikasvaraṃ yathā, " uruguṃ dyuguru yutsu cukruśustuṣṭuvuḥ puru| lulubhuḥ pupuṣurmutsu mumuhurnumuhurmuhuḥ| | " iti) | itthaṃ svarāṇāṃ catvāro bhedā darśitā iti bodhyam| | 87| | 

atha sthānaniyam darśayitavye prathamaṃ nirauṣṭhyaṃ catuḥsthānaṃ darśayati, nayaneti | he nayanānandajanane! netrotsavadāyini! , aṅgane! sundari! aghane megharahite, ata eva nakṣatragaṇaśālini tārānikarabhūṣite, gagane ākāśe, sakṛt ekavāraṃ, dṛṣṭiḥ netraṃ, dīyatāṃ nikṣipyatām| (nayanānandajanane iti gagane ityasyāpi viśeśaṇaṃ saṅgacchate| māninīṃ sāntvayato nāyakasya tādṛśagagane dṛṣṭipotokteḥ kāmoddīpakatvāt tādṛśagaganasya anāyāsenaiva mānabhaṅgaḥ syāditi tātpayryam| atra dantyatālavyakaṇṭhyamūdrdhanyaireva varṇaiḥ padyanibandhanāt catuḥ sthānatvamavagantavyamṣamūdrdhanyavirahitaṃ catuḥsthānaṃ yathā, " śalabhā iva dhāvantaḥ sāyakāstasya bhūbhujaḥ| nipetuḥ sāyakacchinnstena saṃyugasīmani| | " iti| niṣkaṇṭhyaṃ yathā, " bhūrabhitiṃ pṛthiprītimurumūrtiṃta parusthitim| viriÏñca sūcikucidhīḥ! śucibhirnucubhirdinu| | " iti| nistālavyaṃ yathā, " sphuratkuṇḍalaratnaughamaghavaddhanukarburaḥ| meghānādo'tha saṅgame prāvṛṭkālavadābabhau| | " iti| nirdantyaṃ yathā, " pāmpāpahārī saṇakarmaśauṇḍaścaṇḍaīśamiśro mama cakrapāṇiḥ| bhūyācchriyāpaśramayekṣyamāṇo mokṣayā mukhyāmarapūgapūjyaḥ| | " iti) | | 88| | 

tristhānaṃ nirauṣṭhyamūdrdhanyaṃ darśayati, alīti| he ghamastani! niviḍapayodhare! , avalaya iva bhramarā iva, nīlāḥ kṛṣṇavarṇāḥ, alakalatāḥ latāvadvinyastāścūrṇakuntalā yatra tat, nalinacchāye padmasadṛśe, nayane yatra tādṛsaṃ, tathā śaśinaḥ kāntiriva kāntiryasya tathābhūtaṃ, te tava, ānanaṃ vadanaṃ, kaṃ janaṃ, na hanti? nākulayati? api tu sarvamevetyarthaḥ| ( atra kaṇṭhyadantyatālavyaireva varṇaiḥ padyabandhaḥ iti trīṇi sthānāni madrdhanyauṣṭhyavarṇarahitānīti jñeyam| evaṃ nirauṣṭhyadantyaṃ tristhāmaṃ yathā, "jīyājjagajjyeṣṭhagariṣṭhacārascakrārccaṣā kṛṣṇakahākāyaḥ| harirhiraṇyākṣaśarīrahārī khageśagaḥ śrī śrayaṇīyaśayyaḥ| | " iti) | | 89| | 

danatyakaṇṭhyaiḥ dvisthānacitramudāharati, anaṅgeti| he sadānagha! sadā anagha! duḥkhavivarjita! , sarvāvastāsveva vyathārahita! ityartaḥ, niścinta! iti bhāvaḥ, ("aghantu vyasane duḥkhe durite ca napuṃsakam" iti medinī) tathā he sadānandanatāṅga| sadā ānandena gītavāditrādyāmodena, natāni vasīkṛtāni, vyāpṛtāni iti yāvat, aṅgāni avayavā yasya tatsambuddhau, sadaṅgamṛnā sādhvī nārī, apīti śeṣaḥ, asaṅgasaṅgataḥ nāsti saṅgo yeṣāṃ te asaṅgāḥ dirjanāḥ, teṣāṃ saṅgaḥ samparkaḥ tasmāt durjanasamparkāt ityarthaḥ, anaṅgalaṅghanayā kāmākramaṇena, lagnāḥ saṃsṛṣṭāḥ, jātāḥ ityarthaḥ, nānā vividhāḥ, ātaṅkāḥ santāpāḥ śaṅkāḥ vā ("ātaṅko rogasantāpaśṅkāsu murajadhvanau" iti medinī) yasyāṃ tādṛsī bhavati, bhatrtuviraheṇa satī api durjanāsaṅgena bhraśyatīti bhāvaḥ| (tasmāt bhāyryāṃ praticintaya kevalaṃ vṛthā''modenālamiti sakhāyaṃ prati kasyaciduktiḥ| atra dantyakaṇṭhyaireva padyaracanā iti dvisthānatvam| "natāṅgāsaṅgasaṅgataḥ" ityatra "natāṅgīmaṅgasaṅgatā! " ityapi pāṭhaḥ| tatra he sadānanda! sadaiva priyālābhenānandayukta! iti pṛthak sambodhanam; tathā he natāṅgyāḥ kasyaścidanyasyā nāyikāyāḥ saṅge saṅgata! iti sambodhanāntaraṃ, tava sadaṅganā kāmākramaṇāya nānārūpatayā vatrtate, atastāmanukampasva iti sopālambhaprārthanamityarthaḥ| pāṭho'yantu na manoramaḥ, īkārasya kālavyatvāt kevalaṃ dantyakaṇṭhātvahāneriti) | | 90| | 

kaṇṭhāvarmairekasthānacitramudāharati, agā iti| (padavibhāgo yathā, āgāḥ, gāṃ, gāṅgakākākagāhaka, aghakakākahā; ahāhāṅga, khagāṅkāgakaṅka, agakhagakākaka iti) he gāṅgakākākagāhaka! gaṅgāyā idaṃ gāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍagaṃ, yat kaṃ jalaṃ, tasya ākākaḥ saśabdavakragatiḥ, (āṅpūrvāt " kai śabde" ityasmāt bhāvekvipi ākāḥ, "aka kuṭulagatau" ityasmāt bhāve ṅapratyate akaḥ) ākā saha akaḥ ākākaḥ taraṅga ityarthaḥ, taṃ gāhate iti tathoktaḥ, tatsambuddau, he gaṅgājalataraṅgāvagāhin! ityarthaḥ, te tathā he ahāhāṅga! hāhāṃ dīnatāsūcakadhvaniviśeṣam, aṅgati gacchatīti hāhāṅgaḥ, na hāhāṅgaḥ ahāhāhgaḥ tatsambuddhau, he sadānanda! ityarthaḥ, kiñca he khagāṅkāgakaṅka! khaṃ gaganaṃ gacchantīti khagāḥ sūyryādayaḥ, te aṅkāściḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanāni yasya sa khagāṅkaḥ, sa ca asau agaḥ parvataśceti khagāṅkāgaḥ sumerurityarthaḥ, taṃ kaṅkate gacchatīti tathoktaḥ, ("kaki gatau" ityasmāt aṇapratyayaḥ) tatsambuddhau, puṇyabalena sumeruparvatapayryantamamanārha! ityarthaḥ, punaśca he agakhagakākaka! agati gacchati iti agaṃ naśvaramityarthaḥ, khāni indriyāṇi gacchatīti khagam indriyaviṣayaṃ, tatasca agaṃ khagaca yat kaṃ sukhaṃ, tadarthaṃ na kakate na icchati, na abhilaṣatītyarthaḥ, iti agakhagakākakaḥ tatsambuddhau, ("kak garveccālaulyaiṣu" ityasya rūpam) indriyasukhena anāsakta ityarthaḥ, (“ ahāhāṅgakhagāṅkāgakaṅkāgakhagakākaka iti samastāpāṭhe tu, hānaṃ hāḥ, na hāḥ ahāḥ, tāṃ jihīte prāpnotīti ahāhaṃ, tādṛśamaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍagaṃ yasya saḥ akṣayaśarīra ityarthaḥ, yaḥ khagaḥ garuḍaḥ sūyryo vā, sa aṅkaḥ ciḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanabhūto yasya kādṛsaḥ, agaḥ parvataḥ, samerurityarthaḥ, tatra ye kaṅkāḥ tadākhyāḥ, agakhagāḥ vṛkṣavāsinaḥ pakṣiṇaḥ, te‘pi kākakāḥ gamanaspṛhayalavo yasya tatsambuddhe patatribhirapyabhinanditagamana ityarthaḥ) tvam aghakakākahā aghakāni kutsitāni pāpāni eva, kākāḥ vāyasāḥ, anarthahetutvāditi bhāvaḥsa, tān hantīti tathoktaḥ apāpaḥ, san, gāṃ pṛthivīm, agāḥ gatavānasi, pradakṣaṇīcakarthetyarthaḥ| yadvā, gā svargaṃ, ("svargeṣupaśuvāgvajradiṅnetraghṛṇībhūjale| lakṣyadṛṣṭyā striyāṃ puṃsi gauḥ" ityamaraḥ) agāḥ yāyā ityarthaḥ| ( āśaṃsāyāṃ bhūtapratyayaḥ kasyaciddeśapayryāṭakasya stutiriyam| atra kaṇṭhyaireva varṇaiḥ padyaracanā) | | 91| | 

atha citrālaṅkārabhede varṇaniyame darśayitavye svarasya pṛthak nirdeśāt varṇapadena vyañjanarūpavarṇasyaiva grahaṇamati vyañjanātmakaṃ caturvarṇamudāharati, re re iti| ( re re iti nīcasambodhanasūcakamavyayam) re re māmama! māyāṃ lakṣmyāṃ, mama ( ithyavyayaṃ) mamatā ityarthaḥ, yasya saḥ tatsambuddhau, kṛpaṇa ityarthaḥ, ("māyām amama amamatvaṃ yasya saḥ lakṣmīśūnyaḥ" iti kecit) māṃ mā mā na na, ( iti niṣedhavācakaṃ, sambhramādyatireke dviruktiriti) ama gaccha, matsamīpaṃ mā gacchetyarthaḥ, tvamiti śeṣaḥ, yataḥ kākaḥ vāyasaḥ, kiṃ kekākākukaḥ kekā mayūradhvaniḥ, tasyāḥ kākuḥ madanitavikāraḥ, taṃ kāyati śabdāyate, śabdena prākāsayatītyarthaḥ, ("kai śabde" ityasmāt ḍapratyayaḥ) tādṛśaḥ bhavati? iti śeṣaḥ, api tu naivetyarthaḥ, kāko yathā mayūrakekābhilāṣe vyarthamanoratha eva bhavati tathā matprāptyabhilāṣo tvamapīti bhāvaḥ ; kiñca rorūrurūrorugāgogaḥ rorūyate bhayena punaḥ punaratuśayena vā rautacīti rorūḥ, (rauteryaṅlugantāt kvip) sa ca asau rururmugaviśeṣaśceti rorūruruḥ, tasya urasaḥ vakṣasaḥ, yā ruk śaravedhajanitā vyathā, sā eva āgaḥ aparādhaḥ, pāpamityarthaḥ, tat gacchati prāpnotīti tatābhūtaḥ, nirīhajīvahiṃsakatvāt prāpīyānityarthaḥ sa nicakāyryakāritayā tvaṃ na me yogya iti bhāvaḥ ; punaśca tvam agāṅgagaḥ agasya parvatasya, aṅgam ekadeśaṃ, gacchati adhivasatītyarthaḥ, tathoktaḥ pārvatya ityartaḥ, nikṛṣṭadeśavāsitvācca tvaṃ na me yogya iti bhāvaḥ ; tathā agaguḥ na gacchati na saṅgacchate iti agā asambaddhāit yarthaḥ, gauḥ vāk ("svargaiṣupaśuvāgvajradiṅnetraghṛṇibhūjale| lakṣyadṛṣṭyā striyāṃ puṃsi gauḥ" ityamaraḥ) ysaya tādṛsaḥ, asambadadhabhāṣītyarthaḥ, ato'pi tvaṃ na me yogya iti bhāvaḥ| (vārāṅranāmabhilāṣantaṃ kañcitaṃ vyādhaṃ prati tasyāḥ pratyākhyānoktiriyam| atra "ragakama" ici caturbhireva vyañjanavarṇaiḥ padyabandhaḥ| varṇapadena ca padyapūrakavarṇānāṃ grahaṇaṃ , tena aṅgeti ṅakārayoge'pi na cāturvaṇryavyāghātaḥ, tasya padyapūrakatvābhāvātṣa| ṅakārayogādidamudāharaṇaṃ keṣāñcidaśuddhatvenābhimataṃ cet śiddhodāharaṇaṃ yathā, "jajaujojājijijjājī taṃ tato'titatātitut| bhābhā'bhobhābhibhībhābhūrārāririrī raraḥ| | " iti) | | 92| | 

varṇacitreṣu trivyañjanamudāharati, devānāmati| devānām indrādīnāṃ, nandano daityadamanāt prītijananaḥ, tathā vedanindino vedanindakasya nāstikajanasya ityarthaḥ, ("vedanindinaḥ" ityatra " vedanindinām" iti pāṭhāntaram) nodanaḥ nirāsaka) , devaḥ nṛsiṃharūpī bhagavān, dānavān nandayati tribhuvanavijayena santoṣayatīti tathoktasya dānavandinaḥ hiraṇyakasipoḥ, dāne vakṣovidāraṇe, ("do avakhaṇḍane" ityasmāt anaṭprātyayaḥ) nādane siṃhanādena, divam antarīkṣaṃ dudāva tāpitavān| ( atra "davana" iti tribhireva vyañjanaiḥ padyabandhaḥ) | | 93| | 

divyañjanātmakaṃ varṇacitramudāharati, sūririti| sūriḥ vidvān, tathā surāsurāsārisāraḥ surān asurāṃśca āsarati āskandati iti tathoktaḥ sāro balaṃ yasya tādṛśaḥ kiñca sasūruḥ su śobhanau, ūrū sūrū, tābhyāṃ sahitaḥ vāmorurityarthaḥ, punasca surārasī surāyāṃ madhupāne, rasaḥ āsvādaḥ, anurāga ityartaḥ, vidyate asyeti surāraso, sa prasiddhaḥ, sīrī sīraṃ lāṅgasamasyāstīti tathoktaḥ baladevaḥ, sārasasārasāḥ ārasena uccaiḥ śabdena saha vatrtamānāḥ sārasāḥ uccaiḥ śabdaṃ kurvāṇāḥ, sarasi bhavāḥ sārasāḥ pakṣiviśeṣāḥ yāsu tāḥ, sarasoḥ tāḍaāgān, sasāra jalakrīḍaārthaṃ gatavān| (baladevasya jalakrītāprākaraṇoktaṃ padmitam| atra dvābhyāmeva "sara" iti varṇābhyāṃ padyabandhaḥ| yathā vā, " bhūribhirbhārirbhīraurbhūbhārairabhirebhire| bherīrebhibhirabhrābhairabhīrubhairibhāḥ| | " iti) | | 94| | 

citrālaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍakārabedamekavyañjanamudāharati, nūnamiti| ananena prabalena, anena purato dṛsyamānenetyartaḥ, enena etena, jhr"dvitīyāṭaussvenaḥ" iti idamaḥ etado vā enādeśaḥṭa ripuṇā iti bhāvaḥ, yadvā, ina prabhuḥ, a inaḥ ena aprabhuḥ, tena prabhuprati7eṇa, śatruṇā ityarthaḥ ānanena mukhainaiva karaṇena, bhrukuṭimateti bāvaḥ, naḥ asmākam, ananāni prāmāḥ, nūnaṃ niścitaṃ, nunnani apanītāni, na iti na, api tu nunnānyeva, asya bhrubhaÏṅga dṛṣṭveva vayaṃ mṛtaprāyāḥ, kā kathā samprahārasya iti bhāvaḥ ; parantu nanu bhoḥ| , inaḥ prabhuḥ, nā puruṣaḥ, asmākaṃ praburutyarityarthaḥ anān prāṇān, ninīḥ rakṣitamicchuḥ sannityartaḥ, (netumiṭacchatīti nidhātoḥ sanantāt kvipi prathamaikavacanam) na anenāḥ na apāpaḥ, bhavati iti śeṣaḥ, śatruvijitasya yuddhe maraṇaṃ jīvitādapi varam ato'nena yathāśakti yudjhaṃ katrtavyamiti bhāvaḥ| (ripuparājitasya kasyacit nṛpasya sainyānāṃ dainyaktiriyam| atra nakārarūpavarṇaireva padyabandaḥ| evaṃ kramasta sarvavyañjanavarṇairapi bandhe vaicitryātiśayāt tadapyatra kecit aṅgīkurvanti; tadudāharaṇaṃ yathā, "kaḥ khagaughāṅacicchaujā jhāññāṭoṭhīḍaḍaṃḍhaṇaḥ| tathodadhīna papharvābīrmayo rilvāśiṣāṃ sahaḥ| | " iti| tathā myarastajabhanagalāntā ye daśavarṇāśchando lakṣaṇasiddhaye nirṇītāḥ, tairnibaddhaṃ chando'kṣaravyañjanarūpamapyuktaṃ, yathā, "saratsarārātibhayāya jāgrato jagatyalaṃ stotṛjanasya jāyatām| smitaṃ smarārergirijāsyanīraje sametanetratritayasya bhūtaye| | " iti| tathā " sarigāmapadhānisca varṇāḥ sapta svarādayaḥ| ṣaḍjādikṛtasaṅketā dṛṣṭā gāndharvavedibhiḥ| | " ityunaktalakṣaṇamātravyañjanamayaṃ ṣaḍjādivyañjanaṃ yathā, " sā mamāridhamanīnidhāninī sāmadhāmadhanidhāmasādhinī| māninī sagarimā'papāpapā sāpagā samasamāgamā'samā| | " iti| evamanye'pi bhedā jñeyāḥ) | | 95| | 

citrālaṅkārasya duṣkaratayā granthabāhulyatayā ca kiyantaṃ darśayitvā tamupasaṃharan prahelikāṃ nirdiśati, itīti| iti uktaprākāreṇa, duṣkaramārgeṣapi catustridvekarūpatvāt kṛcchrasādhyaniyame'pi, kaścit alpamātra ityarthaḥ, kramaḥ niyamaḥ, ādarśitaḥ udāhṛtaḥ, ( etāvatā anye'pi duṣkarāścitrālaṅkārāḥ padmabandhaḥdayaḥ prācīnoktāḥ santi, granthavistārabhayena te nātroktāḥ, granthāntarataḥ jñātavyā iti jñāpitam) | punaḥ idānīṃ, prahelikāyāḥ prākārāṇāṃ viśeṣāṇāṃ, gatuḥ niyamaḥ, uddīsyate nirūpyate| (tallakṣaṇancu sāmānyata uktam; yathā, " prāhelikā tu sā jñeyā vacaḥ saṃvṛtikāri yat" iti| tataśca svābhilaṣitārthasya kathīñcit saṃvaraṇātmakavacanavinyāsaḥ prāheliketi lakṣaṇamavagantavyam) | | 96| | 

" ramasya paripanthitvāt nālaṅkāraḥ prāhelikā" ityuktyā yadi ca prāhelikāyā yamakādivat śabdārthopaskārakatvena rasānaguṇyābāvāt nālaṅkāratvaṃ, tathā'pi asyā upayogitvāmāha, krīḍati| krīḍaāgoṣṭhīṣu vihārasabhāsu, ye vinodāḥ prāmedāḥ, yadvā, krīḍaā parasparavākcātuyryarūpakautukaṃ, goṣṭhī vidagdhānāmāsanabandaḥ, vinodaḥ kāvyasamālocanena kālayāpanaṃ teṣu, tathā tajjñaiḥ prahelikābhijñaiḥ saha, ākīrṇe janasaṅkule deśaṃ, yat mantraṇaṃ guptabhā, ṇaṃ, parasparasaṃlāpaḥ ityarthaḥ, tasmin, tathā parasya boddhavyavyatiriktasya janasya, vyāmohane viseṣarūpeṇaarthābodhavaśena manasaḥ vyākulatāyām, athavā avabodhanirākaraṇe viṣaye, prahelitāḥ sopayogāḥ saprayojanāḥ, upakāriṇya ityarthaḥ| (tasmāt asyā krīḍaāgoṣṭhyādyupayogitve alaṅkāratvam anyatra doṣāvahatvamiti bodhyam) | | 97| | 

asyāḥ salakṣaṇanāmāni krameṇāha, āhuriti| padasandhinā padayoḥ suptiṅantādirūpayoḥ, sandhinā sānnidhyajanitasandhikāyryeṇa, gūḍhaḥ durbodhaḥ artho yasyāstādṛśīṃ, prahelikāṃ samāgatāṃ nāma āhuḥ| vañjiteti| anyatra vivakṣitārthabhinne, yatra yasmin sthale rūḍhena prasiddhena, śabdena yā vañcanā pratāraṇā, sā vañcitā vañcitākyā praheliketyarthaḥ | | 98| | 

vyatkrānteti| ativyāvahitānām atiśayena dūravarttipadānāṃ, prayogāt vinyāsāt, mohakāriṇī artāvabodhavaidhuyryavidhāyino, praheliketi śeṣaḥ, vyutkrāntā nāma| seti| yasyāñca prahelikāyāṃ, padāvakṛlī padasamūhaḥ, durbaudhaḥ aprasiddapadaprayogāt dugrrāhyaḥ, artho yasyāstādṛśī, sā prahelikā, pramuṣitā nāma syāt prakarṣeṇa buddhermuṣitatvādtyartaḥ| (vañcitāyām ekaṃ padaṃ durbodhārthamiha tu padāni, kiñca vañcitāyāṃ nānārtasya padasya aprasiddhārthe prayogaḥ iha tu ekārthānāmapi padānāmityato'pi anayorbhedaḥ) | | 99| | 

samānarūpeti| gauṇārthena lākṣaṇikārthena āropitaiḥ upacaritaiḥ, padaiḥ grathitā viracitā, prahelikti śeṣaḥ, samānarūpā nāma śakyārtalakṣyārthayoḥ dvayoḥ sādṛsyanibandhanatvāditi bhāvaḥ| paruṣeti| lakṣaṇasya sūtrasya, astitvamātreṇa kevalaṃ lakṣaṇamastīti hetoḥ, na tu śaktāśaktavivecanayā iti bhāvaḥ, vyutpāditā śrutiḥ prasiddhiḥ śabdaḥ vā yatra tādṛśī, prahelikā paruṣā nāma, aśaktinibandhanatvena śrotrayoḥ pāruṣyāvabodhāditi bhāvaḥ| | 100| | 

saṅkhyāteti| yatra saṅkhyānaṃ varṇagaṇanā saṅkhyāvācakaśabdaḥ vā, vyāmohasya viśeṣeṇa artāvabodhavaidhuyryasya, kāraṇaṃ hetuḥ, sā saṅkhyātā nāma prāhelikā, saṅkhyāghaṭitatvāditi bhāvaḥ| anyatheti| yatra vākyārthaḥ anyatā bhāsate āpātataḥ pratīyamānādarthāt anyarūpaḥ pratīyate, sā prahelikā, prakalpitā nāma, arthāntaraprakalpanāditi bhāvaḥ| | 101| | 

seti| yasyāṃ prahelikāyāṃ, nāmni saṃjñāviṣaye, nānārthakalpanā nānārthānāṃ śabdasya vividharitakatvāt bahūnāmarthānāṃ, kalpanā vikalpaḥ, jāyate iti śeṣaḥ, sā nāmāntaritā nāma, nāmāntareṇāntaritārthakatvāditi bhāvaḥ| nibhṛteti| yasyāñca tulyadharmaspṛśā prastutāprastutayoḥ sādhāraṇadharmaṃ spṛśantyā, girāvācā, nibhṛtaḥ gopitaḥ, anyaḥ aparaḥ, prakṛta ityarthaḥ, artho yatra sā nibhṛtā nāma| (asyāśca sādhāraṇadhamrmabalena viṣayasya sūcanāt samāsoktimūlatā bodhyeti) | | 102| | 

samānaśabdeti| upanyastena uktena, śabdānāṃ prākṛtārthāvabodhakapadānāṃ, payryāyeṇa nāmāntareṇa, sādhitā viracitā, prahelikā samānaśabdā nāma| ( atra ca śabdapayryāyatvaṃ lakṣaṇayā ekārthabodhakatvaṃ, na tvabhidhayā ekārtaśakyatvaṃ, tathātve arthasya saṃvaraṇīyatvābhāvane prākṛkānupayogitvāt prahelikaiva na bhavediti dhyeyam) | sammūḍhet| yā vāk, sākṣāt abhidhāyakabdena, nirdiṣṭaḥ nirūpitaḥ, artho yatra tādṛśī api, vācakaśabdena kathitārthā'pi ityarthaḥ, mūḍaye āpātato vyāmodvāya, bhavatīti śeṣaḥ, sā sammūḍhā nāma| | 103| | 

yogamāleti| yā prahelikā, yogānāṃ yaugikapadānāṃ, mālā samūhaḥ, ātmā svarūpaṃ yasyāṃ tādṛśī, sā yaugikapadamālayā rūḍhapadapratipādyanāmekaikānāmarthānāṃ bodhikā sā praheliketyarthaḥ, parihārikā nāma syāt| (pariharati jhaṭityartabodhaṃ vārayatīti vyutpattyā tathā vyapadeśaḥ, śaktilabhyārthāt yaugikārthasya nānākaṣṭakalpanāmūlatvena sahasā'vabodhavirahāditi bhāvaḥ) | ekacchanneti| yasyām āśritam ādheyaṃ, vyaktaṃ sphuṭam, āśrayasya ādhārasya tu, gopanaṃ, sā ekacchannā nāma ekaḥ āśrayaḥ, channaḥ nigūḍhaḥ yatra sā iti vyutpattyā tadākhyā| | 104| | 

seti| yasyām ubhayasya ādheyasya ādhārasya ca, gopanaṃ sā ubhayacchannā nāma, bhavet, ādheyādhārayoḥ ubhayorapi nibhṛtatvāt iti niṣkarṣaḥ| saṅkīrṇeti| yasyāṃ nānālakṣaṇānām uktānāṃ prahelikābhedānāṃ madhye dvitryādilakṣaṇānāṃ, saṅkaraḥ sāhityenāvasthitiḥ, sā saṅkīrṇā nāma| | 105| | 

etā iti| etāḥ samāgatāprabhṛtayaḥ, ṣoḍaṣa prahelikāḥ pūrvācāyryaiḥ nirdiṣṭāḥ aduṣṭatvena kathitā ityarthaḥ | duṣṭati| taiḥ pūrvācāyryaireva, anyāḥ aparāḥ, caturdaśa duṣṭāḥ prahelikāśca cyutadattākṣarādirūpāḥ api ityarthaḥ, adhītāḥ paṭhitāḥ, sadoṣatvena kīrttitā ityarthaḥ| (tatra prathamaṃ cyutākṣarādīnāṃ ṣaḍbhedā anyairuktāḥ yathā, "prahelikā sakṛt praśnaḥ sā'pi ṣoḍhā cyutākṣarā| dattākṣarobhayaṃ muṣṭirvindumatyatrvatyapi| | " iti| tāsu cyutākṣarā yathā, "payodharabharākrāntā saṃnamantī pade pade| padamekaṃ na kā yāti yadi hāreṇa varjjatā" atra vihaṅgikā kāhāreṇa varjjatā na yātīti vaktavye hāraṇe varjitā ityuktatvāt kā ityakṣarasya cyutiriti cyutākṣarā| dattākṣarā yathā, " kāntayānugataḥ ko'yaṃ pīnaskandho madoddhataḥ| mṛgāṇāṃ pṛṣṭhato yāti śaṃvaro rūḍayovanaḥ| | " atra śavara iti vaktavye śaṃvara ityukteranusvāro datta dattākṣarā| cyutadattākṣarā yathā, "vidagdhaḥ saraso rāgī nitambopari saṃsthitaḥ| tanvaṅgyāliṅgikaḥ kaṇṭhe kalaṃ kūjati ko viṭa? "| | atra viṭa ityasmin pade vikāre cyute ghakāre ca datte ghaṭaḥ kūjati ityāyātamiti cyitadattākṣarā| akṣaramuṣṭiryayā, "atiḥ atiḥ anma alaṃ prīdya radya jadya phadya| melā melā melaṃ melaṃ phasa phasa phasa phasa| | " iyañca akṣārāṇāṃ muṣṭirūpatvāt akṣaramuṣṭikā| atra pādaśaścatuṣpaṅktau likhitāyāṃ caturbhirmurajabandhaiḥ ślokaḥ upatiṣṭhati, yathā, "adya me saphalā prītiradya me saphalā ratiḥ| adya me saphalaṃ janma adya me saphalaṃ phalam| | " iti| vindumatī yathā, " tavāvavādaḥ pratyabdhi patākāḥ pratimaṅgaram| phalaṃ pratyadbhutopāyaṃ yaśāṃmi na nu na kvacit| | " ityanena ślokenoktārthasya yathāsthisvatasvarānusvāravisajrjanīyasaṃyogasya ślokāntarasya vindubhireva sūcanākrama iti vindumatī| yathā, 0u00ā0u00ā0ā0ā0ṃ0u0e 0ṃ0u0e 00ḥ| 0ā00e0ā00e0i0iḥ00rra0000i0rra 0ḥ| | etairvindubhirayaṃ ślekaḥ, " udadhāvudadhāvājñā saṃyuge saṃyugaṃ jayaḥ| sāhase sāhase siddhiḥ tava kītrtayaḥ| | " iti arthavatī yathā, "uttaptakāñcanābhāsaṃ sandaṣṭadaśanacchadam| sarasaṃ cumbyate hṛṣṭairvṛddhairapi kimujjvalam? "| | atra evaṃ praśne yakvāmraphalamutyuttaraṃ, tasya ca dvyarthapadaprayogeṇānavagateriyamarthaprahelikā iti| punaḥ ṣaḍḍḍitdh; vadhamāha, "kriyākārakasambandhaiḥ pādābhiprāyavastubhiḥ| gopitaiḥ ṣaḍḍḍitdh; vadhaṃ prāhurgūḍhaṃ gūḍhārthavedinaḥ| | " iti| kriyākārakasambandhaguptādīnāmudāharaṇaṃ, tathā antaḥpraśnavahi praśnādibedenāsyāḥ prābhūtatamaṃ bhedañca granthabāhulyabhiyā nātra pradarśitaṃ, bhojādau tu tāni dṛṣṭavyāni) | | 106| | 

yadi ca duṣṭāḥ prahelikāḥ pūrvācāyryairuktāstathā'pyasmākaṃ na tāsvabhirucirityāha, doṣāniti| vayaṃ punaḥ doṣān śābdabodhapratibandhakatvarūpānityarthaḥ, aparasaṅkhyeyān bahūn, manyamānāḥ jānantaḥ santaḥ, sādhvoreva utkṛṣṭā eva, doṣādhikyābhāvāt alaṅkāratvaṃ prāptāḥ samāgatāprabhṛtīḥ ṣoḍaśa eva nirdoṣāḥ prahelikāḥ ityarthaḥ, abhidhāsyāmaḥ udāhariṣyāmaḥ ; nanu sādhvīḥ prahelikā eva abhidhāsyanti cet bhavantaḥ kathaṃ śiṣyāṇāṃ duṣṭaprahelikājñānaṃ bhavet ityāśayenāha, tā iti| yāstu alakṣaṇāḥ pūrvoktalakṣaṇānantarbhūtāḥ, tāḥ duṣṭāḥsadoṣāḥ, tāsāmudāharaṇe na mama prayāsa iti bhāvaḥ| | 107| | 

tatra samāgatāmudāharati, neti| he ālohitekṣaṇe! āraktanayane! , kopāditi bhāvaḥ mayā mametyarthaḥ, ( ṣaṣṭhyarthetṛtīyā) cetaḥ gorasasya dugdhādeḥ, abhijñaṃ rasajñaṃ, na, dugdhādikaṃ mayā na hṛtamityarthaḥ, yadvā, gorasābhijñaṃ ceto mayā na, dhāyryate iti adhyāhṛtena kriyāpadenānvayaḥ ; kasmāt prakupyasi? ebhiḥ īdṛsaiḥ, asthānaruditaiḥ akāraṇarodanaiḥ, alam, iti sahajo'rthaḥ saṃvṛtikārakaḥ, saṃvaraṇakāritvaprayojanantu anenāsyā dugdhādyapacayaḥ kvata iti bodhāt kupitā'sau duḥkhena roditi ityevaṃ sādharaṇā jānantviti| gūḍhārthastu me cetaḥ āgasaḥ aparādhasya, nāyikāntasaṅgarūpasya ityartaḥ, ("āgo'parādho mantuśca" ityamaraḥ) rasasya āsvādasya, abhijñaṃ na, nāhaṃ tvahate anyāṃ kāmaye kathaṃ tavehaśo mānaḥ? iti| ( atra " me āgorasābhijñam" iti padacchede sandhisūtreṇa ekārasya ayādeśena mayāgorasābhijñamiti niṣpannatvāt prakṛtārthasya saṃvaraṇaṃ, tataśca sandhinā prākṛtārthagūḍharūpā samāgatā nāmno prahelikeyam| kāñcit māninīṃ gopīṃ prati śrakṛṣṇasya janasamāje uktiriyam) | | 108| | 

vañcitāmudāharati, kubjāmiti| kubjāṃ bhugnapṛṣṭhāṃ kāmapi nārīm, āsevamānasya upabhuñjānasya, te tava, yathā ratiḥ santoṣaḥ, vaddhate, amarastrīviḍambinīḥ te tava, yathā ratiḥ santoṣaḥ, vaddhate, amarastrīvihambinīḥ sirakāminīsadṛśīḥ, nārīḥ anyāḥ starīḥ, nirviśataḥ upabhuñjānasya, te iti śeṣaḥ, evaṃ tathā ratiḥ, na, vadrdhate iti śeṣaḥ, iti sahajo'rthaḥ gūḍhāthastu kubjāṃ kānyakubjanagarīṃ tatratyanārīṃ vetyādiḥ| (atra kubjāśabdo bhugnapṛṣṭhanāyryāmeva prasiddhaḥ, anyatra tu na iti prasiddhaśabdenāprasiddhārthapratipādanarūpeyaṃ vañcitākhyā prahelikā| kānyakubjanagarīṃ vā tatratyanārīṃ prati anuraktajane kasyaciduktiriyam) | | 109| | 

vyutkrāntāmudāharati, daṇḍe iti| haṃsaḥ karkaśakaṇṭake paruṣakaṇṭakānvite, padminyā daṇḍe nāle, aṅgāni ghaṭṭayan gharṣayan, tathā valguravaṃ manojñaṃ nādaṃ, kurvan tuṇḍena mukhena, padminyā mukaṃ cumbitītyanvayaḥ| (atrānvayabodhaghaṭakapadānāmativyavadhānasanniveśavaśāt āsattervyetikramaḥ iti vyutkrāntā prahelikatā) | | 110| | 

pramuṣitāṃ darśayati, khātaya iti| he kani! kumāri! ("kanyā kanī kumārī ca" iti hemacandraḥ) te tava, kālyate kṣipyate iti kālaḥ pādaḥ tasmin, ("kala preraṇe" ityasya ghaṅantasya rūpam) sphātayaḥ sphāyanaṃ sphāḥ vṛddhirityarthaḥ, tasya atiḥ gatiḥ yatra te, sphītā ityarthaḥ, bahava itha yāvat, (" at sātatyagatau" ityasya inpratyayāntasya rūpam) khātayaḥ kham ākāśaṃ, tasyāyāṃ guṇaḥ khaḥ śabda ityarthaḥ, (khasabdādidamartheṣṇaḥ) khasya śabdasya, atirgatiḥ yeṣu te, śabdāyamāmanūpurādayo'laṅkārāḥ ityarthaḥ, sphārhavalgavaḥ sphāṃ sphītatām arhantīti sphārhāḥprabhūtāḥ, valgavaḥ valganāt calanāta jātāḥ dhvanaya ityarthaḥ, tataśca sphārahā valgavaḥ yeṣāṃ tathoktāḥ, bhavalantīti śeṣaḥ, tava pādabhūṣaṇanūpurādayaḥ gamanakāle atiśayaṃ śiñjantītyarthaḥ, candati āḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavādayati iti tasmin candre āḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍalādake, atra saśiñjitanūpurādyalaṅkṛte tava pāde ityartaḥ, sākṣāt pratyakṣīkṛte sati ityarthaḥ, mama vāyavaḥ prāṇāḥ, dhāriṇaḥ avasthitāḥ, susthirāḥ ityarthaḥ, (" dhṛ ṅa avasthāne" ityasya rūpam) bhavanti| (atra aprasiddheḥ bahubhiḥ padaiḥ prakṛtārthasya saṃvaraṇāt pramoṣaṇam iti pramuṣitā) | | 111| | 

samānarūpāṃ darśayati, atretiṣaḥ, atra udyāne mayā pañcapallavā śākhāpañcakaviśiṣṭā, vallarī latā, dṛṣṭā, yasyāṃ latāyāṃ, pallave pallave pratipallavamityarthaḥ, tāmrā āraktā, kusumamañjarī puṣpavallarīḥ, śobhate iti śeṣaḥ| (atra nāyikā kācit udyānatvena adhyāropitā, tasyā bāhuḥ vallarītvena, aṅgulayaḥpallavatvena, nakhāśca kusumamañjarītvena, teṣāñca tāmratvaṃ raktāṅguliprabhayeti bodhyam| iha udyānādipadānāṃ nāyikādyarthābhidhāne gauṇīlakṣaṇayā adhyāsa eva heturiti samānarūpā prahelikā) | | 112| | 

paruṣāṃ darśayati, surā iti| te prasiddhāḥ, surāṃ madyaṃ kurvanti iti surāḥ, (nāmaṇḍintāt surāśabdāt ṅapratyaye rūpaṃ) yadvā, surāḥ eṣāṃ santīti surāḥ śoṇḍitā ityarthaḥ, (arśa āditvādacpratyayaḥ) yadvāsurā su atisena, rāḥ śabdaḥ, dhvanirityarthaḥ, yeṣāṃ te, uccaiḥ cūtkurvantaḥ ityarthaḥ, ("rai śabde" ityasya kvibantasya rūpam) asmin pakṣe surāpā iti katrtupadamūhyam; daśanārccaṣā hāsyena vivṛtāsyatayā dantakiraṇenopalakṣatāḥ santaḥ, samprati surāyā idaṃ tasmin saure surāmaye, sarasi majjantaḥ nimagnībhavanta iva, mattāḥ santaḥ, surālayaṃ madyagāre, svairaṃ svacchandaṃ, bhramanti| (atra surā iti padaṃ devatāvācakāmeva anuśāsanabalāt śauṇḍike surāpe uccaiścītkurvati vā prayuktam iti prayoktuḥ pāruṣaprakāśanāt paruṣā nāma prahelikā) | | 113| | 

saṅkhyātāmudīharati, nāsikyamadhyeti| nāsikyaḥ nāsikāyāṃ bhavaḥ, ñakāra ityarthaḥ, sa madhye yasyāḥ sā madhyasthañakārā ityartaḥ, tathā paritaḥ ubhayataḥ, caturbhiḥ varṇaiḥ akṣaracatuṣṭayai) , vibhūṣitā viracitā, ādau "ka-ā" iti dvau varṇau ante" ca-ī" iti dvau madhye ca ñakāraḥ evaṃrūpā iti yāvat, kācit purī nagarī, kāñcītyarthaḥ, (nāmanāminorabhedopacārāt nāsikyetyādiviśeṣaṇavākyadvayaṃ kāñcībodhakamapi pūyryāṃ yojanīyam) asti, yasyāṃ pūyryāṃ, nṛpāṃ rājānaḥ, aṣṭavarṇāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavayāḥ aṣṭābhivarṇeḥ akṣarāṣṭakaiḥ nibaddhaḥ, āḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavayaḥ ākhyā yeṣāṃ tādṛsāḥ, āsaniti śeṣaḥ| | | 114| | 

prakalpitāṃ darśayati, gireti| vṛddhe! he sthavire! , skhālantyā bhraśayantyā, vādrdhakenāspaṣṭayā ityarthaḥ, girā vācā, namreṇa vādrdhakavaśādeva avanatena, śirasā, dīnayā kātarayā, hīnaśaktikayā ityarthaḥ, dṛsā cakṣuṣā ca, upalakṣitāpi tvaṃ (viśeṣaṇe tṛtīya) sotkampaṃsavepathuṃ, bhayavaśāditi bhāvaḥ, tiṣṭhantamapi māṃ nānukampase? na dayase? iti sahajo'rthaḥ| gūḍhārthastu he vṛddheṃ! he lakṣmi! ("ṛddhiḥ siddhilakṣmyau vṛddherapyāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavayā ime" ityamaraḥ dhanābhāvena skhaladnirā, avanataśirasā dīnacakṣuṣā ca upalakṣitaṃ tathā sotkampaṃ māṃ nānukampase? iti| (atra prathamaṃ pratīyamānādarthāt prastutatvena aparārthakalpānādiyaṃ prakalpitā prahelikā) | | 115| | 

nāmāntaritāṃ darśayati, ādāviti| he adhīrākṣi! cañcalanetre! , ko'pi pārthivaḥ pārthivaśabdapratipādyaḥ, ādau prathamaṃ, rājā, tathā sanātanaḥ nityaśca, iti nāmnā gīyate kīttryate, kintu asau pārthivaḥ, vastuto naiva rājā bhūpatiḥ, nāpi sanātanaḥ nityaḥ, ata eva ko'sau? itipraśnārthaḥ sahajaḥ| uttarārthastu rājātanavṛkṣarūpaḥ gūḍhaḥ| tathā hi ko'pi pārthivaḥ pṛtivīvakārajātaḥ, vṛkṣaviśeṣaḥ ityarthaḥ, ādau tadvācakanāmnaḥ prathamāṃśaṃ, rājā iti rājā iti śabdaḥ, gīyate vatrtate ityarthaḥ, tathā, sa nātanaśca atanaḥ tanaśabdarahitaśca na bhavatīti nātanaḥ, rājā itipadānantaraṃ tanaśabdo'pyastītyartaḥ ; tataśca rājātanavṛkṣaḥ ityuttarabhūto'rthaḥ| (rājātanaśabdena piyālavṛkṣa ucyate| yathā, " rājātanaṃ piyālaḥ syāt" ityamaraḥ| rājātanaṃ rājātanaṃ rājādanamiti ca nāmadvayam| atra rājātaneti nāmni vaktavye nānārthakalpanam iti nāmāntaritā prahelikā| lakṣaṇe nāmapadaṃ vastumātraparatvena vivakṣitaṃ, tena " taruṇyāliṅgitaḥ kaṇṭhe nitambasthalamāśritaḥ| guruṇāṃ sannidhāne'pi kaḥ kūjati muhurmuhuḥ? | | " ityatra sajalakumbharūpavastuni pratipādye nānrthakalpanānnāmāntaritā iti bodhyam) | | 116| | 

nibhṛtāmudāharati, hṛtadravyamiti| nānābhāṅgibhiḥ bahuvidhavilāsaceṣṭitaiḥ, samākṛṣṭāḥ āyattīkṛtāḥ, lokāḥ yābhiḥ tathoktāḥ, tathā dudrdhurāḥ duḥkhena dhriyamāṇāḥ, kathañcidapi avaśyāḥ ityarthaḥ, kāḥ hṛtāni vinaṣṭāni, dravyāṇi dhanāni yasya tādṛsaṃ, naraṃ tyaktavā dhanavantaṃ dhaninaṃ, vajranti? īdṛśalakṣaṇavatyaśca veśyā eva bhavitumarhantīti tadvārayitumāha, veśyā na, etādṛśalakṣaṇā veśyā mama praśnaviṣayā netyarthaḥ, atastāḥ kāḥ? iti praśnaḥ sahajārthakaḥ| uttarabhūtaḥ saṃvaraṇīyārthastu parvatotpannā nadya iti| tathā hi, nānā vividhāḥ, bhaṅgāstaraṅgaḥ santyasminniti nānābhaṅgi jalaṃ, tena samākṛṣṭāḥ samīpamānītāḥ, lokā avataraṇotsakā janā yābhistāḥ, dudrdharāḥ dharāt parvatāt, duḥ duḥkhena gatāḥ, kaṣṭena nirgatā ityarthaḥ, nadya iti bhāvaḥ, hṛtāni srotovegena dhvaṃsitāni, dravyāṇi pārvatīyāni vastūni, druṇāṃ vṛkṣāṇāmimāni dravyāṇi śākhāpallavādīni ityartho vā, yasya taṃ, naraṃ narasadṛśam āśrayabhūtaṃ, parvatamityarthādāyātaṃ, tyaktavā dhanavantaṃ ratnākaraṃ, samudramityarthaḥ, vrajanti| ( atra viśeṣasaṇasādhāraṇyāt ekataraniṣedheanyatarapratītiryukteti nadya iti praśnaviṣayo'rthaḥ| asya ca tulyaviśeṣaṇapratītau api vācakaśabdānupādānāt nibhṛtātvamityāpi bodhyam | | 117| | 

) samānaśabdaṃ darśayati, jiteti| he kalabhāṣiṇi! madhurabhāṣiṇi! , prakṛṣṭasya atyutkṛṣṭasya, keśasya ākhyā tatpayryāyakaṃ nāma, prabālaḥ ityarthaḥ, yadvā, prakṛṣṭo yaḥ keśastasya ākhyā tatpayryīyanāma iva nāma yasya sa iti vigrahaḥ ; jitāḥ tiraṣkṛtāḥ, prakṛṣṭakeśākhyāḥ prābalāḥ yena saḥ, tava yaḥ abhūmisāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavayaḥ bhūmirdharā, nāsti bhūmiryatra saḥ abhūmiḥ adharaḥ ityarthaḥ, tatsāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavayaḥ tasya samānanāmā, oṣṭha ityarthaḥ, saḥ adya māṃ prabhūtotkam atyutsukaṃ, karoti prabālasadṛśastavādharo māṃ vyathayatītyarthaḥ| (atra prakṛṣṭakeśākhyaḥ abhūmiśabdaśca lakṣitalakṣaṇayā prabālādhārau bodhayataḥ, tathā hi prakṛṣṭakeśapadasya prabālaśabde abhūmipadasya ca adharaśabde lakṣaṇā, tayośca lakṣitayoḥ śaktigraheṇa tādṛśārthopasthitiḥ iti prakṛtasya samānaśabdenārthopasthiteḥ samānaśabdeyaṃ prahelikā) | | 118| | 

sammūḍhaṃ darśayati, śayanīye iti| kāminau krudhā kopena, śayanīye śayyāyāṃ, parāvṛtya vimukhībhūya, śayitau, tathaiva śayitau parāvṛtya śayitāvapi ityarthaḥ, rāgāt anurāgāt, svairaṃ svacchandaṃ, mukham acumbatām| (atra krudhā parāvṛtya śayitayoḥ svairaṃ mukhacumbanasya durghaṭatvādāpātato mohaḥ, payryavasāne tu prathamaṃ praṇayakopena parāvṛtya śayitau, paścāt anurāgāt tathaiva śayitau punaoḥ parāvṛtya śayitau iti parāvṛttayoḥ punaḥ parāvatrtanāt pāśrvāntareṇa śayitayoḥ sammukhīnatvāt mukhacambunaṃ sughaṭameveti āpātata eva śrotṛṇāṃ vyāmohanāt sammūḍheyam) | | 119| | 

parihārikāṃ darśayati vijiteti| vinā pakṣiṇā, garuḍenetyarthaḥ, jitaḥ amṛtāharaṇakāle parābhūtaḥ, indra ityarthaḥ, (purā kila māturdāsītvaniṣkrayaṃ nāgādiṣṭamamṛtamāhatrtuṃ suralokaṃ gatena garuḍena surapateḥ prabalaṃ saṅagrāmamabhavat, tatra ca garuḍaḥ nijaparākrameṇa suranāthaṃ vijityaamṛtaṃ gṛhītvā prātiṣṭhata iti vātrtā) tasya ātmabhavaḥ putraḥ, arjuna ityarthaḥ, tasya dveṣī śatruḥ, karṇa ityartaḥ, tasya guruḥ pitā, sūyrya ityarthaḥ, tasya pādaiḥ kiraṇaiḥ, hataḥ santaptaḥ, janaḥ himaṃ śaityam, apahantīti himāpahaḥ agniḥ, tasya amitrāḥ śatravaḥ jalāni ityarthaḥ, teṣāṃ dharāḥ ambhodharāḥ meghāḥ, taiḥ vyāptam āvṛtaṃ, vyoma ākāśam, abhinandati samādarati ityarthaḥ| (atra yaugikaśabdaiḥ jhaṭiti prākṛtārthabodhasya haraṇāt parihārikā) | | 120| | 

ekacchannāṃ darśayati, neti| kasyāpi amanuṣyasya puruṣatayā agaṇyasya ityarthaḥ, hastaḥ jātu kadācidapi, āyudham astraṃ, na spṛśati, strīṇāṃ stanamaṇḍalañca na spṛśati, tathā'pi ayaṃ hastaḥ, na aphalaḥ na vyarthaḥ, kila api tu saphala eva, iti āpātataḥ pratīyate, parantu āyudhastrīstanasparśābhāvena nāyaṃ vīraḥ na vā vilāsītyataḥ kathamasya hastasya phalavattvamiti viruddhavadavabhāsanāt amaniṣyaśabdena gandharvo lakṣyate, tasya hastaḥ gandharvahastaḥ eraṇḍavṛkṣa ityartaḥ| (uktañca hārāvalyām, " amaṇḍapañcāṅgalavadrdhamānā gandharvahastastripuṭīphalañca" iti tasya phalaṃ vidyata eveti, āśritasya phalasya sphuṭatvaṃ na tu āśrayasya vṛkṣasyeti ekacchanneyam) | | 121| |
