ubhayacchannāṃ darśayati, keneti| kaḥ padārthaḥ, kena padārthena saha, sambhūya militvā, sarvakāyryeṣu sannidhiṃ sāmīpyaṃ, labdhvā prāpya ca, bhojanakāle tu (tuśabdo'vadhāraṇe) bhojanakāle eva, yadi dṛṣṭaḥ, bhavati iti śeṣaḥ, tadā nirasyate nirākriyate? uttaramidaṃ yathā, kasya ayaṃ kaḥkeśaḥ, (kaśabdādidamartheṣṇapratyayaḥ) kena mastakena saha, sambhūya tathā sarvapakāyryeṣu sannidhiṃ samādaraṃ, labdhavā bhojanakāle eva dṛṣṭo nirasyate iti| ( atra āśrayāśrayiṇormastakakeśayoruyoreva channateti ubhayacchannā) | | 122| | 

saṅkīrṇāṃ darśayati, sahayeti| sahayā sāśvā, sagajā sahastikā, sabhaṭā bhaṭaiḥ yoddhubhiḥ saha vatrtamānā, iyaṃ senā śātravī camūrityarthaḥ, na jitā cet parābhūtā na yadi, tadā naḥ asmākama, ayaṃ sutaḥ amātrikaḥ mātraṃ svārthaḥ, tat vettītaci mātrikaḥ svārthābhijñaḥ, tādṛśo na bhavatīti amātrikaḥ svārthacintāparāṅmakha ityarthaḥ, yadvāmātrā dravyaṃ, dhanamityarthaḥ, viṣaya iti yāvat, ("mātraṃ tvavadhṛtau svārthe kārtsnye mātrā paricchade| akṣarāvayave dravye māne'lpe karṇabhūṣaṇe| | kāle vṛtte ca" iti haimaḥ) tāmarhatīti mātrikaḥ na mātrikaḥ amātrikaḥ dhanamayryādānabhijñaḥ, viṣayajñānavarjitaḥ ityarthaḥ, ataḥ akṣarajñaśca akṣaraṃ brahma tajjño'pītyarthaḥ, mūḍhaḥ mūrkhaḥ, syāt, svārthajñānābhāvāt veṣayikabuddhyabhāvāt vā brahmavidapi mūrkhatvenaiva gaṇyaḥ iti sahajo'rthaḥ| gūḍhārthastuhakārayakārābhyāṃ gakārajakārābhyāṃ bhakāraṭakārābhyāñca sahitā iyaṃ senā inaḥ prabhuḥ, adhiṣṭhātā ityarthaḥ, akṣarasraṣṭā brahmā iti yāvat, (yaduktaṃ, "dhātrā'kṣarāṇi sṛṣṭāni patrārūḍhānyataḥ purā" iti) tena sahitā sādhiṣṭhātṛkā varṇamālā ityarthaḥ, varṇamālā kīdṛśī, kā vā tāsāṃ adhiṣṭhātrī devatā ityādirūpā iti bhāvaḥ, na jitā anabhyāstā cet, na jñātā cedityarthaḥ, tadā amātrikaḥ mātrā svarādivarṇānāmuccāraṇakālaḥ, tāṃ vettīti mātrikaḥ, na mātrikaḥ amātrikaḥ varṇajñānarahita ityarthaḥ, ataḥ akṣaraṃ vedaṃ jānātīti akṣarajñaḥ abhyastavedaḥ api, mūḍhaḥ mūrkha ityarthaḥ, yadvā, na jitā lekhituṃ na śikṣitā ityartaḥ, tadā amātrikaḥ, mātrā akṣarāvayavaḥ, tāṃ na vettīti amātrikaḥ, kaḥ akṣaraḥ kīdṛśena ākāreṇa lekhanārhaḥ ityādijñānarahita ityarthaḥ, ayam akṣarajñaḥ varṇajño'pi, mūrkha eveti saṅkīrṇeti| | 123| | 

atra saṅkīrṇatāṃ ghaṭayati, seti| sā pūrvoktā iyaṃ saṅkīrṇākhyā prahelikā, atra nāmāntaritāmiśrā hayādiśabdānāṃ nānārthakalpanāt nāmāntaritānāmaprahelikālakṣaṇasaṃyuktā, tathā vañcitārūpayogiṇī senāśabdaś camūrūpārthasya prasiddhasya arthāntarakalpanayā vañcanāt vañcitāyāḥ prahelikāyāḥ, rūpayogiṇī lakṣaṇasamanvitā ca, tanmilitā ityarthaḥ| (ataḥ ullikhite padye prahelikāyāḥ saṅkīrṇateti jñeyam) | evameva anenaiva prākāreṇa, itarāsām anyāsāmapi prahelikānāṃ, saṅkarakramaḥ parasparamiśraṇaprakāraḥ, unneyaḥ svayameva avagantavya ityarthaḥ| | 124| | 

itthaṃ kāvyaśobhākarān dharmān guṇālaṅkārān nirūpya kāvyasya heyatvapratipādakānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ baḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanūnāṃ doṣāṇāṃ savistaravarṇane granthabāhulyabhiyā prādhnyena nitāntajyān daśa doṣānuddiśati, aparthamityādi| apārthaṃ nirarthakaṃ, vyarthaṃ viruddhārtham, ekārtham abhinnārthaṃ, sasaṃśayaṃ sandigdham, apakramaṃ yathākramarahitaṃ, śabdahīnam ūhādinā pūyryaṃ, yatibhraṣṭaṃ vicchedarahitaṃ, bhinnavṛttam asamavṛttaṃ, visandhikam akṛtasandhi, deśādivirodhi ca iti, iti śabdaḥ vākyasamāptau, īdṛśaṃ vākyaṃ duṣṭamityartaḥ, ete apārthatādayaḥ, daśa eva doṣāḥ kāvyāpakarṣakadharmāḥ sūribhiḥ vidvadbhiḥ, kāvyeṣu vajryāḥ tyājyāḥ, śābdabodhapratikūlatayā prayokturajñatāprakaṭanāt iti bhāvaḥ| jhruktañca, duṣprayuktā punargotvaṃ prayoktuḥ saiva śaṃsati" iti| evakāreṇa kiñcidvairasyapratipādakānāṃ śrutukaṭuprabhṛtīnāṃ vyavacchedaḥ, teṣām anvayabodhāprātikūlyena nātyantaṃ heyatvapratipādakatvāt iti niṣkarthaḥ| atredamavadhātavyam; sāmānyalakṣaṇābhidhānānantarameva viśeṣāṇāmabhidhānasyaucitye'pi guṇānāṃ kāvyaśarīrasyotkarṣādhāyakatvena salakṣaṇodāharaṇapradarśanāt tadavagateśca tadvipayryayātmano doṣāḥ iti tusāmathryādeva sāmānyalakṣaṇāvagatam tadapradaśryaiva viśeṣaḥ dariśitā iti| evameva coktaṃ vāmanenāpi, "guṇavipayryayātmano doṣāḥ" " arthatsatadavagamaḥ" iti| "kimarthaṃ te pṛthak prapañcyante? ityāha, "saukumāyryāya prapañca" iti | etena kāvye guṇānāmupādeyadhamryatayā tatrābhimatapratītivyadhāyakatveva ye heyatāmāpādayanti te doṣā iti lakṣaṇamāyātam; taduktaṃ sarasvatīkaṇṭhābharaṇe, "doṣāḥ padānāṃ vākyānāṃ vākyārthānāñca ṣoḍaśa| heyāḥ kāvye kavīndrirye tānevādau pracakṣmahe| | " iti| yadvā, duṣyati vikṛtaṃ bhavati kāvyamanena iti doṣaḥ iti vyutpattibalādeva lakṣaṇāvagatiḥ| prakāśakṛtā'pi "mukhyārthahatiḥ doṣaḥ" iti lakṣaṇaṃ kṛtaṃ , tenāsyabhāvasādhanāntatā sūcitā; tataśca kāvyasyāpakarṣo doṣa iti, tacca heyatvañcāpakṛṣṭatvam, apakarṣaśca śabdānāmārthānāṃ vākyānāṃ vākyārthānāṃ vyaṅgyarasādeśca sambhavati iti bahuvidhatayā kārtsnyena vaktumaśakyamiti daśa evātra darśitāḥ) | | 125| | 

nanu daśaiveti avadhāraṇamanucitaṃ pratijñāhānyoderapi vivakṣitārthapratipattyā doṣatvakītrtanāt| uktañca bhagavatā gautamena, " pratidaṣṭāntadharmābhyunajñāsvadṛṣṭānte pratijñāhāniḥ" iti| ata eva "pratijñāhāniḥ pratijñāntaraṃ pratijñāvirodhaḥ" ityādisūtreṇa pratijñāhānyādīnāṃ nigrahasthānatvaṃ tanoktam| udāhṛtañca yathā bhojarājena, "yāvajjīvamahaṃ mauni brahmacārī pitā mama| mātā ca mama bandhyā''sījaputraśca pitāmahaḥ| | " iti| bhāmahādayastu tasya doṣatvaṃ nāṅgīkuvrvanti| yathā, "pratijñāhetudṛṣṭāntahīnaṃ duṣṭañca neṣyate" iti| tadetaṃ saṃśayamapākatrtuṃ svamatamāha, pratijñeti| pratijñā sādhyanirdeśaḥ, hetuḥ sādhyasādhanaṃ, dṛṣṭāntaḥ prasiddhodāharaṇavinyāsaḥ, teṣāṃ hāniḥ virodhaḥ abhāvaśca, doṣaḥ kāvyasya heyatvapratipādakaḥ, na vā iti saṃśaye, asau etatpraśnaviṣayakaḥ, vicāraḥ niścayaḥ, ekapakṣāvadhāraṇamityarthaḥ, prāyaḥ karkaśaḥ nyāyaśāstrīyatvāt kaṭhinaḥ, karkaśaḥ nīrasa ityarthaḥ vā, tasmāt tena vicāreṇa, ālīḍhena āsvaditena, jñātena ityarthaḥ, kiṃ phalam? na kimapi phalamasti ityarthaḥ| (tathā hi kāvyasya vairasyajanakadharmatayā doṣaṇāmavabodhaḥ boddhṛṇāṃ svata eva bhavati, na ca tadarthaṃ granthabāhulyīkaraṇaṃ yuktaṃ kevalam atyantaparihāyryā eva katipaye kāvyadoṣā atra daśryante, pratijñādihāneśca nātyantaparihāyryātā, apratijñātānām api prasaṅgasaṅgatyā kavibhirvarṇitatvaṃ dṛśyate| hetorabhāvaśca prasiddhasādhye na verasyamāvahati, dṛṣṭāntaśca alaṅkārasvarūpa eva, tadabhāvena na kāvsyātyantavairasyam, analaṅkārāṇām api kāvyatvasya sarvairevāṅkīkṛtātvāt ; ato nyāyasūtrattikṛtā'pyuktaṃ, "dṛṣṭāntasya sāmayikatvenāsārvatrikatve'pi na kṣatiḥ" iti| tataśca rasāpakarṣajanakatvameva dūṣakatāvījamityavadheyam) | | 127| | 

krameṇa doṣaprabhedān lakṣayan prathamam apārthaṃ nirūpayati, samudāyeti| samudāyasya vākyaghaṭakapadasamūhasya mahāvākyaghāṭakavākyasamūhasya ca, yaḥ arthaḥ śābdabodhena ekatāmāpannāḥ pratipādyaḥ, tena śūnyaṃ yata vākyaṃ, tat apārtham apārthanāmadoṣānvitam, iti iṣyate kathyate ityarthaḥ vākyārthānāṃ pādārthānāṃ vā yogyatākāṅkṣāsattiviraheṇa śābdabodhaḥ bādhito bhavati tat apārthavat vākyamiti niṣkarṣaḥ, tacca unmattānām unmādagrastānāṃ, mattānāṃ surāpānena vikṛtānāṃ, tathā bālānāṃ śiśunām, ukteḥ vacanāt, anyatra duṣyati, unmattādivacane tu apārthatvādeḥ na doṣatvam ityarthaḥ| | 128| | 

apārthatvaṃ darśayati, samudra iti| devaiḥ suraiḥ meghairvā, samudraḥ pīyate, (" devaiḥ" itytra "so'yam" iti pāṭhaśca dṛśyate) ahaṃ jarāturaḥ jarākrāntaḥ, asmi, amī jīmūtā meghāḥ, garjanti, airāvaṇaḥ airāvatanāmā hastī, harendrisya, priyaḥ| (atra vākyacatuṣṭayasya parasparakāṅkṣābhāvena aṅgāṅgitvābhāvāt ekavākyatāyā abhāva ityataḥ samudāyasyārtho nāsti| uktañca, " svārthabodhasamāptānām aṅgāṅgitvavyapekṣayā| vākyānām ekavākyatvaṃ punaḥ saṃhatya jāyate| | " iti| tataścāyāṃ vākyārthagata eva doṣaḥ| kaṇṭhābharaṇe'pi tadevoktaṃ yathā, " uktā vākyārthajā doṣāsteṣāṃ vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇamṣa samudāyārthaśūnyaṃ yattadapārthaṃ vacaḥ smṛtam| | " iti| udāharaṇāntaraṃ yathā, "jaradgavaḥ kambalapādukābhyāṃ dvāri sthito gāyati maṅgalāni| taṃ brāhmaṇī pṛcchati putrakāmā rājan! rumāyāṃ laśunasya ko'rdhaḥ? | | iti| idañca bahuvākyagatam apārthatvaṃ jñātavyam| evaṃ bahudagatamapi tat dṛśyate, yathā, vaḍhiddhaṭhṭhaḍananā siñcatītyādau yogyatādyabhāvena samudāyārthaśūnyatvam iti bodhyam"| | 129| | 

idamiti| asvasthacittānām unmattādīnām, idaṃ pūrvoktaṃ, abhidhānaṃ samudraḥ pīyate ityādi vacanam, aninditam aduṣṭatvena parigṛhītaṃ, bhavatīti śeṣaḥ, itaratra anyatra, asvasthacittādibhya iti śeṣaḥ, unmattādīn vinā ityarthaḥ, ko vā kaviḥ evamādikaṃ prayuñjīta? na ko’pi kaviḥ prayuñjītetyarthaḥ| (taduktaṃ kaṇṭhābharaṇe'pi bhojena, "atha vākyārthadoṣāṇāmadoṣaḥ kathyate'dhunāṣa| samudāyārthaśūnyaṃ yat tadapārthaṃ pracakṣate| | tanmattonmattabālānāmukteranyatra duṣyati| | yathā, kvākāyryaṃ śaśalakṣmaṇaḥ kva ca kalaṃ bhayo'pi dṛśyeta sā, doṣāṇāmupaśāntaye śrutamaho! kope'pi kāntaṃ mukham| kiṃ vakṣyantyapakalmaṣāḥ kṛtadhiyo rekaiva sā'nyādṛśī, cetaḥ svāsthyamupaihi kaḥ khalu yuvā dhanyo'dharaṃ dhāsyati? "| | iti| asya samudāyārthaśūnyatayā apārthasyāpi unmattavacanatvādguṇatvamityavadheyam) | | 130| | 

vyarthaṃ nirūpayati, ekavākye iti| ekavākye ekasmin vākye, vā prabandhe mahāvākye, viruddhārthatayā viparītārthatvena, yat pūrvāparaparāhataṃ pūrvāparayoḥ ādyantabhāgayoḥ, parāhataṃ saṅgatirahitaṃ, tat vākyaṃ, vyarthaṃma vyarthākhyadoṣānvitam, iti doṣeṣu madhye paṭhyate gaṇayate| (parasparaviruddhārthatvena pūrvāparayorasaṅgatyā yat kasyacidvyarthatāpratyāyanaṃ tat vyarthatvamiti lakṣaṇam| bhojarājastu vigato'rthaḥ prayojanamasyeti vyarthamiti vyutpattyā viśabdaḥ vigatārtaka ityāhaḥ uktañca, " vyarthamāhurgatārthaṃ yat yacca syānniṣprayojanam"iti| udāharaṇaṃ yathā, "āhiṃṣātāṃ raghuvyāghrau śarabhaṅgāśramaṃ tataḥ| svāmahauṣīttanuṃ vaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanau dṛṣṭavā tau rāmalakṣmaṇau| | " iti| arthavirodhaśca śābdabodhānantaraṃ payryālocanayā pratīyate, apārthe tu ākāṅkṣādīnām abhāvena śābdabodha eva nāstītyanayorbhedaḥ| atra hi viruddhamatikāritvaprakāśitaviruddhatvāmataparārthatvaparipanthirasāṅgādayo doṣā anyairuktā antarbhavitumarhantīti sudhībhirvibhāvyam) | | 131| | 

vyarthatāṃ darśayati, jahīti| kṛtsnaṃ samagraṃ, śatrubalaṃ ripusainyaṃ, jahi nāśaya, imāṃ viśvambharāṃ pṛthivīṃ, jaya, sarvabhūtānukampinaḥ sarvabhūteṣu dayāvataḥ, tava eko'pi vidveṣṭā śatruḥ, na, astīti śeṣaḥ| ( atra vidveṣṭramātraśūnyasya samagraśatruhananaṃ sarvabhūtānukampinaśca pṛthivījayaḥ payryālocanayā viruddhatayā pratīyate, tataśca pūrvāparaviruddhatayā parādrdhavākyārthānāṃ vyarthatāpratyāyanamiti vyarthatādoṣaḥ) | | 132| | 

kadācit vyarthatāyā guṇatvamapītyāha, astīti| sābhiṣaṅgasya viyogādidurghaṭanābhibhūtasya, ("śāpe tvabhiṣaṅgaḥ parābhave" ityamaraḥ) cetasaḥ kācit anirvacanīyā iṣṭāniṣṭaśubhādivicārākṣamā ityarthaḥ, avasthā daśā, asti bhavati, yasyāṃ sā viruddhārthā'pi asaṅgatā'pi, viruddhārthatayā vyarthā'pi ityarthaḥ, bhāratī vāṇī, abhimatā samādṛtā, bhavet| (abhimatā ityanena avasthāvaśāt asya guṇatvamiti jāyate iti sūcitam) | | 133| | 

asyā guṇatvaṃ darśayati, pareti| āyryasya sādhoḥ, me mama, paradārābhilāṣaḥ parastrīṣu abhiratiḥ kathaṃ yujyate? tasyāḥ paranāyryā, parakīyāyā iti bhāvaḥ, taralaṃ lajjābhayanitasambhramāt sakampaṃ, daśanacchadam adharaṃ, kadā nu pibāmi? ( atra pūrvādrdhe śāntabhāvaḥ uttārādrdhe tu punaḥ paradārautsukyamiti virodhāduttarādrdhavākyaṃ vyarthameva, tacca prayokturviyogābhibhūtatayā guṇatvena saṅgacchate| evaṃ viśabdasya vigatārthakatve udāharaṇaṃ yathā, "hṛtkaṇṭhavaktaśrotreṣu kasya nāvasthitaṃ tava| śrīkhaṇḍahārakarpūradantapatraprabhaṃ yaśaḥ? "| | atrāpi ekenaiva upamānena śauklyapratītau śeṣopamānapadānāṃ vyarthatve'pi yaśasaḥ smayryamāṇatvagīyamānanatva stūyamānatvaśrūyamāṇatvaiḥ hṛdayādiṣu śrīkhaṇḍaādivadavasthānasya pratīyamānatvāt guṇatvam| navyāstu viruddhānāṃ bādhyatvenoktau guṇatāmaṅgīkurvanti, yadāha, "sañcāyryāderviruddhasya bādhyatvena vaco guṇaḥ" iti| tadatra doṣasya guṇatvasampādanaṃ sarvasammatam iti bodhyam) | | 134| | 

ekārthaṃ samarthayitumāha, aviśeṣeṇeti| yadi pūrvoktaṃ prāgabhihitam, arthataḥ śabdato vā'pi arthaḥ śabdaścetyartaḥ, (pūrvoktamiti sāmānyatvānnapuṃsakaliṅgam| arthataḥ śabdataḥ iti prathamāntaṃ jñeyam) aviśeṣeṇa abhinnatayā, bhūyo'pi punarapi, kīttryate, tat ekārthamekārthakhyadoṣayuktaṃ, matam| (evañca doṣo'yam arthagataḥ ekārthakaśabdagataśceti, tathā ca arthasya ekārthaśabdasya vā punaruktatvam ekārthatvamiti bodhyam| tena ca punaruktākhyārthadoṣasya kathitapadatvākhyaśabdodoṣasya cānyairuktasyaekārthasaṃjñayā saṅgahaḥ| śabdata ityatra śabdapadaṃ tadarthakaśabdaparaṃ tataśca bhinnārthakaśabdapaunaruttyasya tādṛsavairasyājanakatvena na doṣatvam ityabhiprāyaḥ sūcitaḥ| tadyathā, " surā vipraiḥ sirā nīcaiḥ" ityādau surā devāḥ surā madyānīti bhinnārthatvānna doṣaḥ) | yatheti udāharaṇapradarśanārtham| | 135| | 

ekārthaṃ darśayati utkāmiti| tasyāḥ bālāyāḥ, elakānāmiva keśānāmiva, tviṣaḥ kāntayaḥ yeṣāṃ tādṛśāḥ, stanayitnavaḥ garjanaśīlāḥ, taḍitvantaḥ saudāmanīsahitāḥ, gambhītarāḥ prāgāḍhā ityarthaḥ, ete ambhodharāḥ meghāḥ, utkāṃ viraheṇotkaṇṭhitāṃ, bālām unmanayanti unmanasaṃ kurvanti, uddīpakatvāditi bhāvaḥ| ( atra utkenmana śabdau ambhodharāstaḍitvantaḥ stanitnavaḥ ityete śabdāśca svarūpato bhinnā api ekayryāyatvena ekārthaprātīteḥ punaruktārthāḥ| evaṃ yena kenāpi pīrvoktārthāsya punaḥ prātītau doṣo'yaṃ dṛśyate; yathā darśitaḥ viśvanāthena, "sahasā vidadhīta na kriyāmavivekaḥ paramāpadāṃ padam| vṛṇate hi vimṛṣyakāriṇaṃ guṇalubdhāḥ svayameva sampadaḥ| | atra dvitīyādrdhavyatirekeṇa dvitīyapādasyaivārtha iti punaruktatā| " iti| tathā śabdato'rthato vā ityenenoktadasya śabdagatasya api udāharaṇaṃ darśitaṃ prakāśakṛtā kathitapadatātāṃ yathā, "adhikaratalatatpaṃ kalpitasvāpalīlā, parimilananimīlataṇḍimā gaṇḍapālī| sitana! kathaya kasya vyañjayatyañjamaiva, smaranarapatilīlāyauvarājyābhiṣekam? "| | atra ubhayatra vilāsarūpārthasyaikalīlāpadenaivoktau tātapayryagrāhakābhāvāt līlāpadam ekārtham| śabdārthavibhāgena ekārthasya yat dvaividhyamabhihitaṃ, tasyāyamabhiprāyaḥ, vākyārthadoṣaprakaraṇoktasyaikārthasya vastuto vākyārthadoṣatve'pi śabdārthagatatvena bhedadvayakalpanamakiñcitkarameva, sa ca bhedaḥ yatra payryāyāntaraśabdenārthasya ponaruttyaṃ, tatrārthamātragataḥ ; yatra ekasyaivaikārthaśabdasya tātparyyābhāvo'pi punaḥ prayogastatra śabdagat ityevaṃrūpo bodhyaḥ| navyāstu śabdagatasya kathitapadatvam arthagatasya punaruktatvamiti saṃjñādvayamāhuḥ) | | 136| | 

asya pratiprasavamāha, anukampeti| yadi kaścit anukampādyatiśayaḥ anukampādīnām atiśayaḥ, prakṛtasyeti śeṣaḥ, (ādipadena vihitānuvādādīnām parigrahaḥ, yadiktaṃ "guṇa" ityadhikṛtya darpaṇakāreṇa, "kathitañca padaṃ punaḥ| vihitasyānuvādyatve viṣāde vismaye krudhi| | dainye'tha lāṭānuprāse'nukampāyāṃ prasādane| arthāntarasaṅkamitavācye harṣe'ladhāraṇe| | "iti) vivakṣyate vaktumiṣyate, tadā punarukto'pi na doṣaḥ, pratyuta iyaṃ punaruktirityarthaḥ, alaṅkiyā alaṅkāraḥ, kāvyaśobhādhāyakatvāt iti bhāvaḥ| evañca rasākṣiptacetaso vakturuktau ca ekārthaṃ na duṣyati; tadukta kaṇṭhābharaṇe, "aviśeṣeṇa pūrvoktaṃ yadi bhūyo'pi kīttryate| tadekārthaṃ rasākṣiptacetasāṃ, tanna duṣyati| | " udāharaṇaṃ yathā, "asāraṃ saṃsāraṃ parimuṣitaratnaṃ tribhuvanaṃ, nirālokaṃ lokaṃ maraṇaśaraṇaṃ bāndhavajanam| adarpaṃ kandarpaṃ jananayananirmāṇamaphalaṃ, jagajjīrṇāraṇyaṃ kathamasi vidhātuṃ vyavasitaḥ? "| | atra asāraṃ saṃsāramityuktavā parimuṣitaratnaṃ tribhuvanaṃ nirolokaṃ lokaṃ jagajjīrṇāraṇyamiti yaduktaṃ tasya viśeṣānabhidhāyakatve'pi rasākṣiptena vakrā abhihitatvāt guṇatvam; tathā unmattādyuktāvapi paunaruttyaṃ guṇa eveti jñeyam) | | 137| | 

anikampāyāmudāharati, hanyate iti| sā varārohā uttamāṅganā, akāṇḍavairiṇā akāraṇaripuṇā, smaraṇe kāmena, hanyate, sā cārusarvāṅgī hanyate, sā mañjubhāṣiṇau madhuravādinau, hanyate| (atra hanyate iti kriyāvācakaṃ padaṃ punaḥ punaruktamapi prastutāyā nāyikāyā anukampanīyatvādiprakaṭanāt vaicitryameva janayatīti na doṣāvaham| evamanyatrāpi udāharaṇāni mṛgyāṇi) | | 138| | 

sasaṃśayaṃ nirūpayati, nirṇayārthamiti| nirṇayārthaṃ, śrotuḥ prṛttinivṛttyorekataranidrdhāraṇārthamiti yāvat, prayuktāni vinyastāni, vacāṃsi cet yadi, saṃśayaṃ viṣayadvayātmakajñānaṃ, janayanti utpādayanti, tadā asau eva doṣaḥ sasaṃśayaḥ saṃsaśayākhyadoṣeṇa lipta ityarthaḥ, iti smṛtaḥ| (bhojastu, "sandigdhārthaṃ saṃsaśayam"iti lakṣaṇamāha| asya ca lakṣaṇasya saṅghiptatve'pi boddhavyasya pravṛttinivṛttyanyataranirṇayārthaṃ prayuktaṃ vākyaṃ sandigdhārthaṃ sandehajanakañcedityarthaḥ karaṇīyāḥ, sandehārthameva sandigdhavākyaprayoge tu asya guṇatvamiti svayameva vakṣyamāṇatvāt) | | 139| | 

saṃsaśayamudāharati, manoratheti| manorathapriyālokarasalolekṣaṇe! manorathapriyaḥ vāñchitavallabhaḥ, tasya ālokaḥ darśanaṃ, tasmin yaḥ rasaḥ anurāgaḥ, tena lola cañcale, īkṣaṇe nayane yasyāḥ tatsambuddhau, he jāradarśanavyāpṛtanayane! sakhi! asau mātā, taveti śeṣaḥ, ārādvṛttiḥ dūravarttinītyarthaḥ, ataḥ īdṛśaṃ tava sānurāgāṅganājanayogyaṃ vyavahāraṃ, draṣṭuṃ na kṣamā vyavahitatayā vilokayituṃ na śaktā, tato niḥśaṅkamavalokaya iti bhāvaḥ, athavā, rādvṛttiḥ samīpavarttinī, atastava īdṛśaṃ kulāṅganānucitaṃ cāritryakhaṇḍanarūpaṃ vyavahāramityarthaḥ, draṣṭuṃ na kṣamā na sahiṣṇuḥ, ato nayanacāpalaṃ mā kārṣīriti bhāvaḥ| ("ārāddūrasamīpayoḥ" ityamaraḥ| atra sakhyāḥ katrtavyanirṇayārthaṃ dūrasamīpobhayārthakasya ārātśabdaseya ullekhadvārā prayuktena vākyena mātā me dūravarattinī, ataḥ mamedṛśaṃ kamrma draṣṭuṃ na śakṣyati tat niḥśaṅkaṃ pravatrte iti, kiṃ vā mātā meṃ samīpasthā, ataḥ īdṛśaṃ ninditaṃ kamrma draṣṭuṃ na śakṣyati tat asmāt nivatrte iti nāyikāyāḥ pravṛttinivṛttyoḥ ekataraniscayābhāvāt sandehapayryavasāyitvena sasaṃśayadoṣaḥ| asya ca ubhayārthakaśabdaprayoganibandhanatvāt śabdagatatvam| ayañca doṣaḥ arthagato'pi sambhavati, sa ca arthabodhāntaraṃ vakrādyaniścayena jñāyavyaḥ, udāharaṇaṃ yathā, " mātsayryamutsāyrya vicāyrya kāyryamāyryāḥ samayryādamudāharantu| sevyā nitambā) kimu bhūdharāṇāmuta smarasmeravilāsinīnām? "| | iti| atra prakāṇādyabhāvāt vaktā śamapradhānaḥ śrṛṅgārapradhāno vā iti niścayābhāvāt saṃśayaḥ| jāraṃ sābhilāṣamavalokayantīṃ mātustadācaraṇaparijñānaśaṅkayā ca bhītāṃ nāyikāṃ prati sakhyā uktiḥ) | | 140| | 

asya kadācit guṇatvamāha, īdṛśamiti| yadi vā tu yadyapi, īdṛśaṃ sasaṃśayaṃ vākyaṃ, saṃśayāya saṃśayapratipādanāya eva, prayujyate vyavahriyate, tadā asau saṃśayaḥ, alaṅkāra eva syāt, tatra tādṛśasthale, na doṣaḥ, tathā ca yatra saṃśaya eva vivakṣitaḥ tatra asau guṇa eva, avivakṣitastu doṣa eveti bhāva) | tat tasya udāharaṇaṃ, yatheti vakṣyamāṇasūcanārtham| ("vā tu" ityatra "jātu" iti pāṭho dṛśyate) | | 141| | 

paśyāmīti| aninditāṃ sarvāṅgasundarīmityarthaḥ, tāṃ tava kāntāmityarthaḥ, anaṅgajena kāmakṛtena, ātaṅkena rogeṇa, kāmapīḍayā ityarthaḥ, laṅghitām ākrāntām, ata eva kaṭhoreṇa nirdayena, kālena mṛtyunā, grastāmeva kavilitāmena, paśyāmi, tvadāśayā tava pratyāśayā, naḥ asmākaṃ, kim? kiṃ phalam? tvāmanāsādayantī idānīṃ kāmapīḍayā sā mriyate, ataḥ tava pratyāśayā prayojanaṃ nāstītyarthaḥ| athavā, aṅgajena ātaṅkena śārīrikapīḍayā, na laṅghīteti anaṅgajātaṅkalāṅghitā tāṃ, kaṭhore tīvreṇa, kālena grīṣmeṇetyarthaḥ, grāstāṃ paśyāmi, tvadāśayā naḥ kim? tasyāḥ śarīrikī pīḍaā kā'pi nāstī, kevalaṃ tvadaprāptinimittacintīdibhirapagatanidrā grīṣmakālajanitasantāpena sā nitarāṃ pīḍayate iti pūrvoktārtha evāsya ślokasya pratipādyaḥ kiṃ parārthaḥ ityeṣa saṃśayaḥ| (sa ca dūtyāvivakṣitā eva| proṣitaṃ nāyakaṃ prati dūtyā uktiriyam) | | 142| | 

atra ca asya guṇatvamupapādayati, kāmātrteti| dūtī yuvānam ākulīkatrtuṃ nāyikāsamīpagamanāya utsukaṃ katrtuṃ, narmaṇā bhaṅgyā, kāmātrtā gharmataptā grīṣmātrtā vā, iti aniściyakaraṃ sandehasaṅkulam, iti pūrvoktarūpaṃ, vacaḥ āha, kāmapīḍitā grīṣmapīḍitā vā iti nirṇetumaśakyatayā yuvā'yam ākulībhūya tatsamīpaṃ yāsyatīti manasikṛtya dūtī tādṛśaṃ sandigdhaṃ vacanaṃ provāca ityasya guṇatvamiti bhāvaḥ| | 143| | 

apakramaṃ nirūpayati, uddeśeti| arthānāṃ vaktavyaviṣayāṇām, uddeśāniguṇaḥ uddeśaḥ ādāvipanyāsaḥ ityarthaḥ, tasya anuguṇaḥ anukūlaṛ, anvayayogya ityarthaḥ, anu paścāt, uddeśaḥ upanyāsaḥ, kramānusāreṇa vinyāsa ityarthaḥ cet yadi, na kṛtaḥ, tadā budhāḥ tam apakramaḥ abhidhānaṃ nāma yasya tathoktaṃ, kramalaṅghanāt apakramākhyaṃ, doṣam ācakṣate kathayanti| (tathā ca, kāyryākāraṇabhūtānāmarthānāṃ yathākramamupanyāsa eva gumaḥ, tallaṅghanantu doṣa eveti bhāvaḥ| ayañca doṣaḥ śabdārthadoṣayorakramatvaduṣkramatvayoranyairuktayorantarbhavati, kintu tayornātyantaparihāryyatvam; yaduktaṃ viśvanāthena, "dvayaṃ gataṃ samprati śocanīyatāṃ, samāgamaprārthanayā pinākinaḥ| kalā ca sā kāntimatī kalāvatastvamasya lokasya ca netrakomudī| | atra tvamityanantarameva cakaro yuktaḥ"iti| tathā"dehi me vājinaṃ rājan! gajendraṃ vā madālasam| atra gajendrasya prathamayācanamucitam" iti| dvayamityādau parāmṛṣyamāṇavākyānantaraṃ prayoktavyasya cakārasyānyatra prayuktatayā kramo laṅghito'pi vyavahitasyāpi yojanayā anvayanirbādhāt nātidoṣaṃ bhajate| deh me ityādau ca pradhānasya prathamaprārthanamucitaṃ, taddāne dāturasāmathrye tato nyūnaṃ yācanīyamiti kramaḥ, iha tu tallṅghanāt apakramatvaṃ, kintu anvayanirbādhatvena nātyantaparihāyryatvam ityubhayatrāpi anvayasya bādhābāvānnātyantaparihāyryateti vibhāvyam| asya tu kramikāṇāṃ kramikairevānvaya iti niyamalaṅghanādanvayabodhabādhakatvenātyantaparihāyryatvamiti viśeṣaḥ) | | 144| | 

apakramaṃ darśayati, sthitīti| jagatāṃ sthitiḥ pālanaṃ, nirmāṇaṃ sṛṣṭiḥ saṃhāraḥ dhvaṃsa, teṣāṃ hetavaḥ kāraṇāni, katrtāraḥ ityarthaḥ, amī ete sambhūnārāyaṇāmbhojayonayaḥ śivaviṣṇu bramhāṇaḥ, vaḥ yuṣmān, pālayantu rakṣantu| (atra prathamoddiṣṭānāṃ sthitinirmāṇasaṃhārāṇāṃyathākramamanvaye katrtavye nārāyaṇāmbojayoniśambhava iti vaktumucitaṃ, parantu tadviparītamuktamiti śābdabodhavighaṭakatvāt apakramatā) | | 145| | 

asyādoṣatvamapi kvacidityāha, yatna iti| sambandhasya anvayasya, vijñānaṃ viśeṣeṇāvabodha eva, heturyasya tādṛśaḥ, yatnaḥ viśiṣṭasambandhabodhāyaiva tādṛśakramalaṅghanaprayāsa ityarthaḥ, yadyapi kṛtaḥ, kavineti śeṣaḥ, tadā sūrayaḥ vidvāmalasaḥ, kramalaṅghanamapi dūṣaṇaṃ doṣāvahaṃ, naiva āhuḥ| | 146| | 

apakramasyādoṣatva mudāharatisa bandhityāga iti| bandhutyāgaḥ svajanaparihāraḥ, tanutyāgaḥ dehābhāvaḥ, deśatyāgaḥ videṣāvasthitiḥ, iti triṣu viṣayeṣu madhye ādyantau bandhutyāgadeśatyāgau, āyatāḥ dīrghāḥ, kleśāḥ yayoḥ tau, madhyamastanutyāgastu, kṣaṇikajvaraḥ kṣaṇamātrasantāpakara ityarthaḥ, varaṃ tanutyāgaḥ śreyān na ca punaḥ bandhutyāgaḥ deśatyāgo vā iti bhāvaḥ| (atra yathākramamuddiṣṭeṣu bandhutyāgādiṣu āyatakleśāvityena oādipadagṛhitasya bandhutyāgasyānvayitve tadittaroddiṣṭasya tanu tyāgāsyānvayāyogāt tamullaṅghya tadantasya deśatyāgastānvayitvamiti kramalaṅghanaṃ sambandhavijñānahetutvāt yujyate eva iti na doṣāya, pratyuta tanutyāgāt bandhutyāgadeśatyāgayoḥ garīyastvena vakturabhimatatvāt guṇa eva ityuvadheyam) | | 147| | 

śabjahīnaṃ nirūpayati, śabdahīnamiti| anālakṣyā adṛśyā, aparijñeyā iti yāvat, tasyā aśāstrīyatvāditi bhāvaḥ, lakṣyasya sajātīyodāharaṇāntarasya, tathā lakṣaṇasya anuśāsanasya, sūtrakoṣadhātupāṭhādirūpaniyāmakasyetyarthaḥ, paddhatiḥ niyamaḥ yatra tādṛśaḥ agaṇitānuśāsana ityarthaḥ, tathā aśiṣṭānām ajñānām, iṣṭaḥ anavabodhavijṛmbhita ityarthaḥ, padānāṃ padayoḥ padasya vā prayogaḥ vyavahāraḥ, kṛdabhihitabhāvatvāt prayujyamānaṃ padamityarthaḥ, śabdahīnaṃ śabdena anuśāsanaśāstreṇa hīnaṃ, tadākhyadoṣavān ityarthaḥ, sudhībhiraprayuktatayā niṣpādakānuśāsanābhāvācca yasya sajātīyodāharaṇāntaraṃ nāsti tādṛśapadaprayogaḥ śiṣṭāvyavahṛtapadaprayogaśca śabdahīnākhyadoṣavāniti niṣkarṣaḥ| (tataśca yādṛśapadasyānuśāsanaṃ sajātīyaprayogāntarañca na vidyete tādṛśaṃ padaṃ tathā satyapyanuśāsane tattalliṅgādimattvena tattadarthakatvena vā śiṣṭairaprayuktaoñca padaṃ śabdahīnākhyadoṣavaditi lakṣaṇam) | kiñca śiṣṭānāmuktau anuśāsanaviruddhatvamapi na doṣāyetyāha, śiṣṭeti| śiṣṭeṣṭastu śiṣṭaiḥ sādhubhirbahubhiḥ iṣṭaḥ abhimatatayā vyavahṛtastu, na duṣyati na doṣāya bhavati| jhrpadānāṃ nityatvāt tadvyatpattyarthamevānusāsanaṃ pāṇinyādibhiḥ kṛtamitiśiṣṭānāmuktau sūtrāṇāmabhāve anuśāsanakāriṇa eva daṇḍanīyāḥ, na tatra doṣa itibhāvaḥ| goyīcandreṇāpi "nityānāṃ padānāṃ pratipattyarthaṃ prakṛtipratyayavibhāgaparikalpanaṃ śāstreṇa kriyate" ityuktatayā anuśāsanasya śiṣṭavyavahṛtapadāt paratvameva pratipāditam| tataścānuśāsanābāve'pi bahubhiḥ prayuktapadaprayogo nāsya doṣasya viṣayaḥ, tathā satyapyanuśāsane tādṛśaḥ padaprayogo yadi kairapi na kṛtaḥ tatprayogastu doṣa eveti bodhyam| śabdaśāstraviruddhapadaprayoge tu anyairasādhutvamityuktaṃ, tathā ca vāmanaḥ, "śabdasmṛtiviruddhamasādhu" iti| aprayuktāsamarthadoṣayorapyanyairuktayoranenaiva saṅgrahaḥ kṛtaḥ iti dikṭa| | 148| | 

śabdahīnamudāharati, avate iti| he mahārājan! bhavate bhavataḥ bāhuḥ bhujaḥ, arṇavaḥ sāgaraḥ, śakkarī mekhalā yasyāḥ tāṃ sasāgaramityarthaḥ, ("śakkarī chandaso bhede nadīmekhalayorapi"iti medinī) mahīm avate pālayati, ajijñāsā saṃśayābhāvāt atra jijñāsā na vidyate, satyamevaitadityarta, ityasāṃ prāguktānāmīdṛśīnāṃ girāṃ vācāṃ, rasaḥ mādhuyryānuguṇaḥ āsvādaḥ, nāsti, proktapadānāmasādhutvāt anvayabodhaprātikūlyena śrṛṅgārarasānuprāṇitarājaviṣayakaratibhāvasyānudramāditi bhāvaḥ| (atra avate iti parasmaipadinaḥ avadhātorātmanepadaṃ, bhavate iti ṣaṣṭhīsthāne caturthī, mahārājanniti karmadhārayasamāse ṭacpratyayābhāvaḥ, arṇavaśakkarīmityatra bahavrīhisamāse kapratyayābhāvaśca sarvametate anuśāsanaviruddham aśiṣṭairupanyastam| anyacca śakkarīśabdaḥ mekhalārthe kairapi na vyavahṛtaḥ ityasya aprayuktatvāt prayuktamidaṃ padaṃ śrotṛbudiṃdha vyākulayat anvayabodhavyāghātakaṃ bhavati) | | 149| | 

adūṣitaṃ śabdahīnaṃ darśati, dakṣiṇādrerityādi| dakṣiṇādreḥ malayaparvatasya, upasaran samīpaṃ vān, malayācalaṃ gacchan ityarthaḥ, mārutaḥ vāyuḥ, cūtapādapān tattyān āmratarūn, lalitaṃ sandaraṃ yathā tathā, mṛdulatayā ityarthaḥ, ādhūtāḥ kampitāḥ, ye prabālāṅkurāḥ navapallavaprarohāḥ, abhinavodratapallvā ityarthaḥ, taiḥ śobhinaḥ śobhāśālinaḥ kurute| ityādi pūrvoktarūpaṃ vākyaṃ, śāstrasya anuśāsanasya, yat mahātmāṃ nigūḍhārthaṃ ityarthaḥ, tasya darśane alasaṃ vimukhaṃ, ceto yeṣāṃ tathābhūtānāma, ālasyavaśāt vyākaraṇādiśāstrālocanāparāṅmukhānāṃ viduṣām ityarthaḥ, apabhāṣaṇavat paśabdavat, bhāti pratīyate, na tu apaśabdarūpeṇa ityarthaḥ| jhr ayaṃ bhāvaḥ, karmaṇi dvitīyeti anuśāsanabalena upasaraṇakriyāyāḥ kamrmapadasya dakṣiṇādrerityatra dvitīyā bhavitumarhatyeva, parakaṃ tadakaraṇe'pi nātacra doṣaḥ, sarvatra sambandhavivakṣayā ṣaṣṭhīvidhānasya aduṣṭatvena śiṣṭānumoditatvāt| uktañca dīkṣitena, "karmādīnāmapi sambandhamātravivakṣāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhyeva" iti| kiñca śiṣṭairukteṣu prayogeṣu anuśāsanābhāve'pi na doṣaḥ ; yathā "lajjālolaṃ balantī" "cetaḥ paraṃ balati śailavanasthalīṣu" ityādau balerdhātorātmanepaditvāt parasmaipaditvāmanusāsanaviruddhamapi ma duṣṭam; ata eva vāmanena tatsiddhaye kvacidanudāttettvanibandhanasyātmanepadasyānityatvaṃ jñāpakena samarthitam; yathā, "balerātmanepadamanityaṃ jñāpakāt" iti dvitīyāya abhāve'pi śrotṝṇāṃ na vairasyodaya ityāha, naceti| saubhāgyañca lālityañca, śiṣṭasammatatvāt prayogasauṣṭhavañcetyarthaḥ, na ujjhati na tyajati, tathā ca īdṛśasthale śrotṛvirāge doṣa eveti bhāva| (vāyunā kampitasya malayaparvatīyacūtatarorvarṇanamidam| dakṣiṇādrerupasarpannityasya dakṣiṇādreḥ malayācalāt upasarpan āgacchannityarthakatve na kaścit payryanuyoga iti bodhyam) | | 150| | 

151| | 

yatiṃ tadbhraṃśañca nirūpayati, ślokeṣviti| ślokeṣu padyeṣu, na tu gadyeṣu iti bhāvaḥ, niyataṃ chandaḥ śāstrajñaiḥ nirūpitaṃ, sthānaṃ yasya tādṛsaṃ, padasya suptiṅantarūpasya, chedaṃ viratiṃ yatiṃ viduḥ jānanti, budhā iti śeṣaḥ, padānte jiḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍaveṣṭaviśrāmasthānaṃ yatiriti niṣkarṣaḥ ; tadapetaṃ tasyacāḥ vicyutam, ata eva śravaṇayoḥ śrotrayoḥ udvejanam asikhajanakam ityarthaḥ, yatibhraṣṭaṃ yatibhraṣṭākhyadoṣavadityarthaḥ padamiti śeṣaḥ, yatra virāme śrutikaṭureva bhavati ślokaḥ, tatra doṣa iti bhāvaḥ| jhrata evoktaṃ vāmanena, "virasavirāmaṃ yatibhraṣṭam" iti| | 152| | 

strīṇāmiti| ayam ādityavaṃśyaḥ sūyryavaṃśīyaḥ, narendraḥ rājā, śiṣṭaiḥ sajjanaiḥ, amā saha, akliṣṭaḥ manojñaḥ, rasaḥ svādaḥ mādhuyryamityarthaḥ, yasya tādṛsaṃ, strīṇāṃ saḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍagītavidhiṃ samyak gītavidhānaṃ, nāṭyagītavādyatrayamityartaḥ, iha atra sabhāyāṃ, paśyati, ityādi evamādikaṃ padyaṃ, duṣṭaṃ yatibhraṣṭatvādanādaraṇāyamityarthaḥ| jhrtathā hi saptadaśākṣare mandākrāntāvatte asmin āditaśṭacaturthe tataḥ ṣaṣṭhe tataḥ saptame yatirniveśanīyāḥ ; tathā coktaṃ, "mandākrāntāmbudhirasanagairmoṃbhanau tai gayugmam"iti| atra tu caturthādivarṇānāṃ padāntargatatvāt yatibhraṣṭatā| tathā ca "strīṇāṃ saṅgī" iti caturthavarṇe, tathā "mayamā"iti daśamavarṇe ca viratyabhāvena vairasyāt yatibhraṣṭatvaṃ, tañca saṅgīta iti pratipadikāṃśagatamiti vāmanoktaṃ nāmabhāgabhedodāharaṇamidamityapi bodhyam| vāmanastu dhātunāmabhāgatvenāsya dvaividhyamāha; yathā, "taddhātunāmabāgabhede svarasandhyakṛte prāyeṇa" iti| dhātubhāgabhede mandākrāntāyāṃ, yathā, "etāsāṃ rājati sumanasāṃ dāma kaṇṭhāvalambi" ita| atra "etāsāṃ rā" ityatrarājadhātoraṃśe prāptasya virāmasyābhāvāt vairasyamiti dhātubhāgabhedatvam| lakṣaṇe'smin padabhāgabheda iti lāghavaṃ vihāya dhātunāmagrahaṇena dhātunāmabhāgavyatirikte prakṛtipratyayamadhyavirāme yatibhraṃśo na bhavediti sūcyate; tena "śobhāṃ puṣyatyayamabhinavaḥ sundarīṇāṃ prabādhaḥ" ityādau "śobhāṃ puṣya" ityatra virāmo na vairasyamāvahati, dhātubhāgasya puṣa ityantatvāt| kaṇṭhābharaṇe tu "śobhām" ityādi prathamapādaḥ ; "kiñcidbhāvālasamasaralaprekṣitaṃ kāminīnām" iti dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ, "kāyryākāyryāṇyayam" ityādyaparādrdhamityevamudāhṛtamṭa| asya ca kvacidadoṣatvamāha, kāyryeti| ayaṃ nṛpaḥ āgamenaiva nītiśāstreṇaiva, na tu svecchācāritayeti bhāvaḥ, kāyryākāyryāṇi katrtavyākatrtavyāṇi, avikalāni avyāhatāni, paśyan vaśyāṃ vaśatāmāpannām, urviṃ pṛthivīṃ, vahati pālayati ityarthaḥ, iti evaṃ prayogaśca asti aduṣṭatvena vatrtate ityarthaḥ, ("kāyryākāyryāṇi" ityatra kāyryā ityasya prātipadikabhāgāntatayā nāmabhāgabhedagatasya tasyāprāpteriti bhāvaḥ| taduktaṃ kaṇṭhābharaṇe'pi, "svarasandhyakṛte prāyo dhātubede tadiṣyate| nāmabhede ca śeṣeṣu na doṣa iti sūrayaḥ| | " iti| kiñca "ṇya" ityādisthale svarasandhiśrayaṇe padamadhye "yryā"ityādau yatibhraṃśasya adoṣatvamavagantavyam; yadiktaṃ, "kvacicchandasyāste yatirabhihitā pūrvakṛtibhiḥ padānte sā śobhāṃ śrayati padamadhye tyajati ca| punastatraivāsau svaravihitasandhiḥ śrayati tāṃ, yathā kṛṣṇaḥ puṣṇātyatulamahimā māṃ karuṇayā| | " iti| strīṇāṃ saṅgītaprabhṛtiṣu tu etallakṣaṇābhāvāt doṣa eva iti) | | 153| | 

ubhayatrāpi padamadhye viśrāntirūpasya uktavidhayatibhraṃśasya ekatra sadoṣatvam anyatra adoṣatvañca pratipādayati, lupte iti| padasya subanta tiṅantarūpasya, ante śeṣavarṇe, lupte madhu paṭha ityādau vibhaktilope jāte ityarthaḥ, (atra padānte ityekavacanamavikṣitaṃ rājā ityatradvayorapi lope avaśiṣṭasya rājabhāgasya padatvasvīkārāt ) śiṣṭasya avaśiṣṭasya prakṛtibhāgarūpasya padāṃśasya, yathā padatvaṃ niścitaṃ siddhāntitaṃ, niḥsandhigdhaṃ śiṣṭaiḥ padatvena svīkṛtamityarthaḥ, tathā sandhiḥ svaradvayasammelanaṃ, tatkṛto vikāraḥ dīrghayavaparbhṛtivarṇāntarotpattirūpaḥ, tādṛśavikārayuktavyañjanamiti yāvat, ante yasya tat, padamadhyapapīti śeṣaḥ, padameva padānte eva, iti vaṇryate kavibhirucyate| (tataśca "kāyryākāyryāṇyayam"ityādau "ṇya" ityādisthale svarasandhikṛtayakārādivikārayuktavarṇānte, "yryā"ityādau padamadhye'pi yatirna doṣāvahā| tathā caturthapāde'pi pādasya madhyabhāge niviṣṭakevalasvaratvāt yatibhraṃśe'pi svarasandhikṛtatvādavibhinnanāmadhātuśarīratvācca na dhoṣatvam| etaduktameva prāk| etadvaiparītye doṣa eva, yathā strīṇāmityādi) | | 154| | 

svarasandhāvapi kvaciddoṣaṃ darśayati, tathā'pīti| tathā'pi svarasandhinā padamadhyasya padānttvasvīkāre'pi, kavayaḥ karṇānāṃ kaṭu śrotrāsukhakaraṃ, tasya rājñaḥ dhvajinī senā, ketubhiḥ dhvajaiḥ, udastā utkṣiptāḥ, atyunnatatvāditi bhāvaḥ, jaladā meghā yayā tādṛśī, ityadaḥ evamidaṃ padyaṃ, na prayuñjate na vyavaharanti| (prakṛte ca ketūdastetyatra "ta" iti svarasandhinā kṛtadīrghapadāntamapi"ke" ityakṣaraṃ yatisthānapātitayā śrotṛvairasyaṃ janayatīti svarasandhāvapi śrutikaṭuryatibraṃśa eveti bhāvaḥ) | | 155| | 

bhinnavṛttaṃ nirdiśati, varṇānāmiti| yatretyūhyam, uttaratraha tatreti darśanāt; yatra padye varṇānāṃ varṇasya varṇayoḥ varṇānāṃ vetyarthaḥ, nyūnatā adhikyaṃ vā, tathā guruṇāṃ laghunāṃ vā varmānām, ayathāsthitiḥ aniyamena vinyāsaḥ, tatra padye, tat bhinnavṛttaṃ bhinnaṃ bhagnaṃ, vṛttaṃ chandaḥ, chandobhaṅga ityarthaḥ, syāt, chandaḥ śāstralakṣaṇacyutatayā yadvṛttaṃ kṛtaṃ tat vṛttabhaṅgadoṣavaditi niṣkaraṣaḥ| jhrtaduktaṃ vāmanena, "svalakṣaṇacyutavṛttaṃ bhinnavattam" iti eṣa vṛttabhaṅgākhyaḥ doṣaḥ suninditaḥ kavayitucchando'nabijñatayā upahāsāspadatvajñāpanāditi bhāvaḥ| (prakāsakārādibhiśca doṣā'yaṃ hatavṛttasaṃjñayā abhihitaḥ, tacca hatavṛttaṃ lakṣaṇānusaraṇe'pi aśravyatvarūpam aprāptagadurubhāvāntalaghurūpaṃ, rasānanuguṇarūpañceti trividhaṃ bodhyam| tatrodāhṛtaṃ yathā, "amṛtamamṛtaṃ kaḥ sandeho madhinyapi nānyathā, madhuramadhikaṃ cūtatasyāpi prasannarasaṃ phalam| sakṛdapi punarmadhyasthaḥ san rasāntaravijjano, vadatu yadihānyat svādu syāt priyāradanacchadāt| | "iti) | | 156| | 

nyūnatādhikye udāharati, indupādā iti| śiśirāḥ śītalāḥ, indoścandrasya, pādāḥ kiraṇāḥ, ("pādā raśmyaḍhiddhaṭhṭhaḍaghatuyryāṃśāḥ" ityamaraḥ) spṛśanti, ityatra ūnavarṇatā prāthame pāde aṣṭākṣare vaktavye saptākṣarāṇīti eko varṇaḥ nyana ityarthaḥ, sahakārasya āmrasya, kisalayāni pallavāni, ādrrāṇi, iti atrāpi, adhikākṣaraṃ tatīye pāde aṣṭākṣare vaktavye navākṣarāṇīti eko varṇo'dhika ityarthaḥ| | 157| | 

gurulaghvavayathāsthitimudāharati, kāmeneti| kāmena mṛgekṣaṇāsu niśitā bāṇā vimuktāḥ, ityatacra, ayathāgurutvaṃ "kā" iti prathamapādasthaprathamavarṇasya laghutvaniyame'pi dīrghakaraṇāt iti bodhyam| (tathā ca, atra pūrvodrdhe upendravajrā vṛttam, "upendravajrā jatajāstāto gau"iti lakṣaṇāt, tataśca jagaṇe prathamaṃ niveśye kāiti guruvarṇo niveśitaḥ, jagaṇasca "jo gurumadyagataḥ" ityuktatvāt ādyantau laghū madhyavarṇo gururityevaṃ varṇatrayātmaka iti; evañca prathamavarṇasya dīrghatvādindravajrālakṣaṇalakṣitaḥ prathamapādaḥ, dvitīyapādastu upendravajrālakṣaṇātmaka iti indravajropendravajrayoḥ saṅkarādupajāti vṛttamidam| tatra yadyapi prathamamindravajrāpādāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍagīkāre prathamavarṇasya dīrghatvameva yujyate, na ca ayathāgurutvadoṣo'pi sambhavati, tathā'pi upajātivṛttānāṃ prathamapādākṣarāṇāṃ laghutvaniyamādayathāgurutvaṃ sambhavatyeva| uktañca yathā, "upajātivikalpānāṃ siddhī yadyapi saṅkaraḥ| tathā'pi prathamaṃ kuyryāt pūrvapādākṣaraṃ laghu| | gurvakṣareṇa saṃruddhaṃ grānthiyuktamivāgrataḥ| karoti prathamaṃ sthūlaṃ kiñcit karṇakadarthanām| | "iti| ata eva "astyuttarasyām" itiyādimahākaviprayogo'pi doṣodāharaṇe darśitaḥ iti bodhyam) | tathā, smarasya niśitā bāṇā vāmekṣaṇāsu patatanti, ityatra, ayathālaghutvam| (tathā ca, uttarādrdhe indravajrā vṛttaṃ "syādindravajrā tatajāstato gau" iti lakṣaṇāt, tataśca tagaṇe prathamaṃ niveśye "sma" iti laghuvarṇo niveśitaḥ, tagaṇaśca "antalaghusta" itiyuktatvāt prathamadvitīyau guru antyaśca laghurityevaṃ varṇatrayātmaka iti bodhyam| atra ādāvindravajrā paścādupendravajrā ityavamupajāticchanda idamiti svīkṛte nāyaṃ dūṣaṇāvasaraḥ| vastutastu upendrabajrāyā indravajrāyā vā udāharaṇamidamiti prakalpya vṛttabhaṅgatvaṃ darśitam| diṅmātramidaṃ, śuddhodāharaṇaṃ yathā, "yasmin pañca pañcajanā ākāśaśca pratiṣṭhitaḥ| tameva dhīro vijñāya prajñāṃ kurvīta brāhmaṇaḥ| | " iti| atra pañcamavarṇasya laghoḥ sthaāne guroḥ karaṇāt vṛttabhaṅgaḥ| yathā vā, ""ayi! paśyasi saudhamāśritamaviralasumanomālabhāriṇīm" iti| vaitālīyayugmapāde "tāśca same syurno nirantarāḥ"iti samapāde laghvakṣaraṣaṭkasya nairantayryaṃ niṣiddham, atra tu "aviralasuma" iti ṣaḍapi laghvakṣarāṇi prayuktāni| kecittudvitīyādipādatrayasya laghvāditvena gurvāditvena vā sāmyamabhipretya dūṣaṇadvayapradarśanamidam, anyathā ayathāgurutvodāharaṇe ayathālaghutvam ayathālaghutvodāharaṇe ca ayathāgurutvamapi sambhavati, pādadvayādau dvayorapi gurulaghvoḥ sattvāditi uktavā upndravajrendravajrayoḥ saṅkarādupajātyākhyaṃ chando'ntaraṃ ye na manyante teṣāṃ mate dūṣaṇadvayamidamityāhuḥ ; tathā "kāmena" ityatra "svabhuvā" "smarasya" ityatra "madana" iti pāṭhaṃ prakalpya pūrvatra tṛtīyasya gurutvam uttaratra dvitīyasya laghutvamayathāprayuktamiti saṅgamayanti ca| kiñca navīnairyaduktamaprāptagurubhāvāntalaghutvaṃ, tasyāpi ayathālaghutvaṃ boddhavyaṃ, yathā, "vikasitasahakārabhārahāraparimala eṣa samāgato vasantaḥ" ityādau bhāraṅāri ityatra "ri" ityasya laghuvarṇasya prathamapādāntatvāt gurutvābhāvaḥ iti vṛttabhaṅgaḥ, pādāntalaghorgurutvantu dvitīyacaturthapādaviṣayatvenābhihitatvāt ityupi sudhībhiḥ dhyeyam) | | 158| | 

visandhikaṃ nirdiśati, neti| saṃhitāṃ varṇayormirlinarūpāṃ saÏndha, na vivakṣāmi na vaktumicchāmi, tadvidhāyakasūtre satyapi na karomīti bhāvaḥ, (etena "sandhistu puruṣecchayā"iti "saivānyatravibhāṣita" ityādikamanyeṣāṃ mataṃ pratyuktam) | iti ityabhipretya, padeṣu padasaṃyojakavarṇeṣu, yat asandhānam asaṃyojanaṃ, tattatsūtreṇa sandhyabhāva ityarthaḥ tat visandhi vigataḥ sandhiḥ sandhisūtreṇa parasparamilanaṃ yatra tat tādṛśaṃ, visandhyākhyadoṣavat ityarthaḥ, vākyamiti śeṣaḥ, (padamidaṃ vākyaviśeṣaṇam, anyathā lkīvatvānupapatteriti bodhyam) iti nirdiṣṭaṃ kathitamityarthaḥ| vakturicchākṛtasandhyabhāvasyaiva doṣatvamiti yaduktaṃ tadeva spaṣṭayati, neti| pragṛhyaṃ sandhiniṣedhakānuśāsanaviśeṣaḥ, tadādiḥ, tatprabhṛtireva, (ādinā anukaraṇādīnāṃ parigrahaḥ) hetuḥ yatra tat, sandhisūtreṇa yatra sandhirniṣiddhaḥ ityarthaḥ, na tatra visandhi doṣo na bhavati ityarthaḥ jhrtatra pragṛhyahetuko yathā, amī īśā ityādi| asiddhihetuko yathā, "tat udita udāhāridyutiḥ" ityādi| atra"lopaḥ śākatyasya" iti sūtreṇa vihitasyāpi lopasya "ādguṇaḥ" ityanena prāptaṃ guṇaṃ prati "pūrvatrāsiddham" ityanenāsiddhividhānādasiddhihetuko'yaṃ viśleṣaḥ| pragṛhyādihetukasya sandhyabhāvasya sakṛta prayoge eva doṣābhāvaḥ, asakṛta prayoge tu bandhaśaithilyanibandhanaśravaṇodvejakatvāt doṣa eva| atra viśvanātha yathā, "dalite utpale ete akṣiṇī amalāṅgi! te" evaṃvidhasndhiviśleṣasya asakṛta prayoga eva doṣaḥ| anuśāsanamullaṅghya vṛttabhaṅgabhayāt sandhiviśleṣasya tu sakṛdapi| yathā, "vāsavāśāmukhe bhāti induścandanavinduvat" iti| tena tu sandhau jugupsāvyañjakāślīlatvakaṣṭatvayoḥ yaddoṣatvamuktaṃ, tattu śābdabodhāpratikūlatayaiva nāsyābhimatam| tacca darśitaṃ yathā, "calaṇḍaāmaraceṣṭim" atra sandhau laṇḍaā iti padasya jugupsāvyañjakamaślīlatvam| "urvasāvatra tarvāli marvante cārvavasthitiḥ" atra sandhau kaṣṭatvamṭa| | 159| | 

visandidoṣamudāharati, mandānileneti| nabhasiākāśe, calatā prāvahatā, mandanilena aṅganāyāḥ gaṇḍamaṇḍale kapoladeśe, asmadvapuṣi asmākaṃ śarīre'pi udbhedi utpannaḥ, gharmāmbhaḥ svedajalaṃ, luptam apanītam| (atra calatā ityākārasya aṅganetyakāreṇa saha dīrghatvavidhāyakasūtre satyapi kevalaṃ vṛttabhaṅgabhayena sandhirna kṛta ita visandhidoṣaḥ) | | 160| | 

pragṛhyādihetukaṃ sandhyabhāvaḥ darśayati, māneṣrye ita| iha himaṛtau hemante, āsu vatrtamānāsu, rātrisu hemantaniśāsu ityarthaḥ, priye praṇayini, strīṇāṃ māneṣrye mānaḥ praṇayakopaḥ, īṣryā nāyikāntarāsaṅgajanitaḥ kopaviśeṣaśca te, śīyryete śārṇe bhavataḥ, naśyata ityarthaḥ, ita atra sthale, īdṛśaṃ vyastaṃ sandhyabhāvaḥ, prājñaiḥ sūtrakṛdbhiḥ paṇḍitaiḥ, āmrātaṃ kathitam| jhrtathā ca, māneṣrye iha ityatra "īdūdedvivacanaṃ pragṛhyam" iti sūtreṇa dvivacananiṣpannasya ekārasya sandhirniṣiddha, tathā himaṛtau ityatra "ṛtyakaḥ" iti sūtreṇa ṛkāre pare akārasya vikalpema sandhirniṣiddhaḥ iti bodhyamṭa| | 161| | 

atha deśādīnāṃ virodhaṃ nirūpayiṣyan teṣāṃ lakṣaṇāni prathamaṃ nirdiśati, deśa iti| adriḥ parvataḥ, vanaṃ rāṣṭraṃ rājyam, ityevamādirdeśaḥ, (ādipadena samudrādīnāṃ grahaṇam) rātrirdivā ṛtavaśca kālaḥ, (bahuvacanāk māsavatsarādīnāṃ grahaṇam) kāmārthasaṃśrayāḥ kāmaśca abhilāṣaśca, arthaśca tau saṃśrayau janakatvenāśrayabhūtau ityarthaḥ, yeṣāṃ tathoktāḥ, kāmasādhakāḥ arthasādhakāśca, nṛtyagītaprabhṛtayaḥ (prabhṛtiśabdena vāditrādiparigrahaḥ) kalāḥ tantroktāḥ catuḥṣaṭiprakārāḥ śilpavidyāviśeṣāḥ; (tāśca asmatpitāmahapādakṛtavācaspatyābhidhāne kalāśabde darśitāḥ yathā, "gītādividyābhede, sā ca catuḥṣaṣṭividhā śaivatantroktā yathāgītaṃ vādyaṃ nṛtyaṃ nāṭyam ālekhyaṃ viśāṣakacchedyaṃ taṇḍulakusumabalivikārāḥ puṣpāstaraṇaṃ daśamavasanāṅgarāgāḥ maṇibhūmikākamrma śayanaracanam udakavādyam udakaghātaḥ citrāyogāḥ mālyagrathanavikalpāḥ keśaśekharāpīḍayojanaṃ nepathyayogāḥ karṇapatrabhaṅgāḥ gandhayuktiḥ bhūṣaṇayojanam indrajālaṃ kaucumārayogāḥ hastalāghavaṃ citraśākapūpabhakṣyavikārakriyā pānakarasarāgāsavayojanaṃ sūcīvāpakamrma vīṇāḍamarukasūtrakrīḍaā prahelikā pratimā durvacakayogāḥ pustakavācanaṃ nāṭikākyāyikādarśanaṃ kāvyasamasyāpūraṇaṃ paṭṭikāvetarabāṇavikalpāḥ tarkūkarmāṇi lakṣamaṃ vāstuvidyā rūpyaratnaparīkṣā dhītuvādaḥ maṇirāgajñānam ākarajñānaṃ vṛkṣāyurvedayagāḥ meṣakukkuṭalāvakayuddhavidhiḥ śukasārikāpralāpanam utsādanaṃ keśamārjanakauśalam akṣaramuṣṭikākathanaṃ mlecchitakavikalpāḥ deśabhāṣājñānaṃ puṣpaśakaṭikānimittajñānaṃ yantrāmātṛkā dhāraṇāmātṛkā sampāṭyaṃ mānasī kāvyakriyā kriyāvikalpāḥ chalitakayogāḥ abhidhānakoṣacchandojñānaṃ vastragopanāni dyūtiviśeṣaḥ ākarṣaṇakrīḍaā bālakakrīḍanakāni vaināyikīnāṃ vidyānāṃ jñānaṃ vaijayikīnāṃ vidyānāṃ jñānaṃ vaitalikīnāṃ vidyānāṃ jñānam iti bhāgaśloke ṭīkāyāṃ śrīdharasvāmilikhitāḥ") | | 162| | 

carācarāṇāmiti| carācarāṇāṃ jaṅgamasthāvarāṇāṃ, bhūtānāṃ prāṇināṃ kṣityādīnāñca, pravṛttiḥ vātrtā, lokasaṃjñitā lokaśabdapratipādyā itrthaḥ| (loka iti saṃjñā jātā asyā iti asyajātārthe itacpratyayaḥ) hetuvidyātmakaḥ hetuḥ kāramaṃ, taḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavacitā vidyā jñānaṃ, hetuvidyā yuktimūlakaśāstraṃ, darśanaśāstramityarthaḥ, tāni ca tarkādīna boddhaśāstrāṇi ca iti jñātavyaṃ, tadātmakaḥ tanmayaḥ, nyāyaḥ yuktimūlakadarśanaśāstrameva nyāyapadavācyamiti niṣkarṣaḥ, tathā sasmṛtiḥ smṛtiḥ vedārthasmaraṇajanyam ṛṣivākyaṃ, manvādisaṃhitā iti yāvataṃ, tatsahitā śrutirvedaḥ, āgamaḥ āgamaśabdāvācya ityarthaḥ| | 163| | 

deśādivirodhalakṣaṇāmāha, teṣvati| teṣi teṣu ukteṣu deśādiṣu, ayathārūḍham aprasiddhaṃ, kiñcit yadi kaveḥ kavayituḥ, pramādāt anavadānāt, pravatrtate upanyasto bhavati ityevaṃrūpeṇa varṇito bhavatītyarthaḥ, tadā etat īdṛśaṃ vacanaṃ, deśādivirodhi tadākhyadoṣavadityarthaḥ, iti ucyate, deśavirodhi kālavirodhi ityādisaṃjñayā abhidhīyate| (deśakālalokavirodhastu khyātaviruddhatāsaṃjñayā kalānyāyāgamavirodhaśca vidyāviruddhatāsaṃjñayā navīnairabhihita iti boddhavyam| sasmṛtiśrutirityatra śrutipadena kāmakṛṣigajaturagakhaṅgādilakṣaṇaśāstrāṇāṃ grahaṇamāganyate iti dik) | | 164| | 

deśavirodhamudāharati, karpūreti| malayānilaḥ malayācalapravāhitaḥ vāyuḥ karparapādapānāṃ karparavṛkṣāṇām, āmarsena samparkeṇa, surabhiḥ, sugandhiḥ, tathā kaliḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍagavanasambhītāḥ kaliṅgadeśīyāraṇyasamutpannāḥ, majaṅgajāhastinaḥ, mṛgaprāyāḥ mṛgavat atikṣudrā ityarthaḥ| (atra malayādrau karpūrapādānāṃ notpattiḥ, cīnādidese eva tadutpattiriti prasiddhābhāvāt adrirūpa deśavirodhaḥ; tathā kaliṅgavane mṛgasadṛsāḥ kṣadratarā mataṅjāḥ na sambhavanti ityaprasiddavarṇanāt vanarūpadeśavirodhaśca vijñeyaḥ) | | 165| | 

adrivanarūpayordeśayorudāharamaṃ darśayitvā rāṣṭrarūpadeśavirodhamudāharati, colā iti| colāḥ karṇāṭāntargatadeśabhedāḥ, kālagurubhiḥ kṛṣṇavarṇāgurubhiḥ, śyāmāḥ kṛṣṇavarṇāḥ kāveyryāḥ kāverīnadyāḥ tīrabhūmayaḥ yeṣu tathoktāḥ| (atra kāverītīre kālaguravo na jāyante ita rāṣṭrarūpadeśavirodhaḥ) deśavirodhinyāḥ vācaḥ iti īdṛśam uktarūpaṃ, prāsthānaṃ prakṛṣṭaṃ sthānaṃ dṛṣṭāntasthalamityarthaḥ| (atra "colāḥ kālaguruśyāmāḥ keralāḥ kuṅkumāruṇāḥ| nameruvanasaṃcchannāḥ kāverītīrabhūmayaḥ| | "iti pāṭhāntare pustakāntareṣu dṛśyate| udāharaṇāntaraṃ yathā, "sauvīreṣvasti nagarī mathurānāma viśrutā| akṣoṭanālikerāḍhyā yasyāḥ payryantabhūmayaḥ| | " iti) | | 166| | 

"kālo rātrindivatrtavaḥ" ityuktalakṣaṇasya rātridivasaṣaḍaturūpasya kālasya yathākramaṃ pirodhaṃ darśayati, padminīti| padmini naktaṃ rātrau, unnadrā vikasitā| (divasa eva padminyā vikāsasya prasiddheḥ rātrāviti nidrdeśāt atra rātrirūpakālavirodhaḥ) | kumudvatī aḍhiddhaṭhṭhaḍanadivā, sphuṭati vikasati| (rātrāveva kumudinyā vikāsasya prasiddheḥ aḍhiddhaṭhṭhaḍana ityukteḥ atra divārūpakālavirodhaḥ) madhuḥ vasantaḥ, utphullāḥ vikasatpuṣpāḥ, niculāḥsa ijjalavṛkṣā yatra tathoktaḥ| (niculānāṃ prāvṛṣyevasasya prasiddheḥ miti varṇanāt atra vasantatrturūpakālavirodhaḥ) | nadāghaḥ grīṣmaḥ, meghena meghāvaraṇena, durdinaṃ duḥkhajanakadinaṃ yatra tathoktaḥ| (varṣāsveva durdinatvaprasiddheḥ nidāgha iti kathānāt atra nidāghatrturūpakālavirodhaḥ| yadyapi nidāghe'pi kadācit durdinatvaṃ sambhavati, varṇitañca akāladurdinaṃ mṛcchakaṭukādau, tathā'pyatra virodhapartipādanāt ṛtuviśeṣe prasiddhasya vastunaḥ ṛtvantare varṇanameva doṣa iti nidāgho meghadurdina ityanena sūcyate| kecittu "nidāgho himajāṅyākṛt" iti pāṭhāntaramaṅgīkṛtya samādhānaṃ kurvanti| "nidāghī meghaniḥsvanaḥ" iti ca pāṭhāntaram) | | 167| | 

śravyeti| varṣāḥ śravyāḥ śrutisukhaghakaratvāt madhurā ityarthaḥ, haṃsānāṃ giraḥ yāsu tāḥ (atra śaratsveva haṃsānāṃ madhuranādasya prasiddheḥ varṣāḥ iti kālavirodhaḥ) śaradaḥ mattāḥ hṛṣṭāḥ, varhiṇaḥ mayūrāḥ yāsu tāḥ| (atra varṣāsveva mayūrāṇāṃ mattatāyāḥ prasiddheḥ śarada iti varṇanāt śaradṛturūpakālavirodhaḥ) | hemantaḥ nirmalaḥ himāvaraṇābhāvāt pradīptaḥ, ādityo yatra tathābhūtaḥ| (atra hemante ādityasya himāvṛtatvāt nirmalatvam aprasiddham iti, hemantatrturūpakālavirodhaḥ) ; tathā śiśiraḥ śītatrtuḥ, ślāghyam ādaraṇīyaṃ, candanaṃ candanānulepa ityarthaḥ, yasmin saḥ| (atra candanasya śaityāt śiśire tasya ślāghyatvam aprasiddham iti śiśiratrturūpakālavirodhaḥ| evañca ṛtūnāṃ ṣaṇāmeva virodhāḥ daśitāḥ| udāhāraṇāntaraṃ yathā, "kadambakusumasmeraṃ madhau vanamaśobhata"iti) | | 168| | 

kalāsu tauyryatrikasyaiva samadhikaprītipradatvena prādhānatāyā ādau tadvirodhameva pradarśayati, vīreti| vīraśrṛṅgarayoḥ tadākhyayoḥ rasayoḥ, krodhavismayau sthāyinau bāvau, viruddhāviti śeṣaḥ, tathā hi vīre utsāhaḥ, śrṛṅgāre ratiḥ sthāyibhāvatvena kathyate, krodhaḥ raudrarasya visamayaḥ adbhutarasasya sthāyitvena nirdiṣṭau, tadevaṃ sthite vīraśrṛṅgārarasayoḥ vyabhicārirūpayoḥ krodhavismayayoḥ sthāyitvena nirdesaḥ viruddhaḥ, sa ca doṣa eva iti bhāvaḥ| (sthāyibhāvaśca darpaṇakāreṇoktaḥ, yathā, "aviruddhā viruddhā vā yaṃ tirodhātumakṣamāḥ| āsvādāṅikurakando'sau bhāvaḥ sthāyīti sammataḥ| | " iti| "rasāvasthaḥ paraṃ bhāvaḥ sthāyitāṃ pratipadyate" iti ca| krodhavismayau ca tenaivoktau yathā, "pratikūleṣu taikṣṇyasyāvabodhaḥ krodha iṣyate| | "tathā"vividheṣu padārtheṣu lokasīmātivarttiṣu| visphāraścetaso yastu sa vismaya udāhṛtaḥ| | " iti| rasavivekasya bharatādibhiḥ nāṭyaśāstre eva kṛtatvāt, "aṣṭau nāṭye rasāḥ smṛtāḥ" iti prakāśakārokteśca nāṭyarūpakalāvirodho'yam) | gītarūpakalāyā api nāṭyāṅgatvena tathātvāttadvirodhamāha, pūrṇoti| pūrṇāḥ sākalyena prayuktāḥ, sapta saptasaṅkhyakā eva, svarāḥ niṣādādayaḥ yatra tathoktaḥ, ("ni, ādarṣabhagāndhāraṣaḍajamadhyamadhaivatāḥ| pañcamaścetyamī pta tantrīkaṇṭhotthitāḥ svarāḥ| | " ityāmaraḥ) so'yaṃ bhinnamārgaḥ bhinnaḥ tattatkālaniṣiddhasvarāsambalitaḥ, mārgaḥ prayogapanthāḥ yasya tādṛśaḥ san, pravatrtate pracalati, saṅgītaśāstre hi saptasvarāṇāmekasmin kāle prayogasya ninditatvena tatprayogo na vidheya iti bhāvaḥ| (atra sākalyena prayuktasaptasvaratvam amiśsvaraprayogasya viruddhamiti kalāvirodhadoṣa eveti phalitārthaḥ| kālaviseṣe svaraviśeṣeprayogasya niṣiddhatvam uktaṃ bharatena yathā, "prabhāte surato nindyaḥ ṛṣabhaḥ pañcamo'pi ca| janayete pradhanaṃ hyukṣā pañcatvaṃ pañcamo'pi ca| | pañcamasya viśeṣo'yaṃ kathitaḥ pūrvasūribhiḥ| prage pragīto janayeddarśanasya vipayryayam| | " iti) | | 170| | 

kālavirodhamupasaṃharati, itthamiti| ittham anena prakāreṇa, kalānāṃ catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ tasyāḥ virodhaḥ, sādhu samyak yathā tathā, nīyatāṃ jñātatāmityarthaḥ, yathā nāṭyagītarūpayoḥ kalayorvirodhaḥ pradarśitaḥ tathā anyāsāmapi kalānāṃ virodhaḥ tattadudāharaṇeṣu dṛśyatāmiti niṣkarṣaḥ| nanu catuḥṣaṣṭividhāstāvat kalā uktāḥ, tañjñānamantareṇa kathaṃ tadvirodho jñātavya ityāha, tasyā iti| kalāparicchede tadākhyagranthe, tasyāḥkalāyāḥ, rūpaṃ svarūpaṃ, nāma ityarthaḥ, avirbhaviṣyati prakaṭaṣyati, anantaraṃ kalāparicchedaṃ nāma granthaṃ kariṣyāmi tatraiva tāsāṃ viśeṣo draṣṭavya iti bhāvaḥ| | 171| | 

lokaviruddhatāṃ darśayati, ādhūteti| ayaṃ hastī ādhūtāḥ kampitāḥ , keśarā jaṭāḥ yasya tathoktaḥ, turaṅgamaḥ aśvaḥ, tīkṣṇe sūkṣmāgre, śrṛṅge ysaya tādṛsaḥ, eraṇḍaḥ tadākhyavṛkṣaḥsa, guruḥ mahān, sāraḥ sthirāṃśo yasya tathābhūtaḥ, khadiradrumaḥ niḥ nāsti sāro yasya tādṛk| (hastinaḥ sakeśaratvaṃ turaṅgamasya tīkṣṇaśrṛṅgatvam eraṇḍasya sāravattvaṃ tathā khadirataroḥ niḥsāratvaṃ lokaviruddham| atra pūrvodrdhe carabhūtayoruttarādrdhe acarabhūtayoḥ pravṛttirityavagantavyam) | | 172| | 

lokavirodhamupasaṃhṛtya nyāyavirodhaṃ prapañcayitumāha, itīti| iti uktarūpaḥ, laukikaḥ lokaprasiddha eva, ayaṃ virodhaḥ sarvaiḥ vidvidbhiḥ, garhitaḥ ninditaḥ, tathā ca deśādivirodhāpekṣayā lokaviruddhopanyāsaḥ prayokturnitarāṃ hāsyāyeti bhāvaḥ| samprati nyāyākhyāsu nyāyanāmnīṣu, hetuvidyāsu yuktimūlakaśāstreṣu, virodhaḥ nidaśryate udāhriyate| | 173| | 

saugatadarśanarūpanyāyavirodhaṃ darśayati, satyamiti| sugataḥ buddhaḥ, saṃskārān smṛtihetukabhāvanākhyātmavṛttiguṇabhedān, pūrvānubhavajanyajñānaviśeṣān ityarthaḥ, avinaśyarān cirasthāyinaḥ, satyameva yathārthameva, āha, tathā hi tamevārthaṃ jānīhi, sā pūrvānubhūtā, cakorākṣī adyāpi me hṛdi manasi, sthitaiva smaraṇaviṣayībhavatītyartaḥ, smṛtiṃ prati saṃskārasya hetutvāt tasya cāvinasvatvena sārvakālikatvāt iti bhāvaḥ| (padārthamātrasya kṣaṇabhaṅguratvamiti vādināntu teṣāṃ saugatānāṃ mate bhāvamātrasyaiva kṣaṇasthāyitvāt saṃskārasyāpi kṣaṇikatvameva atastasyāvinaśvaratvakītrtanaṃ saugatamataviruddhamiti hetuvidyāvirodhaḥ) | | 174| | 

sāṅkhyadarśanarūpanyāyavirodhaṃ daraśayati, kāpilairiti| kāpilaiḥ kapilamatānusāribhiḥ sāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍakhyāvidbhiḥ, asadudbhūtiḥ āsatām anityānāṃ duṣṭānāñca, udbhūtiḥ udbava, sthāna eva yuktameva, upavaṇryate kūttryate, smāt asmābhiḥ asakāmeva sadbhinnānāmeva, duṣṭānāmeva ityarthaḥ, udbhavāḥ prādurbhāvāḥ, dṛśyante, prāyeṇa durjanā eva jāyante na tu sādhava iti bhāvaḥ| (ayaṃ bhāvaḥ, kapilānusāriṇaḥ sataḥ sadeva jāyate na tu asaditi manyante, tataścāsadutpattirna tanmatasiddhā, evaṃ sthite'pi asacchabdasya śliṣṭatvena vākyārthayorhetuhetumadbhāvāt tasyā yuktatvavarṇanamiti sāṅkhyamataviruddhameva; tathā coktam; "asadakaraṇādupādānagrahaṇāt sarvasambhavābhāvāt| śaktasya śakyakaraṇāt kāraṇabhāvācca satkāyryam| | "iti) | | 175| | 

nyāyavirodhamupasaṃharati, gatīti| nyāyavirodhasya sā eṣā evaṃpradarśitasaugatakāpiloktaprākārā, gatiḥ jñānaṃ, dṛṣṭānta ityarthaḥ, sarvatra vaiśaṣikādiṣvapi, dṛśyate jñāyate| ("dṛśyate" ityatra "darśitā" iti pāṭhāntaram) | āgamavirodhamudāhatrtumāha, atheti| atha idānīm, āgamavirodhasya śrutismṛtivirodhasya, prasthānaṃ gatiḥ upadiśyate| | 176| | 

śrutivirodhaṃ darśayati, anāhiteti| ete akliṣṭācārakbhūṣaṇā akṣarasarvācārasamanvitāḥ, jātaputrā viprāḥ anāhitāgnayaḥ akṛtāgnyādhānāḥ api, vaiśvānarīṃ viśvānarasambandhinīṃ, (visvānarastu bhūtasamūhātmakaḥ virāṭ puruṣaḥ, tathā ca, "viśvānaramupāste vidyadādityavāyākāśodakapṛthivyātmakaiḥ ṣaḍḍḍitdh; bharupetam" iti śruteḥ) iÏṣṭa yāgaṃ, vitanvate kurvanti| (atra ca śrutau kṛtāgnyādhānādereva vaiśvānareṣṭyādhikārapratipādānāt anāhitāgnīnāṃ tadvarṇanaṃ śrutiviruddham) | | 177| | 

smṛtivirodharūpamāgamavirodhaṃ darśayati, asāviti| asau janaḥ, anupanītaḥ akṛtopanayasaṃskāraḥ api, guroḥ sakāśāt vedān adhijage adhītavān; tathā hi svabhāvena prakṛtyā eva, śuddhaḥ nirmalaḥ, sphuṭikaḥ tadākhyamaṇiviśeṣaḥ, saṃskāraṃśudiṃdha, na apekṣati| (atra anupanītasya vedādhyayanaṃ smṛtiviruddham; uktañca manunā, "nābhivyāhārayet brahmā svadhāniyamanādṛte| śūdreṇa hi samastāvat yāvat vede na jāyate| | " iti| anye tu "dhamrmārthakāmaśāstrādivirodhaḥ ko'pi yo bhavet| tamāgamavirodhākhyaṃ doṣamācakṣate budhāḥ| | " ityāgamavirodhasya lakṣaṇamabhidhāya trividhaṃ bhedamaṅgīkurvanti| tatra dharmaśāstravirodhasyodāharaṇamidameva padyamāhuḥ| arthaśāstravirodho yathā, "kāmopabhogasākalyaphalo rājñāṃ mahījayaḥ| ahaṅkāre jīyante dviṣantaḥ kiṃ nayaśriyā? "| | iti| atra mahījayasya phalatvena kāmopabhogānāmarthaśāstrakārairananumatatvāt śatruvijaye ca ahaṅkārasyāhetutvādarthaśāstravirodhaḥ| kāmaśāstravirodho yathā, "tavottaroṣṭhe vimboṣṭhi| | " iti| atra uttaroṣṭhe daśanāṅkasya kāmaśāstrakārairananujñātatvāt kāmaśāstravirodhaḥ iti) | | 178| | 

prāguktadeśādivirodhākhyadoṣasya kadācit guṇatvamāha, virodha iti| eṣa uktarūpaḥ, sakalaḥ samagraḥ api, deśādyāgamapayryantaviṣayo'pītyarthaḥ, virodhaḥ kaveḥ kauśalāt varṇanācātuyryeṇa, viruddhavarṇanasyāpi vaicitryapratipādanena ityarthaḥ, kadācit doṣagaṇanāṃ doṣāntarbhūtatām, utkramya vihāya, guṇavīthīṃ guṇamārgaṃ, vigāhate guṇatvena gaṇanīyo bhavatītyarthaḥ| | 179| | 

tatra deśavirodhasya guṇatvaṃ darśayati, tasyeti| tasya rājñaḥ, prabhāveṇa mahim nā, tadudyānāni tasya rājñaḥ udyānāni, ādrrāṇi navāni ityarthaḥ, aṃśukāni vastrāṇi eva, prakābālāni kiśalayāni yeṣāṃ tādṛśānāṃ, suraśākhinā devatarūṇāṃ, kalpavṛkṣāṇāmityarthaḥ, āspadaṃ sthānaṃ, jajñire lebhire ityarthaḥ| (atra mānavodyāneṣu suraśākhisambhavo deśaviruddhatayā varṇito'pi varṇanīyasya rājñaḥ prabhāvātiśayaṃ tathā aiśvayryātiśayavarṇapodāttālaṅkārapratipādanāt kaveḥ kauśalañca dyotayatīti camatkāravyañjanāt guṇavattvamāpadyate iti phalitārthaḥ) | | 180| | 

kālavirodhasya kadācit guṇatvamudāharati, rājñāmiti| rājñāṃ pratipakṣanṛpāṇāṃ, vināśapiśunaḥ vināśasūcakaḥ, ("piśunau khalasūcakau" ityamaraḥ) kharamārutaḥ pracaṇḍavāyuḥ saptacchadodagamān saptaparṇapuṣpāṇi, dhanvan kampayan, kadambarajasā nopaparāgeṇa saha, cacāra vavau| (atra rājñāmityanenapratipakṣottyā varṇanīyasya rājñaḥ yuddhayātrāyāḥ pratītiḥ, mā ca yuddhāyātrā saptacchadodgamaśca śaratsu eva bhavati, kadambapuṣpāṇi tu tadā na jāyante varṣāsveva teṣāṃ prādurbhāvāt; tataśca tadrajasā saha saptacchadodgamasyaikakālatacvapratipādanāt kālavirodhaḥ| "akāle phalapuṣpaṇi deśavidaravakāraṇam" iti viṣṇudharmottarīyavacanena akāle'pi phalapuṣpodgamasya sambhāvanaṃ, tathā kharamārutavat tasyāśubhasūcakatvañca pratīyate, tena pratipakṣīyanarapatīnāṃ niyatavināśasūcanāt vijigīṣoḥ utkarṣātiśayavyañjakatayā guṇa eva, kavikauśalañcātra tādṛsavyaṅgyapratipādanameva ityavadheyam) | | 181| | 

kalāvirodhasya kvacit guṇatvamudāharati, dolti| dolāyāḥ yānaviśeṣasya, abhipreraṇena savegacālanena, trastā bhītāḥ, ye babhūjanāḥ teṣāṃ mukhebhyaḥ udgatam uccaritaṃ, layasya gītavādyapādādinyāmānāṃ kriyākālayoḥ sāmyasya, vaiṣamyaṃ veparītyaṃ yatra tādṛśama, apīti śeṣaḥ, geyaṃ gītaṃ, bhayacakitatvāt layāsaṅgatamapi gānamityarthaḥ, kāmināṃ rāgam anurāgam, avadrdhayat| (gānasya layaśuddhireva rāgavadrdhanīti kalāniyamaḥ, atra ca tadvaiparītyamiti kalāvirodhaḥ, badhūjanān prati kāmināmatyanurāgavyañjakatayāsa guṇa eva iti) | | 182| | 

lokavirodhasyāpi guṇatvamudāharati, aindavāditi| ayaṃ kāmo kāmaparatantraḥ, abalāyā viraheṇa yaḥ kleśaḥ, tena viḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavalaḥ vyākulaḥ san, aindevāt candrasambandhinaḥ, arciṣaḥ kiraṇāt, kiraṇamapekṣyetyarthaḥ, (lyablope pañcamī) havyavāhanam aÏgna, śiśiraṃ śītalaṃ, gaṇayati manyate| (atra havyavāhanasya śiśiratvaṃ lokaviruddhaṃ, tacca virahiṇāṃ candrakiraṇasyātyuddīpakatvavyañjanāt guṇa eva) | | 183| | 

nyāyavirodhasya guṇatvamudāharati, prameya ita| bhagavatstutiriyam| prameyaḥ pramāṇajanyajñānaviṣayo'pi, aprameyaḥ ajñeyaḥ asi, saphalaḥ phalaṃ kāyryaṃ, viśvarūpamityarthaḥ, tatsahitaḥ api, niṣphalaḥ phalarahitaḥ, kāyryeṣu nirlipta ityarthaḥ, asi, tvam eko'dvitīyo'pi, anekaḥbahurūpaḥ, asi, ataḥ viśvamūtrtaye jagatsvarūpāya, te tubhyaṃ, namaḥ ityanvayaḥ| (prameyatvādikaṃ parasparaviruddhaṃ naikādhāravatrtīti naiyāyikī yuktiḥ, sā cātra viruddhatayā bhāsamānā'pi paramātmano'cintyatacvaṃ vyañjayanto guṇatvamevārohati) | | 184| | 

āgamavirodhasya guṇatvamudāharati, pañcanāmiti| pāñcalaputrikā draupadī, pañcānāṃ pāṇḍuputrāṇāṃ yudhiṣṭhirabhīmārjunanakulasahadevānāṃ, patnī bhāyryā, athaca satīnāṃ sādhvīnām, agraṇīḥ śreṣṭhā ca, āsīt, hi yataḥ daivaḥ devasasambandhau, vidhiḥ vidhānam, īdṛśaḥ evaṃvidhaḥ, āgamānadhīnaḥ ityarthaḥ, teṣāṃ tasyāśca devāṃśatayā śāstroktatavāditi bhāvaḥ| (ekasyā bahupatitvaṃ satītvañca āgamaviruddhaṃ, taccātra daivavidheḥ śāstrānadhīnatayā vaicitryamāvahadguṇa eveti| kecittu dhamrmaviruddhe udāharaṇamidaṃ manyante) | | 185| | 

prākpratijñātaguṇālaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍakārādinirūpaṇānantaraṃ granthamimamupasaṃhārati, śabdeti| iha asmin kāvyādarśākhye granthe, kāvyānāṃ śabdārthālaṅkiyāḥ śabdārthobhayaniṣṭhāḥ svabhāvākhyānādayaḥ alaṅkārāḥ, teṣāṃ śabdārthobhayaniṣṭhāḥ svabhāvākhyānādayaḥ alaṅkārāḥ, teṣāṃ śabdārthābhyāṃ śobhājanakatvādubhayaniṣṭhatvamityabhiprāyaḥ, tathā sukarā duṣkarāśca citramārgāḥ citrālaṅkārāṇāṃ mārgā niyamāḥ, śabdamātraniṣṭhā yamakādaya ityarthaḥ, tathā guṇāḥ śleṣādayaḥ, doṣāśca apārthatvādayaḥ, (cakārāt kāvyalakṣaṇa tanmargādīnāṃ grahaṇam) saṅkṣipya darśitāḥ saṅkṣepeṇa nirīpitāḥ, bahubhedatayā ca sākalyena guṇādīnāṃ bhedasyādarśitatvāditi bhāvaḥ| | 186| | 

saṅkṣepadarśite'pyasmin kathaṃ phalasiddhirbhavatītyupapādayan granthaṃ samāpayati, vyutpanneti| vyutpannā saṃskṛtā, buddhiryasya tathoktaḥ viśuddhamatirityarthaḥ, dhanyaḥ sukṛto, janaḥ iti śeṣaḥ, amunā etadgranthoktena, vidhidarśiṃtena vidhayo lakṣaṇāni, tairdarśitena nirdiṣṭena, doṣaguṇayoḥ apakarṣotkarṣadhamrmayoḥ proktalakṣaṇayoriti yāvat, mārgeṇa pathā, rītyā ityarthaḥ, (guṇapadamatrālaṅkārāṇāmulakṣaka, teṣāmapi kāvyaśobhājanakatvenotkaparṣādhāyakatvāditi bādhyam) kṛtam abhisaraṇama anugamanaṃ yena tādṛśaḥ san, kāvyanirmitau tadavagatau vā kṛtayatnaḥsan ityarthaḥ, vaśavarttinībhiḥ, vaśavarttinībhiḥ premādhīnābhiḥ, kāntābhiḥ, vidhidarśitena vidyutaprakāśādirūpadaivaprākāśitena, doṣaguṇayormārgeṇa anena pathā gamane mahān doṣaḥ, anena pathā ca tadabhāvarūpaḥ gumaḥ, ityevaṃ vivicya nirūpitena pathetyarthaḥ, kṛtābhisaraṇaḥ kṛtābhisāraḥ, yuvā iva, ramate prītimanubhavati, kīrttiśca labhate, (yathā kaścityuvakaḥ daivānukūlatayāparijñātena supathāvahāṅganābhisārāya gatvā tadaṅganāpremāspadībhūtaḥ prītaḥ kīrttimāṃścabhavati, tathā deśākāladiviṣayapariśīlanakuśalamatiḥ sukṛtau matpradarśitasaṅkṣiptaguṇālaṅkāradoṣādilakṣaṇaghaṭakavākyārthaparijñānena kāvyamavagantuṃ katrtuṃ vāpravṛttaḥsan saphalatayā prītiḥ labdhakīrttiśca bhavatīti bhāvaḥ") | vasantatilakaṃ vṛttam| | 187| | 



iti śrīmajjīvānandavidyāsāgarabhaṭṭācāryaviracitākāvyādarśavivṛtiḥ
